        ap += float(np.max(prec[mask])) if np.any(mask) else 0.0
    return float(ap / 101.0)


def _deepx_compute_mini_coco_ap50(samples: List[Dict[str, Any]], detections_by_image: List[Dict[str, Any]], *, iou_thr: float = 0.5) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Compute a small COCO-style AP50 for DeepX full detections.

    The result intentionally matches the lightweight AP50 used by the ORT/TRT
    split runner closely enough for smoke/reporting.  It uses COCO category-id
    mapping to model class indices and 101-point AP at IoU=0.5.
    """
    import numpy as np  # type: ignore
    gt_by_image: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    for sample in list(samples or []):
        key = str(Path(str(sample.get("path") or "")).name)
        anns = sample.get("annotations") if isinstance(sample.get("annotations"), list) else []
        gt: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for ann in list(anns or []):
            if not isinstance(ann, dict):
                continue
            det = _deepx_coco_ann_to_det(ann)
            if det is not None:
                gt.append(det)
        if gt:
            gt_by_image[key] = gt

    pred_by_image: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    for rec in list(detections_by_image or []):
        key = str(Path(str(rec.get("image") or "")).name)
        preds: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for det_index, detection in enumerate(list(rec.get("detections") or [])):
            if not isinstance(detection, Mapping):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"deepx_quality_detection_not_mapping:{key}:{det_index}"
                )
            box, score, class_id = _deepx_detection_record_components(
                detection, image_id=f"{key}:{det_index}",
            )
            preds.append({
                "class_id": int(class_id),
                "score": float(score),
                "x1": float(box[0]),
                "y1": float(box[1]),
                "x2": float(box[2]),
                "y2": float(box[3]),
            })
        pred_by_image[key] = preds

    class_ids = sorted({int(d.get("class_id", -1)) for dets in gt_by_image.values() for d in dets if int(d.get("class_id", -1)) >= 0})
    ap_values: List[float] = []
    total_gt = 0
    total_pred = 0
    per_class: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for cls_id in class_ids:
        gt_records: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        preds: List[Tuple[float, str, List[float]]] = []
        gt_count = 0
        pred_count = 0
        for image_id, dets in gt_by_image.items():
            boxes = []
            for d in list(dets or []):
                if int(d.get("class_id", -1)) == int(cls_id):
                    boxes.append([float(d.get("x1",0.0)), float(d.get("y1",0.0)), float(d.get("x2",0.0)), float(d.get("y2",0.0))])
            gt_records[image_id] = {"boxes": boxes, "matched": [False] * len(boxes)}
            gt_count += len(boxes)
        for image_id, dets in pred_by_image.items():
            for d in list(dets or []):
                if int(d.get("class_id", -1)) != int(cls_id):
                    continue
                preds.append((float(d.get("score", 0.0)), image_id, [float(d.get("x1",0.0)), float(d.get("y1",0.0)), float(d.get("x2",0.0)), float(d.get("y2",0.0))]))
                pred_count += 1
        total_gt += int(gt_count)
        total_pred += int(pred_count)
        if gt_count <= 0:
            continue
        preds.sort(key=lambda x: float(x[0]), reverse=True)
        tp = np.zeros((len(preds),), dtype=np.float32)
        fp = np.zeros((len(preds),), dtype=np.float32)
        for i, (_score, image_id, box) in enumerate(preds):
            rec = gt_records.get(image_id) or {"boxes": [], "matched": []}
            best_iou = 0.0
            best_j = -1
            for j, gt_box in enumerate(list(rec.get("boxes") or [])):
                matched = list(rec.get("matched") or [])
                if j < len(matched) and matched[j]:
                    continue
                iou = _deepx_box_iou_xyxy(box, gt_box)
                if iou > best_iou:
                    best_iou = float(iou); best_j = int(j)
            if best_j >= 0 and best_iou >= float(iou_thr):
                rec["matched"][best_j] = True
                tp[i] = 1.0
            else:
                fp[i] = 1.0
        tp_cum = np.cumsum(tp, dtype=np.float32)
        fp_cum = np.cumsum(fp, dtype=np.float32)
        recall = tp_cum / float(max(gt_count, 1))
        precision = tp_cum / np.maximum(tp_cum + fp_cum, 1e-12)
        ap50 = _deepx_mini_ap50_101(recall, precision)
        ap_values.append(float(ap50))
        per_class[str(cls_id)] = {"class_id": int(cls_id), "gt_instances": int(gt_count), "predictions": int(pred_count), "ap50": float(ap50)}
    return {
        "enabled": bool(gt_by_image),
        "status": "ok" if gt_by_image else "no_annotations",
        "iou_threshold": float(iou_thr),
        "images": int(len(gt_by_image)),
        "classes_with_gt": int(len(ap_values)),
        "gt_instances": int(total_gt),
        "predictions": int(total_pred),
        "ap50": (float(sum(ap_values) / len(ap_values)) if ap_values else None),
        "ap50_by_class": per_class,
    }

def _deepx_contract_candidates(root: Path, run: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Tuple[Path, str]]:
    """Return likely DeepX contract/config paths for full semantic validation.

    Older benchmark suites do not always carry a per-run
    ``deepx/deepx_m1/full/output_contract.json``.  In evaluation mode the most
    reliable metadata is often the aggregated ``deepx/deepx_output_contracts``
    file or the DX-COM config.  The semantic validator must use this metadata;
    otherwise classification models silently fall back to the YOLO default of
    640x640 and produce useless Top-k values.
    """
    out: List[Tuple[Path, str]] = []

    def add(raw: Any, label: str) -> None:
        if raw is None:
            return
        text = str(raw).strip()
        if not text:
            return
        q = Path(text).expanduser()
        if not q.is_absolute():
            q = (root / q).resolve()
        out.append((q, label))

    add(run.get("contract_path"), "run.contract_path")
    add(run.get("output_contract"), "run.output_contract")
    add(run.get("deepx_output_contract"), "run.deepx_output_contract")
    add(run.get("config_path"), "run.config_path")
    add(run.get("dxcom_config"), "run.dxcom_config")

    # Per-suite common locations.
    for rel, label in [
        ("deepx/deepx_m1/full/output_contract.json", "suite.full.output_contract"),
        ("deepx/deepx_output_contracts.json", "suite.deepx_output_contracts"),
        ("deepx/full/config_deepx.json", "suite.deepx_full_config"),
        ("deepx/deepx_m1/full/config_deepx.json", "suite.deepx_m1_full_config"),
        ("deepx/deepx_m1/full/config_yolo11.json", "suite.deepx_m1_full_config_yolo"),
    ]:
        add(rel, label)

    # Nearby paths around the DXNN artefact.
    try:
        dxnn_rel = str(run.get("dxnn_path") or run.get("artifact") or "deepx/deepx_m1/full/model.dxnn").strip()
        dxnn = Path(dxnn_rel).expanduser()
        if not dxnn.is_absolute():
            dxnn = (root / dxnn).resolve()
        parent = dxnn.parent
        for name in ["output_contract.json", "config_deepx.json", "config_yolo11.json"]:
            out.append((parent / name, f"dxnn_sibling.{name}"))
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Deduplicate while preserving order.
    seen = set()
    uniq: List[Tuple[Path, str]] = []
    for q, label in out:
        key = str(q)
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        uniq.append((q, label))
    return uniq


def _deepx_endpoint_json_sha256(value: Any) -> str:
    encoded = json.dumps(
        value, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False,
    ).encode("utf-8")
    return hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()


def _deepx_authoritative_endpoint_attestation(
    root: Path, run: Dict[str, Any],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Load the recorded DeepX Full endpoint decision from output_contracts.

    A runtime tensor shape is an observation, not a semantic declaration.
    Only the workflow's backend-specific, ``recorded`` contract may attest
    whether a DeepX Full output is decoded/final or needs a host tail.
    """
    path = root / "output_contracts.json"
    if not path.is_file():
        return {
            "status": "unavailable", "pass": False,
            "reason": "authoritative_output_contracts_missing", "path": str(path),
        }
    try:
        payload = _read_json(path)
    except Exception as exc:
        return {
            "status": "conflict", "pass": False,
            "reason": "authoritative_output_contracts_unreadable",
            "path": str(path), "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
        }
    if not isinstance(payload, dict) or not isinstance(payload.get("contracts"), list):
        return {
            "status": "conflict", "pass": False,
            "reason": "authoritative_output_contracts_invalid", "path": str(path),
        }
    expected_model = str(payload.get("model_id") or run.get("model_id") or "").strip()
    matches: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for raw in list(payload.get("contracts") or []):
        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
            continue
        backend = str(raw.get("backend") or "").strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
        variant = str(raw.get("variant") or "full").strip().lower()
        model_id = str(raw.get("model_id") or expected_model).strip()
        if backend not in {"deepx", "deepx_m1", "dx_m1", "dxm1"} or variant != "full":
            continue
        if expected_model and model_id and model_id != expected_model:
            continue
        matches.append(dict(raw))
    if not matches:
        return {
            "status": "unavailable", "pass": False,
            "reason": "recorded_deepx_full_endpoint_contract_missing", "path": str(path),
        }
    signatures = set()
    for contract in matches:
        endpoint_mode = str(contract.get("endpoint_mode") or "").strip().lower()
        host_tail = contract.get("host_tail_required")
        postprocessing = contract.get("postprocessing_required")
        valid = bool(
            str(contract.get("contract_status") or "").strip().lower() == "recorded"
            and endpoint_mode in {
                "decoded", "decoded_pre_nms", "raw_detection_head",
            }
            and isinstance(host_tail, bool)
            and isinstance(postprocessing, bool)
            and (
                (endpoint_mode == "decoded" and not host_tail and not postprocessing)
                or (
                    endpoint_mode in {
                        "decoded_pre_nms", "raw_detection_head",
                    }
                    and host_tail and postprocessing
                )
            )
        )
        if not valid:
            return {
                "status": "conflict", "pass": False,
                "reason": "recorded_deepx_full_endpoint_contract_invalid",
                "path": str(path), "invalid_contract": contract,
            }
        signatures.add((endpoint_mode, host_tail, postprocessing))
    if len(signatures) != 1:
        return {
            "status": "conflict", "pass": False,
            "reason": "recorded_deepx_full_endpoint_contract_ambiguous",
            "path": str(path), "signatures": [list(item) for item in sorted(signatures)],
        }
    endpoint_mode, host_tail, postprocessing = next(iter(signatures))
    authoritative = dict(matches[0])
    attestation = {
        "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/deepx-endpoint-semantic-attestation",
        "schema_version": 1,
        "status": "attested", "pass": True,
        "reason": "authoritative_recorded_output_contract_bound",
        "model_id": str(authoritative.get("model_id") or expected_model),
        "backend": "deepx_m1", "variant": "full",
        "endpoint_mode": endpoint_mode,
        "host_tail_required": bool(host_tail),
        "postprocessing_required": bool(postprocessing),
        "source_endpoint_semantics": (
            (
                "decoded_final_output"
                if endpoint_mode == "decoded"
                else endpoint_mode
            )
        ),
        "source_endpoint_has_integrated_nms": bool(endpoint_mode == "decoded"),
        "authoritative_contract": authoritative,
        "authoritative_contract_sha256": _deepx_endpoint_json_sha256(authoritative),
        "source_path": str(path),
        "source_file_sha256": hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
    }
    task = str(run.get("benchmark_task") or "").strip().lower()
    if task == "detection" and endpoint_mode == "decoded":
        attestation.update({
            "stage": "decoded_nms",
            "output_format": "bn6_detections",
            "declared_output_semantics": "xyxy_score_class",
            "semantic_attestation_policy": "recorded_endpoint_plus_runtime_value_validation",
        })
    elif task == "detection" and endpoint_mode == "decoded_pre_nms":
        attestation.update({
            "stage": "decoded_pre_nms",
            "output_format": "ultralytics_decoded",
            "declared_output_semantics": "xywh_class_scores_pre_nms",
            "semantic_attestation_policy": (
                "recorded_endpoint_plus_runtime_structure_validation"
            ),
        })
    return attestation


def _deepx_bind_authoritative_endpoint_contract(
    root: Path, run: Dict[str, Any], artifact_contract: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    if not isinstance(artifact_contract, dict):
        return artifact_contract
    bound = dict(artifact_contract)
    authoritative = _deepx_authoritative_endpoint_attestation(root, run)
    if not bool(authoritative.get("pass")):
        bound["endpoint_contract_binding_status"] = str(authoritative.get("status") or "unavailable")
        bound["endpoint_contract_binding_reason"] = str(authoritative.get("reason") or "")
        bound["endpoint_semantic_attestation"] = authoritative
        return bound

    expected_mode = str(authoritative.get("endpoint_mode") or "")
    expected_host = bool(authoritative.get("host_tail_required"))
    expected_post = bool(authoritative.get("postprocessing_required"))
    conflicts: List[str] = []
    authoritative_model_id = str(
        authoritative.get("model_id") or ""
    ).strip()
    artifact_model_id = str(bound.get("model_id") or "").strip()
    if (
        artifact_model_id and authoritative_model_id
        and artifact_model_id != authoritative_model_id
    ):
        conflicts.append("model_id")
    if bound.get("endpoint_mode") not in (None, "", expected_mode):
        conflicts.append("endpoint_mode")
    if bound.get("host_tail_required") is not None and bound.get("host_tail_required") is not expected_host:
        conflicts.append("host_tail_required")
    if bound.get("postprocessing_required") is not None and bound.get("postprocessing_required") is not expected_post:
        conflicts.append("postprocessing_required")
    post = bound.get("postprocessing") if isinstance(bound.get("postprocessing"), dict) else {}
    if post.get("host_required") is not None and post.get("host_required") is not expected_host:
        conflicts.append("postprocessing.host_required")
    if post.get("nms_on_host") is not None and post.get("nms_on_host") is not expected_post:
        conflicts.append("postprocessing.nms_on_host")
    embedded = bound.get("endpoint_semantic_attestation") if isinstance(bound.get("endpoint_semantic_attestation"), dict) else None
    if embedded is not None:
        embedded_contract = embedded.get("authoritative_contract")
        embedded_sha = str(embedded.get("authoritative_contract_sha256") or "")
        if not isinstance(embedded_contract, dict) or embedded_sha != _deepx_endpoint_json_sha256(embedded_contract):
            conflicts.append("embedded_attestation_hash")
        elif embedded_sha != str(authoritative.get("authoritative_contract_sha256") or ""):
            conflicts.append("embedded_attestation_source")
        for key in ("endpoint_mode", "host_tail_required", "postprocessing_required"):
            if embedded.get(key) != authoritative.get(key):
                conflicts.append("embedded_attestation." + key)
    if conflicts:
        bound["endpoint_contract_binding_status"] = "conflict"
        bound["endpoint_contract_binding_reason"] = "per_artifact_endpoint_contract_disagrees_with_recorded_contract"
        bound["endpoint_contract_binding_conflicts"] = sorted(set(conflicts))
        bound["endpoint_semantic_attestation"] = authoritative
        return bound

    task = str(run.get("benchmark_task") or (bound.get("input") or {}).get("task") or "").strip().lower()
    bound["model_id"] = authoritative_model_id
    bound["endpoint_mode"] = expected_mode
    bound["host_tail_required"] = expected_host
    bound["postprocessing_required"] = expected_post
    bound["endpoint_semantic_attestation"] = authoritative
    bound["endpoint_contract_binding_status"] = "attested"
    bound["endpoint_contract_binding_reason"] = "authoritative_recorded_output_contract_bound"
    if task == "detection" and expected_mode == "decoded":
        bound["contract_family"] = "decoded_nms"
        bound["postprocessing"] = {
            "type": "integrated_decoded_nms", "host_required": False, "nms_on_host": False,
        }
    elif task == "detection" and expected_mode == "decoded_pre_nms":
        bound["contract_family"] = "decoded_pre_nms"
        bound["postprocessing"] = {
            "type": "ultralytics_decoded_classaware_nms", "host_required": True, "nms_on_host": True,
        }
    elif task == "detection" and expected_mode == "raw_detection_head":
        bound["contract_family"] = "raw_head"
        bound["postprocessing"] = {
            "type": "yolo_host_decode", "host_required": True, "nms_on_host": True,
        }
    return bound


def _deepx_decoded_bn6_attestation(contract: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    if not isinstance(contract, dict):
        return False, "endpoint_contract_missing"
    if str(contract.get("endpoint_contract_binding_status") or "") != "attested":
        return False, str(contract.get("endpoint_contract_binding_reason") or "endpoint_contract_not_bound")
    attestation = contract.get("endpoint_semantic_attestation")
    if not isinstance(attestation, dict) or not bool(attestation.get("pass")):
        return False, "endpoint_semantic_attestation_missing"
    authoritative = attestation.get("authoritative_contract")
    declared_sha = str(attestation.get("authoritative_contract_sha256") or "")
    if not isinstance(authoritative, dict) or declared_sha != _deepx_endpoint_json_sha256(authoritative):
        return False, "endpoint_semantic_attestation_hash_invalid"
    required = {
        "endpoint_mode": "decoded",
        "host_tail_required": False,
        "postprocessing_required": False,
    }
    for key, value in required.items():
        if attestation.get(key) != value or authoritative.get(key) != value or contract.get(key) != value:
            return False, "endpoint_semantic_attestation_" + key + "_mismatch"
    if (
        attestation.get("stage") != "decoded_nms"
        or attestation.get("output_format") != "bn6_detections"
        or attestation.get("declared_output_semantics") != "xyxy_score_class"
    ):
        return False, "endpoint_semantic_attestation_bn6_semantics_missing"
    post = contract.get("postprocessing") if isinstance(contract.get("postprocessing"), dict) else {}
    if post.get("host_required") is not False or post.get("nms_on_host") is not False:
        return False, "endpoint_semantic_attestation_host_tail_mismatch"
    return True, "authoritative_recorded_decoded_endpoint"


def _deepx_bn6_runtime_semantic_attestation(
    output: Any, contract: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Combine the recorded declaration with runtime value semantics.

    ``[1,N,6]`` is necessary but never sufficient: the candidate must also be
    finite and behave as ``xyxy, score, class``.  This mirrors the native
    endpoint gate while remaining self-contained in the generated suite.
    """
    import numpy as np  # type: ignore
    declared, declaration_reason = _deepx_decoded_bn6_attestation(contract)
    try:
        array = np.asarray(output)
    except Exception as exc:
        return {
            "status": "failed", "pass": False, "attested": False,
            "reason": "runtime_output_not_array",
            "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
            "declaration_reason": declaration_reason,
        }
    shape = [int(value) for value in array.shape]
    base = {
        "output_shape": shape, "output_dtype": str(array.dtype),
        "declaration_attested": bool(declared),
        "declaration_reason": declaration_reason,
    }
    if not declared:
        return {"status": "failed", "pass": False, "attested": False, "reason": declaration_reason, **base}
    if array.ndim != 3 or array.shape[0] != 1 or array.shape[-1] != 6 or array.shape[1] <= 0:
        return {"status": "failed", "pass": False, "attested": False, "reason": "exact_nonempty_bn6_shape_required", **base}
    try:
        rows = np.asarray(array, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 6)
    except Exception as exc:
        return {
            "status": "failed", "pass": False, "attested": False,
            "reason": "runtime_output_not_numeric", "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", **base,
        }
    finite = np.isfinite(rows).all(axis=1)
    score_valid = (rows[:, 4] >= -1e-6) & (rows[:, 4] <= 1.0 + 1e-6)
    class_valid = (rows[:, 5] >= -1e-6) & (np.abs(rows[:, 5] - np.rint(rows[:, 5])) <= 1e-4)
    xyxy_valid = (rows[:, 2] + 1e-6 >= rows[:, 0]) & (rows[:, 3] + 1e-6 >= rows[:, 1])
    fractions = {
        "finite_fraction": float(np.mean(finite)),
        "score_range_fraction": float(np.mean(score_valid)),
        "integer_nonnegative_class_fraction": float(np.mean(class_valid)),
        "ordered_xyxy_fraction": float(np.mean(xyxy_valid)),
    }
    threshold = 1.0
    failures = [name for name, value in fractions.items() if value < threshold]
    if failures:
        return {
            "status": "failed", "pass": False, "attested": False,
            "reason": "runtime_bn6_semantics_failed:" + ",".join(failures),
            "fraction_threshold": threshold, **fractions, **base,
        }
    return {
        "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/deepx-bn6-runtime-semantic-attestation",
        "schema_version": 1,
        "status": "passed", "pass": True, "attested": True,
        "reason": "recorded_decoded_endpoint_and_runtime_xyxy_score_class_verified",
        "values_decoded_xyxy_score_class": True,
        "source_endpoint_has_integrated_nms": True,
        "fraction_threshold": threshold, "row_count": int(rows.shape[0]),
        **fractions, **base,
    }


def _deepx_input_geometry(payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Resolve an explicit DeepX image-input contract without guessing layout.

    DX-COM full-model contracts describe the *runtime* input, which is commonly
    an unbatched HWC image (for example ``[224, 224, 3]``).  The previous
    resolver silently required four dimensions, discarded these valid
    contracts, and later reported every contract field as missing.  Layout is
    mandatory here so a three-dimensional shape can never be interpreted by a
    channel-position heuristic.
    """
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        return None
    inp = payload.get("input") if isinstance(payload.get("input"), dict) else None
    if not isinstance(inp, dict):
        return None
    shape_raw = inp.get("shape")
    layout = str(inp.get("layout") or "").strip().upper()
    expected_rank = {"HWC": 3, "CHW": 3, "NHWC": 4, "NCHW": 4}.get(layout)
    if expected_rank is None or not isinstance(shape_raw, (list, tuple)) or len(shape_raw) != expected_rank:
        return None
    shape: List[int] = []
    try:
        for raw in shape_raw:
            value = int(raw)
            # Reject lossy coercion (for example 224.5 -> 224) and dynamic or
            # non-positive dimensions.  Prepared-feed evidence must bind an
            # exact runtime tensor.
            if isinstance(raw, float) and float(raw) != float(value):
                return None
            if value <= 0:
                return None
            shape.append(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
        return None

    if layout == "HWC":
        height, width, channels = shape
        batch = None
        batch_shape = [1, height, width, channels]
    elif layout == "CHW":
        channels, height, width = shape
        batch = None
        batch_shape = [1, channels, height, width]
    elif layout == "NHWC":
        batch, height, width, channels = shape
        batch_shape = list(shape)
    else:  # NCHW
        batch, channels, height, width = shape
        batch_shape = list(shape)

    # The benchmark repeats one prepared image.  Multi-image batch contracts
    # need a different work-unit definition and therefore fail closed here.
    if batch not in (None, 1) or channels not in (1, 3, 4):
        return None
    # Current preprocessing has one scalar target size and cannot faithfully
    # construct rectangular feeds.  Do not silently crop or stretch one side.
    if height != width:
        return None
    return {
        "shape": shape,
        "layout": layout,
        "height": height,
        "width": width,
        "channels": channels,
        "batch": batch,
        "batch_shape": batch_shape,
    }


def _deepx_shape_from_contract_payload(payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[List[int]]:
    geometry = _deepx_input_geometry(payload)
    return list(geometry.get("shape") or []) if geometry else None


def _deepx_shape_from_dxcom_config(payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[List[int]]:
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        return None
    inputs = payload.get("inputs")
    if isinstance(inputs, dict):
        resolved: List[List[int]] = []
        for _, shape in inputs.items():
            if isinstance(shape, list) and len(shape) == 4:
                try:
                    values = [int(x) for x in shape]
                except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
                    continue
                # DX-COM config inputs describe source-model NCHW tensors.
                # Accept only an exact single-image vision shape.  Multiple
                # inputs are ambiguous for this one-feed benchmark.
                if all(x > 0 for x in values) and values[0] == 1 and values[1] in (1, 3, 4):
                    resolved.append(values)
        if len(resolved) == 1:
            return resolved[0]
    return None


def _deepx_input_size_from_shape(shape: List[int], default: int = 640, layout: str = "NCHW") -> int:
    geometry = _deepx_input_geometry({"input": {"shape": shape, "layout": layout}})
    if geometry is None:
        raise ValueError(f"unsupported_or_ambiguous_deepx_input_shape:{layout}:{shape}")
    return int(geometry.get("height") or default)


def _deepx_prepare_image_from_contract(
    image_bgr: Any,
    input_size: int,
    contract: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
    task: str,
) -> Tuple[Any, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Apply the archived DeepX preprocessing contract without guessing."""
    import numpy as np  # type: ignore
    inp = contract.get("input") if isinstance(contract, dict) and isinstance(contract.get("input"), dict) else {}
    required = ("shape", "dtype", "layout", "normalization", "color_space", "preprocess_mode")
    missing = [key for key in required if inp.get(key) in (None, "", [])]
    if missing:
        raise ValueError("explicit_input_contract_missing:" + ",".join(missing))
    layout = str(inp.get("layout") or "").strip().upper()
    dtype_name = str(inp.get("dtype") or "").strip().lower()
    color_space = str(inp.get("color_space") or "").strip().upper()
    preprocess_mode = str(inp.get("preprocess_mode") or "").strip().lower()
    normalization = str(inp.get("normalization") or "").strip().lower()
    geometry = _deepx_input_geometry(contract or {})
    if geometry is None:
        raise ValueError("explicit_input_contract_invalid")
    if layout not in {"HWC", "NHWC", "CHW", "NCHW"}:
        raise ValueError(f"explicit_layout_unsupported:{layout}")
    if color_space != "RGB":
        raise ValueError(f"explicit_color_space_unsupported:{color_space}")

    orig_h, orig_w = image_bgr.shape[:2]
    try:
        from onnx_splitpoint_tool.preprocessing_contract import (
            canonical_image_preprocessing_contract,
            prepare_rgb_uint8_image,
            preprocessing_contract_sha256,
            runtime_numeric_input_identity,
        )
    except ModuleNotFoundError:
        from splitpoint_runners.preprocessing_contract import (  # type: ignore
            canonical_image_preprocessing_contract,
            prepare_rgb_uint8_image,
            preprocessing_contract_sha256,
            runtime_numeric_input_identity,
        )
    canonical_preprocessing = canonical_image_preprocessing_contract(
        task, [int(geometry["height"]), int(geometry["width"])],
    )
    if (
        preprocess_mode != canonical_preprocessing["preprocess_mode"]
        or color_space != canonical_preprocessing["color_space"]
        or int(
            inp.get("letterbox_pad_value")
            if inp.get("letterbox_pad_value") is not None else 0
        ) != int(canonical_preprocessing["letterbox_pad_value"])
    ):
        raise ValueError("explicit_preprocessing_contract_not_canonical_v2")
    source_rgb = np.ascontiguousarray(np.asarray(image_bgr)[..., ::-1])
    prepared, canonical_geometry = prepare_rgb_uint8_image(
        source_rgb, canonical_preprocessing,
    )
    scale_xy = list(canonical_geometry.get("scale_xy") or [1.0, 1.0])
    pad_ltrb = list(canonical_geometry.get("pad_ltrb") or [0, 0, 0, 0])
    # Letterbox inverse geometry uses the nominal gain before integer resize
    # rounding.  ``scale_xy`` records the effective resized pixel ratios and
    # can differ on the short axis (e.g. 333x500 -> 426x640).  Keep those
    # canonical pixels/padding unchanged, but pass the same nominal gain as
    # the decoded-NMS geometry contract.  Plain classification resize retains
    # its existing scalar convention.
    scale = (
        min(int(geometry["width"]) / float(orig_w), int(geometry["height"]) / float(orig_h))
        if preprocess_mode == "letterbox" else float(min(scale_xy))
    )
    pad_x, pad_y = int(pad_ltrb[0]), int(pad_ltrb[1])
    pad_value = int(canonical_preprocessing["pad_value"])
    converted = prepared
    if dtype_name in {"uint8", "u8"}:
        if normalization not in {"raw", "none", "embedded_dxcom_preprocessing"}:
            raise ValueError(f"uint8_normalization_contract_invalid:{normalization}")
        feed = converted.astype(np.uint8, copy=False)
    elif dtype_name in {"float32", "float", "fp32"}:
        feed = converted.astype(np.float32)
        if normalization in {"scale_0_1", "divide_255", "model_preprocessing_float32"}:
            feed /= 255.0
        elif normalization in {"imagenet_mean_std", "mean_std"}:
            mean = np.asarray(inp.get("mean") or [0.485, 0.456, 0.406], dtype=np.float32)
            std = np.asarray(inp.get("std") or [0.229, 0.224, 0.225], dtype=np.float32)
            value_scale = float(inp.get("value_scale") or 255.0)
            feed = (feed / value_scale - mean) / std
        elif normalization not in {"raw", "none", "embedded_dxcom_preprocessing"}:
            raise ValueError(f"explicit_normalization_unsupported:{normalization}")
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"explicit_dtype_unsupported:{dtype_name}")
    if layout == "NHWC":
        feed = feed[None, ...]
    elif layout == "CHW":
        feed = np.transpose(feed, (2, 0, 1))
    elif layout == "NCHW":
        feed = np.transpose(feed, (2, 0, 1))[None, ...]
    feed = np.ascontiguousarray(feed)
    expected_shape = tuple(int(x) for x in list(geometry.get("shape") or []))
    prepared_shape = tuple(int(x) for x in feed.shape)
    if prepared_shape != expected_shape:
        raise ValueError(
            "explicit_input_shape_mismatch:"
            f"expected={list(expected_shape)}:prepared={list(prepared_shape)}"
        )
    preprocessing_sha = preprocessing_contract_sha256(
        canonical_preprocessing
    )
    numeric_identity, numeric_sha = runtime_numeric_input_identity(
        backend="native_full_deepx",
        task=task,
        preprocessing_contract_sha256_value=preprocessing_sha,
        runtime_input_name=str(inp.get("name") or "input"),
        runtime_input_shape=[int(x) for x in feed.shape],
        runtime_input_dtype=str(feed.dtype),
        runtime_input_layout=layout,
        runtime_color_space=color_space,
        runtime_normalization=normalization,
    )
    feed_bytes = feed.tobytes()
    prepared_rgb_bytes = np.ascontiguousarray(prepared).tobytes()
    prepared_tensor_binding = {
        "prepared_input_sha256": hashlib.sha256(feed_bytes).hexdigest(),
        "prepared_input_bytes": len(feed_bytes),
        "prepared_input_name": str(inp.get("name") or "input"),
        "prepared_input_shape": [int(x) for x in feed.shape],
        "prepared_input_dtype": str(feed.dtype),
        "prepared_input_layout": layout,
        "prepared_rgb_uint8_sha256": hashlib.sha256(
            prepared_rgb_bytes
        ).hexdigest(),
        "prepared_rgb_uint8_bytes": len(prepared_rgb_bytes),
        "runtime_preprocessing_identity": canonical_preprocessing,
        "runtime_preprocessing_sha256": preprocessing_sha,
        "runtime_numeric_input_identity": numeric_identity,
        "runtime_numeric_input_sha256": numeric_sha,
        "binding_verified": True,
    }
    audit = {
        "shape": [int(x) for x in feed.shape], "dtype": str(feed.dtype), "layout": layout,
        "color_space": color_space, "preprocess_mode": preprocess_mode, "normalization": normalization,
        "scale": float(scale), "pad_x": int(pad_x), "pad_y": int(pad_y),
        "letterbox_pad_value": int(pad_value),
        "source_shape_hw": [int(orig_h), int(orig_w)], "contract_source": inp.get("contract_source"),
        "runtime_preprocessing_identity": canonical_preprocessing,
        "runtime_preprocessing_sha256": preprocessing_sha,
        "runtime_numeric_input_identity": numeric_identity,
        "runtime_numeric_input_sha256": numeric_sha,
        "prepared_tensor_binding": prepared_tensor_binding,
    }
    return feed, audit


def _deepx_input_size_from_contract(root: Path, run: Dict[str, Any], default: int = 640) -> Tuple[int, Optional[Dict[str, Any]]]:
    for p, label in _deepx_contract_candidates(root, run):
        try:
            if not p.is_file():
                continue
            payload = _read_json(p)
            # Aggregated deepx_output_contracts.json.
            if isinstance(payload, dict) and isinstance(payload.get("contracts"), list):
                contracts = payload.get("contracts") or []
                preferred = []
                for c in contracts:
                    if not isinstance(c, dict):
                        continue
                    if str(c.get("variant") or "").lower() in ("full", ""):
                        preferred.append(c)
                candidates = preferred or [c for c in contracts if isinstance(c, dict)]
                resolved = [(c, _deepx_input_geometry(c)) for c in candidates]
                resolved = [(c, g) for c, g in resolved if g is not None]
                if len(resolved) != len(candidates):
                    return int(default), {
                        "contract_resolution_status": "invalid",
                        "source_path": str(p),
                        "source_label": label,
                        "reason": "aggregate_contains_invalid_or_layout_ambiguous_input_contract",
                        "candidate_count": len(candidates),
                        "valid_candidate_count": len(resolved),
                    }
                signatures = {
                    (
                        tuple(g.get("shape") or []),
                        str(g.get("layout") or ""),
                        str((c.get("input") or {}).get("dtype") or "").strip().lower(),
                        str((c.get("input") or {}).get("normalization") or "").strip().lower(),
                        str((c.get("input") or {}).get("color_space") or "").strip().upper(),
                        str((c.get("input") or {}).get("preprocess_mode") or "").strip().lower(),
                        (
                            ""
                            if (c.get("input") or {}).get("letterbox_pad_value") is None
                            else str((c.get("input") or {}).get("letterbox_pad_value")).strip()
                        ),
                    )
                    for c, g in resolved
                }
                if len(signatures) > 1:
                    return int(default), {
                        "contract_resolution_status": "ambiguous",
                        "source_path": str(p),
                        "source_label": label,
                        "candidate_signatures": [
                            {
                                "shape": list(shape),
                                "layout": layout,
                                "dtype": dtype,
                                "normalization": normalization,
                                "color_space": color_space,
                                "preprocess_mode": preprocess_mode,
                                "letterbox_pad_value": letterbox_pad_value,
                            }
                            for shape, layout, dtype, normalization, color_space, preprocess_mode, letterbox_pad_value in sorted(signatures)
                        ],
                    }
                if len(signatures) == 1 and resolved:
                    c, geometry = resolved[0]
                    meta = dict(c)
                    meta.setdefault("source_path", str(p))
                    meta.setdefault("source_label", label)
                    meta["contract_resolution_status"] = "resolved"
                    meta["resolved_input_geometry"] = dict(geometry or {})
                    return int((geometry or {}).get("height") or default), meta
                # An aggregate explicitly claiming contracts but containing no
                # valid full input contract must not fall through to heuristics.
                return int(default), {
                    "contract_resolution_status": "invalid",
                    "source_path": str(p),
                    "source_label": label,
                    "reason": "no_unambiguous_explicit_image_input_contract",
                }
            # Single contract JSON.
            geometry = _deepx_input_geometry(payload)
            if geometry:
                meta = dict(payload)
                meta.setdefault("source_path", str(p))
                meta.setdefault("source_label", label)
                meta["contract_resolution_status"] = "resolved"
                meta["resolved_input_geometry"] = dict(geometry)
                return int(geometry.get("height") or default), meta
            if isinstance(payload, dict) and isinstance(payload.get("input"), dict):
                return int(default), {
                    **dict(payload),
                    "contract_resolution_status": "invalid",
                    "source_path": str(p),
                    "source_label": label,
                    "reason": "invalid_or_layout_ambiguous_explicit_input_contract",
                }
            # DX-COM config JSON.
            shape = _deepx_shape_from_dxcom_config(payload)
            if shape:
                meta = {"input": {"shape": shape, "layout": "NCHW"}, "source_path": str(p), "source_label": label, "source_type": "dxcom_config", "contract_resolution_status": "incomplete_dxcom_config"}
                return _deepx_input_size_from_shape(shape, default=default, layout="NCHW"), meta
        except Exception:
            continue
    return int(default), None


def _deepx_input_size_from_contract(
    root: Path, run: Dict[str, Any], default: int = 640,
    _unbound: Any = _deepx_input_size_from_contract,
) -> Tuple[int, Optional[Dict[str, Any]]]:
    """Add endpoint binding while keeping isolated legacy extraction working.

    Several compatibility tests extract only same-named function definitions
    from the generated template.  Capturing the preceding input-only resolver
    as a default keeps that use valid without weakening the full-suite binding.
    """
    input_size, contract = _unbound(root, run, default)
    binder = globals().get("_deepx_bind_authoritative_endpoint_contract")
    return input_size, binder(root, run, contract) if callable(binder) else contract


def _run_deepx_semantic_validation(root: Path, dxnn: Path, run: Dict[str, Any], args: Any, results_dir: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Run optional dx_engine semantic validation for DeepX full models.

    This is intentionally best-effort.  run_model remains the raw latency source;
    dx_engine validates that preprocessing/postprocessing is semantically sane on
    provided validation images.  For YOLO11-like [1,84,8400] outputs this writes
    detections.json and returns detection_count/confidence summaries.
    """
    source = str(getattr(args, "validation_images", "") or run.get("validation_images") or "").strip()
    task = str(run.get("benchmark_task") or getattr(args, "benchmark_task", "auto") or "auto").strip().lower()
    max_images = int(run.get("validation_max_images") or getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0)
    input_size, input_contract = _deepx_input_size_from_contract(root, run, default=640)
    resolution_status = str((input_contract or {}).get("contract_resolution_status") or "").strip().lower()
    if resolution_status in {"ambiguous", "invalid"}:
        return {
            "enabled": True,
            "status": f"explicit_input_contract_{resolution_status}",
            "input_size": input_size,
            "input_contract": input_contract,
        }
    endpoint_binding_status = str((input_contract or {}).get("endpoint_contract_binding_status") or "").strip().lower()
    if task == "detection" and endpoint_binding_status != "attested":
        return {
            "enabled": True,
            "status": "explicit_endpoint_contract_" + (endpoint_binding_status or "unavailable"),
            "error_class": "deepx_semantic_endpoint_contract_not_attested",
            "endpoint_contract_binding_reason": str((input_contract or {}).get("endpoint_contract_binding_reason") or ""),
            "input_size": input_size,
            "input_contract": input_contract,
        }
    samples = _deepx_collect_validation_samples(source, root, max_images)
    imgs = [Path(x.get("path")) for x in samples if x.get("path")]
    if not imgs:
        return {"enabled": False, "status": "no_validation_images", "validation_images": source}
    try:
        import numpy as np  # type: ignore
        from dx_engine import InferenceEngine  # type: ignore
    except Exception as exc:
        return {"enabled": True, "status": "dx_engine_import_failed", "error_class": "deepx_semantic_import_failed", "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", "image_count": len(imgs)}
    try:
        engine = InferenceEngine(str(dxnn))
    except Exception as exc:
        return {"enabled": True, "status": "dx_engine_init_failed", "error_class": "deepx_semantic_init_failed", "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", "image_count": len(imgs)}
    detections_by_image: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    cls_by_image: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    errors: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    times: List[float] = []
    preprocess_audits: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    decoder_contracts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    runtime_output_observations: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    classification_endpoint_stages: List[str] = []
    for sample in samples:
        img_path = Path(sample.get("path"))
        try:
            try:
                import cv2  # type: ignore
            except ImportError:
                from PIL import Image
                with Image.open(img_path) as source_image:
                    img = np.ascontiguousarray(np.asarray(source_image.convert("RGB"))[..., ::-1])
            else:
                img = cv2.imread(str(img_path))
            if img is None:
                errors.append({"image": str(img_path), "error": "cv2.imread returned None"})
                continue
            feed, preprocess_audit = _deepx_prepare_image_from_contract(img, input_size, input_contract, task)
            source_image_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(
                img_path.read_bytes()
            ).hexdigest()
            prepared_tensor_binding = preprocess_audit.get(
                "prepared_tensor_binding"
            )
            if not isinstance(prepared_tensor_binding, dict):
                raise ValueError("prepared_tensor_binding_missing")
            prepared_tensor_binding.update({
                "source_image_id": img_path.name,
                "source_image_sha256": source_image_sha256,
            })
            preprocess_audits.append(dict(preprocess_audit))
            t0 = __import__("time").perf_counter()
            outs = engine.run([feed])
            t1 = __import__("time").perf_counter()
            times.append((t1 - t0) * 1000.0)
            outs_l = outs if isinstance(outs, (list, tuple)) else [outs]
            runtime_output_observations.append({
                "outputs": [
                    {
                        "index": int(index),
                        "shape": [int(value) for value in np.asarray(value).shape],
                        "dtype": str(np.asarray(value).dtype),
                    }
                    for index, value in enumerate(outs_l)
                ],
            })
            arr = None
            for o in outs_l:
                a = np.asarray(o)
                if a.ndim in (2, 3) and (84 in a.shape or (a.ndim == 2 and min(a.shape) <= 1000)):
                    arr = a
                    break
            if arr is None and outs_l:
                arr = np.asarray(outs_l[0])
            if task == "classification" or (arr is not None and arr.size <= 2000 and arr.ndim <= 2 and "yolo" not in str(run.get("id") or "").lower()):
                vec = np.asarray(arr).reshape(-1).astype(np.float32)
                probability_like = bool(
                    vec.size >= 2
                    and np.isfinite(vec).all()
                    and np.all(vec >= -1.0e-4)
                    and np.all(vec <= 1.0 + 1.0e-4)
                    and np.isclose(float(np.sum(vec)), 1.0, atol=2.0e-3, rtol=2.0e-3)
                )
                classification_endpoint_stages.append(
                    "classification_probabilities" if probability_like else "classification_logits"
                )
                top = vec.argsort()[::-1][:5].tolist()
                label_id = sample.get("label_id")
                try:
                    label_id_i = int(label_id) if label_id is not None else None
                except Exception:
                    label_id_i = None
                top5 = [int(x) for x in top]
                cls_by_image.append({
                    "image": str(img_path),
                    "label_id": label_id_i,
                    "label_name": sample.get("label_name"),
                    "top1": int(top[0]) if top else None,
                    "top5": top5,
                    "scores": [float(vec[x]) for x in top],
                    "top1_correct": bool(label_id_i is not None and top and int(top[0]) == label_id_i),
                    "top5_correct": bool(label_id_i is not None and label_id_i in top5),
                    "preprocessing_audit": dict(preprocess_audit),
                })
            else:
                dets, decoder_contract = _deepx_detection_decode(
                    root=root, run=run, contract=input_contract, outputs=list(outs_l),
                    orig_shape=img.shape,
                    scale=float(preprocess_audit.get("scale") or 1.0),
                    pad_x=int(preprocess_audit.get("pad_x") or 0),
                    pad_y=int(preprocess_audit.get("pad_y") or 0),
                    conf=0.25,
                )
                decoder_contracts.append(dict(decoder_contract))
                if not bool(decoder_contract.get("pass")):
                    errors.append({"image": str(img_path), "error": str(decoder_contract.get("status") or "decoder_contract_failed"), "decoder_contract": decoder_contract})
                    continue
                detections_by_image.append({
                    "image": str(img_path), "detections": dets,
                    "num_detections": len(dets), "decoder_contract": decoder_contract,
                    # Candidate records are decoded to original-image space.
                    # Preserve the per-image transform as audit evidence; the
                    # central exporter keeps predictions and GT in this same
                    # canonical original-image coordinate space.
                    "preprocessing_audit": dict(preprocess_audit),
                })
        except Exception as exc:
            errors.append({"image": str(img_path), "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"})
    if not detections_by_image and not cls_by_image:
        return {"enabled": True, "status": "semantic_runtime_failed", "error_class": "deepx_semantic_runtime_failed", "image_count": len(imgs), "errors": errors[:20]}
    payload: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "enabled": True,
        "status": "semantic_runtime_incomplete" if errors else "ok",
        "image_count": len(imgs),
        "validated_image_count": len(detections_by_image) + len(cls_by_image),
        "error_count": len(errors), "errors": errors[:20],
        "wall_time_ms_mean": (sum(times) / len(times)) if times else None,
        "input_size": input_size, "input_contract": input_contract,
        "preprocessing_contract": {
            "status": "ok" if preprocess_audits else "unavailable",
            "pass": bool(preprocess_audits),
            "sample": (preprocess_audits[0] if preprocess_audits else None),
            # Scale and padding are derived per image and must vary with the
            # source aspect ratio.  Contract homogeneity concerns only the
            # invariant preprocessing policy; the per-image transform is bound
            # to each detection record separately.
            "invariant_sample": (
                {k: v for k, v in preprocess_audits[0].items() if k not in {"source_shape_hw", "scale", "pad_x", "pad_y", "prepared_tensor_binding"}}
                if preprocess_audits else None
            ),
            "all_samples_same_contract": bool(
                preprocess_audits and all(
                    {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k not in {"source_shape_hw", "scale", "pad_x", "pad_y", "prepared_tensor_binding"}}
                    == {k: v for k, v in preprocess_audits[0].items() if k not in {"source_shape_hw", "scale", "pad_x", "pad_y", "prepared_tensor_binding"}}
                    for rec in preprocess_audits
                )
            ),
            "per_image_transform_fields": ["source_shape_hw", "scale", "pad_x", "pad_y"],
        },
        "decoder_postprocess_contract": {
            "status": ("ok" if decoder_contracts and all(bool(x.get("pass")) for x in decoder_contracts) else ("not_applicable" if cls_by_image else "failed")),
            "pass": (True if cls_by_image and not decoder_contracts else bool(decoder_contracts and all(bool(x.get("pass")) for x in decoder_contracts))),
            "validated_contract_count": int(len(decoder_contracts)),
            "all_samples_same_contract": bool(
                decoder_contracts
                and all(
                    (
                        str(item.get("frozen_postprocess_invariant_contract_sha256") or "")
                        == str(decoder_contracts[0].get("frozen_postprocess_invariant_contract_sha256") or "")
                        and bool(item.get("frozen_postprocess_invariant_contract_sha256"))
                    )
                    if decoder_contracts[0].get("frozen_postprocess_invariant_contract_sha256")
                    else (
                        json.dumps(item, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
                        == json.dumps(decoder_contracts[0], ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
                    )
                    for item in decoder_contracts
                )
            ),
            "invariant_identity": (
                dict(decoder_contracts[0].get("frozen_postprocess_invariant_identity") or {})
                if decoder_contracts else {}
            ),
            "invariant_contract_sha256": (
                str(decoder_contracts[0].get("frozen_postprocess_invariant_contract_sha256") or "")
                if decoder_contracts else ""
            ),
            "per_image_binding_fields": [
                "per_image_original_wh", "frozen_postprocess_contract_sha256",
                "frozen_postprocess_result",
            ],
            "contracts": decoder_contracts[:20],
        },
        "runtime_output_contract": {
            "status": "ok" if runtime_output_observations else "unavailable",
            "pass": bool(runtime_output_observations),
            "validated_observation_count": int(len(runtime_output_observations)),
            "all_samples_same_contract": bool(
                runtime_output_observations
                and all(item == runtime_output_observations[0] for item in runtime_output_observations)
            ),
            "sample": runtime_output_observations[0] if runtime_output_observations else None,
        },
        "classification_source_endpoint_contract": {
            "status": (
                "ok" if classification_endpoint_stages
                and len(set(classification_endpoint_stages)) == 1
                else ("not_applicable" if not classification_endpoint_stages else "inconsistent")
            ),
            "pass": bool(
                classification_endpoint_stages
                and len(set(classification_endpoint_stages)) == 1
            ),
            "stage": (
                classification_endpoint_stages[0]
                if classification_endpoint_stages
                and len(set(classification_endpoint_stages)) == 1
                else ""
            ),
            "validated_observation_count": len(classification_endpoint_stages),
            "attestation_source": "deepx_runtime_values_probability_simplex_check_v1",
        },
    }
    if detections_by_image:
        det_path = results_dir / "detections.json"
        _write_json(det_path, {"images": detections_by_image})
        n = [int(x.get("num_detections") or 0) for x in detections_by_image]
        confs: List[float] = []
        for record_index, rec in enumerate(detections_by_image):
            image_key = str(Path(str(rec.get("image") or record_index)).name)
            for det_index, detection in enumerate(list(rec.get("detections") or [])):
                if not isinstance(detection, Mapping):
                    raise RuntimeError(
                        f"deepx_quality_detection_not_mapping:{image_key}:{det_index}"
                    )
                _box, score, _class_id = _deepx_detection_record_components(
                    detection, image_id=f"{image_key}:{det_index}",
                )
                confs.append(float(score))
        conf_mean = (sum(confs) / len(confs)) if confs else None
        score_semantics = "raw_or_unnormalized_score" if conf_mean is not None and float(conf_mean) > 1.0 else "probability_0_1"
        ap50_payload = _deepx_compute_mini_coco_ap50(samples, detections_by_image, iou_thr=0.5)
        payload.update({
            "task": "detection",
            "detections_json": str(det_path.relative_to(root)),
            "detection_count_mean": (sum(n) / len(n)) if n else 0.0,
            "confidence_mean": conf_mean,
            "raw_score_mean": conf_mean,
            "confidence_mean_clipped_0_1": (min(max(float(conf_mean), 0.0), 1.0) if conf_mean is not None else None),
            "score_semantics": score_semantics,
            "mini_coco_ap50": {
                "enabled": bool(ap50_payload.get("enabled")),
                "status": ap50_payload.get("status"),
                "reference_source": "deepx_full_detection_semantic_validation",
                "variants": {"full": ap50_payload},
            },
            "mini_coco_ap50_full": ap50_payload.get("ap50"),
            "mini_coco_ap50_primary": ap50_payload.get("ap50"),
            "mini_coco_ap50_primary_variant": "full",
            "detection_ap50": ap50_payload.get("ap50"),
            "coco_ap50": ap50_payload.get("ap50"),
            "map50": ap50_payload.get("ap50"),
            "ap50_status": ap50_payload.get("status"),
            "ap50_gt_instances": ap50_payload.get("gt_instances"),
            "ap50_predictions": ap50_payload.get("predictions"),
            "ap50_images": ap50_payload.get("images"),
        })
    if cls_by_image:
        cls_path = results_dir / "classification_topk.json"
        _write_json(cls_path, {"images": cls_by_image})
        labeled = [r for r in cls_by_image if r.get("label_id") is not None]
        top1 = (sum(1 for r in labeled if r.get("top1_correct")) / len(labeled)) if labeled else None
        top5 = (sum(1 for r in labeled if r.get("top5_correct")) / len(labeled)) if labeled else None
        payload.update({
            "task": "classification",
            "classification_topk_json": str(cls_path.relative_to(root)),
            "classification_samples": len(cls_by_image),
            "classification_labeled_samples": len(labeled),
            "classification_top1_accuracy": top1,
            "classification_top5_accuracy": top5,
        })
    return payload


def _deepx_quality_json_safe(value: Any) -> Any:
    """Return deterministic JSON primitives for DeepX quality evidence."""
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {str(k): _deepx_quality_json_safe(v) for k, v in sorted(value.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]))}
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return [_deepx_quality_json_safe(v) for v in value]
    if isinstance(value, Path):
        return str(value)
    if hasattr(value, "item"):
        try:
            return value.item()
        except Exception:
            pass
    return value


def _deepx_quality_json_sha256(value: Any) -> str:
    encoded = json.dumps(
        _deepx_quality_json_safe(value), ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False,
    ).encode("utf-8")
    return hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()


def _deepx_quality_file_sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as stream:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _deepx_portable_dataset_manifest_sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    """Return the host-independent identity of a content manifest."""
    manifest_path = Path(path)
    payload = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if (
        not isinstance(payload, Mapping)
        or payload.get("schema") != "onnx-splitpoint/dataset-manifest"
        or not isinstance(payload.get("items"), list)
        or not payload.get("items")
    ):
        return _deepx_quality_file_sha256(manifest_path)

    identity_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    legacy_identity_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    legacy_writer_shape = True
    for raw in payload["items"]:
        if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
            raise RuntimeError("dataset manifest contains a non-object item")
        raw_digest = raw.get("sha256")
        raw_sample_id = raw.get("sample_id")
        raw_relative_path = raw.get("relative_path")
        raw_class_name = raw.get("class_name", "")
        digest_is_bare = bool(
            isinstance(raw_digest, str)
            and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", raw_digest)
        )
        digest_is_legacy = bool(
            isinstance(raw_digest, str)
            and re.fullmatch(r"sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}", raw_digest)
        )
        if not (digest_is_bare or digest_is_legacy):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "portable dataset identity requires per-item content SHA-256"
            )
        if (
            not isinstance(raw_sample_id, str)
            or not raw_sample_id
            or raw_sample_id != raw_sample_id.strip()
            or not isinstance(raw_relative_path, str)
            or not raw_relative_path
            or raw_relative_path != raw_relative_path.strip()
            or raw_relative_path.startswith("/")
            or "\\" in raw_relative_path
            or any(
                part in {"", ".", ".."}
                for part in raw_relative_path.split("/")
            )
            or not isinstance(raw_class_name, str)
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "portable dataset identity requires sample IDs and relative paths"
            )
        digest = raw_digest[7:] if digest_is_legacy else raw_digest
        identity_rows.append({
            "sample_id": raw_sample_id,
            "relative_path": raw_relative_path,
            "sha256": digest,
            "class_name": raw_class_name,
        })
        legacy_identity_rows.append({
            "sample_id": raw.get("sample_id"),
            "relative_path": raw.get("relative_path"),
            "sha256": raw.get("sha256", ""),
            "class_name": raw.get("class_name", ""),
        })
        legacy_writer_shape = legacy_writer_shape and digest_is_legacy
    items_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(identity_rows)
    legacy_items_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(legacy_identity_rows)
    raw_declared_items_sha = payload.get("items_identity_sha256")
    declared_items_sha = (
        raw_declared_items_sha[7:]
        if isinstance(raw_declared_items_sha, str)
        and raw_declared_items_sha.startswith("sha256:")
        else raw_declared_items_sha
    )
    if not (
        isinstance(declared_items_sha, str)
        and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", declared_items_sha)
        and (
            declared_items_sha == items_sha
            or (
                legacy_writer_shape
                and declared_items_sha == legacy_items_sha
            )
        )
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("dataset manifest item identity SHA-256 mismatch")

    def _component_sha(name: str) -> str:
        component = payload.get(name)
        if not isinstance(component, Mapping):
            return ""
        value = str(component.get("sha256") or "").strip().lower()
        return value.split(":", 1)[1] if value.startswith("sha256:") else value

    portable_identity = {
        "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/portable-dataset-identity",
        "schema_version": 1,
        "dataset_id": str(payload.get("dataset_id") or ""),
        "task": str(payload.get("task") or "").strip().lower(),
        "role": str(payload.get("role") or "").strip().lower(),
        "split": str(payload.get("split") or ""),
        "hash_mode": str(payload.get("hash_mode") or "").strip().lower(),
        "item_count": len(identity_rows),
        "items_identity_sha256": items_sha,
        "annotations_sha256": _component_sha("annotations"),
        "labels_sha256": _component_sha("labels"),
    }
    return _deepx_quality_json_sha256(portable_identity)


def _deepx_endpoint_runtime_signature(value: Any) -> Any:
    """Return the precision-neutral part of an observed runtime signature.

    Output endpoint identity answers *what* is produced.  DXNN identity below
    independently answers *with which compiled precision* it was produced.
    Keeping dtype/quantization tokens out of this projection prevents the same
    logical endpoint from becoming backend-precision dependent.
    """
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {
            str(key): _deepx_endpoint_runtime_signature(item)
            for key, item in value.items()
            if str(key).strip().lower() not in {
                "dtype", "data_type", "precision", "quantization",
                "quantization_parameters", "scale", "zero_point",
            }
        }
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return [_deepx_endpoint_runtime_signature(item) for item in value]
    return _deepx_quality_json_safe(value)


def _deepx_runtime_tensor_shape_signature(value: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Project DeepX observations to native-output-endpoint v3 shapes."""
    payload = value if isinstance(value, Mapping) else {}
    outputs = list(payload.get("outputs") or [])
    tensors: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for fallback_index, output in enumerate(outputs):
        if not isinstance(output, Mapping) or not isinstance(output.get("shape"), (list, tuple)):
            raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_runtime_output_shape_missing")
        shape = [int(item) for item in list(output.get("shape") or [])]
        tensors.append({
            "index": int(output.get("index") if output.get("index") is not None else fallback_index),
            "rank": len(shape),
            "shape": shape,
        })
    if not tensors:
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_runtime_output_shape_missing")
    return {"tensor_count": len(tensors), "tensors": tensors}


def _deepx_verified_endpoint_semantic_declaration(
    output_contract: Mapping[str, Any], *, task: str,
    source_has_integrated_nms: Optional[bool],
    expected_model_id: str, expected_backend: str,
    expected_variant: str,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Project semantics only from the hash-verified recorded attestation."""
    task_l = str(task or "").strip().lower()
    attestation = output_contract.get("endpoint_semantic_attestation")
    if not isinstance(attestation, Mapping):
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_endpoint_semantic_attestation_missing")
    authoritative = attestation.get("authoritative_contract")
    declared_sha = str(
        attestation.get("authoritative_contract_sha256") or ""
    ).strip().lower()
    if (
        attestation.get("schema")
        != "onnx-splitpoint/deepx-endpoint-semantic-attestation"
        or int(attestation.get("schema_version") or 0) != 1
        or attestation.get("pass") is not True
        or str(attestation.get("status") or "") != "attested"
        or not isinstance(authoritative, Mapping)
        or len(declared_sha) != 64
        or declared_sha != _deepx_endpoint_json_sha256(authoritative)
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_endpoint_semantic_attestation_invalid")
    authoritative = dict(authoritative)
    endpoint_mode = str(attestation.get("endpoint_mode") or "").strip().lower()
    host_tail = attestation.get("host_tail_required")
    postprocessing = attestation.get("postprocessing_required")
    if (
        authoritative.get("contract_status") != "recorded"
        or str(authoritative.get("task") or "").strip().lower() != task_l
        or str(authoritative.get("endpoint_mode") or "").strip().lower()
        != endpoint_mode
        or authoritative.get("host_tail_required") is not host_tail
        or authoritative.get("postprocessing_required") is not postprocessing
        or not isinstance(host_tail, bool)
        or not isinstance(postprocessing, bool)
        or endpoint_mode not in {
            "decoded", "decoded_pre_nms", "raw_detection_head",
        }
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_endpoint_semantic_authority_conflict")
    expected_identity = {
        "model_id": str(expected_model_id or "").strip(),
        "backend": str(expected_backend or "").strip(),
        "variant": str(expected_variant or "").strip(),
    }
    if not all(expected_identity.values()):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "deepx_quality_endpoint_semantic_expected_identity_incomplete"
        )
    for key in ("backend", "variant", "model_id"):
        attested_raw = attestation.get(key)
        recorded_raw = authoritative.get(key)
        attested = str(attested_raw or "").strip()
        recorded = str(recorded_raw or "").strip()
        if (
            not isinstance(attested_raw, str)
            or not isinstance(recorded_raw, str)
            or attested_raw != attested
            or recorded_raw != recorded
            or not attested or not recorded or attested != recorded
            or attested != expected_identity[key]
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "deepx_quality_endpoint_semantic_" + key + "_conflict"
            )
        top_level = str(output_contract.get(key) or "").strip()
        if top_level and top_level != attested:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "deepx_quality_endpoint_semantic_top_level_" + key + "_conflict"
            )
    top_task = str(
        output_contract.get("task")
        or (
            output_contract.get("input", {}).get("task")
            if isinstance(output_contract.get("input"), Mapping) else ""
        )
        or ""
    ).strip().lower()
    if top_task and top_task != task_l:
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_endpoint_semantic_top_level_task_conflict")
    for key, expected in (
        ("endpoint_mode", endpoint_mode),
        ("host_tail_required", host_tail),
        ("postprocessing_required", postprocessing),
    ):
        observed = output_contract.get(key)
        if observed not in (None, "") and observed != expected:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "deepx_quality_endpoint_semantic_top_level_" + key + "_conflict"
            )
    if task_l == "detection":
        expected_integrated_nms = endpoint_mode == "decoded"
        if (
            source_has_integrated_nms is not expected_integrated_nms
            or attestation.get("source_endpoint_has_integrated_nms")
            is not expected_integrated_nms
            or host_tail is expected_integrated_nms
            or postprocessing is expected_integrated_nms
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "deepx_quality_endpoint_semantic_runtime_stage_conflict"
            )
    semantic_keys = (
        "coordinate_format", "coordinate_space", "decoder_id",
        "decoder_sha256", "nms_implementation", "class_aware",
        "iou_threshold", "score_threshold", "max_detections",
    )
    semantic = {
        key: authoritative.get(key)
        for key in semantic_keys
        if authoritative.get(key) not in (None, "")
    }
    if endpoint_mode == "decoded" and (
        not isinstance(semantic.get("coordinate_format"), str)
        or not str(semantic.get("coordinate_format") or "").strip()
        or semantic.get("coordinate_format")
        != str(semantic.get("coordinate_format") or "").strip()
        or not isinstance(semantic.get("coordinate_space"), str)
        or not str(semantic.get("coordinate_space") or "").strip()
        or semantic.get("coordinate_space")
        != str(semantic.get("coordinate_space") or "").strip()
    ):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "deepx_quality_endpoint_semantic_decoded_coordinates_missing"
        )
    for key in semantic_keys:
        observed = output_contract.get(key)
        if observed not in (None, "") and observed != semantic.get(key):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "deepx_quality_endpoint_semantic_top_level_" + key + "_conflict"
            )
    return semantic


def _deepx_standard_runtime_endpoint_identity(
    *, task: str, runtime_observation: Any, output_contract: Mapping[str, Any],
    source_has_integrated_nms: Optional[bool] = None,
    classification_observed_stage: str = "",
    expected_model_id: str = "", expected_backend: str = "",
    expected_variant: str = "",
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Build the exact precision-neutral Native endpoint-hash identity.

    This intentionally mirrors ``native_output_endpoint._endpoint_hash``.  It
    describes the measured *pre-adapter* runtime endpoint.  DeepX host
    decoder/NMS or Top-K provenance is stored in a separate quality-record
    endpoint contract and therefore cannot change this join key.
    """
    signature = _deepx_runtime_tensor_shape_signature(runtime_observation)
    task_l = str(task or "").strip().lower()
    semantic: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    if task_l == "detection":
        semantic = _deepx_verified_endpoint_semantic_declaration(
            output_contract,
            task=task_l,
            source_has_integrated_nms=source_has_integrated_nms,
            expected_model_id=expected_model_id,
            expected_backend=expected_backend,
            expected_variant=expected_variant,
        )
        semantic_attestation = output_contract.get(
            "endpoint_semantic_attestation"
        )
        declared_endpoint_mode = str(
            (semantic_attestation or {}).get("endpoint_mode")
            if isinstance(semantic_attestation, Mapping) else ""
        ).strip().lower()
        if source_has_integrated_nms is True:
            stage, output_format = "decoded_nms", "bn6_detections"
        elif source_has_integrated_nms is False:
            if declared_endpoint_mode == "decoded_pre_nms":
                stage, output_format = (
                    "decoded_pre_nms", "ultralytics_decoded",
                )
            else:
                stage, output_format = "raw_head", "raw_detection_tensors"
        else:
            raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_detection_runtime_endpoint_stage_unattested")
    elif task_l == "classification":
        declared_values: List[str] = []
        for key in ("stage", "contract_family", "output_format", "endpoint_mode"):
            declared_values.append(str(output_contract.get(key) or ""))
        for row in list(output_contract.get("outputs") or []):
            if isinstance(row, Mapping):
                declared_values.extend([
                    str(row.get("name") or ""),
                    str(row.get("stage") or row.get("output_format") or ""),
                ])
        joined = " ".join(declared_values).strip().lower()
        if "probabil" in joined or "softmax" in joined:
            stage = "classification_probabilities"
        elif "logit" in joined or "class_score" in joined:
            stage = "classification_logits"
        else:
            stage = str(classification_observed_stage or "").strip().lower()
        if stage not in {"classification_logits", "classification_probabilities"}:
            raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_classification_runtime_endpoint_stage_unattested")
        output_format = stage
        semantic = {}
    else:
        raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_runtime_endpoint_task_unsupported:{task_l}")
    comparison_signature = signature
    if task_l == "classification" and stage.startswith("classification_"):
        tensors: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for row in list(signature.get("tensors") or []):
            shape = [int(value) for value in list(row.get("shape") or [])]
            rank = int(row.get("rank") or len(shape))
            # Match native_output_endpoint v3: classifier APIs may return [C]
            # or [1,C], which is one semantic endpoint.  The original runtime
            # observation above remains available as physical-shape evidence;
            # only the cross-runner hash projection drops a singleton batch.
            if len(shape) == 2 and shape[0] == 1:
                shape = shape[1:]
                rank = 1
            tensors.append({
                "index": int(row.get("index") or 0),
                "rank": rank,
                "shape": shape,
            })
        comparison_signature = {
            "tensor_count": int(signature.get("tensor_count") or 0),
            "tensors": tensors,
        }
    return {
        "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/output-endpoint-contract",
        "schema_version": 3,
        "task": task_l,
        "stage": stage,
        "output_format": output_format,
        "tensor_signature": comparison_signature,
        "semantic": semantic,
    }


def _deepx_write_quality_json(path: Path, payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    encoded = json.dumps(
        _deepx_quality_json_safe(payload), ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False,
    ).encode("utf-8")
    tmp = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    tmp.write_bytes(encoded)
    tmp.replace(path)
    return {"path": str(path), "size_bytes": len(encoded), "sha256": hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()}


def _deepx_normalize_quality_policy(raw: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Project a plan gate to the exact AccuracyGatePolicy v2 hash contract."""
    data = dict(raw or {}) if isinstance(raw, Mapping) else {}
    if isinstance(data.get("quality_gate"), Mapping):
        data = dict(data.get("quality_gate") or {})
    stats = dict(data.get("statistics") or {}) if isinstance(data.get("statistics"), Mapping) else {}
    cls = dict(data.get("classification") or {}) if isinstance(data.get("classification"), Mapping) else {}
    cls_guard = dict(cls.get("guardrails") or {}) if isinstance(cls.get("guardrails"), Mapping) else {}
    det = dict(data.get("detection") or {}) if isinstance(data.get("detection"), Mapping) else {}
    det_guard = dict(det.get("guardrails") or {}) if isinstance(det.get("guardrails"), Mapping) else {}
    native = dict(data.get("native_contract") or {}) if isinstance(data.get("native_contract"), Mapping) else {}

    def _float(value: Any, default: float) -> float:
        try:
            return float(default if value in (None, "") else value)
        except Exception:
            return float(default)

    def _int(value: Any, default: int) -> int:
        try:
            return int(default if value in (None, "") else value)
        except Exception:
            return int(default)

    def _bool(value: Any, default: bool = False) -> bool:
        if value is None:
            return bool(default)
        if isinstance(value, bool):
            return value
        return str(value).strip().lower() in {"1", "true", "yes", "on", "pass", "ok"}

    raw_reps = stats.get("bootstrap_repetitions", stats.get("repetitions", data.get("bootstrap_repetitions")))
    reps = max(1, _int(raw_reps, 500))
    confidence = min(max(_float(stats.get("confidence_level", data.get("confidence_level")), 0.95), 0.5), 0.999)
    seed = _int(stats.get("seed", data.get("bootstrap_seed")), 20260710)
    decision = str(stats.get("decision") or stats.get("decision_rule") or data.get("decision_rule") or "lower_one_sided_bound")
    execution = str(stats.get("execution_location") or data.get("execution_location") or "local").strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
    if execution in {"management", "management_node", "central", "central_cpu"}:
        execution = "central_management"
    tier = str(data.get("dataset_tier") or data.get("tier") or "screening").strip().lower()
    if tier not in {"screening", "final"}:
        tier = "screening"
    name = str(data.get("name") or data.get("profile_id") or "thesis_task_quality_v2")
    profile_id = str(data.get("profile_id") or name)
    policy_schema_version = _int(data.get("schema_version"), 3)
    native_detection = (
        dict(native.get("detection_self_reference") or {})
        if isinstance(native.get("detection_self_reference"), Mapping)
        else {}
    )
    native_min_match = _float(
        native_detection.get(
