            "min_reference_match_ratio",
            native.get(
                "detection_self_reference_min_match_ratio",
                data.get("native_self_reference_min_match"),
            ),
        ),
        0.90 if policy_schema_version < 3 else 0.80,
    )
    native_policy_id = str(
        native_detection.get("policy_id")
        or native.get("detection_self_reference_policy_id")
        or "class_aware_iou50_postnms_v2"
    )
    native_min_mean_iou = _float(
        native_detection.get(
            "min_mean_matched_iou",
            native.get("detection_self_reference_min_mean_iou"),
        ),
        0.0 if policy_schema_version < 3 else 0.85,
    )
    native_iou_threshold = _float(
        native_detection.get("iou_threshold"), 0.50,
    )
    native_confidence_threshold = _float(
        native_detection.get("confidence_threshold"), 0.25,
    )
    native_denominator = str(
        native_detection.get("denominator") or "reference_detections"
    )
    native_class_aware = _bool(
        native_detection.get("class_aware"), True,
    )
    numerical_required = _bool(
        native.get("numerical_similarity_required_for_claim"), True,
    )
    effective = {
        "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/task-quality-policy",
        "schema_version": policy_schema_version,
        "name": name, "profile_id": profile_id,
        "frozen_before_final_campaign": _bool(data.get("frozen_before_final_campaign", data.get("frozen")), False),
        "dataset_tier": tier,
        "canonical_reference": str(data.get("canonical_reference") or "canonical_full_onnx"),
        "confidence_level": confidence, "bootstrap_repetitions": reps,
        "bootstrap_seed": seed, "decision_rule": decision,
        "classification_primary_metric": str(cls.get("primary_metric") or data.get("classification_primary_metric") or "top1_accuracy"),
        "classification_max_top1_drop": _float(cls.get("non_inferiority_margin", data.get("classification_max_top1_drop")), 0.01),
        "classification_max_top5_drop": _float(cls_guard.get("top5_accuracy_margin", data.get("classification_max_top5_drop")), 0.01),
        "detection_primary_metric": str(det.get("primary_metric") or data.get("detection_primary_metric") or "coco_ap_50_95"),
        "detection_max_ap_drop": _float(det.get("non_inferiority_margin", data.get("detection_max_ap_drop")), 0.01),
        "detection_max_ap50_drop": _float(det_guard.get("ap50_margin", data.get("detection_max_ap50_drop")), 0.01),
        "native_self_reference_min_match": native_min_match,
        "contract_only_eligible_for_ranking": _bool(native.get("self_reference_counts_as_task_accuracy", data.get("contract_only_eligible_for_ranking")), False),
        "screening_eligible_for_ranking": _bool(data.get("screening_eligible_for_ranking"), False),
        "legacy_point_estimate_eligible_for_ranking": _bool(data.get("legacy_point_estimate_eligible_for_ranking"), False),
    }
    if policy_schema_version >= 3:
        effective.update({
            "native_self_reference_policy_id": native_policy_id,
            "native_self_reference_min_mean_iou": native_min_mean_iou,
            "native_self_reference_iou_threshold": native_iou_threshold,
            "native_self_reference_confidence_threshold": native_confidence_threshold,
            "native_self_reference_denominator": native_denominator,
            "native_self_reference_class_aware": native_class_aware,
            "numerical_similarity_required_for_claim": numerical_required,
        })
    policy_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(effective)
    return {
        "schema": effective["schema"],
        "schema_version": policy_schema_version,
        "name": name,
        "profile_id": profile_id, "frozen_before_final_campaign": effective["frozen_before_final_campaign"],
        "dataset_tier": tier, "canonical_reference": effective["canonical_reference"],
        "screening_eligible_for_ranking": effective["screening_eligible_for_ranking"],
        "legacy_point_estimate_eligible_for_ranking": effective["legacy_point_estimate_eligible_for_ranking"],
        "classification": {
            "primary_metric": effective["classification_primary_metric"],
            "non_inferiority_margin": effective["classification_max_top1_drop"],
            "guardrails": {"top5_accuracy_margin": effective["classification_max_top5_drop"]},
        },
        "detection": {
            "primary_metric": effective["detection_primary_metric"],
            "non_inferiority_margin": effective["detection_max_ap_drop"],
            "guardrails": {"ap50_margin": effective["detection_max_ap50_drop"], "ap75_margin": _float(det_guard.get("ap75_margin"), 0.01)},
        },
        "native_contract": {
            "detection_self_reference_min_match_ratio": effective["native_self_reference_min_match"],
            **({
                "detection_self_reference": {
                    "policy_id": native_policy_id,
                    "min_reference_match_ratio": native_min_match,
                    "min_mean_matched_iou": native_min_mean_iou,
                    "iou_threshold": native_iou_threshold,
                    "confidence_threshold": native_confidence_threshold,
                    "denominator": native_denominator,
                    "class_aware": native_class_aware,
                },
                "numerical_similarity_required_for_claim": numerical_required,
            } if policy_schema_version >= 3 else {}),
            "self_reference_counts_as_task_accuracy": effective["contract_only_eligible_for_ranking"],
        },
        "statistics": {
            "method": str(stats.get("method") or "paired_bootstrap"),
            "confidence_level": confidence, "bootstrap_repetitions": reps,
            "bootstrap_repetitions_source": "profile" if raw_reps not in (None, "") else "compatibility_default_500",
            "seed": seed, "decision": decision, "execution_location": execution,
            "workers": max(1, min(64, _int(stats.get("workers"), 4 if execution == "central_management" else 1))),
        },
        "effective_policy": effective, "policy_sha256": policy_sha,
    }


def _deepx_resolve_quality_manifest(root: Path, run: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Path:
    source = str(run.get("validation_images") or "").strip()
    if not source:
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_validation_source_missing")
    path = Path(source).expanduser()
    if not path.is_absolute():
        path = (root / path).resolve()
    manifest = path if path.is_file() and path.suffix.lower() == ".json" else path / "manifest.json"
    if not manifest.is_file():
        raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_manifest_missing:{manifest}")
    return manifest


def _deepx_resolve_source_onnx(root: Path) -> Path:
    candidates = []
    for path in list((root / "models").glob("*.onnx")) + list(root.glob("*.onnx")):
        token = path.name.lower()
        if path.is_file() and "part1" not in token and "part2" not in token:
            candidates.append(path.resolve())
    unique = sorted({str(path): path for path in candidates}.values(), key=lambda path: str(path))
    if len(unique) != 1:
        raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_source_onnx_ambiguous:count={len(unique)}")
    return unique[0]


def _deepx_quality_expected_run_identity(
    run: Mapping[str, Any], *, expected_model_id: str = "",
    expected_backend: str = "", expected_variant: str = "",
) -> Dict[str, str]:
    """Resolve one conflict-free suite/run identity for endpoint attestation."""
    model_suite = (
        run.get("model_suite")
        if isinstance(run.get("model_suite"), Mapping) else {}
    )
    stage1 = run.get("stage1") if isinstance(run.get("stage1"), Mapping) else {}
    stage2 = run.get("stage2") if isinstance(run.get("stage2"), Mapping) else {}
    raw_variants = run.get("variants")
    variant_values = (
        [str(item or "").strip() for item in raw_variants]
        if isinstance(raw_variants, (list, tuple)) else []
    )

    def _one(label: str, values: Sequence[Any]) -> str:
        normalized = [str(value or "").strip() for value in values]
        observed = {value for value in normalized if value}
        if len(observed) != 1:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"deepx_quality_expected_{label}_missing_or_conflicting"
            )
        return next(iter(observed))

    model_id = _one("model_id", (
        expected_model_id, run.get("model_id"), run.get("model"),
        model_suite.get("primary"),
    ))
    backend = _one("backend", (
        expected_backend, run.get("backend"), run.get("provider"),
        stage1.get("backend"), stage2.get("backend"),
    ))
    variant = _one("variant", (
        expected_variant, run.get("variant"), *variant_values,
    ))
    return {"model_id": model_id, "backend": backend, "variant": variant}


def _deepx_full_only_quality_identity_duplicates(
    identity: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]], *, expected_model_id: str,
    expected_backend: str, expected_source_run_id: str,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Validate and materialize one exact non-performance DeepX identity.

    The returned fields are copied into the producer, candidate and request
    *before* their respective hashes are calculated.  In particular, no
    model/setup/backend value is inferred from a results-directory name.
    """
    if identity is None:
        return {}
    if not isinstance(identity, Mapping):
        raise RuntimeError("DeepX Full-only quality identity must be an object")
    raw = dict(identity)
    allowed = {
        "schema", "schema_version", "quality_canary_id", "eval_run_id",
        "model_id", "setup_id", "source_run_id", "backend", "variant",
        "execution_role", "performance_claims_emitted",
    }
    unknown = sorted(set(raw) - allowed)
    required_text = (
        "quality_canary_id", "eval_run_id", "model_id", "setup_id",
        "source_run_id", "backend", "variant", "execution_role",
    )
    invalid = [
        field for field in required_text
        if not str(raw.get(field) or "").strip()
    ]
    canonical = {
        "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/full-only-quality-request-identity",
        "schema_version": 1,
        "quality_canary_id": str(raw.get("quality_canary_id") or "").strip(),
        "eval_run_id": str(raw.get("eval_run_id") or "").strip(),
        "model_id": str(raw.get("model_id") or "").strip(),
        "setup_id": str(raw.get("setup_id") or "").strip(),
        "source_run_id": str(raw.get("source_run_id") or "").strip(),
        "backend": str(raw.get("backend") or "").strip().lower(),
        "variant": "full",
        "execution_role": "full_quality_only",
        "performance_claims_emitted": False,
    }
    if (
        unknown or invalid
        or str(raw.get("schema") or "")
        != "onnx-splitpoint/full-only-quality-request-identity"
        or type(raw.get("schema_version")) is not int
        or raw.get("schema_version") != 1
        or str(raw.get("variant") or "").strip().lower() != "full"
        or str(raw.get("execution_role") or "").strip().lower()
        != "full_quality_only"
        or raw.get("performance_claims_emitted") is not False
        or canonical["model_id"] != str(expected_model_id or "").strip()
        or canonical["backend"] != str(expected_backend or "").strip().lower()
        or canonical["backend"] != "deepx_m1"
        or canonical["source_run_id"]
        != str(expected_source_run_id or "").strip()
    ):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "DeepX Full-only quality identity is not an exact sealed "
            "non-performance DeepX Full identity"
        )
    identity_sha256 = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(canonical)
    return {
        "full_only_plan_identity_required": True,
        "full_only_plan_identity": canonical,
        "full_only_plan_identity_sha256": identity_sha256,
        "quality_canary_id": canonical["quality_canary_id"],
        "eval_run_id": canonical["eval_run_id"],
        "model_id": canonical["model_id"],
        "setup_id": canonical["setup_id"],
        "source_run_id": canonical["source_run_id"],
        "backend": "deepx_m1",
        "variant": "full",
        "execution_role": "full_quality_only",
        "performance_claims_emitted": False,
    }


def _deepx_export_central_quality_request(
    root: Path, dxnn: Path, run: Mapping[str, Any], semantic: Mapping[str, Any],
    results_dir: Path,
    completed_task_evidence: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
    *, expected_model_id: str = "", expected_backend: str = "",
    expected_variant: str = "",
    full_only_quality_identity: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Export an exact DeepX Full candidate without impersonating the CPU adapter.

    Performance timing has already ended before this function runs.  The
    candidate execution contract attests the *actual* DeepX preprocessing,
    decoder/NMS implementation, output contract and opaque DXNN precision
    artifact.  It is deliberately separate from the management CPU reference
    contract and is joined only at canonical per-image records.
    """
    expected_identity = _deepx_quality_expected_run_identity(
        run,
        expected_model_id=expected_model_id,
        expected_backend=expected_backend,
        expected_variant=expected_variant,
    )
    full_only_duplicates = _deepx_full_only_quality_identity_duplicates(
        full_only_quality_identity,
        expected_model_id=expected_identity["model_id"],
        expected_backend=expected_identity["backend"],
        expected_source_run_id=str(run.get("id") or "deepx_m1_full"),
    )
    policy = _deepx_normalize_quality_policy(run.get("task_quality_gate"))
    if str((policy.get("statistics") or {}).get("execution_location") or "") != "central_management":
        return {}
    task = str(semantic.get("task") or run.get("benchmark_task") or "").strip().lower()
    if task not in {"classification", "detection"}:
        raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_task_unsupported:{task}")
    if str(semantic.get("status") or "") != "ok" or int(semantic.get("error_count") or 0) != 0:
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_semantic_validation_incomplete")
    expected = int(semantic.get("image_count") or 0)
    validated = int(semantic.get("validated_image_count") or 0)
    if expected <= 0 or validated != expected:
        raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_cardinality_mismatch:expected={expected}:validated={validated}")

    # Detection quality is authoritative for a Native-Full performance row
    # only when it is bound to the decoded-NMS completion observed inside that
    # row's measured hot loop.  Keep the physical accelerator endpoint in the
    # producer contract, but add the verified backend-independent completed
    # endpoint as the scientific join identity.
    completed_task_contract: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    completed_task_hash = ""
    completed_task_output_id = ""
    completed_task_attestation_sha = ""
    if task == "detection" and completed_task_evidence is not None:
        evidence = dict(completed_task_evidence)
        try:
            try:
                from splitpoint_runners.native_detection_postprocess import (
                    verify_completed_detection_comparison_endpoint_contract,
                )
            except ModuleNotFoundError:
                # The generated suite imports the deployed runner package;
                # source-tree contract tests use the owning tool package.
                from onnx_splitpoint_tool.native_detection_postprocess import (
                    verify_completed_detection_comparison_endpoint_contract,
                )
            completed_task_contract = (
                verify_completed_detection_comparison_endpoint_contract(
                    evidence.get(
                        "completed_task_comparison_endpoint_contract"
                    )
                )
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "deepx_quality_completed_task_endpoint_invalid:"
                f"{type(exc).__name__}:{exc}"
            ) from exc
        completed_task_hash = str(
            completed_task_contract.get("endpoint_contract_hash") or ""
        ).strip().lower()
        completed_task_output_id = str(
            completed_task_contract.get("output_endpoint_id") or ""
        )
        attestation = evidence.get("completed_task_endpoint_attestation")
        if not isinstance(attestation, Mapping):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "deepx_quality_completed_task_attestation_missing"
            )
        try:
            completed_frames = int(evidence.get("completed_frames") or 0)
            postprocessed_frames = int(
                evidence.get("postprocess_completed_frames") or 0
            )
        except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "deepx_quality_completed_task_frame_count_invalid"
            ) from exc
        if (
            evidence.get("status") != "ok"
            or evidence.get("postprocess_included") is not True
            or evidence.get("postprocess_completion_verified") is not True
            or completed_frames <= 0
            or postprocessed_frames != completed_frames
            or str(evidence.get("completed_task_stage") or "")
            != "decoded_nms"
            or str(evidence.get("completed_task_contract_family") or "")
            != "decoded_nms"
            or str(
                evidence.get(
                    "completed_task_comparison_endpoint_contract_hash"
                ) or ""
            ).strip().lower() != completed_task_hash
            or str(
                evidence.get(
                    "completed_task_comparison_output_endpoint_id"
                ) or ""
            ) != completed_task_output_id
            or attestation.get("attested") is not True
            or str(attestation.get("status") or "").strip().lower()
            != "passed"
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "deepx_quality_completed_task_attestation_invalid"
            )
        completed_task_attestation_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(
            attestation
        )

    manifest = _deepx_resolve_quality_manifest(root, run)
    source_onnx = _deepx_resolve_source_onnx(root)
    contract_raw = str(run.get("contract_path") or "").strip()
    if not contract_raw:
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_output_contract_missing")
    output_contract_path = Path(contract_raw).expanduser()
    if not output_contract_path.is_absolute():
        output_contract_path = (root / output_contract_path).resolve()
    if not output_contract_path.is_file():
        raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_output_contract_missing:{output_contract_path}")
    output_contract = _read_json(output_contract_path)
    if not isinstance(output_contract, Mapping):
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_output_contract_invalid")
    if output_contract.get("contract_complete_enough_for_reports") is False:
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_output_contract_incomplete")
    if not dxnn.is_file():
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_dxnn_missing")
    runner_path = Path(__file__).resolve()
    if not runner_path.is_file():
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_runner_missing")

    samples = _deepx_collect_validation_samples(str(run.get("validation_images") or ""), root, expected)
    if len(samples) != expected:
        raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_sample_cardinality_mismatch:expected={expected}:samples={len(samples)}")
    samples_by_id: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for sample in samples:
        image_id = Path(str(sample.get("path") or "")).name
        if not image_id or image_id in samples_by_id:
            raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_image_ids_not_unique")
        samples_by_id[image_id] = dict(sample)

    preprocessing = semantic.get("preprocessing_contract") if isinstance(semantic.get("preprocessing_contract"), Mapping) else {}
    if not bool(preprocessing.get("pass")) or not bool(preprocessing.get("all_samples_same_contract")):
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_preprocessing_contract_not_attested")
    preprocess_sample = dict(preprocessing.get("invariant_sample") or {}) if isinstance(preprocessing.get("invariant_sample"), Mapping) else {}
    if not preprocess_sample:
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_preprocessing_contract_empty")
    canonical_preprocess_identity = (
        dict(preprocess_sample.get("runtime_preprocessing_identity") or {})
        if isinstance(
            preprocess_sample.get("runtime_preprocessing_identity"), Mapping,
        ) else {}
    )
    canonical_preprocess_sha = str(
        preprocess_sample.get("runtime_preprocessing_sha256") or ""
    ).strip().lower()
    canonical_numeric_identity = (
        dict(preprocess_sample.get("runtime_numeric_input_identity") or {})
        if isinstance(
            preprocess_sample.get("runtime_numeric_input_identity"), Mapping,
        ) else {}
    )
    canonical_numeric_sha = str(
        preprocess_sample.get("runtime_numeric_input_sha256") or ""
    ).strip().lower()
    if (
        canonical_preprocess_identity.get("schema")
        != "onnx-splitpoint/image-preprocessing-contract"
        or int(canonical_preprocess_identity.get("schema_version") or 0) != 2
        or _deepx_quality_json_sha256(canonical_preprocess_identity)
        != canonical_preprocess_sha
        or canonical_numeric_identity.get("schema")
        != "onnx-splitpoint/runtime-numeric-input-identity"
        or int(canonical_numeric_identity.get("schema_version") or 0) != 1
        or _deepx_quality_json_sha256(canonical_numeric_identity)
        != canonical_numeric_sha
        or str(
            canonical_numeric_identity.get(
                "preprocessing_contract_sha256"
            ) or ""
        ).strip().lower() != canonical_preprocess_sha
    ):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "deepx_quality_runtime_preprocessing_identity_invalid"
        )
    if not isinstance(completed_task_evidence, Mapping):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "deepx_quality_performance_prepared_input_binding_missing"
        )
    evidence = dict(completed_task_evidence)
    performance_image_text = str(evidence.get("image") or "").strip()
    performance_image_path = Path(performance_image_text).expanduser()
    if not performance_image_path.is_absolute():
        performance_image_path = (root / performance_image_path).resolve()
    performance_source_sha = str(
        evidence.get("prepared_input_source_image_sha256") or ""
    ).strip().lower()
    performance_source_id = str(
        evidence.get("prepared_input_source_image_id")
        or performance_image_path.name
    ).strip()
    performance_prepared_binding: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "prepared_input_sha256": str(
            evidence.get("prepared_input_sha256") or ""
        ).strip().lower(),
        "prepared_input_bytes": int(
            evidence.get("prepared_input_bytes") or 0
        ),
        "prepared_input_name": str(
            evidence.get("prepared_input_name") or ""
        ).strip(),
        "prepared_input_shape": list(
            evidence.get("prepared_input_shape") or []
        ),
        "prepared_input_dtype": str(
            evidence.get("prepared_input_dtype") or ""
        ).strip().lower(),
        "prepared_input_layout": str(
            evidence.get("prepared_input_layout") or ""
        ).strip().upper(),
    }
    canonical_runtime_shape = list(
        canonical_numeric_identity.get("runtime_input_shape") or []
    )
    canonical_runtime_dtype = str(
        canonical_numeric_identity.get("runtime_input_dtype") or ""
    ).strip().lower()
    canonical_runtime_layout = str(
        canonical_numeric_identity.get("runtime_input_layout") or ""
    ).strip().upper()
    canonical_runtime_name = str(
        canonical_numeric_identity.get("runtime_input_name") or ""
    ).strip()
    canonical_dtype_bytes = {
        "uint8": 1, "int8": 1, "uint16": 2, "int16": 2,
        "float16": 2, "uint32": 4, "int32": 4,
        "float32": 4, "float64": 8,
    }
    canonical_runtime_bytes = int(
        math.prod(canonical_runtime_shape)
        * canonical_dtype_bytes.get(canonical_runtime_dtype, 0)
    ) if canonical_runtime_shape else 0
    if (
        dict(evidence.get("runtime_preprocessing_identity") or {})
            != canonical_preprocess_identity
        or str(
            evidence.get("runtime_preprocessing_sha256") or ""
        ).strip().lower() != canonical_preprocess_sha
        or dict(evidence.get("runtime_numeric_input_identity") or {})
        != canonical_numeric_identity
        or str(
            evidence.get("runtime_numeric_input_sha256") or ""
        ).strip().lower() != canonical_numeric_sha
        or evidence.get("prepared_input_binding_verified") is not True
        or not performance_image_path.is_file()
        or not performance_source_id
        or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", performance_source_sha)
        or hashlib.sha256(performance_image_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        != performance_source_sha
        or not re.fullmatch(
            r"[0-9a-f]{64}",
            performance_prepared_binding["prepared_input_sha256"],
        )
        or performance_prepared_binding["prepared_input_bytes"] <= 0
        or not performance_prepared_binding["prepared_input_name"]
        or not performance_prepared_binding["prepared_input_shape"]
        or not performance_prepared_binding["prepared_input_dtype"]
        or not performance_prepared_binding["prepared_input_layout"]
        or performance_prepared_binding["prepared_input_name"]
        != canonical_runtime_name
        or performance_prepared_binding["prepared_input_shape"]
        != canonical_runtime_shape
        or performance_prepared_binding["prepared_input_dtype"]
        != canonical_runtime_dtype
        or performance_prepared_binding["prepared_input_layout"]
        != canonical_runtime_layout
        or performance_prepared_binding["prepared_input_bytes"]
        != canonical_runtime_bytes
    ):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "deepx_quality_performance_prepared_input_binding_mismatch"
        )
    runtime_output = semantic.get("runtime_output_contract") if isinstance(semantic.get("runtime_output_contract"), Mapping) else {}
    if (
        not bool(runtime_output.get("pass"))
        or not bool(runtime_output.get("all_samples_same_contract"))
        or int(runtime_output.get("validated_observation_count") or 0) != expected
        or not isinstance(runtime_output.get("sample"), Mapping)
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_runtime_output_contract_not_attested")

    records_path_key = "classification_topk_json" if task == "classification" else "detections_json"
    records_raw = str(semantic.get(records_path_key) or "").strip()
    records_path = Path(records_raw).expanduser()
    if not records_path.is_absolute():
        records_path = (root / records_path).resolve()
    if not records_path.is_file():
        raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_records_missing:{records_path_key}")
    payload = _read_json(records_path)
    raw_records = list(payload.get("images") or []) if isinstance(payload, Mapping) else []
    if len(raw_records) != expected:
        raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_record_cardinality_mismatch:expected={expected}:records={len(raw_records)}")

    candidate_records: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    per_image_transforms: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for raw_record in raw_records:
        if not isinstance(raw_record, Mapping):
            raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_record_not_object")
        image_id = Path(str(raw_record.get("image") or "")).name
        if not image_id or image_id in seen or image_id not in samples_by_id:
            raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_record_image_id_invalid:{image_id}")
        seen.add(image_id)
        sample = samples_by_id[image_id]
        audit = raw_record.get("preprocessing_audit") if isinstance(raw_record.get("preprocessing_audit"), Mapping) else {}
        if not audit or len(list(audit.get("source_shape_hw") or [])) != 2:
            raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_per_image_preprocess_audit_missing:{image_id}")
        tensor_binding = (
            dict(audit.get("prepared_tensor_binding") or {})
            if isinstance(audit.get("prepared_tensor_binding"), Mapping)
            else {}
        )
        sample_path = Path(str(sample.get("path") or "")).expanduser()
        if not sample_path.is_absolute():
            sample_path = (root / sample_path).resolve()
        sample_source_sha = (
            hashlib.sha256(sample_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            if sample_path.is_file() else ""
        )
        if (
            tensor_binding.get("binding_verified") is not True
            or not re.fullmatch(
                r"[0-9a-f]{64}",
                str(tensor_binding.get("prepared_input_sha256") or ""),
            )
            or int(tensor_binding.get("prepared_input_bytes") or 0) <= 0
            or not str(tensor_binding.get("prepared_input_name") or "")
            or not isinstance(tensor_binding.get("prepared_input_shape"), list)
            or not tensor_binding.get("prepared_input_shape")
            or not str(tensor_binding.get("prepared_input_dtype") or "")
            or not str(tensor_binding.get("prepared_input_layout") or "")
            or str(tensor_binding.get("source_image_id") or "")
            != image_id
            or not re.fullmatch(
                r"[0-9a-f]{64}",
                str(tensor_binding.get("source_image_sha256") or ""),
            )
            or str(
                tensor_binding.get("source_image_sha256") or ""
            ).strip().lower() != sample_source_sha
            or not re.fullmatch(
                r"[0-9a-f]{64}",
                str(
                    tensor_binding.get("prepared_rgb_uint8_sha256") or ""
                ),
            )
            or int(tensor_binding.get("prepared_rgb_uint8_bytes") or 0) <= 0
            or not isinstance(
                tensor_binding.get("runtime_preprocessing_identity"), Mapping,
            )
            or _deepx_quality_json_sha256(
                tensor_binding["runtime_preprocessing_identity"]
            ) != str(
                tensor_binding.get("runtime_preprocessing_sha256") or ""
            )
            or not isinstance(
                tensor_binding.get("runtime_numeric_input_identity"), Mapping,
            )
            or _deepx_quality_json_sha256(
                tensor_binding["runtime_numeric_input_identity"]
            ) != str(
                tensor_binding.get("runtime_numeric_input_sha256") or ""
            )
            or dict(
                tensor_binding.get("runtime_preprocessing_identity") or {}
            ) != canonical_preprocess_identity
            or str(
                tensor_binding.get("runtime_preprocessing_sha256") or ""
            ).strip().lower() != canonical_preprocess_sha
            or dict(
                tensor_binding.get("runtime_numeric_input_identity") or {}
            ) != canonical_numeric_identity
            or str(
                tensor_binding.get("runtime_numeric_input_sha256") or ""
            ).strip().lower() != canonical_numeric_sha
            or str(tensor_binding.get("prepared_input_name") or "").strip()
            != canonical_runtime_name
            or list(tensor_binding.get("prepared_input_shape") or [])
            != canonical_runtime_shape
            or str(
                tensor_binding.get("prepared_input_dtype") or ""
            ).strip().lower() != canonical_runtime_dtype
            or str(
                tensor_binding.get("prepared_input_layout") or ""
            ).strip().upper() != canonical_runtime_layout
            or int(tensor_binding.get("prepared_input_bytes") or 0)
            != canonical_runtime_bytes
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"deepx_quality_prepared_tensor_binding_invalid:{image_id}"
            )
        per_image_transform: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "image_id": image_id,
            "source_shape_hw": [int(value) for value in list(audit.get("source_shape_hw") or [])],
            "scale": float(audit.get("scale")),
            "pad_x": int(audit.get("pad_x")), "pad_y": int(audit.get("pad_y")),
            "preprocess_mode": str(audit.get("preprocess_mode") or ""),
            "prepared_tensor_binding": tensor_binding,
        }
        if task == "classification":
            label = raw_record.get("label_id")
            if label is None or sample.get("label_id") is None or int(label) != int(sample.get("label_id")):
                raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_classification_label_missing_or_mismatch:{image_id}")
            top1 = raw_record.get("top1")
            top5 = raw_record.get("top5")
            scores = raw_record.get("scores")
            if top1 is None or not isinstance(top5, list) or not top5 or not isinstance(scores, list):
                raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_topk_incomplete:{image_id}")
            if any(not math.isfinite(float(value)) for value in scores):
                raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_nonfinite_classification_score:{image_id}")
            top1_hit = bool(int(top1) == int(label))
            top5_hit = bool(int(label) in [int(value) for value in top5])
            if raw_record.get("top1_correct") is not top1_hit or raw_record.get("top5_correct") is not top5_hit:
                raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_topk_hit_attestation_mismatch:{image_id}")
            # ``label_id`` is the authoritative class identity.  A vendor
            # runtime may decorate it with names from an external label map,
            # but those bytes are not part of the frozen validation contract.
            # Seal the candidate with the name from the exact manifest sample
            # whose file hash is carried by the quality contract instead.
            canonical_label_name = sample.get("label_name")
            candidate_records.append({
                "image_id": image_id, "label_id": int(label),
                "label_name": canonical_label_name,
                "candidate": {"top1_hit": top1_hit, "top5_hit": top5_hit, "top1": int(top1), "top5": [int(v) for v in top5]},
            })
            per_image_transforms.append(per_image_transform)
        else:
            source_hw = list(audit.get("source_shape_hw") or [])
            target_size = int(semantic.get("input_size") or 0)
            target_hw = [target_size, target_size]
            letterbox = str(audit.get("preprocess_mode") or "").strip().lower() == "letterbox"
            if len(source_hw) != 2 or target_size <= 0:
                raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_detection_transform_missing:{image_id}")
            per_image_transform.update({
                "target_shape_hw": target_hw,
                "canonical_record_coordinate_space": "original_image_xyxy_pixels",
                "original_shape_hw": [int(source_hw[0]), int(source_hw[1])],
            })
            ground_truth: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
            for ann in list(sample.get("annotations") or []):
                projected = _deepx_coco_ann_to_det(dict(ann))
                if projected is None:
                    continue
                class_id = int(projected["class_id"])
                if class_id < 0 or class_id >= len(_DEEPX_COCO80):
                    raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_ground_truth_class_invalid:{image_id}")
                box = _deepx_clip_box_to_original_image(
                    [projected["x1"], projected["y1"], projected["x2"], projected["y2"]],
                    source_hw=source_hw,
                )
                if box[2] <= box[0] or box[3] <= box[1]:
                    continue
                ground_truth.append({
                    "x1": box[0], "y1": box[1], "x2": box[2], "y2": box[3],
                    "score": 1.0, "class_id": class_id, "class_name": _DEEPX_COCO80[class_id],
                })
            predictions: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
            for detection in list(raw_record.get("detections") or []):
                if not isinstance(detection, Mapping):
                    raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_detection_not_object:{image_id}")
                coords_original, score, class_id = (
                    _deepx_detection_record_components(
                        detection, image_id=image_id,
                    )
                )
                if class_id < 0 or class_id >= len(_DEEPX_COCO80):
                    raise RuntimeError(f"deepx_quality_detection_class_invalid:{image_id}")
                coords = _deepx_clip_box_to_original_image(
                    coords_original, source_hw=source_hw,
                )
                predictions.append({
                    "x1": coords[0], "y1": coords[1], "x2": coords[2], "y2": coords[3],
                    "score": score, "class_id": class_id, "class_name": _DEEPX_COCO80[class_id],
                })
            candidate_records.append({"image_id": image_id, "ground_truth": ground_truth, "candidate": predictions})
            per_image_transforms.append(per_image_transform)
    if set(samples_by_id) != seen:
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_candidate_image_set_incomplete")
    candidate_records.sort(key=lambda record: str(record["image_id"]))
    per_image_transforms.sort(key=lambda record: str(record["image_id"]))
    performance_quality_matches = [
        row for row in per_image_transforms
        if str(row.get("image_id") or "") == performance_source_id
        and str(
            (row.get("prepared_tensor_binding") or {}).get(
                "source_image_sha256"
            ) or ""
        ).strip().lower() == performance_source_sha
    ]
    if len(performance_quality_matches) != 1:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "deepx_quality_performance_source_image_not_unique"
        )
    matched_quality_binding = dict(
        performance_quality_matches[0]["prepared_tensor_binding"]
    )
    quality_prepared_binding = {
        "prepared_input_sha256": str(
            matched_quality_binding.get("prepared_input_sha256") or ""
        ).strip().lower(),
        "prepared_input_bytes": int(
            matched_quality_binding.get("prepared_input_bytes") or 0
        ),
        "prepared_input_name": str(
            matched_quality_binding.get("prepared_input_name") or ""
        ).strip(),
        "prepared_input_shape": list(
            matched_quality_binding.get("prepared_input_shape") or []
        ),
        "prepared_input_dtype": str(
            matched_quality_binding.get("prepared_input_dtype") or ""
        ).strip().lower(),
        "prepared_input_layout": str(
            matched_quality_binding.get("prepared_input_layout") or ""
        ).strip().upper(),
    }
    if quality_prepared_binding != performance_prepared_binding:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "deepx_quality_performance_prepared_input_byte_mismatch"
        )
    performance_quality_input_binding = {
        "schema": (
            "onnx-splitpoint/deepx-performance-quality-input-binding"
        ),
        "schema_version": 1,
        "binding_verified": True,
        "source_image_id": performance_source_id,
        "source_image_sha256": performance_source_sha,
        **performance_prepared_binding,
        "runtime_preprocessing_sha256": canonical_preprocess_sha,
        "runtime_numeric_input_sha256": canonical_numeric_sha,
    }
    performance_quality_input_binding_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(
        performance_quality_input_binding
    )
    image_ids = [str(record["image_id"]) for record in candidate_records]
    typed_ids = sorted(f"str:{value}" for value in image_ids)
    image_ids_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(typed_ids)
    if task == "classification":
        gt_identity = [
            {
                "image_id": record["image_id"],
                "label_id": int(record.get("label_id")),
            }
            for record in candidate_records
        ]
    else:
        gt_identity = [
            {"image_id": record["image_id"], "ground_truth": record.get("ground_truth")}
            for record in candidate_records
        ]
    ground_truth_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(gt_identity)

    decoder_contract = semantic.get("decoder_postprocess_contract") if isinstance(semantic.get("decoder_postprocess_contract"), Mapping) else {}
    runtime_endpoint_signature = _deepx_endpoint_runtime_signature(runtime_output.get("sample"))
    decoder_identity: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    nms_identity: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    if task == "detection":
        if not bool(decoder_contract.get("pass")) or not bool(decoder_contract.get("all_samples_same_contract")):
            raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_decoder_contract_not_attested")
        if int(decoder_contract.get("validated_contract_count") or 0) != expected:
            raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_decoder_contract_cardinality_mismatch")
        contracts = list(decoder_contract.get("contracts") or [])
        if not contracts or not isinstance(contracts[0], Mapping):
            raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_decoder_contract_missing")
        actual_decoder = dict(contracts[0])
        if not bool(actual_decoder.get("pass")) or not str(actual_decoder.get("decoder_id") or ""):
            raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_decoder_identity_incomplete")
        if not str(actual_decoder.get("source_endpoint_semantics") or ""):
            raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_source_endpoint_semantics_missing")
        if actual_decoder.get("source_endpoint_has_integrated_nms") is False and (
            actual_decoder.get("host_decoder_applied") is not True
            or actual_decoder.get("host_nms_applied") is not True
            or actual_decoder.get("nms_iou_threshold") is None
            or actual_decoder.get("nms_max_detections") is None
        ):
            raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_host_decoder_nms_contract_incomplete")
        contract_model_id = _deepx_contract_model_id(
            root, dict(run), dict(output_contract),
        )
        if not contract_model_id:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "deepx_quality_completed_task_model_identity_missing"
            )
        if completed_task_contract and (
            str(completed_task_contract.get("model_id") or "")
            != contract_model_id
            or float(completed_task_contract.get("score_threshold") or -1.0)
            != float(actual_decoder.get("confidence_threshold") or -1.0)
            or float(completed_task_contract.get("iou_threshold") or -1.0)
            != float(actual_decoder.get("nms_iou_threshold") or -1.0)
            or int(completed_task_contract.get("max_detections") or 0)
            != int(actual_decoder.get("nms_max_detections") or 0)
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "deepx_quality_completed_task_semantic_contract_mismatch"
            )
        decoder_identity = {
            "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/deepx-decoder-contract", "schema_version": 1,
            "canonical_record_endpoint": "decoded_xyxy_score_class_detections",
            "source_endpoint_semantics": actual_decoder.get("source_endpoint_semantics"),
            "source_endpoint_has_integrated_nms": actual_decoder.get("source_endpoint_has_integrated_nms"),
            "decoder_id": actual_decoder.get("decoder_id"),
            "host_decoder_applied": actual_decoder.get("host_decoder_applied"),
            "confidence_threshold": actual_decoder.get("confidence_threshold"),
            "implementation_runner_sha256": _deepx_quality_file_sha256(runner_path),
            "implementation_function": "_deepx_detection_decode",
            "output_contract_sha256": _deepx_quality_file_sha256(output_contract_path),
            "frozen_postprocess_invariant_contract_sha256": str(
                actual_decoder.get("frozen_postprocess_invariant_contract_sha256") or ""
            ),
        }
        nms_identity = {
            "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/deepx-nms-contract", "schema_version": 1,
            "canonical_record_endpoint": "decoded_xyxy_score_class_detections",
            "source_endpoint_has_integrated_nms": actual_decoder.get("source_endpoint_has_integrated_nms"),
            "host_nms_applied": actual_decoder.get("host_nms_applied"),
            "iou_threshold": actual_decoder.get("nms_iou_threshold"),
            "max_detections": actual_decoder.get("nms_max_detections"),
            "class_aware": True,
            "implementation_runner_sha256": _deepx_quality_file_sha256(runner_path),
            "implementation_function": (
                "_deepx_yolo_nms" if actual_decoder.get("host_nms_applied") is True
                else "DetectionCompletionRuntime.process"
            ),
        }
        quality_record_endpoint_identity = {
            "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/deepx-detection-endpoint-contract", "schema_version": 1,
            "canonical_record_endpoint": "decoded_xyxy_score_class_detections",
            "source_endpoint": {
                "semantics": actual_decoder.get("source_endpoint_semantics"),
                "has_integrated_nms": actual_decoder.get("source_endpoint_has_integrated_nms"),
                "observed_outputs": runtime_endpoint_signature,
            },
            "host_decoder_nms": {
                "decoder_id": actual_decoder.get("decoder_id"),
                "decoder_applied": actual_decoder.get("host_decoder_applied"),
                "nms_applied": actual_decoder.get("host_nms_applied"),
                "confidence_threshold": actual_decoder.get("confidence_threshold"),
                "iou_threshold": actual_decoder.get("nms_iou_threshold"),
                "max_detections": actual_decoder.get("nms_max_detections"),
            },
            "decoder_contract": actual_decoder,
            "implementation_runner_sha256": _deepx_quality_file_sha256(runner_path),
            "implementation_functions": ["_deepx_detection_decode", (
                "_deepx_yolo_nms" if actual_decoder.get("host_nms_applied") is True
                else "DetectionCompletionRuntime.process"
            )],
            "output_contract_sha256": _deepx_quality_file_sha256(output_contract_path),
            "runtime_observed_outputs": runtime_endpoint_signature,
            "canonical_coordinate_space": "original_image_xyxy_pixels",
        }
    else:
        quality_record_endpoint_identity = {
            "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/deepx-classification-endpoint-contract", "schema_version": 1,
            "canonical_record_endpoint": "classification_topk_hits",
            "postprocessor": "numpy_argsort_descending_top5",
            "implementation_runner_sha256": _deepx_quality_file_sha256(runner_path),
            "implementation_functions": ["_run_deepx_semantic_validation"],
            "output_contract_sha256": _deepx_quality_file_sha256(output_contract_path),
            "runtime_observed_outputs": runtime_endpoint_signature,
        }
    quality_record_endpoint_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(quality_record_endpoint_identity)
    classification_source = (
        semantic.get("classification_source_endpoint_contract")
        if isinstance(semantic.get("classification_source_endpoint_contract"), Mapping)
        else {}
    )
    if task == "classification" and (
        classification_source.get("pass") is not True
        or int(classification_source.get("validated_observation_count") or 0) != expected
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_quality_classification_runtime_endpoint_not_attested")
    runtime_endpoint_identity = _deepx_standard_runtime_endpoint_identity(
        task=task,
        runtime_observation=runtime_output.get("sample"),
        output_contract=output_contract,
        source_has_integrated_nms=(
            actual_decoder.get("source_endpoint_has_integrated_nms")
            if task == "detection" else None
        ),
        classification_observed_stage=str(classification_source.get("stage") or ""),
        expected_model_id=expected_identity["model_id"],
        expected_backend=expected_identity["backend"],
        expected_variant=expected_identity["variant"],
    )
    endpoint_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(runtime_endpoint_identity)
    dxnn_sha = _deepx_quality_file_sha256(dxnn)
    precision_identity = {
        "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/deepx-runtime-precision-contract", "schema_version": 1,
        "artifact_kind": "dxnn", "artifact_name": dxnn.name, "artifact_sha256": dxnn_sha,
        "artifact_size_bytes": dxnn.stat().st_size,
        "precision_semantics": "opaque_vendor_compiled_artifact_identity",
        "declared_precision": output_contract.get("precision") or output_contract.get("numeric_precision"),
    }
    precision_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(precision_identity)
    # This identity is intentionally the shared canonical-v2 contract used by
    # CPU/TensorRT/Hailo and by the sealed DeepX performance tensor.  Per-image
    # geometry and exact numeric tensor hashes are separate evidence and must
    # never be folded into (or weaken) this cross-backend identity.
    preprocess_identity = canonical_preprocess_identity
    preprocess_sha = canonical_preprocess_sha
    per_image_prepared_inputs_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(
        per_image_transforms
    )
    decoder_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(decoder_identity) if decoder_identity else ""
    nms_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(nms_identity) if nms_identity else ""
    quality_contract: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/deepx-central-quality-record-contract",
        "schema_version": 1,
        "contract_scope": "canonical_quality_record_semantics",
        "task": task,
        "variant": "full",
        "model": {
            "source_onnx_name": source_onnx.name,
            "source_onnx_sha256": _deepx_quality_file_sha256(source_onnx),
        },
        "dataset": {
            "manifest_name": manifest.name,
            "manifest_sha256": (
                _deepx_portable_dataset_manifest_sha256(manifest)
            ),
            "image_ids_sha256": image_ids_sha,
            "ground_truth_sha256": ground_truth_sha,
            "image_count": len(image_ids),
            **({"coordinate_space": "original_image_xyxy_pixels"} if task == "detection" else {"class_identity": "label_id"}),
        },
        "preprocessing": {"identity": preprocess_identity, "sha256": preprocess_sha},
        "prepared_input_evidence": {
            "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/deepx-quality-prepared-input-set",
            "schema_version": 1,
            "record_count": len(per_image_transforms),
            "records_sha256": per_image_prepared_inputs_sha,
            "runtime_numeric_input_identity": canonical_numeric_identity,
            "runtime_numeric_input_sha256": canonical_numeric_sha,
            "performance_quality_input_binding": (
                performance_quality_input_binding
            ),
            "performance_quality_input_binding_sha256": (
                performance_quality_input_binding_sha
            ),
        },
        "quality_record_endpoint": {
            "identity": quality_record_endpoint_identity,
            "sha256": quality_record_endpoint_sha,
        },
        "quality_record_endpoint_contract_sha256": quality_record_endpoint_sha,
        "canonical_record_endpoint": quality_record_endpoint_identity.get("canonical_record_endpoint"),
        **({"canonical_coordinate_space": "original_image_xyxy_pixels"} if task == "detection" else {}),
    }
    if decoder_identity:
        quality_contract["decoder"] = {"identity": decoder_identity, "sha256": decoder_sha}
    if nms_identity:
        quality_contract["nms"] = {"identity": nms_identity, "sha256": nms_sha}
    quality_contract_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(quality_contract)
    quality_contract = {
        **quality_contract, "quality_contract_sha256": quality_contract_sha,
    }
    producer = {
        "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/central-quality-producer-identity", "schema_version": 1,
        "backend": "deepx_m1", "source_run_id": str(run.get("id") or "deepx_m1_full"),
        "case_id": "full", "variant": "full", "task": task,
        "model": {
            "source_onnx_name": source_onnx.name, "source_onnx_sha256": _deepx_quality_file_sha256(source_onnx),
            "runtime_artifact_name": dxnn.name, "runtime_artifact_sha256": dxnn_sha,
        },
        "dataset": {
            "manifest_name": manifest.name,
            "manifest_sha256": (
                _deepx_portable_dataset_manifest_sha256(manifest)
            ),
            "image_ids_sha256": image_ids_sha, "ground_truth_sha256": ground_truth_sha,
            "image_count": len(image_ids),
        },
        "preprocessing": {"identity": preprocess_identity, "sha256": preprocess_sha},
        "prepared_input_evidence": dict(
            quality_contract["prepared_input_evidence"]
        ),
        "endpoint": {"identity": runtime_endpoint_identity, "sha256": endpoint_sha},
        "quality_record_endpoint": {
            "identity": quality_record_endpoint_identity,
            "sha256": quality_record_endpoint_sha,
        },
        "precision": {"identity": precision_identity, "sha256": precision_sha},
        "quality_contract": quality_contract,
        "quality_contract_sha256": quality_contract_sha,
        "preprocessing_contract_sha256": preprocess_sha,
        "prepared_input_evidence_sha256": per_image_prepared_inputs_sha,
        "prepared_input_join_binding": performance_quality_input_binding,
        "prepared_input_join_binding_sha256": (
            performance_quality_input_binding_sha
        ),
        "decoder_contract_sha256": decoder_sha,
        "nms_contract_sha256": nms_sha,
        "endpoint_contract_hash": endpoint_sha,
        "quality_record_endpoint_contract_sha256": quality_record_endpoint_sha,
        "runtime_precision_identity": f"deepx_dxnn_sha256:{dxnn_sha}",
        "implementation_runner_sha256": _deepx_quality_file_sha256(runner_path),
        **full_only_duplicates,
        **({
            "completed_task_endpoint_contract": completed_task_contract,
            "completed_task_endpoint_contract_hash": completed_task_hash,
            "completed_task_output_endpoint_id": completed_task_output_id,
            "completed_task_endpoint_attestation": dict(attestation),
            "completed_task_endpoint_attestation_sha256": (
                completed_task_attestation_sha
            ),
            "quality_join_endpoint": "completed_task_decoded_nms",
        } if completed_task_contract else {}),
    }
    producer_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(producer)
    producer = {**producer, "producer_identity_sha256": producer_sha}

    base = results_dir / "task_quality_inputs"
    candidate_payload = {
        "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/task-quality-candidate-input", "schema_version": 1,
        "task": task, "variant": "full", "pairing_key": "image_id",
        "record_count": len(candidate_records),
        "producer_provenance_required": True, "producer_identity": producer,
        "producer_identity_sha256": producer_sha,
        "quality_contract": quality_contract,
        "quality_contract_sha256": quality_contract_sha,
        "preprocessing_contract_sha256": preprocess_sha,
        "prepared_input_evidence_sha256": per_image_prepared_inputs_sha,
        "prepared_input_join_binding": performance_quality_input_binding,
        "prepared_input_join_binding_sha256": (
            performance_quality_input_binding_sha
        ),
        "decoder_contract_sha256": decoder_sha,
        "nms_contract_sha256": nms_sha,
        "quality_record_endpoint_contract_sha256": quality_record_endpoint_sha,
        "runtime_precision_identity": producer["runtime_precision_identity"],
        **({
            "completed_task_endpoint_contract": completed_task_contract,
            "completed_task_endpoint_contract_hash": completed_task_hash,
            "completed_task_output_endpoint_id": completed_task_output_id,
            "completed_task_endpoint_attestation": dict(attestation),
            "completed_task_endpoint_attestation_sha256": (
                completed_task_attestation_sha
            ),
            "quality_join_endpoint": "completed_task_decoded_nms",
        } if completed_task_contract else {}),
        "per_image_transforms": per_image_transforms,
        "records": candidate_records,
        **full_only_duplicates,
    }
    candidate_descriptor = _deepx_write_quality_json(base / "full_candidate.json", candidate_payload)
    candidate_descriptor["path"] = Path(str(candidate_descriptor["path"])).name
    reference_descriptor = {
        "source": "management_cpu_reference", "reference_role": "canonical_cpu_ort",
        "semantic_reference_only": True, "required": True,
        "expected_image_ids": image_ids, "expected_image_ids_sha256": image_ids_sha,
        "record_count": len(image_ids),
    }
    stats = dict(policy.get("statistics") or {})
    request_payload = {
        "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/central-quality-evaluation-request", "schema_version": 1,
        "status": "pending_central_evaluation", "task": task, "variant": "full",
        "pairing_key": "image_id", "execution_location": "management_node",
        "requested_by": "central_management", "reference": reference_descriptor,
        "candidate": candidate_descriptor, "record_count": len(candidate_records),
        "reference_record_count": len(candidate_records), "expected_image_ids": image_ids,
        "expected_image_ids_sha256": image_ids_sha,
        "producer_provenance_required": True, "producer_identity": producer,
        "producer_identity_sha256": producer_sha,
        "quality_contract": quality_contract,
        "quality_contract_sha256": quality_contract_sha,
        "preprocessing_contract_sha256": preprocess_sha,
        "prepared_input_evidence_sha256": per_image_prepared_inputs_sha,
        "prepared_input_join_binding": performance_quality_input_binding,
        "prepared_input_join_binding_sha256": (
            performance_quality_input_binding_sha
        ),
        "decoder_contract_sha256": decoder_sha,
        "nms_contract_sha256": nms_sha,
        "endpoint_contract_hash": endpoint_sha,
        "quality_record_endpoint_contract_sha256": quality_record_endpoint_sha,
        "runtime_precision_identity": producer["runtime_precision_identity"],
        **full_only_duplicates,
        **({
            "completed_task_endpoint_contract": completed_task_contract,
            "completed_task_endpoint_contract_hash": completed_task_hash,
            "completed_task_output_endpoint_id": completed_task_output_id,
            "completed_task_endpoint_attestation": dict(attestation),
            "completed_task_endpoint_attestation_sha256": (
                completed_task_attestation_sha
            ),
            "quality_join_endpoint": "completed_task_decoded_nms",
        } if completed_task_contract else {}),
        "policy_sha256": str(policy.get("policy_sha256") or ""),
        "metric_gate_config": dict(policy.get(task) or {}),
        "statistics": {
            "method": str(stats.get("method") or "paired_bootstrap"),
            "bootstrap_repetitions": int(stats.get("bootstrap_repetitions") or 500),
            "seed": int(stats.get("seed") or 20260710),
            "confidence_level": float(stats.get("confidence_level") or 0.95),
            "decision": str(stats.get("decision") or "lower_one_sided_bound"),
        },
    }
    request_descriptor = _deepx_write_quality_json(base / "full_request.json", request_payload)
    request_descriptor["path"] = str(Path(str(request_descriptor["path"])).resolve())
    return {**request_payload, "request": request_descriptor, "policy": policy}




def _deepx_find_prepared_feed_image(root: Path, input_size: int, task: str, run: Dict[str, Any], args: Any) -> Tuple[Optional[Path], str]:
    """Select one frozen validation record for the shared sealed feed.

    The Generic timing loop and later Native Full replay must use bytes derived
    from the quality-validation cohort.  Suite convenience images are only a
    compatibility fallback when no frozen validation source was supplied.
    """
    # Native Full energy pairs must replay the same source image as their
    # Split row.  Keep this single-feed selector separate from semantic
    # validation so measurement-only mode can still bind an exact image while
    # dataset-wide validation remains disabled.
    explicit = str(getattr(args, "prepared_feed_image", "") or "").strip()
    if explicit:
        raw = Path(explicit).expanduser()
        candidates = [raw] if raw.is_absolute() else [root / raw]
        for candidate in candidates:
            if candidate.is_file():
                return candidate.resolve(), "explicit_prepared_feed_image"
        return None, "explicit_prepared_feed_image_missing"

    # Prefer the first deterministic record from the frozen validation source.
    # This is still a single feed repeated, not a dataset replay timing loop.
    src = str(getattr(args, "validation_images", "") or run.get("validation_images") or "").strip()
    try:
        samples = _deepx_collect_validation_samples(src, root, 1)
        if samples:
            p0 = Path(str(samples[0].get("path") or ""))
            if p0.is_file():
                return p0.resolve(), "frozen_validation_manifest_first_record"
    except Exception:
        pass

    task_l = str(task or "").strip().lower()
    td = root / "test_images"
    cands: List[Tuple[Path, str]] = []
    if task_l == "classification" or int(input_size or 0) <= 384:
        cands.extend([(td / "test_image_imagenet.png", "suite_test_image_imagenet_fallback"), (td / "test_image.png", "suite_test_image_legacy_fallback")])
    else:
        cands.extend([(td / "test_image_coco.png", "suite_test_image_coco_fallback"), (td / "test_image.png", "suite_test_image_legacy_fallback")])
    for path, label in cands:
        if path.is_file():
            return path.resolve(), label
    return None, "not_found"


def _deepx_load_sealed_prepared_feed(
    manifest_path: Path,
    *,
    input_contract: Mapping[str, Any],
    image_path: Path,
    task: str,
    np: Any,
) -> Tuple[Any, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Load the exact Semantic-Dump tensor; never rebuild numeric input."""

    def _absolute_without_resolving(path: Path) -> Path:
        candidate = path.expanduser()
        if not candidate.is_absolute():
            candidate = Path.cwd() / candidate
        return candidate

    def _contains_symlink(path: Path) -> bool:
        """Inspect the complete chain before resolve() can hide a link."""
        candidate = _absolute_without_resolving(path)
        current = Path(candidate.anchor)
        for part in candidate.parts[1:]:
            current = current / part
            if current.is_symlink():
                return True
        return False

    manifest_path = _absolute_without_resolving(manifest_path)
    if (
        _contains_symlink(manifest_path)
        or manifest_path.name != "native_full_input_manifest.json"
        or not manifest_path.is_file()
    ):
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_manifest_missing")
    manifest_path = manifest_path.resolve(strict=True)
    payload = _read_json(manifest_path)
    if not isinstance(payload, Mapping):
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_manifest_invalid")
    payload = dict(payload)
    if (
        payload.get("schema") != "onnx-splitpoint/native-full-input-dump"
        or int(payload.get("schema_version") or 0) != 2
        or str(payload.get("backend") or "").strip().lower()
        != "native_full_deepx"
        or str(payload.get("task") or "").strip().lower()
        != str(task or "").strip().lower()
    ):
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_manifest_identity_mismatch")

    inp = (
        dict(input_contract.get("input") or {})
        if isinstance(input_contract.get("input"), Mapping) else {}
    )
    shape = payload.get("runtime_input_shape")
    if (
        not isinstance(shape, list) or not shape
        or any(isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, int) or v <= 0 for v in shape)
    ):
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_shape_invalid")
    shape = [int(v) for v in shape]
    if shape != [int(v) for v in list(inp.get("shape") or [])]:
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_contract_shape_mismatch")
    dtype_aliases = {
        "u8": "uint8", "uint8": "uint8",
        "float": "float32", "fp32": "float32", "float32": "float32",
    }
    dtype_name = dtype_aliases.get(
        str(payload.get("runtime_input_dtype") or "").strip().lower(), "",
    )
    contract_dtype = dtype_aliases.get(
        str(inp.get("dtype") or "").strip().lower(), "",
    )
    if not dtype_name or dtype_name != contract_dtype:
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_contract_dtype_mismatch")
    layout = str(payload.get("runtime_input_layout") or "").strip().upper()
    if layout != str(inp.get("layout") or "").strip().upper():
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_contract_layout_mismatch")
    color_space = str(payload.get("runtime_color_space") or "").strip().upper()
    normalization = str(payload.get("runtime_normalization") or "").strip().lower()
    preprocess = (
        dict(payload.get("preprocess") or {})
        if isinstance(payload.get("preprocess"), Mapping) else {}
    )
    preprocess_mode = str(
        payload.get("runtime_preprocess_mode")
        or preprocess.get("mode") or preprocess.get("preprocess_mode") or ""
    ).strip().lower()
    canonical_preprocess_mode = (
        preprocess_mode[:-len("_rgb_uint8")]
        if preprocess_mode.endswith("_rgb_uint8") else preprocess_mode
    )
    if (
        color_space != str(inp.get("color_space") or "").strip().upper()
        or normalization != str(inp.get("normalization") or "").strip().lower()
        or canonical_preprocess_mode
        != str(inp.get("preprocess_mode") or "").strip().lower()
    ):
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_preprocessing_contract_mismatch")

    semantic_identity = payload.get("runtime_preprocessing_identity")
    numeric_identity = payload.get("runtime_numeric_input_identity")
    semantic_sha = str(payload.get("runtime_preprocessing_sha256") or "").strip().lower()
    numeric_sha = str(payload.get("runtime_numeric_input_sha256") or "").strip().lower()
    canonical = lambda value: json.dumps(
        dict(value), sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False,
    ).encode("utf-8")
    if (
        not isinstance(semantic_identity, Mapping)
        or not isinstance(numeric_identity, Mapping)
        or semantic_identity.get("schema")
        != "onnx-splitpoint/image-preprocessing-contract"
        or int(semantic_identity.get("schema_version") or 0) != 2
        or numeric_identity.get("schema")
        != "onnx-splitpoint/runtime-numeric-input-identity"
        or int(numeric_identity.get("schema_version") or 0) != 1
        or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", semantic_sha)
        or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", numeric_sha)
        or hashlib.sha256(canonical(semantic_identity)).hexdigest() != semantic_sha
        or hashlib.sha256(canonical(numeric_identity)).hexdigest() != numeric_sha
    ):
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_runtime_identity_invalid")
    numeric_expected = {
        "backend": "native_full_deepx",
        "task": str(task or "").strip().lower(),
        "preprocessing_contract_sha256": semantic_sha,
        "runtime_input_name": str(payload.get("runtime_input_name") or "").strip(),
        "runtime_input_shape": shape,
        "runtime_input_dtype": dtype_name,
        "runtime_input_layout": layout,
        "runtime_color_space": color_space,
        "runtime_normalization": normalization,
    }
    for key, expected in numeric_expected.items():
        observed = numeric_identity.get(key)
        if key == "runtime_input_shape":
            observed = list(observed or [])
        elif key in {"runtime_input_layout", "runtime_color_space"}:
            observed = str(observed or "").strip().upper()
        else:
            observed = str(observed or "").strip().lower()
            expected = str(expected or "").strip().lower()
        if observed != expected:
            raise ValueError(f"prepared_input_numeric_identity_{key}_mismatch")

    source_sha = str(payload.get("input_image_sha256") or "").strip().lower()
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", source_sha) or not image_path.is_file():
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_source_image_binding_missing")
    if hashlib.sha256(image_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != source_sha:
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_source_image_sha256_mismatch")

    tensor_path = Path(str(payload.get("runtime_input_file") or "")).expanduser()
    if not tensor_path.is_absolute():
        tensor_path = manifest_path.parent / tensor_path
    tensor_path = _absolute_without_resolving(tensor_path)
    if _contains_symlink(tensor_path):
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_tensor_metadata_invalid")
    tensor_path = tensor_path.resolve(strict=True)
    if tensor_path != manifest_path.parent / "runtime_input.bin":
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_tensor_metadata_invalid")
    declared_sha = str(payload.get("runtime_input_sha256") or "").strip().lower()
    expected_bytes = int(math.prod(shape)) * int(np.dtype(dtype_name).itemsize)
    if (
        tensor_path.is_symlink() or not tensor_path.is_file()
        or isinstance(payload.get("runtime_input_bytes"), bool)
        or int(payload.get("runtime_input_bytes") or 0) != expected_bytes
        or int(tensor_path.stat().st_size) != expected_bytes
        or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", declared_sha)
    ):
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_tensor_metadata_invalid")
    tensor_bytes = tensor_path.read_bytes()
    if hashlib.sha256(tensor_bytes).hexdigest() != declared_sha:
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_tensor_sha256_mismatch")
    # DXRT may require a writable C-contiguous buffer.  ``frombuffer`` is
    # read-only for immutable ``bytes`` even when already contiguous.
    feed = np.frombuffer(
        tensor_bytes, dtype=np.dtype(dtype_name),
    ).reshape(shape).copy(order="C")
    if feed.tobytes() != tensor_bytes:
        raise ValueError("prepared_input_tensor_roundtrip_mismatch")
    return feed, {
        "prepared_input_manifest": str(manifest_path),
        "prepared_input_manifest_sha256": hashlib.sha256(
            manifest_path.read_bytes()
        ).hexdigest(),
        "prepared_input_file": str(tensor_path),
        "prepared_input_sha256": declared_sha,
        "prepared_input_file_sha256": declared_sha,
        "prepared_input_bytes": expected_bytes,
        "prepared_input_name": numeric_expected["runtime_input_name"],
        "prepared_input_shape": shape,
        "prepared_input_dtype": dtype_name,
        "prepared_input_layout": layout,
        "prepared_input_source_image_id": image_path.name,
        "prepared_input_source_image_sha256": source_sha,
        "runtime_color_space": color_space,
        "runtime_normalization": normalization,
        "runtime_preprocess_mode": canonical_preprocess_mode,
        "runtime_preprocessing_identity": dict(semantic_identity),
        "runtime_preprocessing_sha256": semantic_sha,
        "runtime_numeric_input_identity": dict(numeric_identity),
        "runtime_numeric_input_sha256": numeric_sha,
        "prepared_input_binding_verified": True,
        "prepared_input_source": "sealed_semantic_dump_runtime_tensor",
        "preprocess": preprocess,
    }


def _run_deepx_prepared_feed_benchmark(root: Path, dxnn: Path, run: Dict[str, Any], args: Any, results_dir: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Prepared-feed DeepX full benchmark via dx_engine.

    This is the fair comparison path against ORT/TensorRT/Hailo timing loops:
    warmup/runs repeat one already-preprocessed feed.  Disk I/O and image
    preprocessing are outside the timed loop.  `run_model` remains a diagnostic
    DXRT microbenchmark and may report buffered internal throughput.
    """
    diagnostic_only = bool(getattr(args, "deepx_output_diagnostic_only", False))
    runs = max(1, int(getattr(args, "runs", 1) or 1))
    # Energy windows pass their requested inference count via
    # --throughput-frames.  Same-backend DeepX has no separate streaming loop,
    # so the prepared-feed loop itself must execute and attest that exact count.
    if bool(getattr(args, "energy_measurement_only", False)):
        runs = max(1, int(getattr(args, "throughput_frames", 0) or runs))
    warmup = max(0, int(getattr(args, "warmup", 0) or 0))
    task = str(run.get("benchmark_task") or getattr(args, "benchmark_task", "auto") or "auto").strip().lower()
    input_size, input_contract = _deepx_input_size_from_contract(root, run, default=640)
    resolution_status = str((input_contract or {}).get("contract_resolution_status") or "").strip().lower()
    if resolution_status in {"ambiguous", "invalid"}:
        return {
            "enabled": True,
            "status": f"explicit_input_contract_{resolution_status}",
            "input_contract": input_contract,
            "input_size": input_size,
        }
    img_path, img_source = _deepx_find_prepared_feed_image(root, input_size, task, run, args)
    if img_path is None:
        return {"enabled": True, "status": "no_prepared_feed_image", "input_size": input_size, "image_source": img_source}
    try:
        from PIL import Image  # type: ignore
        import numpy as np  # type: ignore
        from dx_engine import InferenceEngine  # type: ignore
        perf = __import__("time").perf_counter
    except Exception as exc:
        return {"enabled": True, "status": "import_failed", "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", "input_size": input_size}
    try:
        # The runtime tensor is prepared/loaded by native_full_input (Pillow).
        # Read only source geometry here, using the same encoded orientation:
        # no EXIF transpose, no OpenCV resize/conversion, no numeric feed changes.
        # Decode once outside the timed loop so a corrupt image still fails
        # even when a sealed input already exists.
        try:
            with Image.open(img_path) as source_image:
                source_image.load()
                original_wh = [int(value) for value in source_image.size]
            if len(original_wh) != 2 or any(value <= 0 for value in original_wh):
                raise ValueError("invalid_original_image_dimensions")
        except Exception as exc:
            return {
                "enabled": True, "status": "image_read_failed",
                "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
                "image": str(img_path), "input_size": input_size,
            }
        inp = input_contract.get("input") if isinstance(input_contract, dict) and isinstance(input_contract.get("input"), dict) else {}
        required = ("shape", "dtype", "layout", "normalization", "color_space", "preprocess_mode")
        missing = [key for key in required if inp.get(key) in (None, "", [])]
        if missing:
            return {
                "enabled": True, "status": "explicit_input_contract_missing",
                "missing_contract_fields": missing, "input_contract": input_contract,
                "image": str(img_path), "input_size": input_size,
            }
        layout = str(inp.get("layout") or "").strip().upper()
        dtype_name = str(inp.get("dtype") or "").strip().lower()
        color_space = str(inp.get("color_space") or "").strip().upper()
        preprocess_mode = str(inp.get("preprocess_mode") or "").strip().lower()
        normalization = str(inp.get("normalization") or "").strip().lower()
        geometry = _deepx_input_geometry(input_contract)
        if geometry is None:
            return {
                "enabled": True, "status": "explicit_input_contract_invalid",
                "input_contract": input_contract, "image": str(img_path),
                "input_size": input_size,
            }
        if layout not in {"HWC", "NHWC", "CHW", "NCHW"} or color_space != "RGB":
            return {
                "enabled": True, "status": "explicit_input_contract_unsupported",
                "layout": layout, "color_space": color_space,
                "input_contract": input_contract, "image": str(img_path),
            }
        try:
            from splitpoint_runners.native_full_input import (
                load_sealed_deepx_native_full_input,
                prepare_and_seal_deepx_native_full_input,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            return {
                "enabled": True,
                "status": "prepared_input_runtime_import_failed",
                "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
                "image": str(img_path),
                "input_size": input_size,
            }
        # Bind the portable prepared input to an explicit model identity.  A
        # remote suite commonly lives below ``.../<run>/1/suite``; the numeric
        # invocation directory is never a model identifier.  The workflow
        # supplies ``quality_evidence_model_id`` for the physical target and
        # the generated Full input contract may repeat the same value.  Treat
        # a disagreement between those two authoritative sources as a hard
        # conflict before any bytes are sealed.
        workflow_model = str(
            getattr(args, "quality_evidence_model_id", "") or ""
        ).strip()
        contract_model = str(input_contract.get("model_id") or "").strip()
        if (
            workflow_model and contract_model
            and workflow_model != contract_model
        ):
            return {
                "enabled": True,
                "status": "prepared_input_model_identity_conflict",
                "workflow_model_id": workflow_model,
                "contract_model_id": contract_model,
                "image": str(img_path),
                "input_size": input_size,
            }
        prepared_model = str(
            workflow_model
            or contract_model
            or _deepx_contract_model_id(root, run, input_contract)
            or run.get("model_id")
            or run.get("model")
            or ""
        ).strip()
        if (
            not prepared_model
            or prepared_model.isdigit()
            or prepared_model in {".", ".."}
            or Path(prepared_model).name != prepared_model
        ):
            return {
                "enabled": True,
                "status": "prepared_input_model_identity_missing",
                "image": str(img_path),
                "input_size": input_size,
            }
        prepared_setup_id = str(
            run.get("setup_id")
            or run.get("hardware_setup_id")
            or getattr(args, "quality_evidence_setup_id", "")
            or ""
        )
        prepared_comparison_backend = str(
            run.get("comparison_backend") or "deepx"
        )
        manifest_text = str(
            getattr(args, "prepared_input_manifest", "") or ""
        ).strip()
        if not manifest_text:
            try:
                sealed = prepare_and_seal_deepx_native_full_input(
                    image_path=img_path,
                    input_contract=input_contract,
                    task=task,
                    out_dir=results_dir / "prepared_input",
                    model=prepared_model,
                    setup_id=prepared_setup_id,
