                    comparison_backend=prepared_comparison_backend,
                )
                manifest_text = str(sealed["manifest_path"])
                setattr(args, "prepared_input_manifest", manifest_text)
            except Exception as exc:
                return {
                    "enabled": True,
                    "status": "prepared_input_manifest_creation_failed",
                    "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
                    "image": str(img_path),
                    "input_size": input_size,
                }
        try:
            strict_sealed = load_sealed_deepx_native_full_input(
                Path(manifest_text), image_path=img_path,
                input_contract=input_contract, task=task,
                expected_model=prepared_model,
                expected_setup_id=prepared_setup_id,
                expected_comparison_backend=prepared_comparison_backend,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            return {
                "enabled": True,
                "status": "prepared_input_manifest_validation_failed",
                "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
                "image": str(img_path),
                "input_size": input_size,
            }
        feed, prepared_input_binding = _deepx_load_sealed_prepared_feed(
            Path(manifest_text), input_contract=input_contract,
            image_path=img_path, task=task, np=np,
        )
        strict_feed = strict_sealed.get("runtime_input")
        if (
            strict_feed is None
            or list(getattr(strict_feed, "shape", ())) != list(feed.shape)
            or str(getattr(strict_feed, "dtype", "")) != str(feed.dtype)
            or strict_feed.tobytes() != feed.tobytes()
        ):
            raise ValueError("prepared_input_shared_loader_tensor_mismatch")
        feed = strict_feed
        pad_value = int(
            inp.get("letterbox_pad_value")
            if inp.get("letterbox_pad_value") is not None else 0
        )
        sealed_preprocess = dict(
            prepared_input_binding.get("preprocess") or {}
        )
        preprocess_audit = {
            "shape": [int(x) for x in feed.shape],
            "dtype": str(feed.dtype),
            "layout": layout,
            "color_space": color_space,
            "preprocess_mode": preprocess_mode,
            "normalization": normalization,
            "letterbox_pad_value": int(pad_value),
            "source_shape_hw": [original_wh[1], original_wh[0]],
            "contract_source": inp.get("contract_source"),
            "prepared_input_binding_verified": True,
            "prepared_input_source": "sealed_semantic_dump_runtime_tensor",
            "image_decode_used_for_numeric_input": False,
            "sealed_preprocess": sealed_preprocess,
        }
    except Exception as exc:
        detail = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
        return {
            "enabled": True,
            "status": str(exc).split(":", 1)[0]
            if str(exc).startswith("prepared_input_")
            else "prepared_input_verification_failed",
            "error": detail, "image": str(img_path),
            "input_size": input_size, "input_contract": input_contract,
        }
    try:
        engine = InferenceEngine(str(dxnn))
    except Exception as exc:
        return {"enabled": True, "status": "engine_init_failed", "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", "input_size": input_size}
    frozen_postprocessor = None
    frozen_postprocess_contract: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    frozen_postprocess_result: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    direct_normalizer = None
    direct_normalization_contract: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    direct_normalization_result: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    completion_attestation: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    completion_kind = ""
    source_endpoint_contract_hash = ""
    source_output_endpoint_attestation: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    runtime_endpoint_contract_family = ""
    runtime_endpoint_contract_complete = False
    output_value_diagnostics: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    structural_outputs: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    diagnostic_phase = "structural_probe"
    engine_call_count = 0
    raw_output_snapshot: Dict[str, Any] = {}

    def _engine_run() -> Any:
        nonlocal engine_call_count
        engine_call_count += 1
        return engine.run([feed])

    def _named_runtime_outputs(raw: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        values = list(raw) if isinstance(raw, (list, tuple)) else [raw]
        declared_outputs = list(input_contract.get("outputs") or []) if isinstance(input_contract, dict) else []
        named: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        for index, value in enumerate(values):
            name = (
                str(declared_outputs[index].get("name") or f"output_{index}")
                if index < len(declared_outputs) and isinstance(declared_outputs[index], dict)
                else f"output_{index}"
            )
            named[name] = np.asarray(value)
        return named

    def _capture_probe_outputs(outputs: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        nonlocal output_value_diagnostics, raw_output_snapshot
        from splitpoint_runners.native_detection_postprocess import inspect_decoded_pre_nms_values
        output_value_diagnostics = inspect_decoded_pre_nms_values(outputs)
        byte_count = sum(int(value.nbytes) for value in outputs.values())
        raw_output_snapshot = {
            "status": "not_saved", "phase": diagnostic_phase,
            "unmodified": True, "source_bytes": byte_count,
            "max_source_bytes": 16 * 1024 * 1024,
            "tensors": [
                {"key": f"tensor_{index:03d}", "name": name,
                 "shape": [int(size) for size in value.shape], "dtype": str(value.dtype)}
                for index, (name, value) in enumerate(outputs.items())
            ],
        }
        if byte_count > raw_output_snapshot["max_source_bytes"]:
            raw_output_snapshot["status"] = "size_limit_exceeded"
            return
        if any(value.dtype.hasobject for value in outputs.values()):
            raw_output_snapshot["status"] = "object_dtype_rejected"
            return
        try:
            # One snapshot only, replaced by the completion observation if it
            # is reached. Normal measurement runs never enter this function.
            destination = results_dir / "deepx_output_value_probe_outputs.npz"
            np.savez(destination, **{
                f"tensor_{index:03d}": value
                for index, value in enumerate(outputs.values())
            })
            raw_output_snapshot.update(status="saved", file=destination.name)
        except Exception as exc:
            raw_output_snapshot.update(status="save_failed", error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")

    try:
        contract_family = str(
            input_contract.get("contract_family")
            or input_contract.get("endpoint_mode")
            or ((input_contract.get("postprocessing") or {}).get("type") if isinstance(input_contract.get("postprocessing"), dict) else "")
            or ""
        ).lower()
        raw_detection = bool(
            task == "detection"
            and ("raw_head" in contract_family or input_contract.get("host_tail_required") is True)
        )
        direct_detection = bool(
            task == "detection"
            and contract_family in {"decoded", "decoded_nms"}
            and input_contract.get("host_tail_required") is False
        )
        if raw_detection:
            from splitpoint_runners.native_detection_postprocess import (
                FrozenDetectionPostprocessor,
                build_detection_completion_execution_contract,
                build_frozen_postprocess_contract,
            )
            from splitpoint_runners.native_output_endpoint import (
                load_authoritative_output_contract,
                runtime_output_contract,
            )
            structural_outputs = _named_runtime_outputs(_engine_run())
            if diagnostic_only:
                _capture_probe_outputs(structural_outputs)
            decoder_model_id = _deepx_contract_model_id(
                root, run, input_contract,
            )
            if not decoder_model_id:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "native_full_raw_detection_model_identity_missing"
                )
            authoritative_output = load_authoritative_output_contract(
                root,
                backend="deepx_m1",
                model_id=decoder_model_id,
                variant="full",
                task="detection",
            )
            if (
                authoritative_output.get(
                    "authoritative_output_contract"
                )
                is not True
                or authoritative_output.get(
                    "contract_resolution_status"
                )
                != "attested"
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "deepx_raw_head_output_declaration_unattested"
                )
            runtime_endpoint = runtime_output_contract(
                "detection",
                structural_outputs,
                raw_fallback=True,
                declared_contract=authoritative_output,
            )
            source_endpoint_contract_hash = str(
                runtime_endpoint.get("endpoint_contract_hash") or ""
            ).strip().lower()
            source_output_endpoint_attestation = (
                dict(runtime_endpoint["output_endpoint_attestation"])
                if isinstance(
                    runtime_endpoint.get(
                        "output_endpoint_attestation"
                    ),
                    dict,
                )
                else {}
            )
            runtime_endpoint_contract_family = str(
                runtime_endpoint.get("contract_family") or ""
            ).strip().lower()
            runtime_endpoint_contract_complete = (
                runtime_endpoint.get("endpoint_contract_complete") is True
            )
            if (
                runtime_endpoint_contract_family != (
                    "decoded_pre_nms"
                    if input_contract.get("endpoint_mode") == "decoded_pre_nms"
                    else "raw_head"
                )
                or not runtime_endpoint_contract_complete
                or len(source_endpoint_contract_hash) != 64
                or any(
                    char not in "0123456789abcdef"
                    for char in source_endpoint_contract_hash
                )
                or source_output_endpoint_attestation.get("attested")
                is not True
                or str(
                    source_output_endpoint_attestation.get("status")
                    or ""
                ).strip().lower()
                != "passed"
                or str(
                    source_output_endpoint_attestation.get(
                        "endpoint_contract_hash"
                    )
                    or ""
                ).strip().lower()
                != source_endpoint_contract_hash
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "deepx_raw_head_runtime_endpoint_unattested"
                )
            completion_source_endpoint = dict(runtime_endpoint)
            source_model_sha256 = _deepx_contract_model_sha256(
                run, input_contract,
            )
            if source_model_sha256:
                completion_source_endpoint["model_sha256"] = (
                    source_model_sha256
                )
            completion_execution_contract = (
                build_detection_completion_execution_contract(
                    model_id=decoder_model_id,
                    outputs=structural_outputs,
                    input_hw=[
                        int(geometry["height"]), int(geometry["width"]),
                    ],
                    original_wh=[
                        original_wh[0], original_wh[1],
                    ],
                    source_endpoint_contract=completion_source_endpoint,
                )
            )
            registered_model_sha256 = str(
                completion_execution_contract[
                    "processor_contract"
                ].get("model_sha256") or ""
            )
            frozen_postprocess_contract = build_frozen_postprocess_contract(
                model_id=decoder_model_id,
                model_sha256=registered_model_sha256,
                source_contract_family=runtime_endpoint_contract_family,
                outputs=structural_outputs,
                input_hw=[
                    int(geometry["height"]), int(geometry["width"]),
                ],
                original_wh=[original_wh[0], original_wh[1]],
            )
            frozen_postprocessor = FrozenDetectionPostprocessor(
                frozen_postprocess_contract
            )
            completion_kind = "raw_host_tail"
        elif direct_detection:
            from splitpoint_runners.native_detection_postprocess import (
                FrozenDecodedNmsPostprocessor,
                build_frozen_decoded_nms_normalization_contract,
            )
            from splitpoint_runners.native_output_endpoint import (
                load_authoritative_output_contract,
                runtime_output_contract,
            )
            structural_outputs = _named_runtime_outputs(_engine_run())
            decoder_model_id = _deepx_contract_model_id(
                root, run, input_contract,
            )
            if not decoder_model_id:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "native_full_direct_detection_model_identity_missing"
                )
            authoritative_output = load_authoritative_output_contract(
                root,
                backend="deepx_m1",
                model_id=decoder_model_id,
                variant="full",
                task="detection",
            )
            if (
                authoritative_output.get(
                    "authoritative_output_contract"
                )
                is not True
                or authoritative_output.get(
                    "contract_resolution_status"
                )
                != "attested"
                or
                authoritative_output.get("source_coordinate_space")
                != "model_input_letterbox_xyxy_pixels"
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "deepx_direct_bn6_source_coordinate_space_unsealed"
                )
            runtime_endpoint = runtime_output_contract(
                "detection",
                structural_outputs,
                raw_fallback=True,
                declared_contract=authoritative_output,
            )
            if (
                runtime_endpoint.get("contract_family") != "decoded_nms"
                or runtime_endpoint.get("endpoint_contract_complete")
                is not True
                or not isinstance(
                    runtime_endpoint.get("output_endpoint_attestation"),
                    dict,
                )
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "deepx_direct_bn6_runtime_endpoint_unattested"
                )
            source_endpoint_contract_hash = str(
                runtime_endpoint.get("endpoint_contract_hash") or ""
            ).strip().lower()
            source_output_endpoint_attestation = dict(
                runtime_endpoint["output_endpoint_attestation"]
            )
            runtime_endpoint_contract_family = str(
                runtime_endpoint.get("contract_family") or ""
            ).strip().lower()
            runtime_endpoint_contract_complete = (
                runtime_endpoint.get("endpoint_contract_complete") is True
            )
            direct_normalization_contract = (
                build_frozen_decoded_nms_normalization_contract(
                    model_id=decoder_model_id,
                    outputs=structural_outputs,
                    input_hw=[
                        int(geometry["height"]),
                        int(geometry["width"]),
                    ],
                    original_wh=[
                        original_wh[0], original_wh[1],
                    ],
                    preprocess={
                        "mode": preprocess_mode,
                        "letterbox_pad_value": int(pad_value),
                        "color_space": color_space,
                    },
                    source_coordinate_space=str(
                        authoritative_output.get(
                            "source_coordinate_space"
                        ) or ""
                    ),
                    source_endpoint_contract_hash=str(
                        runtime_endpoint.get(
                            "endpoint_contract_hash"
                        ) or ""
                    ),
                    source_output_endpoint_attestation=(
                        runtime_endpoint[
                            "output_endpoint_attestation"
                        ]
                    ),
                )
            )
            direct_normalizer = FrozenDecodedNmsPostprocessor(
                direct_normalization_contract
            )
            completion_kind = "direct_bn6_normalization"

        def _run_full_task_once() -> None:
            nonlocal frozen_postprocess_result
            nonlocal direct_normalization_result
            raw_outputs = _engine_run()
            if diagnostic_only:
                _capture_probe_outputs(_named_runtime_outputs(raw_outputs))
            if frozen_postprocessor is not None:
                frozen_postprocess_result = frozen_postprocessor.process(
                    _named_runtime_outputs(raw_outputs),
                    original_wh=[original_wh[0], original_wh[1]],
                )
            elif direct_normalizer is not None:
                direct_normalization_result = direct_normalizer.process(
                    _named_runtime_outputs(raw_outputs),
                    original_wh=[
                        original_wh[0], original_wh[1],
                    ],
                )

        if diagnostic_only:
            if runtime_endpoint_contract_family != "decoded_pre_nms" or frozen_postprocessor is None:
                raise RuntimeError("deepx_value_probe_requires_declared_decoded_pre_nms")
            # Explicit probe only: the structural tensor above is inspected
            # outside all timed loops. Exercise the same completion method once
            # as well; a later failing tensor carries its own exception detail.
            diagnostic_phase = "untimed_completion_probe"
            _run_full_task_once()
            return {
                "enabled": True, "status": "diagnostic_pass",
                "diagnostic_only": True, "counts_as_benchmark": False,
                "engine_call_count": engine_call_count,
                "diagnostic_phase": diagnostic_phase,
                "model_id": prepared_model, "setup_id": prepared_setup_id,
                "input_size": input_size, "image": str(img_path),
                "image_source": img_source,
                "input_contract": input_contract,
                "prepared_feed_shape": [int(x) for x in feed.shape],
                "preprocessing_contract_audit": preprocess_audit,
                **prepared_input_binding,
                "runtime_endpoint_contract_family": runtime_endpoint_contract_family,
                "runtime_endpoint_contract_complete": runtime_endpoint_contract_complete,
                "output_value_diagnostics": output_value_diagnostics,
                "runtime_output_name_source": "declared_output_contract_by_position_with_index_fallback",
                "output_diagnostic_observation": diagnostic_phase,
                "raw_output_snapshot": raw_output_snapshot,
                "postprocess_completion_verified": frozen_postprocessor.completed_count == 1,
                "completed_detection_count": frozen_postprocess_result.get("detection_count"),
                "decoded_pre_nms_score_normalization": dict(
                    frozen_postprocess_result.get("decoded_pre_nms_score_normalization") or {}
                ),
                "decoded_pre_nms_score_policy": dict(
                    frozen_postprocess_contract.get("decoded_pre_nms_score_policy") or {}
                ),
            }

        diagnostic_phase = "warmup"
        for _ in range(warmup):
            _run_full_task_once()
        times: List[float] = []
        # The outer interval is the canonical throughput measurement.  Per-call
        # samples below remain request-latency evidence; their reciprocal is not
        # used as a substitute for measured makespan throughput.
        makespan_start = perf()
        diagnostic_phase = "timed_loop"
        for _ in range(runs):
            t0 = perf()
            _run_full_task_once()
            t1 = perf()
            times.append((t1 - t0) * 1000.0)
        makespan_end = perf()
    except Exception as exc:
        exception_diagnostics = dict(getattr(exc, "diagnostics", {}) or {})
        failure = {
            "enabled": True,
            "status": "runtime_failed",
            "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
            "output_value_diagnostics": exception_diagnostics or output_value_diagnostics,
            "runtime_output_name_source": "declared_output_contract_by_position_with_index_fallback",
            "output_diagnostic_observation": (
                "failing_tensor" if exception_diagnostics else
                diagnostic_phase if output_value_diagnostics else "unavailable"
            ),
            "diagnostic_phase": diagnostic_phase,
            "engine_call_count": engine_call_count,
            "diagnostic_only": diagnostic_only,
            "raw_output_snapshot": raw_output_snapshot,
            "run_count": runs,
            "warmup_count": warmup,
            "input_size": input_size,
            "image": str(img_path),
            "image_source": img_source,
            "model_id": prepared_model, "setup_id": prepared_setup_id,
            "input_contract": input_contract,
            "preprocessing_contract_audit": preprocess_audit,
            **prepared_input_binding,
        }
        # Preserve the first error and the input that reached it. Previously
        # this catch discarded the manifest, making downstream logs wrongly
        # suggest that preparation was the original failure.
        try:
            (results_dir / "deepx_prepared_feed_benchmark.json").write_text(
                json.dumps(failure, indent=2, allow_nan=False), encoding="utf-8",
            )
        except Exception:
            pass
        return failure
    if not times:
        return {"enabled": True, "status": "no_timed_runs", "run_count": runs, "input_size": input_size, "image": str(img_path)}
    vals = sorted(float(x) for x in times)
    mean_ms = float(sum(vals) / len(vals))
    p50_ms = float(np.percentile(np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64), 50.0)) if vals else mean_ms
    p95_ms = float(np.percentile(np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64), 95.0)) if vals else mean_ms
    makespan_s = max(0.0, float(makespan_end - makespan_start))
    fps_makespan = (float(len(times)) / makespan_s) if makespan_s > 0.0 else None
    completion_processor = frozen_postprocessor or direct_normalizer
    postprocess_completed_frames = (
        int(completion_processor.completed_count) - int(warmup)
        if completion_processor is not None else 0
    )
    completed_result_persistence: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "completed_task_result_artifact_saved": False,
        "completed_task_result_artifact": {},
        "completed_task_result_artifact_sha256": "",
        "completed_task_result_artifact_path": "",
        "completed_task_result_artifact_file_sha256": "",
        "completed_task_result_artifact_verification_status": (
            "not_applicable" if completion_processor is None else "missing"
        ),
    }
    if (
        completion_processor is not None
        and postprocess_completed_frames == len(times)
    ):
        from splitpoint_runners.native_detection_postprocess import (
            build_completed_detection_endpoint_attestation,
            build_normalized_detection_endpoint_attestation,
            persist_completed_result_artifact,
        )
        completion_result = (
            frozen_postprocess_result
            if completion_kind == "raw_host_tail"
            else direct_normalization_result
        )
        try:
            completed_result_persistence = persist_completed_result_artifact(
                completion_result.get("completed_result_artifact") or {},
                expected_sha256=str(
                    completion_result.get(
                        "completed_result_artifact_sha256"
                    ) or ""
                ),
                output_path=(
                    results_dir
                    / "deepx_prepared_feed.completed_task_result_artifact.json"
                ),
            )
            completed_result_persistence[
                "completed_task_result_artifact_verification_status"
            ] = "verified_exact"
        except Exception as exc:
            return {
                "enabled": True,
                "status": "completed_task_result_artifact_persistence_failed",
                "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
                "completed_frames": len(times),
                "postprocess_completed_frames": postprocess_completed_frames,
                **prepared_input_binding,
            }
        if completion_kind == "raw_host_tail":
            completion_attestation = (
                build_completed_detection_endpoint_attestation(
                    frozen_postprocess_contract,
                    frozen_postprocess_result,
                    completed_frames=len(times),
                    postprocess_completed_frames=(
                        postprocess_completed_frames
                    ),
                    source_endpoint_contract_hash=(
                        source_endpoint_contract_hash
                    ),
                )
            )
        elif completion_kind == "direct_bn6_normalization":
            completion_attestation = (
                build_normalized_detection_endpoint_attestation(
                    direct_normalization_contract,
                    direct_normalization_result,
                    completed_frames=len(times),
                    postprocess_completed_frames=(
                        postprocess_completed_frames
                    ),
                )
            )
    out = {
        "enabled": True,
        "status": "ok",
        "benchmark_kind": "prepared_feed_dx_engine",
        "task": task,
        "run_count": runs,
        "requested_frames": runs,
        "completed_frames": len(times),
        "completed_work_units": len(times),
        "completed_work_units_source": (
            "dx_engine_prepared_feed_frozen_completion_timed_loop"
            if completion_processor is not None
            else "dx_engine_prepared_feed_timed_loop"
        ),
        "completed_work_units_status": "exact_runtime_counter",
        "warmup_count": warmup,
        "unique_input_images": 1,
        "sequence_policy": "single_prepared_feed_repeated",
        "same_image_schedule_as_ort_trt_hailo": True,
        "uses_validation_dataset_images": False,
        "semantic_validation_uses_dataset_images": bool(str(getattr(args, "validation_images", "") or run.get("validation_images") or "").strip()),
        "preprocessing_timed": False,
        "disk_io_timed": False,
        "input_size": input_size,
        "prepared_feed_shape": [int(x) for x in feed.shape],
        "original_image_wh": [original_wh[0], original_wh[1]],
        "prepared_feed_contract_version": DEEPX_PREPARED_FEED_CONTRACT_VERSION,
        "input_contract": input_contract,
        "input_contract_mode": "explicit",
        "preprocessing_contract_audit": preprocess_audit,
        **prepared_input_binding,
        "image": str(img_path),
        "image_source": img_source,
        "mean_ms": mean_ms,
        "p50_ms": p50_ms,
        "p95_ms": p95_ms,
        "latency_semantics": "synchronous_request_end_to_end",
        "makespan_s": makespan_s,
        "fps_makespan": fps_makespan,
        "fps_semantics": "completed_frames_over_outer_measured_makespan",
        "fps_from_mean_latency": (1000.0 / mean_ms) if mean_ms > 0 else None,
        "runs_ms": times[: min(len(times), 32)],
        "host_postprocess_frozen": (
            completion_kind == "raw_host_tail"
        ),
        "normalization_frozen": (
            completion_kind == "direct_bn6_normalization"
        ),
        "runtime_endpoint_contract_family":
            runtime_endpoint_contract_family,
        "runtime_endpoint_contract_complete":
            runtime_endpoint_contract_complete,
        "source_endpoint_contract_hash":
            source_endpoint_contract_hash,
        "source_output_endpoint_attestation":
            source_output_endpoint_attestation,
        "postprocess_included": completion_processor is not None,
        "postprocess_completed_frames": postprocess_completed_frames,
        "postprocess_completion_verified": bool(
            completion_processor is None
            or postprocess_completed_frames == len(times)
        ),
        "frozen_host_postprocess_contract": frozen_postprocess_contract,
        "frozen_host_postprocess_contract_sha256": str(
            frozen_postprocess_contract.get("contract_sha256") or ""
        ),
        "frozen_host_postprocess_result": frozen_postprocess_result,
        "frozen_decoded_nms_normalization_contract": (
            direct_normalization_contract
        ),
        "frozen_decoded_nms_normalization_contract_sha256": str(
            direct_normalization_contract.get("contract_sha256") or ""
        ),
        "frozen_decoded_nms_normalization_result": (
            direct_normalization_result
        ),
        "completed_task_stage": str(
            completion_attestation.get("stage") or ""
        ),
        "completed_task_contract_family": (
            "decoded_nms"
            if completion_attestation.get("attested") is True else ""
        ),
        "completed_task_endpoint_contract_hash": str(
            completion_attestation.get("endpoint_contract_hash") or ""
        ),
        "completed_task_output_endpoint_id": str(
            completion_attestation.get("output_endpoint_id") or ""
        ),
        "completed_task_comparison_endpoint_contract": (
            completion_attestation.get(
                "completed_task_comparison_endpoint_contract"
            )
            if isinstance(
                completion_attestation.get(
                    "completed_task_comparison_endpoint_contract"
                ),
                dict,
            )
            else {}
        ),
        "completed_task_comparison_endpoint_contract_hash": str(
            completion_attestation.get(
                "completed_task_comparison_endpoint_contract_hash"
            ) or ""
        ),
        "completed_task_comparison_output_endpoint_id": str(
            completion_attestation.get(
                "completed_task_comparison_output_endpoint_id"
            ) or ""
        ),
        "completed_task_completion_mode": str(
            completion_attestation.get(
                "completed_task_completion_mode"
            ) or ""
        ),
        "completed_task_endpoint_attestation": completion_attestation,
        **completed_result_persistence,
    }
    try:
        (results_dir / "deepx_prepared_feed_benchmark.json").write_text(json.dumps(out, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    except Exception:
        pass
    return out


def run_deepx_output_value_probe(
    root: Path, dxnn: Path, run: Dict[str, Any], args: Any, results_dir: Path,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Inspect an existing Full DXNN through the actual prepared-feed path.

    No build, quality dataset, warmup, energy or performance loop is started.
    There are at most two engine calls: structural probe and one completion.
    The caller selects existing suite/artifact/image/contract paths explicitly.
    """
    import copy
    probe_args = copy.copy(args)
    probe_args.deepx_output_diagnostic_only = True
    probe_args.energy_measurement_only = False
    probe_args.runs = 1
    probe_args.warmup = 0
    results_dir = Path(results_dir)
    results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    result = _run_deepx_prepared_feed_benchmark(
        Path(root), Path(dxnn), dict(run), probe_args, results_dir,
    )
    result = {
        **result, "diagnostic_only": True, "counts_as_benchmark": False,
        "probe_schema": "onnx-splitpoint/deepx-output-value-probe-v1",
        "dxnn_path": str(dxnn), "suite_path": str(root),
    }
    (results_dir / "deepx_output_value_probe.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(result, indent=2, allow_nan=False), encoding="utf-8",
    )
    return result


def _run_deepx_full_run(
    root: Path, run: Dict[str, Any], args: Any, *,
    expected_endpoint_identity: Mapping[str, str],
    full_only_quality_identity: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    run_id = str(run.get("id") or "deepx_m1_full")
    dxnn_rel = str(run.get("dxnn_path") or run.get("artifact") or "deepx/deepx_m1/full/model.dxnn").strip()
    dxnn = (root / dxnn_rel).resolve() if not os.path.isabs(dxnn_rel) else Path(dxnn_rel)
    results_dir = root / "results" / run_id
    results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stdout_path = results_dir / "run_model_stdout.txt"
    stderr_path = results_dir / "run_model_stderr.txt"
    if not dxnn.is_file():
        msg = f"DeepX DXNN missing: {dxnn}"
        stderr_path.write_text(msg + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        return [{
            "case_id": "full",
            "run_id": run_id,
            "provider": "deepx_m1",
            "backend": "deepx_m1",
            "primary_variant": "full",
            "variant": "full",
            "compile_ok": False,
            "runtime_ok": False,
            "validation_ok": False,
            "buildable": False,
            "runtime_executable": False,
            "contract_consistent": False,
            "task_valid": False,
            "accuracy_gate_pass": False,
            "accuracy_gate_source": "none",
            "accuracy_gate_reason": "missing_artifact",
            "eligible_for_ranking": False,
            "error_class": "missing_artifact",
            "error_detail": msg,
        }]
    measurement_only = bool(getattr(args, "energy_measurement_only", False))
    quality_only = full_only_quality_identity is not None
    diagnostic_skipped = bool(measurement_only or quality_only)
    cmd = ["run_model", "-m", str(dxnn), "-b", "-l", str(max(1, int(getattr(args, "runs", 1) or 1))), "-v", "--use-ort"]
    if diagnostic_skipped:
        # uRECS command windows must contain exactly the explicitly contracted
        # prepared-feed loop.  Running the DXRT diagnostic first would add
        # uncounted inferences to the energy numerator.
        cp = type("_SkippedDeepXDiagnostic", (), {
            "returncode": 0,
            "stdout": "",
            "stderr": (
                "run_model diagnostic intentionally skipped in "
                + (
                    "Full-only quality mode; DXNN/dx_engine is the sole "
                    "admitted runtime"
                    if quality_only
                    else "energy-measurement-only mode"
                )
            ),
        })()
    else:
        try:
            cp = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=str(root), stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace", timeout=getattr(args, "timeout", None))
        except FileNotFoundError:
            cp = type("_MissingDeepXDiagnostic", (), {
                "returncode": 127,
                "stdout": "",
                "stderr": "run_model CLI not found; canonical dx_engine prepared-feed execution remains available",
            })()
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
            cp = type("_TimedOutDeepXDiagnostic", (), {
                "returncode": 124,
                "stdout": str(exc.stdout or ""),
                "stderr": str(exc.stderr or "") + f"\nTIMEOUT after {getattr(args, 'timeout', None)}s",
            })()
    stdout_path.write_text(cp.stdout or "", encoding="utf-8")
    stderr_path.write_text(cp.stderr or "", encoding="utf-8")
    parsed = _parse_deepx_run_model_output((cp.stdout or "") + "\n" + (cp.stderr or ""))
    dxrt_total = parsed.get("latency_ms") or parsed.get("npu_processing_time_ms")

    # v57: Hailo/ORT/TensorRT timing loops repeat one prepared feed.  Use the
    # same prepared-feed dx_engine loop as the canonical DeepX full timing path.
    # Keep run_model metrics as diagnostics only.
    prepared_bench: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    try:
        if str(os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_DEEPX_FULL_PREPARED_FEED_BENCHMARK", "1")).strip().lower() not in ("0", "false", "no", "off"):
            prepared_bench = _run_deepx_prepared_feed_benchmark(root, dxnn, run, args, results_dir)
    except Exception as _pf_exc:
        prepared_bench = {"enabled": True, "status": "error", "error": f"{type(_pf_exc).__name__}: {_pf_exc}"}

    allow_diagnostic_fallback = str(os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_DEEPX_ALLOW_DIAGNOSTIC_FALLBACK", "0")).strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "on")
    if prepared_bench.get("status") == "ok" and prepared_bench.get("mean_ms") is not None:
        total = float(prepared_bench.get("mean_ms"))
        performance_source = "dx_engine_prepared_feed"
    elif allow_diagnostic_fallback:
        total = dxrt_total
        performance_source = "dxrt_run_model_diagnostic_fallback"
    else:
        total = None
        performance_source = "explicit_deepx_contract_required"

    prepared_runtime_ok = bool(
        prepared_bench.get("status") == "ok"
        and performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed"
        and total is not None
        and float(total) > 0.0
    )
    # ``run_model`` is a DXRT diagnostic microbenchmark.  The explicitly
    # contracted dx_engine prepared-feed loop above is the canonical Full
    # runtime and must not be invalidated merely because the diagnostic CLI
    # exits non-zero after producing its own warnings.  Diagnostic fallback is
    # still allowed only when explicitly requested and remains ineligible for
    # contract/ranking claims below.
    diagnostic_fallback_ok = bool(
        allow_diagnostic_fallback and not diagnostic_skipped and cp.returncode == 0
        and total is not None and float(total) > 0.0
    )
    runtime_ok = bool(prepared_runtime_ok or diagnostic_fallback_ok)
    semantic = {}
    if runtime_ok and not measurement_only:
        semantic = _run_deepx_semantic_validation(root, dxnn, run, args, results_dir)
    semantic_enabled = bool(semantic.get("enabled"))
    semantic_status = semantic.get("status")

    # v55p: DeepX full semantic validation must not be marked valid solely
    # because the dx_engine loop completed.  For classification datasets with
    # labels, a status=ok row with top1/top5=0.0 is a real task failure, not a
    # successful semantic validation.  Keep thresholds deliberately weak here:
    # this is a smoke/regression gate, not a final accuracy target.
    semantic_metric_gate: Dict[str, Any] = {
        "enabled": bool(semantic_enabled),
        "status": semantic_status,
        "task": semantic.get("task"),
        "pass": None,
        "reason": "not_evaluated",
    }
    semantic_ok = (semantic_status == "ok") if semantic_enabled else None
    try:
        if semantic_enabled and semantic_status == "ok":
            task_s = str(semantic.get("task") or "").strip().lower()
            if task_s == "classification" and int(semantic.get("classification_labeled_samples") or 0) > 0:
                top1 = semantic.get("classification_top1_accuracy")
                top5 = semantic.get("classification_top5_accuracy")
                t1 = float(top1) if top1 is not None else None
                t5 = float(top5) if top5 is not None else None
                min_top1 = float(os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_DEEPX_FULL_MIN_TOP1", "0.05") or 0.05)
                min_top5 = float(os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_DEEPX_FULL_MIN_TOP5", "0.20") or 0.20)
                metric_pass = bool((t1 is not None and t1 >= min_top1) or (t5 is not None and t5 >= min_top5))
                semantic_ok = metric_pass
                semantic_metric_gate.update({
                    "pass": metric_pass,
                    "reason": "classification_topk_threshold",
                    "top1": t1,
                    "top5": t5,
                    "min_top1": min_top1,
                    "min_top5": min_top5,
                    "labeled_samples": int(semantic.get("classification_labeled_samples") or 0),
                })
            elif task_s == "detection":
                ap50 = next((
                    value for value in (
                        semantic.get("mini_coco_ap50_full"),
                        semantic.get("detection_ap50"),
                        semantic.get("coco_ap50"),
                    ) if value is not None
                ), None)
                if ap50 is not None:
                    min_ap50 = float(os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_DEEPX_FULL_MIN_AP50", "0.05") or 0.05)
                    ap50_f = float(ap50)
                    metric_pass = bool(ap50_f >= min_ap50)
                    semantic_ok = metric_pass
                    semantic_metric_gate.update({
                        "pass": metric_pass,
                        "reason": "detection_ap50_threshold",
                        "ap50": ap50_f,
                        "min_ap50": min_ap50,
                        "gt_instances": semantic.get("ap50_gt_instances"),
                        "predictions": semantic.get("ap50_predictions"),
                    })
                else:
                    # If annotations are unavailable, keep a detection-count smoke gate.
                    semantic_metric_gate.update({"pass": True, "reason": "detection_smoke_status_ok_no_ap50"})
            else:
                semantic_metric_gate.update({"pass": bool(semantic_ok), "reason": "status_ok_no_task_metric"})
    except Exception as _metric_exc:
        semantic_ok = False
        semantic_metric_gate.update({"pass": False, "reason": f"metric_gate_error:{type(_metric_exc).__name__}:{_metric_exc}"})

    # v52e: keep latency/runtime success separate from semantic correctness.
    # A run_model benchmark may be valid while dx_engine semantic validation is
    # unavailable or failed; do not report that as validation_ok=true.
    validation_ok = bool(semantic_ok) if semantic_enabled else False
    preprocessing_contract = semantic.get("preprocessing_contract") if isinstance(semantic.get("preprocessing_contract"), dict) else {}
    decoder_postprocess_contract = semantic.get("decoder_postprocess_contract") if isinstance(semantic.get("decoder_postprocess_contract"), dict) else {}
    prepared_preprocess = prepared_bench.get("preprocessing_contract_audit") if isinstance(prepared_bench.get("preprocessing_contract_audit"), dict) else {}
    prepared_contract_ok = bool(prepared_preprocess) or bool(
        prepared_bench.get("status") == "ok" and prepared_bench.get("input_contract_mode") in {None, "explicit"}
    )
    preprocessing_contract_ok = bool(preprocessing_contract.get("pass")) if semantic_enabled else prepared_contract_ok
    run_task = str(run.get("benchmark_task") or getattr(args, "benchmark_task", "auto") or "auto").strip().lower()
    semantic_task = str(semantic.get("task") or run_task or "").strip().lower()
    decoder_contract_ok = bool(decoder_postprocess_contract.get("pass")) if semantic_task == "detection" else True
    io_contract_ok = bool(
        runtime_ok and performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed"
        and preprocessing_contract_ok and (decoder_contract_ok if semantic_enabled else True)
    )
    quality_export: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    quality_export_error = ""
    if runtime_ok and semantic_status == "ok" and not measurement_only:
        try:
            quality_export = _deepx_export_central_quality_request(
                root, dxnn, run, semantic, results_dir,
                completed_task_evidence=prepared_bench,
                expected_model_id=str(
                    expected_endpoint_identity.get("model_id") or ""
                ),
                expected_backend=str(
                    expected_endpoint_identity.get("backend") or ""
                ),
                expected_variant=str(
                    expected_endpoint_identity.get("variant") or ""
                ),
                full_only_quality_identity=full_only_quality_identity,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            # Quality coverage is fail-closed but independent of a valid
            # performance loop.  Preserve the latency row and make the missing
            # scientific evidence explicit instead of fabricating a metric.
            quality_export_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}:{exc}"
    observed_full_endpoint = ""
    if prepared_runtime_ok:
        if semantic_task == "detection":
            observed_full_endpoint = (
                "completed_detection"
                if prepared_bench.get("postprocess_included") is True
                and prepared_bench.get("postprocess_completion_verified") is True
                else "raw_model_outputs"
            )
        elif semantic_task == "classification":
            observed_full_endpoint = "classification_logits"
    row = {
        "case_id": "full",
        "run_id": run_id,
        "provider": "deepx_m1",
        "backend": "deepx_m1",
        "task": semantic_task,
        "primary_variant": "full",
        "variant": "full",
        "setup_id": str(run.get("setup_id") or run.get("hardware_setup_id") or getattr(args, "quality_evidence_setup_id", "") or ""),
        "measurement_endpoint": observed_full_endpoint,
        "full_measurement_endpoint": observed_full_endpoint,
        "artifact_kind": "dxnn",
        "dxnn_path": str(dxnn),
        "compile_ok": True,
        "runtime_ok": runtime_ok,
        "latency_ok": runtime_ok,
        "semantic_validation_ok": bool(semantic_ok) if semantic_enabled else False,
        "validation_ok": validation_ok,
        "buildable": True,
        "runtime_executable": bool(runtime_ok),
        "contract_consistent": io_contract_ok,
        "interface_valid": io_contract_ok,
        "interface_status": "pass" if io_contract_ok else "fail",
        "execution_ok": bool(runtime_ok),
        "task_valid": bool(semantic_ok) if semantic_enabled else False,
        "accuracy_gate_pass": bool(semantic_ok) if semantic_enabled else False,
        "accuracy_gate_source": "dataset_or_declared_task_metric" if semantic_enabled else "none",
        "accuracy_gate_reason": (semantic_metric_gate.get("reason") if isinstance(semantic_metric_gate, dict) else "semantic_metric_gate"),
        "accuracy_gate_thresholds": {
            "classification_top1_max_drop": float(os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_GATE_CLS_TOP1_MAX_DROP", "0.01") or 0.01),
            "classification_top5_max_drop": float(os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_GATE_CLS_TOP5_MAX_DROP", "0.01") or 0.01),
            "detection_ap50_max_drop": float(os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_GATE_DET_AP50_MAX_DROP", "0.01") or 0.01),
        },
        # Dataset smoke metrics are not the paired non-inferiority decision.
        # Eligibility is granted only after the canonical quality gate is merged.
        "eligible_for_ranking": False,
        "ranking_eligible": False,
        "performance_eligible": False,
        "energy_eligible": False,
        "pareto_eligible": False,
        "thesis_valid": False,
        "quality_valid": False,
        "evidence_complete": False,
        "ranking_exclusion_reason": "paired_task_quality_gate_pending" if semantic_ok else "semantic_validation_failed_or_unavailable",
        "semantic_validation_status": semantic_status,
        "semantic_validation_enabled": semantic_enabled,
        "semantic_validation_intentionally_skipped": bool(measurement_only),
        "semantic_validation_metric_gate": dict(semantic_metric_gate),
        "deepx_preprocessing_contract": preprocessing_contract or {"status": "prepared_feed_only", "pass": bool(prepared_preprocess), "sample": prepared_preprocess},
        "deepx_preprocessing_contract_pass": preprocessing_contract_ok,
        "decoder_postprocess_contract": decoder_postprocess_contract,
        "decoder_contract_pass": decoder_contract_ok if semantic_task == "detection" else None,
        "decoder_id": (((decoder_postprocess_contract.get("contracts") or [{}])[0] or {}).get("decoder_id") if isinstance(decoder_postprocess_contract.get("contracts"), list) and decoder_postprocess_contract.get("contracts") else None),
        "nms_ok": decoder_contract_ok if semantic_task == "detection" else None,
        "host_tail_available": decoder_contract_ok if semantic_task == "detection" else None,
        "semantic_validation_image_count": semantic.get("image_count"),
        "detection_count": semantic.get("detection_count_mean"),
        "confidence_mean": semantic.get("confidence_mean"),
        "raw_score_mean": semantic.get("raw_score_mean"),
        "confidence_mean_clipped_0_1": semantic.get("confidence_mean_clipped_0_1"),
        "score_semantics": semantic.get("score_semantics"),
        "mini_coco_ap50": semantic.get("mini_coco_ap50"),
        "mini_coco_ap50_enabled": bool((semantic.get("mini_coco_ap50") or {}).get("enabled")) if isinstance(semantic.get("mini_coco_ap50"), dict) else False,
        "mini_coco_ap50_status": semantic.get("ap50_status"),
        "mini_coco_ap50_full": semantic.get("mini_coco_ap50_full"),
        "mini_coco_ap50_primary": semantic.get("mini_coco_ap50_primary"),
        "mini_coco_ap50_primary_variant": semantic.get("mini_coco_ap50_primary_variant"),
        "detection_ap50": semantic.get("detection_ap50"),
        "coco_ap50": semantic.get("coco_ap50"),
        "map50": semantic.get("map50"),
        "ap50_gt_instances": semantic.get("ap50_gt_instances"),
        "ap50_predictions": semantic.get("ap50_predictions"),
        "ap50_images": semantic.get("ap50_images"),
        "classification_top1_accuracy": semantic.get("classification_top1_accuracy"),
        "classification_top5_accuracy": semantic.get("classification_top5_accuracy"),
        "total_latency_ms": total,
        "full_latency_ms": total,
        "full_mean_ms": total,
        "full_e2e_latency_ms": total if prepared_runtime_ok else None,
        "full_e2e_mean_ms": total if prepared_runtime_ok else None,
        "diagnostic_full_e2e_latency_ms": parsed.get("latency_ms"),
        "npu_processing_time_ms": parsed.get("npu_processing_time_ms"),
        "performance_benchmark_source": performance_source,
        "prepared_feed_contract_version": (
            str(prepared_bench.get("prepared_feed_contract_version") or "")
            if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else ""
        ),
        "diagnostic_run_model_status": (
            "intentionally_skipped_full_only_quality"
            if quality_only else
            "intentionally_skipped_measurement_only"
            if diagnostic_skipped else
            ("ok" if cp.returncode == 0 else "failed")
        ),
        "diagnostic_run_model_ok": None if diagnostic_skipped else bool(cp.returncode == 0),
        "diagnostic_run_model_returncode": None if diagnostic_skipped else int(cp.returncode),
        "deepx_prepared_feed_benchmark": prepared_bench,
        "fps_makespan": prepared_bench.get("fps_makespan") if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else None,
        "full_backend_throughput_fps": prepared_bench.get("fps_makespan") if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else None,
        "throughput_primary_fps": prepared_bench.get("fps_makespan") if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else None,
        "throughput_primary_metric": "fps_makespan",
        "throughput_primary_source": ("dx_engine_prepared_feed_outer_makespan" if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else "unavailable_without_canonical_makespan"),
        "throughput_kind": "full_backend",
        "pipeline_applicable": False,
        "full_backend_cycle_ms": total,
        "prepared_feed_image": prepared_bench.get("image") if isinstance(prepared_bench, dict) else None,
        "prepared_feed_image_source": prepared_bench.get("image_source") if isinstance(prepared_bench, dict) else None,
        "prepared_feed_unique_input_images": prepared_bench.get("unique_input_images") if isinstance(prepared_bench, dict) else None,
        "prepared_feed_sequence_policy": prepared_bench.get("sequence_policy") if isinstance(prepared_bench, dict) else None,
        "prepared_feed_fps_from_mean_latency": prepared_bench.get("fps_from_mean_latency") if isinstance(prepared_bench, dict) else None,
        "latency_mean_ms": prepared_bench.get("mean_ms") if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else total,
        "latency_p50_ms": prepared_bench.get("p50_ms") if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else None,
        "latency_p95_ms": prepared_bench.get("p95_ms") if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else None,
        "latency_semantics": prepared_bench.get("latency_semantics") if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else "diagnostic_only",
        "measured_makespan_s": prepared_bench.get("makespan_s") if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else None,
        # DXRT/run_model may report an internal throughput value.  Keep it for
        # diagnostics, but do not treat it as pipeline FPS.  The steady-state
        # full-path cycle defaults to full latency unless a real streaming probe
        # reports otherwise.
        "fps": parsed.get("fps"),
        "dxrt_tool_fps": parsed.get("fps"),
        "dxrt_tool_fps_semantics": "dxrt_run_model_internal_throughput_not_pipeline_fps",
        "benchmark_input_policy": (prepared_bench if prepared_bench.get("status") == "ok" else {
            "schema_version": 1,
            "mode": "deepx_run_model_backend_microbench",
            "loop_count": int(max(1, int(getattr(args, "runs", 1) or 1))),
            "unique_dataset_images": None,
            "uses_validation_dataset_images": False,
            "same_image_schedule_as_ort_trt_hailo": False,
            "note": "Fallback: DeepX run_model -l submits backend/runtime loops. Canonical v57 mode uses dx_engine prepared-feed if available.",
        }),
        "benchmark_loop_count": int(max(1, int(getattr(args, "runs", 1) or 1))),
        "requested_frames": prepared_bench.get("requested_frames") if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else None,
        "completed_frames": prepared_bench.get("completed_frames") if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else None,
        "completed_work_units": prepared_bench.get("completed_work_units") if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else None,
        "completed_work_units_source": prepared_bench.get("completed_work_units_source") if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else None,
        "completed_work_units_status": prepared_bench.get("completed_work_units_status") if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else "unavailable",
        "benchmark_unique_dataset_images": prepared_bench.get("unique_input_images") if prepared_bench.get("status") == "ok" else None,
        "benchmark_input_mode": (prepared_bench.get("sequence_policy") if prepared_bench.get("status") == "ok" else "deepx_run_model_backend_microbench"),
        "pipeline_cycle_selected_ms": total,
        "pipeline_fps_selected": prepared_bench.get("fps_makespan") if performance_source == "dx_engine_prepared_feed" else None,
        "pipeline_cycle_source": "full_outer_makespan_throughput",
        "stage1_provider": "deepx_m1",
        "stage2_provider": "deepx_m1",
        "semantic_validation_passed": bool(validation_ok),
        # v55d: expose DeepX full as a normal final-pass row, not only via
        # final_pass_all.  Several dashboard/report paths look at final_pass
        # directly and older DeepX-full rows looked "valid but not final".
        "final_pass": bool(runtime_ok and validation_ok),
        "final_pass_all": bool(runtime_ok and validation_ok),
        "variant_status": {"full": "ok" if runtime_ok else "error", "part1": "missing", "part2": "missing", "composed": "missing"},
        "returncode": 0 if runtime_ok else int(cp.returncode),
        "stdout_path": str(stdout_path.relative_to(root)),
        "stderr_path": str(stderr_path.relative_to(root)),
    }
    if quality_export:
        quality_policy = dict(quality_export.pop("policy") or {})
        task_policy = dict(quality_policy.get(semantic_task) or {})
        primary_metric = str(task_policy.get("primary_metric") or (
            "top1_accuracy" if semantic_task == "classification" else "coco_ap_50_95"
        ))
        candidate_metric = (
            semantic.get("classification_top1_accuracy")
            if semantic_task == "classification" else None
        )
        margin = float(task_policy.get("non_inferiority_margin") or 0.01)
        pending_gate = {
            "status": "pending_central_evaluation", "decision": "pending_central_evaluation",
            "tier": str(quality_policy.get("dataset_tier") or "screening"),
            "policy": dict(quality_policy.get("effective_policy") or {}),
            "policy_sha256": str(quality_policy.get("policy_sha256") or ""),
            "primary": {
                "metric": primary_metric, "candidate": candidate_metric,
                "reference": None, "delta": None, "ci_low": None, "ci_high": None,
                "margin": margin, "bootstrap_repetitions": 0,
                "bootstrap_repetitions_requested": int((quality_policy.get("statistics") or {}).get("bootstrap_repetitions") or 500),
                "bootstrap_skipped_reason": "delegated_to_central_management",
            },
            "guardrails": ({
                "ap50": {"candidate": semantic.get("mini_coco_ap50_full"), "reference": None, "delta": None}
            } if semantic_task == "detection" else {
                "top5_accuracy": {"candidate": semantic.get("classification_top5_accuracy"), "reference": None, "delta": None}
            }),
            "quality_input_request": quality_export,
        }
        producer = quality_export.get("producer_identity") if isinstance(quality_export.get("producer_identity"), dict) else {}
        row.update({
            "task_quality_policy": quality_policy,
            "task_quality_policy_sha256": str(quality_policy.get("policy_sha256") or ""),
            "task_quality_gate": pending_gate,
            "task_quality_gates_by_variant": {"full": pending_gate},
            "task_quality_input_requests_by_variant": {"full": quality_export},
            "task_quality_gate_status": "pending_central_evaluation",
            "task_quality_gate_decision": "pending_central_evaluation",
            "quality_evaluation_pending": True,
            "quality_source_run_id": run_id, "quality_source_variant": "full",
            "endpoint_contract_hash": producer.get("endpoint_contract_hash"),
            "runtime_precision_identity": producer.get("runtime_precision_identity"),
            "execution_precision": producer.get("runtime_precision_identity"),
            "candidate_execution_contract": producer,
            "candidate_execution_contract_sha256": producer.get("producer_identity_sha256"),
            "ranking_exclusion_reason": "paired_task_quality_gate_pending",
        })
    elif isinstance(run.get("task_quality_gate"), dict) and str(
        ((run.get("task_quality_gate") or {}).get("statistics") or {}).get("execution_location") or ""
    ).strip().lower().replace("-", "_") in {"central_management", "management_node"}:
        reason = quality_export_error or "deepx_central_quality_request_not_emitted"
        unavailable_gate = {
            "status": "unavailable", "decision": "unavailable",
            "reason": reason, "quality_input_request": {},
        }
        row.update({
            "task_quality_gate": unavailable_gate,
            "task_quality_gates_by_variant": {"full": unavailable_gate},
            "task_quality_gate_status": "unavailable",
            "task_quality_gate_decision": "unavailable",
            "quality_evaluation_pending": False,
            "central_quality_export_error": reason,
            "ranking_exclusion_reason": "central_quality_candidate_evidence_unavailable",
            "eligible_for_ranking": False, "ranking_eligible": False,
            "performance_eligible": False, "energy_eligible": False,
            "pareto_eligible": False, "thesis_valid": False,
        })
    if semantic:
        row["deepx_semantic_validation"] = semantic
        if semantic.get("detections_json"):
            row["detections_json"] = semantic.get("detections_json")
        if semantic.get("classification_topk_json"):
            row["classification_topk_json"] = semantic.get("classification_topk_json")
        if semantic_enabled and semantic_ok is False:
            row["error_class"] = semantic.get("error_class") or "deepx_semantic_validation_failed"
            row["error_detail"] = semantic.get("error") or str(semantic.get("errors") or "")[-1200:]
    if not runtime_ok:
        row["error_class"] = "deepx_runtime_failed"
        row["error_detail"] = str(
            prepared_bench.get("error") or prepared_bench.get("status") or
            cp.stderr or cp.stdout or "explicit DeepX prepared-feed runtime unavailable"
        )[-1200:]
    elif not diagnostic_skipped and cp.returncode != 0:
        row["diagnostic_warning_class"] = "deepx_run_model_diagnostic_failed"
        row["diagnostic_warning_detail"] = (cp.stderr or cp.stdout or "")[-1200:]
    elif semantic_enabled and not semantic_ok:
        row["error_class"] = "deepx_semantic_validation_failed"
        row["error_detail"] = str(semantic.get("error") or semantic.get("status") or "semantic validation failed")[-1200:]
    elif not semantic_enabled and not measurement_only:
        row["error_class"] = "deepx_semantic_validation_not_run"
        row["error_detail"] = str(semantic.get("status") or "no_validation_images")
    return [row]

def _run_matches_provider_override(run: Dict[str, Any], provider: str) -> bool:
    """Return True if a plan row belongs to a provider override.

    In plan mode the remote GUI still passes --provider.  Older harnesses ignored
    that as soon as --plan was present, which made quick DeepX-only smoke tests
    impossible when the same plan also contained TensorRT/ORT rows.  Keep the
    filter conservative: auto means no filtering, deepx_m1 matches type/backend,
    and ORT providers match provider/full_provider/stage tokens.
    """
    p = str(provider or "auto").strip().lower()
    if not p or p == "auto":
        return True
    aliases = {
        "cpu": {"cpu", "cpu_ort"},
        "cpu_ort": {"cpu", "cpu_ort"},
        "cuda": {"cuda", "cuda_ort"},
        "cuda_ort": {"cuda", "cuda_ort"},
        "tensorrt": {"tensorrt", "trt"},
        "trt": {"tensorrt", "trt"},
        "deepx": {"deepx", "deepx_m1", "dx_m1"},
        "deepx_m1": {"deepx", "deepx_m1", "dx_m1"},
        "dx_m1": {"deepx", "deepx_m1", "dx_m1"},
        "hailo8": {"hailo8", "hailo"},
        "hailo10": {"hailo10", "hailo10h", "hailo10n", "hailo"},
        "hailo10h": {"hailo10", "hailo10h", "hailo"},
        "hailo10n": {"hailo10", "hailo10n", "hailo"},
    }.get(p, {p})
    vals: List[str] = []
    for k in ("type", "kind", "provider", "backend", "full", "full_provider", "stage1_provider", "stage2_provider", "hw_arch"):
        v = run.get(k)
        if isinstance(v, str):
            vals.append(v.strip().lower())
    for sk in ("stage1", "stage2"):
        st = run.get(sk)
        if isinstance(st, dict):
            for k in ("type", "kind", "provider", "backend", "hw_arch"):
                v = st.get(k)
                if isinstance(v, str):
                    vals.append(v.strip().lower())
        elif isinstance(st, str):
            vals.append(st.strip().lower())
    if any(v in aliases for v in vals):
        return True
    # Matrix id fallbacks such as hailo8_to_tensorrt or deepx_m1_full.
    rid = str(run.get("id") or run.get("name") or "").strip().lower()
    return any(a and a in rid for a in aliases)

def _pearson(x: List[float], y: List[float]) -> float:
    if len(x) != len(y) or len(x) < 2:
        return float("nan")
    xm = sum(x) / len(x)
    ym = sum(y) / len(y)
    num = sum((a - xm) * (b - ym) for a, b in zip(x, y))
    denx = math.sqrt(sum((a - xm) ** 2 for a in x))
    deny = math.sqrt(sum((b - ym) ** 2 for b in y))
    den = denx * deny
    return (num / den) if den > 0 else float("nan")


def _spearman(x: List[float], y: List[float]) -> float:
    # Spearman correlation via Pearson on ranks (average ranks for ties).
    def _ranks(vals: List[float]) -> List[float]:
        n = len(vals)
        order = sorted(range(n), key=lambda i: vals[i])
        ranks = [0.0] * n
        i = 0
        # ranks are 1..n
        while i < n:
            j = i
            while j + 1 < n and vals[order[j + 1]] == vals[order[i]]:
                j += 1
            # average rank for ties
            avg = (i + 1 + j + 1) / 2.0
            for k in range(i, j + 1):
                ranks[order[k]] = avg
            i = j + 1
        return ranks

    if len(x) != len(y) or len(x) < 2:
        return float("nan")
    rx = _ranks([float(v) for v in x])
    ry = _ranks([float(v) for v in y])
    return _pearson(rx, ry)


def _kendall_tau(x: List[float], y: List[float]) -> float:
    # simple O(n^2) kendall tau-a (good enough for small K)
    n = len(x)
    if n != len(y) or n < 2:
        return float("nan")
    conc = 0
    disc = 0
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            dx = x[i] - x[j]
            dy = y[i] - y[j]
            s = dx * dy
            if s > 0:
                conc += 1
            elif s < 0:
                disc += 1
    denom = conc + disc
    return (conc - disc) / denom if denom > 0 else float("nan")


def _linreg(x: List[float], y: List[float]) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
    # y = a*x + b, returns (a,b,R^2)
    import numpy as np  # type: ignore

    if len(x) != len(y) or len(x) < 2:
        return float("nan"), float("nan"), float("nan")

    X = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    Y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    mask = np.isfinite(X) & np.isfinite(Y)
    X = X[mask]
    Y = Y[mask]

    if X.size < 2:
        return float("nan"), float("nan"), float("nan")

    A = np.vstack([X, np.ones_like(X)]).T
    try:
        a, b = np.linalg.lstsq(A, Y, rcond=None)[0]
    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:
        return float("nan"), float("nan"), float("nan")

    Yhat = a * X + b
    ss_res = float(np.sum((Y - Yhat) ** 2))
    ss_tot = float(np.sum((Y - float(np.mean(Y))) ** 2))
    r2 = 1.0 - (ss_res / ss_tot) if ss_tot > 0 else float("nan")
    return float(a), float(b), float(r2)



def _fmt(x: Any, nd: int = 3) -> str:
    try:
        if x is None:
            return "-"
        if isinstance(x, bool):
            return "True" if x else "False"
        xf = float(x)
        if math.isnan(xf):
            return "nan"
        return f"{xf:.{nd}f}"
    except Exception:
        return str(x)




def _as_finite_float(value: Any) -> Optional[float]:
    try:
        x = float(value)
    except Exception:
        return None
    return x if math.isfinite(x) else None


def _derive_interleaved_pipeline_metrics(row: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Derive latency-vs-throughput metrics for two-stage split pipelines.

    Single-frame composed timing is a latency measurement: P1, transfer/input
    mapping and P2 are executed on the critical path of one sample.  For the
    double-accelerator streaming model we care about the steady-state cycle time:

        cycle ~= max(stage1 lane, stage2 lane)

    where the stage2 lane includes the interface transfer/input mapping if it
    cannot be hidden.  These estimates are intentionally stored next to the raw
    timings so reports can distinguish latency from throughput.
    """
    p1 = _as_finite_float(row.get("part1_mean_ms"))
    p2 = _as_finite_float(row.get("part2_mean_ms"))
    if p1 is None or p2 is None:
        return
    composed = _as_finite_float(row.get("composed_mean_ms"))
    full = (
        _as_finite_float(row.get("full_e2e_mean_ms"))
        or _as_finite_float(row.get("full_mean_ms"))
        or _as_finite_float(row.get("total_latency_ms"))
    )
    transfer = _as_finite_float(row.get("composed_phase_stage2_input_mapping_mean_ms"))
    transfer_source = "phase_profile_stage2_input_mapping"
    if transfer is None:
        if composed is not None:
            transfer = max(0.0, composed - p1 - p2)
            transfer_source = "composed_minus_parts"
        else:
            transfer = 0.0
            transfer_source = "missing_assumed_zero"
    # Stage2 total may include a hardware prefix plus host tail.  Prefer it when
    # phase profiling is available; otherwise use the part2 benchmark mean.
    stage2_total = _as_finite_float(row.get("composed_phase_stage2_total_mean_ms")) or p2
    stage1_lane = p1
    stage2_lane_no_transfer = stage2_total
    stage2_lane_with_transfer = transfer + stage2_total
    cycle_no_transfer = max(stage1_lane, stage2_lane_no_transfer)
    cycle_with_transfer = max(stage1_lane, stage2_lane_with_transfer)
    latency = composed if composed is not None else (p1 + transfer + stage2_total)

    row.setdefault("split_latency_e2e_ms", latency)
    row.setdefault("pipeline_model", "interleaved_two_stage")
    row.setdefault("pipeline_analysis_available", True)
    row.setdefault("pipeline_stage1_lane_ms", stage1_lane)
    row.setdefault("pipeline_stage2_lane_ms", stage2_lane_with_transfer)
    row.setdefault("pipeline_stage2_lane_no_transfer_ms", stage2_lane_no_transfer)
    row.setdefault("pipeline_transfer_est_ms", transfer)
    row.setdefault("pipeline_transfer_est_source", transfer_source)
    row.setdefault("pipeline_cycle_no_transfer_ms", cycle_no_transfer)
    row.setdefault("pipeline_cycle_with_transfer_ms", cycle_with_transfer)
    if cycle_no_transfer > 0:
        row.setdefault("pipeline_fps_no_transfer", 1000.0 / cycle_no_transfer)
    if cycle_with_transfer > 0:
        row.setdefault("pipeline_fps_with_transfer", 1000.0 / cycle_with_transfer)
    # If an actual threaded streaming probe was run, preserve it separately and
    # prefer it for measured throughput claims.  The analytic estimate remains
    # useful when no streaming probe was run or when hardware cannot be accessed.
    measured_cycle = _as_finite_float(row.get("throughput_cycle_est_ms"))
    measured_fps = _as_finite_float(row.get("throughput_fps_cycle_est")) or _as_finite_float(row.get("throughput_fps_makespan"))
    if measured_cycle is not None:
        row.setdefault("pipeline_cycle_measured_ms", measured_cycle)
        if measured_cycle > 0:
            row.setdefault("pipeline_fps_measured", 1000.0 / measured_cycle)
    elif measured_fps is not None and measured_fps > 0:
        row.setdefault("pipeline_fps_measured", measured_fps)
        row.setdefault("pipeline_cycle_measured_ms", 1000.0 / measured_fps)
    row.setdefault("pipeline_cycle_selected_ms", row.get("pipeline_cycle_measured_ms") or cycle_with_transfer)
    if _as_finite_float(row.get("pipeline_cycle_selected_ms")):
        row.setdefault("pipeline_fps_selected", 1000.0 / float(row.get("pipeline_cycle_selected_ms")))
    if full is not None and full > 0:
        row.setdefault("pipeline_speedup_full_over_cycle", full / cycle_with_transfer if cycle_with_transfer > 0 else None)
        row.setdefault("pipeline_speedup_full_over_cycle_no_transfer", full / cycle_no_transfer if cycle_no_transfer > 0 else None)
        if latency and latency > 0:
            row.setdefault("pipeline_latency_penalty_vs_full", latency / full)
    row.setdefault(
        "pipeline_note",
        "Latency is single-frame composed time; pipeline cycle estimates steady-state throughput with interleaving/streaming.",
    )

def _objective_value(row: Dict[str, Any], objective: str) -> Optional[float]:
    full = row.get("full_mean_ms")
    p1 = row.get("part1_mean_ms")
    p2 = row.get("part2_mean_ms")
    comp = row.get("composed_mean_ms")
    if objective == "full":
        return float(full) if full is not None else None
    if objective == "composed":
        return float(comp) if comp is not None else None
    if objective == "sum_parts":
        if p1 is None or p2 is None:
            return None
        return float(p1) + float(p2)
    if objective == "max_parts":
        if p1 is None or p2 is None:
            return None
        return max(float(p1), float(p2))
    if objective in {"pipeline_cycle", "cycle", "throughput"}:
        val = row.get("pipeline_cycle_selected_ms") or row.get("pipeline_cycle_with_transfer_ms") or row.get("throughput_cycle_est_ms")
        return float(val) if val is not None else None
    raise ValueError(f"unknown objective: {objective}")



def _augment_result_rows_throughput_semantics(rows: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    """Add explicit throughput taxonomy fields to raw benchmark rows.

    Raw suite rows often contain both a full-model timing and same-backend
    composed diagnostics.  Reports must not infer that the composed pipeline FPS
    is the full-backend throughput.  This helper keeps the small decision
    metrics explicit at the producer side.
    """
    for row in rows or []:
        if not isinstance(row, dict):
            continue
        def _f(v):
            try:
                if v is None or v == "":
                    return None
                return float(v)
            except Exception:
                return None
        full_ms = _f(row.get("full_mean_ms") or row.get("full_e2e_mean_ms") or row.get("total_latency_ms") if str(row.get("variant") or row.get("primary_variant") or "").lower() == "full" else row.get("full_mean_ms"))
        comp_ms = _f(row.get("composed_mean_ms") or row.get("total_latency_ms") if str(row.get("variant") or row.get("primary_variant") or "").lower() == "composed" else row.get("composed_mean_ms"))
        if full_ms and full_ms > 0:
            full_fps = 1000.0 / full_ms
            row.setdefault("full_backend_cycle_ms", full_ms)
            row.setdefault("full_backend_throughput_fps", full_fps)
            row.setdefault("full_backend_throughput_source", "full_latency_fps")
        if comp_ms and comp_ms > 0:
            # Preserve existing pipeline_fps_selected if it was computed from a
            # two-stage timing model; otherwise provide a conservative composed
            # diagnostic FPS from composed latency.
            if row.get("pipeline_fps_selected") is not None:
                row.setdefault("same_backend_composed_fps", row.get("pipeline_fps_selected"))
                row.setdefault("same_backend_composed_fps_source", "pipeline_fps_selected")
            else:
                row.setdefault("same_backend_composed_fps", 1000.0 / comp_ms)
                row.setdefault("same_backend_composed_fps_source", "composed_latency_fps")
        run_id_l = str(row.get("run_id") or row.get("backend") or row.get("provider") or "").lower()
        variant_l = str(row.get("variant") or row.get("primary_variant") or "").lower()
        has_split_diag = comp_ms is not None and (row.get("part1_mean_ms") is not None or row.get("part2_mean_ms") is not None or row.get("pipeline_cycle_selected_ms") is not None)
        is_hetero = "_to_" in run_id_l or "to_trt" in run_id_l or "trt_to" in run_id_l
        if variant_l == "full" or str(row.get("case_id") or "").lower() == "full":
            row.setdefault("throughput_kind", "full_backend")
            row.setdefault("pipeline_applicable", False)
            if full_ms and full_ms > 0:
                # Compatibility only: legacy tables still display pipeline_fps_selected.
                # Semantics are stated explicitly so this is not mistaken for a
                # heterogeneous split pipeline.
                row.setdefault("pipeline_cycle_selected_ms", full_ms)
                row.setdefault("pipeline_fps_selected", 1000.0 / full_ms)
                row.setdefault("pipeline_cycle_source", "full_latency_fps_compat")
                row.setdefault("pipeline_note", "Full backend repeated-feed throughput; not a heterogeneous split pipeline.")
                row.setdefault("throughput_primary_fps", 1000.0 / full_ms)
                row.setdefault("throughput_primary_metric", "full_backend_throughput_fps")
                row.setdefault("throughput_primary_source", row.get("throughput_primary_source") or "full_latency_fps")
        elif is_hetero:
            row.setdefault("throughput_kind", "heterogeneous_pipeline")
            row.setdefault("pipeline_applicable", True)
            if row.get("pipeline_fps_selected") is not None:
                row.setdefault("heterogeneous_pipeline_fps", row.get("pipeline_fps_selected"))
                row.setdefault("throughput_primary_fps", row.get("pipeline_fps_selected"))
                row.setdefault("throughput_primary_metric", "heterogeneous_pipeline_fps")
                row.setdefault("throughput_primary_source", "heterogeneous_pipeline_cycle_or_streaming")
        elif has_split_diag:
            row.setdefault("throughput_kind", "same_backend_split_diagnostic")
            row.setdefault("pipeline_applicable", False)
            if row.get("same_backend_composed_fps") is not None:
                row.setdefault("throughput_primary_fps", row.get("same_backend_composed_fps"))
                row.setdefault("throughput_primary_metric", "same_backend_composed_fps")
                row.setdefault("throughput_primary_source", "same_backend_split_diagnostic")
        if row.get("dxrt_tool_fps") is not None or row.get("fps") is not None:
            row.setdefault("backend_tool_fps", row.get("dxrt_tool_fps") or row.get("fps"))
            row.setdefault("backend_tool_fps_semantics", row.get("dxrt_tool_fps_semantics") or "backend_internal_tool_throughput_not_pipeline_fps")

def _write_results_csv(path: Path, rows: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    import csv

    if not rows:
        return
    keys: List[str] = []
    # stable-ish key order; rows can be heterogeneous when some cases were skipped
    preferred = [
        "setup_id",
        "measurement_endpoint",
        "full_measurement_endpoint",
        "quality_endpoint",
        "boundary",
        "cut_mib",
        "n_cut_tensors",
        "flops_left",
        "flops_right",
        "imbalance_pred",
        "score_pred",
        "eps_pass",
        "full_mean_ms",
        "full_std_ms",
        "full_host_tail_mean_ms",
        "full_e2e_mean_ms",
        "full_endpoint_mode",
        "part1_mean_ms",
        "part1_std_ms",
        "part2_mean_ms",
        "part2_std_ms",
        "part2_prefix_mean_ms",
        "part2_host_tail_mean_ms",
        "part2_endpoint_mode",
        "composed_mean_ms",
        "composed_std_ms",
        "composed_phase_wall_mean_ms",
        "composed_phase_stage1_mean_ms",
        "composed_phase_stage2_prefix_mean_ms",
        "composed_phase_stage2_host_tail_mean_ms",
        "composed_phase_stage2_total_mean_ms",
        "throughput_fps_makespan",
