        "throughput_cycle_est_ms",
        "split_latency_e2e_ms",
        "pipeline_stage1_lane_ms",
        "pipeline_stage2_lane_ms",
        "pipeline_transfer_est_ms",
        "pipeline_cycle_no_transfer_ms",
        "pipeline_cycle_with_transfer_ms",
        "pipeline_cycle_selected_ms",
        "pipeline_fps_with_transfer",
        "pipeline_fps_selected",
        "pipeline_speedup_full_over_cycle",
        "pipeline_latency_penalty_vs_full",
        "pipeline_transfer_est_source",
        "raw_head_drift_pass",
        "raw_head_drift_max_abs",
        "raw_head_drift_num_compared_tensors",
        "semantic_validation_gate_applied",
        "sum_parts_ms",
        "overhead_ms",
        "speedup_full_over_composed",
        "backend_drift_status",
        "backend_drift_mode",
        "backend_drift_variant",
        "backend_drift_provider",
        "backend_drift_single_max_abs",
        "backend_drift_single_match_ratio",
        "backend_drift_dataset_global_match_ratio",
        "backend_drift_dataset_global_mean_iou",
        "backend_drift_dataset_count_delta_abs",
        "backend_drift_dataset_top1_agreement",
        "backend_drift_dataset_top5_agreement",
        "backend_drift_dataset_mean_cosine_similarity",
        "mini_coco_ap50_primary",
        "mini_coco_ap50_full",
        "mini_coco_ap50_delta_primary_minus_full",
        "mini_classification_eval_primary_top1",
        "mini_classification_eval_primary_top5",
        "mini_classification_eval_full_top1",
        "mini_classification_eval_full_top5",
        "mini_classification_eval_delta_primary_minus_full_top1",
        "mini_classification_eval_delta_primary_minus_full_top5",
    ]
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for row in rows:
        seen.update(str(k) for k in row.keys())
    for k in preferred:
        if k in seen:
            keys.append(k)
    for row in rows:
        for k in row.keys():
            ks = str(k)
            if ks not in keys:
                keys.append(ks)

    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=keys, extrasaction="ignore")
        w.writeheader()
        for r in rows:
            w.writerow(r)


def _write_table_tex(path: Path, rows: List[Dict[str, Any]], tag: str, objective: str, topk: int = 10) -> None:
    def _tex_escape(text: str) -> str:
        # minimal escaping for captions/tt
        return (
            text.replace("\\", "\\textbackslash{}")
                .replace("_", "\\_")
                .replace("%", "\\%")
                .replace("&", "\\&")
                .replace("#", "\\#")
                .replace("$", "\\$")
                .replace("{", "\\{")
                .replace("}", "\\}")
        )

    label_tag = "bench-" + tag.replace("_", "-")
    # top-k by objective (ascending)
    def keyfn(r: Dict[str, Any]) -> float:
        v = _objective_value(r, objective)
        return float("inf") if v is None else float(v)

    rows_sorted = sorted(rows, key=keyfn)[: max(1, min(topk, len(rows)))]

    lines: List[str] = []
    lines.append(r"\begin{table}[t]")
    lines.append(r"\centering")
    lines.append(r"\small")
    lines.append(r"\setlength{\tabcolsep}{4pt}")
    lines.append(r"\begin{tabular}{rrrrrrrr}")
    lines.append(r"\toprule")
    lines.append(r"Boundary & Cut (MiB) & \#T & $F_L$ (GFLOPs) & $F_R$ (GFLOPs) & $t_{\mathrm{full}}$ (ms) & $t_{\mathrm{comp}}$ (ms) & Pass \\")
    lines.append(r"\midrule")
    for r in rows_sorted:
        b = r.get("boundary", "-")
        cut = _fmt(r.get("cut_mib"), 3)
        nt = r.get("cut_tensors", r.get("n_cut_tensors", "-"))
        fl = _fmt(float(r.get("flops_left", 0.0)) / 1e9 if r.get("flops_left") is not None else None, 2)
        fr = _fmt(float(r.get("flops_right", 0.0)) / 1e9 if r.get("flops_right") is not None else None, 2)
        tf = _fmt(r.get("full_mean_ms"), 2)
        tc = _fmt(r.get("composed_mean_ms"), 2)
        ps = r.get("ok", r.get("eps_pass"))
        ps_s = "True" if ps is True else ("False" if ps is False else "-")
        lines.append(f"{b} & {cut} & {nt} & {fl} & {fr} & {tf} & {tc} & {ps_s} \\\\")
    lines.append(r"\bottomrule")
    lines.append(r"\end{tabular}")
    tag_tt = _tex_escape(tag)
    obj_tt = _tex_escape(objective)
    lines.append(r"\\caption{Top split candidates for \\texttt{" + tag_tt + r"} (sorted by objective: \\texttt{" + obj_tt + r"}).}")
    lines.append(r"\label{tab:benchmark_top_" + label_tag + r"}")
    lines.append(r"\end{table}")
    lines.append("")  # trailing newline

    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("\n".join(lines))


def _save_paper_figures(out_dir: Path, rows: List[Dict[str, Any]], tag: str, objective: str, topk: int = 10) -> List[Path]:
    if not HAVE_PLOT or not rows:
        return []
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # prepare arrays
    flL = [float(r.get("flops_left", float("nan"))) / 1e9 for r in rows]
    flR = [float(r.get("flops_right", float("nan"))) / 1e9 for r in rows]
    t1 = [float(r.get("part1_mean_ms", float("nan"))) for r in rows]
    t2 = [float(r.get("part2_mean_ms", float("nan"))) for r in rows]
    score = [float(r.get("score", r.get("score_pred", float("nan")))) for r in rows]
    cut = [float(r.get("cut_mib", float("nan"))) for r in rows]
    comp = [float(r.get("composed_mean_ms", float("nan"))) for r in rows]
    full = [float(r.get("full_mean_ms", float("nan"))) for r in rows]
    sum_parts = [float(r.get("sum_parts_ms", float("nan"))) for r in rows]
    overhead = [float(r.get("overhead_ms", float("nan"))) for r in rows]

    obj = [float(_objective_value(r, objective) or float("nan")) for r in rows]

    files: List[Path] = []

    # Fig 1: FLOPs vs time (left)
    a, b, r2 = _linreg(flL, t1)
    r = _pearson(flL, t1)
    plt.figure(figsize=(5.2, 4.0))
    plt.scatter(flL, t1)
    xline = [min(flL), max(flL)]
    yline = [a * x + b for x in xline]
    plt.plot(xline, yline)
    plt.xlabel("FLOPs left (GFLOPs)")
    plt.ylabel("Measured time part1 (ms)")
    plt.title(f"Compute model plausibility (left)  r={_fmt(r,2)}, R$^2$={_fmt(r2,2)}")
    p_pdf = out_dir / f"fig_flops_vs_time_left_{tag}.pdf"
    p_svg = out_dir / f"fig_flops_vs_time_left_{tag}.svg"
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(p_pdf)
    plt.savefig(p_svg)
    plt.close()
    files += [p_pdf, p_svg]

    # Fig 2: FLOPs vs time (right)
    a, b, r2 = _linreg(flR, t2)
    r = _pearson(flR, t2)
    plt.figure(figsize=(5.2, 4.0))
    plt.scatter(flR, t2)
    xline = [min(flR), max(flR)]
    yline = [a * x + b for x in xline]
    plt.plot(xline, yline)
    plt.xlabel("FLOPs right (GFLOPs)")
    plt.ylabel("Measured time part2 (ms)")
    plt.title(f"Compute model plausibility (right)  r={_fmt(r,2)}, R$^2$={_fmt(r2,2)}")
    p_pdf = out_dir / f"fig_flops_vs_time_right_{tag}.pdf"
    p_svg = out_dir / f"fig_flops_vs_time_right_{tag}.svg"
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(p_pdf)
    plt.savefig(p_svg)
    plt.close()
    files += [p_pdf, p_svg]

    # Fig 3: score vs objective time (ranking quality proxy)
    # highlight top-k predicted and top-k measured (objective)
    import numpy as np  # type: ignore

    score_arr = np.asarray(score, dtype=float)
    obj_arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=float)
    valid = np.isfinite(score_arr) & np.isfinite(obj_arr)
    score_v = score_arr[valid]
    obj_v = obj_arr[valid]

    if len(score_v) >= 2:
        # indices in original list of valid points
        valid_idx = np.where(valid)[0]
        k = max(1, min(topk, len(score_v)))
        pred_order = np.argsort(score_v)  # smaller score => better
        meas_order = np.argsort(obj_v)    # smaller time => better
        pred_top = set(valid_idx[pred_order[:k]].tolist())
        meas_top = set(valid_idx[meas_order[:k]].tolist())

        colors = []
        for i in range(len(rows)):
            if i in pred_top and i in meas_top:
                colors.append("green")  # agree in top-k
            elif i in pred_top:
                colors.append("red")    # predicted top-k only
            elif i in meas_top:
                colors.append("orange") # measured top-k only
            else:
                colors.append(None)

        plt.figure(figsize=(5.6, 4.2))
        for i, r0 in enumerate(rows):
            if not (math.isfinite(score[i]) and math.isfinite(obj[i])):
                continue
            if colors[i] is None:
                plt.scatter(score[i], obj[i], alpha=0.6)
            else:
                plt.scatter(score[i], obj[i], edgecolors="black", linewidths=0.5, s=60, c=colors[i])
        sp = _spearman([float(s) for s in score_v], [float(t) for t in obj_v])
        kt = _kendall_tau([float(s) for s in score_v], [float(t) for t in obj_v])
        plt.xlabel("Predicted score (higher is better)")
        plt.ylabel(f"Measured objective time: {objective} (ms)")
        plt.title(f"Ranking agreement: Spearman={_fmt(sp,2)}, Kendall={_fmt(kt,2)}")
        p_pdf = out_dir / f"fig_score_vs_{objective}_{tag}.pdf"
        p_svg = out_dir / f"fig_score_vs_{objective}_{tag}.svg"
        plt.tight_layout()
        plt.savefig(p_pdf)
        plt.savefig(p_svg)
        plt.close()
        files += [p_pdf, p_svg]

    # Fig 4: noise summary (CV%)
    def cv(mean: float, std: float) -> float:
        return (std / mean) * 100.0 if mean and mean > 0 else float("nan")

    cv_full = [cv(float(r.get("full_mean_ms", float("nan"))), float(r.get("full_std_ms", float("nan")))) for r in rows]
    cv_p1 = [cv(float(r.get("part1_mean_ms", float("nan"))), float(r.get("part1_std_ms", float("nan")))) for r in rows]
    cv_p2 = [cv(float(r.get("part2_mean_ms", float("nan"))), float(r.get("part2_std_ms", float("nan")))) for r in rows]
    cv_comp = [cv(float(r.get("composed_mean_ms", float("nan"))), float(r.get("composed_std_ms", float("nan")))) for r in rows]

    def _mean_std(vals: List[float]) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        vv = [v for v in vals if math.isfinite(v)]
        if not vv:
            return float("nan"), float("nan")
        m = sum(vv) / len(vv)
        s = math.sqrt(sum((v - m) ** 2 for v in vv) / max(1, len(vv) - 1))
        return m, s

    names = ["full", "part1", "part2", "composed"]
    means = []
    stds = []
    for arr in [cv_full, cv_p1, cv_p2, cv_comp]:
        m, s = _mean_std(arr)
        means.append(m)
        stds.append(s)

    plt.figure(figsize=(5.4, 3.6))
    plt.bar(names, means, yerr=stds)
    plt.ylabel("CV (std/mean) [%]")
    plt.title("Measurement noise (across benchmark cases)")
    p_pdf = out_dir / f"fig_noise_cv_{tag}.pdf"
    p_svg = out_dir / f"fig_noise_cv_{tag}.svg"
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(p_pdf)
    plt.savefig(p_svg)
    plt.close()
    files += [p_pdf, p_svg]

    # Fig 5 (optional): overhead vs cut size
    if any(math.isfinite(v) for v in overhead) and any(math.isfinite(v) for v in cut):
        plt.figure(figsize=(5.4, 3.8))
        plt.scatter(cut, overhead)
        plt.axhline(0.0)
        plt.xlabel("Cut size (MiB)")
        plt.ylabel("Overhead: composed - (part1+part2) [ms]")
        plt.title("Split overhead vs cut size")
        p_pdf = out_dir / f"fig_overhead_vs_cut_{tag}.pdf"
        p_svg = out_dir / f"fig_overhead_vs_cut_{tag}.svg"
        plt.tight_layout()
        plt.savefig(p_pdf)
        plt.savefig(p_svg)
        plt.close()
        files += [p_pdf, p_svg]

    return files


def _write_figures(out_dir: Path, rows: List[Dict[str, Any]], tag: str, objective: str, topk: int = 10) -> List[Path]:
    """Compatibility alias for older scripts that called `_write_figures`.

    The suite uses `_save_paper_figures` for all run types; `_write_figures` keeps
    older call-sites working.
    """
    return _save_paper_figures(out_dir=out_dir, rows=rows, tag=tag, objective=objective, topk=topk)


def _write_summary_md(path: Path, rows: List[Dict[str, Any]], tag: str, objective: str, topk: int = 10) -> None:
    if not rows:
        path.write_text("# Benchmark summary\n\nNo results.\n", encoding="utf-8")
        return

    # derived objective arrays
    obj = [(_objective_value(r, objective) or float("nan")) for r in rows]
    score = [float(r.get("score", r.get("score_pred", float("nan")))) for r in rows]


    # compute model plausibility (if FLOPs metadata is available)
    def _sf(x):
        try:
            return float(x)
        except Exception:
            return float("nan")

    flL = [_sf(r.get("flops_left")) / 1e9 for r in rows]
    flR = [_sf(r.get("flops_right")) / 1e9 for r in rows]
    t1 = [_sf(r.get("part1_mean_ms")) for r in rows]
    t2 = [_sf(r.get("part2_mean_ms")) for r in rows]

    def _finite_pairs(x: list[float], y: list[float]) -> tuple[list[float], list[float]]:
        out_x: list[float] = []
        out_y: list[float] = []
        for a, b in zip(x, y):
            if math.isfinite(a) and math.isfinite(b):
                out_x.append(a)
                out_y.append(b)
        return out_x, out_y

    flL_v, t1_v = _finite_pairs(flL, t1)
    flR_v, t2_v = _finite_pairs(flR, t2)

    if len(flL_v) >= 2:
        rL = _pearson(flL_v, t1_v)
        aL, bL, r2L = _linreg(flL_v, t1_v)
    else:
        rL = float("nan")
        aL = bL = r2L = float("nan")

    if len(flR_v) >= 2:
        rR = _pearson(flR_v, t2_v)
        aR, bR, r2R = _linreg(flR_v, t2_v)
    else:
        rR = float("nan")
        aR = bR = r2R = float("nan")


    # ranking agreement (score vs objective time)
    import numpy as np  # type: ignore

    score_arr = np.asarray(score, dtype=float)
    obj_arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=float)
    valid = np.isfinite(score_arr) & np.isfinite(obj_arr)
    score_v = score_arr[valid].tolist()
    obj_v = obj_arr[valid].tolist()
    sp = _spearman([float(s) for s in score_v], [-float(t) for t in obj_v]) if len(score_v) >= 2 else float("nan")
    kt = _kendall_tau([float(s) for s in score_v], [-float(t) for t in obj_v]) if len(score_v) >= 2 else float("nan")

    # top-k overlap (pred score vs measured objective)
    k = max(1, min(topk, len(score_v)))
    pred_order = sorted(range(len(score_v)), key=lambda i: score_v[i], reverse=True)
    meas_order = sorted(range(len(obj_v)), key=lambda i: obj_v[i])
    overlap = len(set(pred_order[:k]).intersection(set(meas_order[:k])))
    overlap_ratio = overlap / k

    # noise summary (CV)
    def cv(mean: float, std: float) -> float:
        return (std / mean) * 100.0 if mean and mean > 0 else float("nan")

    cv_comp = [cv(float(r.get("composed_mean_ms", float("nan"))), float(r.get("composed_std_ms", float("nan")))) for r in rows]
    cv_comp_v = [v for v in cv_comp if math.isfinite(v)]
    cv_comp_med = sorted(cv_comp_v)[len(cv_comp_v) // 2] if cv_comp_v else float("nan")

    def _sf_or_none(value: Any) -> Optional[float]:
        try:
            out = float(value)
        except Exception:
            return None
        return out if math.isfinite(out) else None

    def _median(values: List[Any]) -> float:
        vv = sorted(v for v in (_sf_or_none(x) for x in values) if v is not None)
        if not vv:
            return float("nan")
        mid = len(vv) // 2
        if len(vv) % 2:
            return float(vv[mid])
        return float((vv[mid - 1] + vv[mid]) / 2.0)

    drift_rows = [r for r in rows if str(r.get("backend_drift_status") or "").strip().lower() == "ok"]
    drift_rows_sorted = sorted(
        drift_rows,
        key=lambda r: (
            _sf_or_none(r.get("backend_drift_dataset_global_match_ratio")) if _sf_or_none(r.get("backend_drift_dataset_global_match_ratio")) is not None else float("inf"),
            -(_sf_or_none(r.get("backend_drift_dataset_count_delta_abs")) or 0.0),
            _sf_or_none(r.get("backend_drift_single_match_ratio")) if _sf_or_none(r.get("backend_drift_single_match_ratio")) is not None else float("inf"),
        ),
    )

    mini_rows = [r for r in rows if _sf_or_none(r.get("mini_coco_ap50_primary")) is not None]
    mini_rows_sorted = sorted(
        mini_rows,
        key=lambda r: _sf_or_none(r.get("mini_coco_ap50_delta_primary_minus_full")) if _sf_or_none(r.get("mini_coco_ap50_delta_primary_minus_full")) is not None else float("inf"),
    )

    # best splits by objective
    def keyfn(r: Dict[str, Any]) -> float:
        v = _objective_value(r, objective)
        return float("inf") if v is None else float(v)

    best = sorted(rows, key=keyfn)[: min(10, len(rows))]

    lines: List[str] = []
    lines.append(f"# Benchmark summary: {tag}\n")
    lines.append(f"- Objective: **{objective}** (lower is better)\n")
    lines.append("## Compute-model plausibility (FLOPs vs measured time)\n")
    lines.append(f"- Left:  Pearson r = {_fmt(rL,2)},  R^2 = {_fmt(r2L,2)},  fit: t(ms) ≈ {_fmt(aL,3)}·GFLOPs + {_fmt(bL,3)}\n")
    lines.append(f"- Right: Pearson r = {_fmt(rR,2)},  R^2 = {_fmt(r2R,2)},  fit: t(ms) ≈ {_fmt(aR,3)}·GFLOPs + {_fmt(bR,3)}\n")
    lines.append("## Ranking agreement (predicted score vs measured objective time)\n")
    lines.append(f"- Spearman ρ = {_fmt(sp,2)}\n")
    lines.append(f"- Kendall τ = {_fmt(kt,2)}\n")
    lines.append(f"- Top-{k} overlap = {overlap}/{k} = {_fmt(overlap_ratio,2)}\n")
    lines.append("## Measurement noise\n")
    lines.append(f"- Median CV(composed) = {_fmt(cv_comp_med,2)} %\n")
    lines.append("## Backend drift vs CPU full\n")
    if drift_rows:
        lines.append(f"- Cases with backend-drift report: {len(drift_rows)}/{len(rows)}\n")
        drift_modes = sorted({str(r.get('backend_drift_mode') or '').strip() for r in drift_rows if str(r.get('backend_drift_mode') or '').strip()})
        if drift_modes:
            lines.append(f"- Modes observed: {', '.join(drift_modes)}\n")
        lines.append(f"- Median single-sample max_abs = {_fmt(_median([r.get('backend_drift_single_max_abs') for r in drift_rows]),4)}\n")
        lines.append(f"- Median single-sample match_ratio = {_fmt(_median([r.get('backend_drift_single_match_ratio') for r in drift_rows]),3)}\n")
        lines.append(f"- Median dataset global_match_ratio = {_fmt(_median([r.get('backend_drift_dataset_global_match_ratio') for r in drift_rows]),3)}\n")
        lines.append(f"- Median dataset global_mean_iou = {_fmt(_median([r.get('backend_drift_dataset_global_mean_iou') for r in drift_rows]),3)}\n")
        lines.append(f"- Median dataset count_delta_abs = {_fmt(_median([r.get('backend_drift_dataset_count_delta_abs') for r in drift_rows]),2)}\n")
        lines.append("| boundary | provider | variant | mode | single max_abs | single match_ratio | dataset match_ratio | dataset mean_iou | dataset count_delta_abs |\n")
        lines.append("|---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|\n")
        for r in drift_rows_sorted[: min(5, len(drift_rows_sorted))]:
            lines.append(
                f"| {r.get('boundary','-')} | {r.get('backend_drift_provider', r.get('provider','-'))} | {r.get('backend_drift_variant','-')} | {r.get('backend_drift_mode','-')} | {_fmt(r.get('backend_drift_single_max_abs'),4)} | {_fmt(r.get('backend_drift_single_match_ratio'),3)} | {_fmt(r.get('backend_drift_dataset_global_match_ratio'),3)} | {_fmt(r.get('backend_drift_dataset_global_mean_iou'),3)} | {_fmt(r.get('backend_drift_dataset_count_delta_abs'),2)} |\n"
            )
    else:
        lines.append("- Not available in this run set.\n")
    lines.append("## Mini-COCO AP50 (optional)\n")
    if mini_rows:
        lines.append(f"- Cases with Mini-COCO AP50: {len(mini_rows)}/{len(rows)}\n")
        lines.append(f"- Median primary AP50 = {_fmt(_median([r.get('mini_coco_ap50_primary') for r in mini_rows]),3)}\n")
        lines.append(f"- Median full AP50 = {_fmt(_median([r.get('mini_coco_ap50_full') for r in mini_rows]),3)}\n")
        lines.append(f"- Median Δ(primary-full) = {_fmt(_median([r.get('mini_coco_ap50_delta_primary_minus_full') for r in mini_rows]),3)}\n")
        lines.append("| boundary | primary variant | primary AP50 | full AP50 | Δ primary-full |\n")
        lines.append("|---:|:---:|---:|---:|---:|\n")
        for r in mini_rows_sorted[: min(5, len(mini_rows_sorted))]:
            lines.append(
                f"| {r.get('boundary','-')} | {r.get('mini_coco_ap50_primary_variant','-')} | {_fmt(r.get('mini_coco_ap50_primary'),3)} | {_fmt(r.get('mini_coco_ap50_full'),3)} | {_fmt(r.get('mini_coco_ap50_delta_primary_minus_full'),3)} |\n"
            )
    else:
        lines.append("- Disabled or unavailable in this run set.\n")
    lines.append("## Heterogeneous pipeline throughput estimate\n")
    pipe_rows = [
        r for r in rows
        if r.get("pipeline_analysis_available") and bool(r.get("pipeline_applicable"))
        and str(r.get("throughput_kind") or "").strip().lower() == "heterogeneous_pipeline"
    ]
    if pipe_rows:
        best_pipe = sorted(pipe_rows, key=lambda r: _sf_or_none(r.get("pipeline_cycle_selected_ms")) if _sf_or_none(r.get("pipeline_cycle_selected_ms")) is not None else float("inf"))[: min(5, len(pipe_rows))]
        lines.append("Latency (`composed`) is the single-frame critical path.  Cycle time estimates steady-state throughput when Stage1 and Stage2 run as a heterogeneous interleaved pipeline.\n")
        lines.append("| boundary | run | latency composed ms | stage1 lane ms | stage2 lane ms | transfer est ms | cycle ms | pipeline FPS | full/cycle speedup |\n")
        lines.append("|---:|:---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|\n")
        for r in best_pipe:
            lines.append(
                f"| {r.get('boundary','-')} | {r.get('run_id', r.get('provider','-'))} | {_fmt(r.get('split_latency_e2e_ms'),2)} | {_fmt(r.get('pipeline_stage1_lane_ms'),2)} | {_fmt(r.get('pipeline_stage2_lane_ms'),2)} | {_fmt(r.get('pipeline_transfer_est_ms'),2)} | {_fmt(r.get('pipeline_cycle_selected_ms'),2)} | {_fmt(r.get('pipeline_fps_selected'),2)} | {_fmt(r.get('pipeline_speedup_full_over_cycle'),2)} |\n"
            )
    else:
        lines.append("- No valid heterogeneous pipeline rows available for this run set.\n")

    diag_rows = [
        r for r in rows
        if r.get("pipeline_analysis_available")
        and str(r.get("throughput_kind") or "").strip().lower() == "same_backend_split_diagnostic"
    ]
    if diag_rows:
        lines.append("## Same-backend split diagnostics\n")
        lines.append("These rows estimate the overhead of splitting a graph while keeping both stages on the same backend. They are diagnostics and are not reported as double-accelerator pipeline gains.\n")
        best_diag = sorted(diag_rows, key=lambda r: _sf_or_none(r.get("pipeline_cycle_selected_ms")) if _sf_or_none(r.get("pipeline_cycle_selected_ms")) is not None else float("inf"))[: min(5, len(diag_rows))]
        lines.append("| boundary | run | composed latency ms | diagnostic cycle ms | diagnostic FPS | note |\n")
        lines.append("|---:|:---|---:|---:|---:|:---|\n")
        for r in best_diag:
            lines.append(
                f"| {r.get('boundary','-')} | {r.get('run_id', r.get('provider','-'))} | {_fmt(r.get('split_latency_e2e_ms'),2)} | {_fmt(r.get('pipeline_cycle_selected_ms'),2)} | {_fmt(r.get('pipeline_fps_selected'),2)} | same-backend diagnostic |\n"
            )
    lines.append("## Best splits by objective\n")
    lines.append("| boundary | cut (MiB) | #T | t_full (ms) | t_comp latency (ms) | pipeline cycle (ms) | sum_parts (ms) | overhead (ms) | pass |\n")
    lines.append("|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|:---:|\n")
    for r in best:
        lines.append(
            f"| {r.get('boundary','-')} | {_fmt(r.get('cut_mib'),3)} | {r.get('n_cut_tensors','-')} | "
            f"{_fmt(r.get('full_mean_ms'),2)} | {_fmt(r.get('composed_mean_ms'),2)} | {_fmt(r.get('pipeline_cycle_selected_ms'),2)} | "
            f"{_fmt(r.get('sum_parts_ms'),2)} | {_fmt(r.get('overhead_ms'),2)} | "
            f"{'✅' if r.get('final_pass', r.get('eps_pass')) else '❌'} |\n"
        )

    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text("".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")



def _slug(text: str) -> str:
    """Convert an arbitrary id into a filesystem-friendly slug."""
    s = str(text or "").strip()
    if not s:
        return "run"
    out = []
    for ch in s:
        if ch.isalnum() or ch in ("-", "_", "."):
            out.append(ch)
        else:
            out.append("_")
    slug = "".join(out).strip("_")
    while "__" in slug:
        slug = slug.replace("__", "_")
    return slug or "run"


def _flatten_hailo_compile_meta(case_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    flat: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    payload = case_meta.get("hailo_compile") if isinstance(case_meta, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        return flat

    def _ctx_single(meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        mode = str(meta.get("context_mode") or "").strip()
        try:
            count = int(float(meta.get("context_count"))) if meta.get("context_count") not in (None, "") else None
        except Exception:
            count = None
        return bool(mode == "single_context_used" or count == 1)

    for hw_arch, hw_meta in payload.items():
        if not isinstance(hw_meta, dict):
            continue
        arch = _slug(str(hw_arch)).lower()
        p1 = hw_meta.get("part1") if isinstance(hw_meta.get("part1"), dict) else {}
        p2 = hw_meta.get("part2") if isinstance(hw_meta.get("part2"), dict) else {}
        for stage, meta in (("part1", p1), ("part2", p2)):
            if not isinstance(meta, dict):
                continue
            for field in (
                "ok",
                "skipped",
                "timed_out",
                "error",
                "failure_kind",
                "unsupported_reason",
                "elapsed_s",
                "context_count",
                "context_mode",
                "partition_iterations",
                "partition_time_s",
                "allocation_time_s",
                "compilation_time_s",
                "calib_source",
                "single_context_failed",
                "single_context_used",
                "multi_context_used",
                "mapping_failed",
                "watchdog_expired",
            ):
                if field in meta:
                    flat[f"hailo_{arch}_{stage}_{field}"] = meta.get(field)
        flat[f"hailo_{arch}_part2_single_context"] = _ctx_single(p2)
        flat[f"hailo_{arch}_both_parts_single_context"] = bool(_ctx_single(p1) and _ctx_single(p2))
    return flat


def _resolve_stage_token(stage: Any) -> Optional[str]:
    """Resolve a stage config into a provider token.

    v49j accepts both the structured benchmark_plan form
    (``{"type":"hailo", "hw_arch":"hailo8"}``) and the compact legacy
    string form (``"hailo8"``/``"tensorrt"``).  v49i direct plans wrote
    compact strings while the remote runner expected dictionaries, which made
    matrix runs skip with "missing stage1/stage2" even though the information
    was present.

    Returns one of:
    - ORT provider token: auto/cpu/cuda/tensorrt
    - Hailo hw_arch token: hailo8/hailo8l/hailo8r/hailo10h/hailo10p
    """
    if isinstance(stage, str):
        s = stage.strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
        if not s:
            return None
        if s in {"cpu", "cpu_ort", "ort_cpu"}:
            return "cpu"
        if s in {"cuda", "cuda_ort", "ort_cuda", "gpu"}:
            return "cuda"
        if s in {"trt", "tensorrt", "tensor_rt"}:
            return "tensorrt"
        if s.startswith("hailo"):
            return s
        if s in {"deepx", "deepx_m1", "dx_m1", "dxm1"} or s.startswith("deepx"):
            return "deepx_m1"
        if "tensorrt" in s or "trt" in s:
            return "tensorrt"
        return s
    if not isinstance(stage, dict):
        return None
    ty = str(stage.get("type") or stage.get("kind") or "").strip().lower()
    if ty in {"hailo"}:
        hw = stage.get("hw_arch") or stage.get("arch") or stage.get("id")
        hw = (str(hw).strip().lower() if hw else "")
        return hw or None
    if ty in {"onnxruntime", "ort"}:
        p = stage.get("provider")
        p = (str(p).strip().lower() if p else "")
        if p in {"cpu_ort", "ort_cpu"}:
            return "cpu"
        if p in {"cuda_ort", "ort_cuda"}:
            return "cuda"
        if p in {"trt", "tensor_rt"}:
            return "tensorrt"
        return p or None
    if ty in {"deepx", "deepx_m1", "dx_m1", "dxm1"}:
        return "deepx_m1"
    backend = str(stage.get("backend") or stage.get("provider") or "").strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
    if backend in {"deepx", "deepx_m1", "dx_m1", "dxm1"}:
        return "deepx_m1"
    return None


def _canonical_hailo_arch_key(value: Any) -> str:
    """Return the physical evidence key shared by Hailo-10/10H aliases."""

    token = str(value or "").strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
    if token == "hailo10p":
        return token
    if token in {"hailo10", "hailo10h"}:
        return "hailo10h"
    if "hailo10h" in token or "hailo10" in token:
        return "hailo10h"
    if token in {"hailo8", "hailo8l", "hailo8r", "hailo10p"}:
        return token
    return token


def _merged_hailo_arch_meta(
    value: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]], hw_arch: Any,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Shallow-merge suite/case metadata for one physical Hailo target."""

    wanted = _canonical_hailo_arch_key(hw_arch)
    raw = value if isinstance(value, Mapping) else {}
    out: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    for raw_arch, raw_meta in raw.items():
        if _canonical_hailo_arch_key(raw_arch) != wanted:
            continue
        if not isinstance(raw_meta, Mapping):
            continue
        for field, field_value in raw_meta.items():
            if isinstance(field_value, bool):
                out[field] = bool(out.get(field) or field_value)
            elif field not in out or out.get(field) in (None, ""):
                out[field] = field_value
    for kind in ("full", "part1", "part2", "composed"):
        if bool(out.get(kind)):
            out[f"{kind}_error"] = ""
            out[f"{kind}_failed"] = False
    return out


def _canonical_plan_run_id(value: Any) -> str:
    """Canonical logical id; short Hailo-10 spelling remains a CLI alias."""

    token = str(value or "").strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
    aliases = {
        "hailo10_to_trt": "hailo10_to_tensorrt",
        "hailo10h_to_trt": "hailo10_to_tensorrt",
        "hailo10h_to_tensorrt": "hailo10_to_tensorrt",
    }
    return aliases.get(token, token)


def _normalize_variants(values: Any) -> Optional[List[str]]:
    if not isinstance(values, list):
        return None
    out: List[str] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for value in values:
        name = str(value or "").strip().lower()
        if not name or name in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(name)
        out.append(name)
    return out


def _canonical_full_backend(value: Any) -> str:
    token = str(value or "").strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
    aliases = {
        "trt": "tensorrt", "tensor_rt": "tensorrt",
        "ort_tensorrt": "tensorrt",
        "cuda": "cuda_ort", "ort_cuda": "cuda_ort", "gpu": "cuda_ort",
        "ort_cpu": "cpu", "cpu_ort": "cpu",
        "hailo10h": "hailo10", "hailo10p": "hailo10",
        "deepx": "deepx_m1", "dx_m1": "deepx_m1", "dxm1": "deepx_m1",
    }
    return aliases.get(token, token)


def _full_backend_for_run(run: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Return the logical Full backend measured by a plan row."""
    explicit = run.get("full_provider") or run.get("full_backend")
    if explicit:
        return _canonical_full_backend(explicit)
    run_type = str(run.get("type") or run.get("kind") or "onnxruntime").strip().lower()
    if run_type in {"deepx", "deepx_m1", "dx_m1", "dxm1"}:
        return "deepx_m1"
    if run_type == "hailo":
        return _canonical_full_backend(
            run.get("hw_arch") or run.get("arch") or run.get("id")
            or _resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage1"))
        )
    stage2 = _resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage2"))
    provider = run.get("provider") or stage2 or _resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage1"))
    return _canonical_full_backend(provider)


def _assign_full_baseline_owners(runs: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Assign one physical Full measurement owner per logical backend.

    Matrix rows may request ``full`` only to obtain an incidental comparison
    next to every split case.  That repeated TensorRT work produced nine Full
    rows in the overnight run.  Prefer an explicit Full-only owner, otherwise
    the same-backend reference profile (for example ``ort_tensorrt``), and
    remove ``full`` from every non-owner before any case is executed.
    """
    prepared = [dict(run or {}) for run in list(runs or [])]
    for run in prepared:
        stage1 = _canonical_full_backend(_resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage1")))
        stage2 = _canonical_full_backend(_resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage2")))
        backend = _full_backend_for_run(run)
        run_type = str(run.get("type") or run.get("kind") or "onnxruntime").strip().lower()
        homogeneous_trt_owner = bool(
            backend == "tensorrt"
            and run_type in {
                "onnxruntime", "ort", "matrix", "split",
                "same_backend_reference",
            }
            and stage1 in {"", "tensorrt"}
            and stage2 in {"", "tensorrt"}
        )
        # The setup scheduler adds the exact ``ort_tensorrt`` recipe to every
        # physical setup.  Only that homogeneous row may own the setup-local
        # Full-quality companion.  Marking an earlier Hailo->TRT split made the
        # companion depend on a plan row which had no endpoint identity and
        # failed every Standard/Quality dispatch before ``ort_tensorrt`` ran.
        run["_native_full_trt_quality_companion"] = homogeneous_trt_owner
    candidates: Dict[str, List[Tuple[int, int]]] = {}
    default_variants = ["full", "part1", "part2", "composed"]
    for index, run in enumerate(prepared):
        variants = _normalize_variants(run.get("variants"))
        run_type = str(run.get("type") or run.get("kind") or "onnxruntime").strip().lower()
        implicit_full = variants is None and run_type in {
            "onnxruntime", "ort", "matrix", "split", "hailo",
            "deepx", "deepx_m1", "dx_m1", "dxm1",
        }
        if variants is not None and "full" not in variants:
            continue
        if not implicit_full and variants is None:
            continue
        backend = _full_backend_for_run(run)
        if not backend:
            continue
        effective = list(variants if variants is not None else default_variants)
        run["variants"] = effective
        stage1 = _canonical_full_backend(_resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage1")))
        stage2 = _canonical_full_backend(_resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage2")))
        full_only = bool(effective) and set(effective) <= {"full"}
        same_backend_reference = bool(stage1 and stage2 and stage1 == stage2 == backend)
        priority = (
            0 if run.get("full_baseline_owner") is True
            else 1 if full_only
            else 2 if same_backend_reference
            else 9
        )
        run["_full_baseline_backend"] = backend
        run["_full_baseline_source_priority"] = priority
        candidates.setdefault(backend, []).append((priority, index))

    for backend, ranked in candidates.items():
        ranked = sorted(ranked, key=lambda item: (item[0], item[1]))
        # An incidental heterogeneous companion never becomes owner merely
        # because a remote invocation filtered the real owner out of its local
        # run list.  The dedicated owner can execute in another setup group;
        # if it is absent globally, the required-result postcondition must show
        # the missing Full row instead of fabricating one here.
        owner_index = ranked[0][1] if ranked[0][0] < 9 else None
        owner_id = (
            str(
                prepared[owner_index].get("id")
                or prepared[owner_index].get("name")
                or f"run_{owner_index}"
            ).strip()
            if owner_index is not None else ""
        )
        for _priority, index in ranked:
            run = prepared[index]
            is_owner = owner_index is not None and index == owner_index
            run["_full_baseline_owner"] = is_owner
            run["_full_baseline_owner_run_id"] = owner_id
            run["_full_baseline_owner_backend"] = backend
            if not is_owner:
                run["variants"] = [
                    value for value in (_normalize_variants(run.get("variants")) or [])
                    if value != "full"
                ]
    return prepared


def _apply_full_owner_case_variant(
    run: Dict[str, Any], variants: Optional[List[str]], case_index: int,
) -> Optional[List[str]]:
    """Keep Full only for the owner's first deterministic case container."""
    if variants is None or "full" not in variants:
        return variants
    if run.get("_full_baseline_owner") is not True or int(case_index) > 0:
        return [value for value in variants if value != "full"]
    return list(variants)


def _case_identifiers(case_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> set[str]:
    ids: set[str] = set()
    for key in ("case_id", "case_dir", "folder", "id", "name"):
        value = case_meta.get(key) if isinstance(case_meta, dict) else None
        if value is not None and str(value).strip():
            ids.add(str(value).strip())
    b = case_meta.get("boundary") if isinstance(case_meta, dict) else None
    if b is not None:
        try:
            ids.add(f"b{int(b):03d}")
            ids.add(str(int(b)))
        except Exception:
            ids.add(str(b).strip())
    return ids



def _parse_case_filter_values(values: Any) -> set[str]:
    out: set[str] = set()
    if not values:
        return out
    if isinstance(values, str):
        values = [values]
    try:
        iterable = list(values)
    except Exception:
        iterable = [values]
    for item in iterable:
        for part in str(item or "").replace(";", ",").split(","):
            tok = part.strip()
            if tok:
                out.add(tok.lower())
    return out

def _filter_cases_cli(cases: List[Dict[str, Any]], selected: set[str]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    if not selected:
        return list(cases)
    return [c for c in cases if {str(x).lower() for x in _case_identifiers(c)} & selected]

def _cases_for_run(cases: List[Dict[str, Any]], run: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Limit case-specific planned runs to their intended case.

    Formal workflow plans contain one matrix run per selected split candidate,
    identified by case_id.  Older benchmark_suite.py treated each planned matrix
    run as a suite-wide run and therefore executed b064_hailo8_to_tensorrt on
    *all* cases, then b128_hailo8_to_tensorrt on all cases again.  v49k keeps
    full/baseline runs suite-wide, but filters split runs to their listed
    case_id/case_ids so output folders and prediction-vs-benchmark rows stay
    one-to-one with the formal candidate plan.
    """
    raw_ids: List[str] = []
    for key in ("case_ids", "cases"):
        value = run.get(key) if isinstance(run, dict) else None
        if isinstance(value, list):
            raw_ids.extend(str(v).strip() for v in value if str(v).strip())
    value = run.get("case_id") if isinstance(run, dict) else None
    if value is not None and str(value).strip():
        raw_ids.append(str(value).strip())
    ids = {x for x in raw_ids if x and x.lower() not in {"all", "*", "full", "baseline", "baselines"}}
    if not ids:
        variants = _normalize_variants(run.get("variants"))
        # A Full-only baseline is a model-level logical run.  It needs one
        # deterministic case directory as an execution container, but it must
        # not be repeated once for every selected split candidate.
        if variants and set(variants) <= {"full"}:
            return list(cases[:1])
        return list(cases)
    out = [c for c in cases if _case_identifiers(c) & ids]
    return out


def _variant_hailo_requirements_for_run(run: Dict[str, Any], variant: str) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
    variant_key = str(variant or "").strip().lower()
    stage1_tok = _resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage1"))
    stage2_tok = _resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage2"))
    run_type = str(run.get("type") or "").strip().lower()
    run_hw = str(run.get("hw_arch") or run.get("id") or "").strip().lower() or None

    req: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []
    if variant_key == "full":
        if run_type == "hailo":
            hw = run_hw or stage1_tok or stage2_tok
            if hw:
                req.append((str(hw), "full"))
        return req
    if variant_key == "part1":
        if stage1_tok and stage1_tok.startswith("hailo"):
            req.append((str(stage1_tok), "part1"))
        return req
    if variant_key == "part2":
        if stage2_tok and stage2_tok.startswith("hailo"):
            req.append((str(stage2_tok), "part2"))
        return req
    if variant_key == "composed":
        if stage1_tok and stage1_tok.startswith("hailo"):
            req.append((str(stage1_tok), "part1"))
        if stage2_tok and stage2_tok.startswith("hailo"):
            req.append((str(stage2_tok), "part2"))
        return req
    return req


def _prune_case_variants_for_run(
    run: Dict[str, Any],
    variants: Optional[List[str]],
    case_meta: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
    suite_hailo_hefs: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
) -> Optional[List[str]]:
    if variants is None:
        return variants
    availability = case_meta.get("hailo_case_variant_availability") if isinstance(case_meta, dict) else None
    availability = availability if isinstance(availability, dict) else {}
    suite_hailo_hefs = suite_hailo_hefs if isinstance(suite_hailo_hefs, dict) else {}

    out: List[str] = []
    for variant in variants:
        req = _variant_hailo_requirements_for_run(run, variant)
        if not req:
            out.append(variant)
            continue
        ok = True
        for hw, kind in req:
            case_meta_for_arch = _merged_hailo_arch_meta(availability, hw)
            case_ok = bool(case_meta_for_arch.get(kind))
            if kind == "full":
                suite_meta = _merged_hailo_arch_meta(suite_hailo_hefs, hw)
                suite_ok = bool(suite_meta.get("full")) and not bool(suite_meta.get("full_error"))
                ok = ok and bool(case_ok or suite_ok)
            else:
                ok = ok and bool(case_ok)
            if not ok:
                break
        if ok:
            out.append(variant)
    return out

_STATUS_VARIANTS = ["full", "part1", "part2", "composed"]

def _agg_status(values: List[str]) -> str:
    """Aggregate a list of per-candidate statuses into one status.

    Precedence is: ok > error > skipped > missing
    """
    vset = {v for v in values if v}
    if "ok" in vset:
        return "ok"
    if "error" in vset:
        return "error"
    if "skipped" in vset:
        return "skipped"
    return "missing"

def _status_symbol(status: str) -> str:
    return {
        "ok": "✅",
        "error": "❌",
        "skipped": "⏭️",
        "missing": "—",
    }.get(status, status)

def _write_status_matrix(
    root: Path, planned_runs: List[Dict[str, Any]], *, preset: Optional[str] = None,
    rows_by_tag: Optional[Mapping[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]]] = None,
) -> None:
    """Write a compact status matrix across all planned runs.

    Uses the `variant_status` dict written by the per-case runner.  Normal suite
    execution supplies its current preset and emitted rows, so skipped runs
    cannot borrow an older result from the same preset. Independent file replay
    remains available when rows_by_tag is omitted, including legacy full tags.
    """
    rows_out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for run in planned_runs:
        tag = run.get("tag") or run.get("id") or ""
        run_id = run.get("id") or tag
        rtype = run.get("type", "?")
        if preset is not None:
            # Match main's result writer exactly.  A display tag, another
            # preset, or an older unsuffixed result must not supply this row.
            rtype = str(run.get("type") or run.get("kind") or "onnxruntime").strip().lower()
            run_id = str(run.get("id") or run.get("name") or rtype).strip() or rtype
            tag = f"{run_id}_{preset}"
        stage1 = _resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage1")) or run.get("provider", "")
        stage2 = _resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage2")) or run.get("provider", "")
        res_path = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.json"
        agg: Dict[str, str] = {k: "missing" for k in _STATUS_VARIANTS}
        if rows_by_tag is not None or res_path.exists():
            try:
                data = rows_by_tag.get(str(tag), []) if rows_by_tag is not None else _read_json(res_path)
                if isinstance(data, list):
                    for k in _STATUS_VARIANTS:
                        vals: List[str] = []
                        for rec in data:
                            if not isinstance(rec, dict):
                                continue
                            vs = rec.get("variant_status")
                            if isinstance(vs, dict) and k in vs:
                                vals.append(str(vs.get(k) or ""))
                                continue
                            # v52j: DeepX full rows and structured case-failure rows may
                            # not carry a legacy variant_status dictionary.  Infer the
                            # status so the matrix does not show a successful artifact as
                            # completely missing.
                            primary = str(rec.get("primary_variant") or rec.get("variant") or "").lower()
                            is_full_row = primary == "full" or str(rec.get("case_id") or "").lower() == "full"
                            if k == "full" and is_full_row:
                                if rec.get("runtime_ok") is True or rec.get("latency_ok") is True:
                                    vals.append("ok")
                                elif rec.get("error_class"):
                                    vals.append("error")
                            elif rec.get("error_class") and str(rec.get("case_dir") or ""):
                                if k in ("part1", "part2", "composed"):
                                    vals.append("error")
                        agg[k] = _agg_status(vals)
            except Exception:
                # Keep defaults if parsing fails
                pass

        row: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "run": str(run_id),
            "tag": str(tag),
            "type": str(rtype),
            "stage1": str(stage1),
            "stage2": str(stage2),
        }
        row.update(agg)
        rows_out.append(row)

    _write_json(root / "benchmark_suite_status_matrix.json", rows_out)

    csv_path = root / "benchmark_suite_status_matrix.csv"
    with csv_path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.writer(f)
        w.writerow(["run", "type", "stage1", "stage2"] + _STATUS_VARIANTS)
        for r in rows_out:
            w.writerow([r["run"], r["type"], r["stage1"], r["stage2"]] + [r[k] for k in _STATUS_VARIANTS])

    md_path = root / "benchmark_suite_status_matrix.md"
    lines: List[str] = []
    lines.append("# Benchmark suite status matrix\n")
    lines.append("Legend: ✅ ok, ❌ error, ⏭️ skipped, — missing\n")
    lines.append("| run | type | stage1 | stage2 | full | part1 | part2 | composed |")
    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|")
    for r in rows_out:
        sym = {k: _status_symbol(str(r.get(k, "missing"))) for k in _STATUS_VARIANTS}
        lines.append(
            f"| {r['run']} | {r['type']} | {r['stage1']} | {r['stage2']} | {sym['full']} | {sym['part1']} | {sym['part2']} | {sym['composed']} |"
        )
    md_path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")



def _safe_float(v: Any) -> Optional[float]:
    try:
        if v is None:
            return None
        x = float(v)
        if math.isnan(x) or math.isinf(x):
            return None
        return x
    except Exception:
        return None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# v60 canonical scientific reporting
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _v60_bool(value: Any) -> Optional[bool]:
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return value
    if value in (None, ""):
        return None
    text = str(value).strip().lower()
    if text in {"1", "true", "yes", "ok", "pass", "passed", "eligible", "claim_ok", "valid"}:
        return True
    if text in {"0", "false", "no", "fail", "failed", "invalid", "error"}:
        return False
    return None


def _v60_first(row: Dict[str, Any], keys: Tuple[str, ...]) -> Any:
    for key in keys:
        value = row.get(key)
        if value not in (None, ""):
            return value
    return None


def _v60_file_sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    if not path.is_file():
        return ""
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return "sha256:" + digest.hexdigest()


def _v60_mean(values: List[Optional[float]]) -> Optional[float]:
    valid = [float(v) for v in values if v is not None and math.isfinite(float(v))]
    return sum(valid) / len(valid) if valid else None


def _v60_median(values: List[float]) -> Optional[float]:
    valid = sorted(float(v) for v in values if math.isfinite(float(v)))
    if not valid:
        return None
    n = len(valid)
    return valid[n // 2] if n % 2 else (valid[n // 2 - 1] + valid[n // 2]) / 2.0


def _v60_average_ranks(values: List[float]) -> List[float]:
    order = sorted(range(len(values)), key=lambda idx: values[idx])
    ranks = [0.0] * len(values)
    pos = 0
    while pos < len(order):
        end = pos + 1
        while end < len(order) and values[order[end]] == values[order[pos]]:
            end += 1
        rank = (pos + 1 + end) / 2.0
        for idx in order[pos:end]:
            ranks[idx] = rank
        pos = end
    return ranks


def _v60_pearson(x: List[float], y: List[float]) -> Optional[float]:
    if len(x) != len(y) or len(x) < 2:
        return None
    mx = sum(x) / len(x)
    my = sum(y) / len(y)
    dx = [v - mx for v in x]
    dy = [v - my for v in y]
    den = math.sqrt(sum(v * v for v in dx) * sum(v * v for v in dy))
    return sum(a * b for a, b in zip(dx, dy)) / den if den > 0 else None


def _v60_spearman(x: List[float], y: List[float]) -> Optional[float]:
    return _v60_pearson(_v60_average_ranks(x), _v60_average_ranks(y))


def _v60_kendall_tau_b(x: List[float], y: List[float]) -> Optional[float]:
    if len(x) != len(y) or len(x) < 2:
        return None
    concordant = discordant = ties_x = ties_y = 0
    for i in range(len(x)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(x)):
            sx = (x[i] > x[j]) - (x[i] < x[j])
            sy = (y[i] > y[j]) - (y[i] < y[j])
            if sx == 0 and sy == 0:
                continue
            if sx == 0:
                ties_x += 1
            elif sy == 0:
                ties_y += 1
            elif sx == sy:
                concordant += 1
            else:
                discordant += 1
    den = math.sqrt((concordant + discordant + ties_x) * (concordant + discordant + ties_y))
    return (concordant - discordant) / den if den > 0 else None


def _v60_runner(row: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    text = " ".join(str(row.get(k) or "").lower() for k in ("run_id", "provider", "runner", "validation_claim_level"))
    return "native_fifo" if "native" in text or "fifo" in text else "generic"


def _v60_direction(row: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    run_id = str(row.get("run_id") or row.get("provider") or "").lower()
    if "_to_" in run_id:
        return run_id
    stage1 = str(row.get("stage1_provider") or "").lower()
    stage2 = str(row.get("stage2_provider") or "").lower()
    if stage1 and stage2 and stage1 != stage2:
        return f"{stage1}_to_{stage2}"
    return run_id


def _v60_is_split(row: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    primary = str(row.get("primary_variant") or row.get("variant") or "").lower()
    stage1 = str(row.get("stage1_provider") or "").lower()
    stage2 = str(row.get("stage2_provider") or "").lower()
    return primary in {"composed", "part1", "part2", "split"} or bool(stage1 and stage2 and stage1 != stage2)


def _v60_task_gate(row: Dict[str, Any], plan: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    block = row.get("task_quality_gate") if isinstance(row.get("task_quality_gate"), dict) else {}
    primary = block.get("primary") if isinstance(block.get("primary"), dict) else {}
    tier = str(block.get("tier") or row.get("task_quality_gate_tier") or row.get("validation_tier") or (plan.get("quality_gate") or {}).get("dataset_tier") or "screening").lower()
    decision = str(block.get("decision") or row.get("task_quality_gate_decision") or block.get("status") or row.get("task_quality_gate_status") or "").lower()
    if decision in {"passed", "ok", "eligible"}:
        decision = "pass"
    if decision in {"failed", "invalid"}:
        decision = "fail"
    metric = primary.get("metric") or row.get("task_quality_primary_metric")
    candidate = _safe_float(primary.get("candidate") if primary else row.get("task_quality_candidate"))
    reference = _safe_float(primary.get("reference") if primary else row.get("task_quality_reference"))
    delta = _safe_float(primary.get("delta") if primary else row.get("task_quality_delta"))
    ci_low = _safe_float(primary.get("ci_low") if primary else row.get("task_quality_ci_low"))
    ci_high = _safe_float(primary.get("ci_high") if primary else row.get("task_quality_ci_high"))
    margin = _safe_float(primary.get("margin") if primary else row.get("task_quality_margin"))
    source = "runtime_paired_non_inferiority" if block else "legacy_point_estimate"
    if not block:
        task = str(row.get("benchmark_task") or row.get("task") or "").lower()
        if task == "classification":
            metric = metric or "top1_accuracy"
            candidate = candidate if candidate is not None else _safe_float(_v60_first(row, ("mini_classification_eval_primary_top1", "classification_top1")))
            reference = reference if reference is not None else _safe_float(row.get("mini_classification_eval_full_top1"))
            margin = margin if margin is not None else 0.01
        else:
            metric = metric or "ap50"
            candidate = candidate if candidate is not None else _safe_float(row.get("mini_coco_ap50_primary"))
            reference = reference if reference is not None else _safe_float(row.get("mini_coco_ap50_full"))
            margin = margin if margin is not None else 0.01
        if delta is None and candidate is not None and reference is not None:
            delta = candidate - reference
        decision = "inconclusive" if candidate is not None or reference is not None else "unavailable"
    status = str(block.get("status") or row.get("task_quality_gate_status") or decision or "unavailable")
    final_pass = decision == "pass" and tier == "final" and ci_low is not None and margin is not None and ci_low >= -margin
    return {
        "tier": tier,
        "status": status,
        "decision": decision or "unavailable",
        "metric": metric,
        "candidate": candidate,
        "reference": reference,
        "delta": delta,
        "ci_low": ci_low,
        "ci_high": ci_high,
        "margin": margin,
        "source": source,
        "final_pass": final_pass,
    }


def _v60_write_csv(path: Path, rows: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fields: List[str] = []
    for row in rows:
        for key in row:
            if key not in fields:
                fields.append(key)
    with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(fh, fieldnames=fields, extrasaction="ignore")
        writer.writeheader()
        for row in rows:
            writer.writerow(row)


def _v60_tex_escape(value: Any) -> str:
    text = str(value if value not in (None, "") else "")
    for src, dst in (("\\", r"\textbackslash{}"), ("&", r"\&"), ("%", r"\%"), ("_", r"\_"), ("#", r"\#"), ("$", r"\$")):
        text = text.replace(src, dst)
    return text


def _v60_tex_value(value: Any) -> str:
    number = _safe_float(value)
    return f"{number:.3f}" if number is not None else "--"


def _v60_write_tex(path: Path, rows: List[Dict[str, Any]], columns: List[Tuple[str, str, str]], caption: str, label: str) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    lines = [r"\begin{table}[t]", r"\centering", r"\small", f"\\caption{{{_v60_tex_escape(caption)}}}", f"\\label{{{_v60_tex_escape(label)}}}", f"\\begin{{tabular}}{{{'l' + 'r' * (len(columns) - 1)}}}", r"\toprule"]
    lines.append(" & ".join(_v60_tex_escape(title) for _, title, _ in columns) + r" \\")
    lines.append(r"\midrule")
    for row in rows:
        lines.append(" & ".join(_v60_tex_value(row.get(key)) if kind == "number" else _v60_tex_escape(row.get(key)) for key, _, kind in columns) + r" \\")
    lines.extend([r"\bottomrule", r"\end{tabular}", r"\end{table}", ""])
    path.write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")


def _v60_cleanup_legacy(root: Path) -> List[str]:
    removed: List[str] = []
    for pattern in ("benchmark_summary_*.md", "benchmark_table_*.tex", "v42_pipeline_summary.*", "v47_pipeline_summary.*"):
        for path in root.glob(pattern):
            if path.is_file():
                path.unlink()
                removed.append(path.name)
    for path in root.glob("paper_figures_*"):
        if path.is_dir():
            shutil.rmtree(path, ignore_errors=True)
            removed.append(path.name + "/")
    return removed


def _v60_make_figures(report_root: Path, rows: List[Dict[str, Any]], ranking: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[str]:
    if not HAVE_PLOT:
        return []
    figure_dir = report_root / "figures"
    figure_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    written: List[str] = []

    quality = [r for r in rows if _safe_float(r.get("task_quality_delta")) is not None]
    if quality:
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(max(6.0, len(quality) * 0.45), 3.8))
        x = list(range(len(quality)))
        ax.scatter(x, [float(r["task_quality_delta"]) for r in quality])
        margins = [_safe_float(r.get("task_quality_margin")) for r in quality]
        if margins and all(m is not None for m in margins) and len({round(float(m), 12) for m in margins if m is not None}) == 1:
            ax.axhline(-float(margins[0]), linestyle="--", linewidth=1.0, label="non-inferiority margin")
            ax.legend()
        ax.axhline(0.0, linewidth=0.8)
        ax.set_ylabel("Task metric difference vs canonical full")
        ax.set_xticks(x)
        ax.set_xticklabels([f"{r.get('backend')}\n{r.get('case_id')}" for r in quality], rotation=45, ha="right")
        fig.tight_layout()
        for ext in ("pdf", "png"):
            path = figure_dir / f"task_quality_deltas.{ext}"
            fig.savefig(path, bbox_inches="tight", dpi=220 if ext == "png" else None)
            written.append(str(path.relative_to(report_root)))
        plt.close(fig)

    pairs: List[Tuple[float, float, str]] = []
    for row in rows:
        pred = _safe_float(row.get("predicted_cycle_ms"))
        meas = _safe_float(row.get("cycle_ms"))
        if pred is not None and meas is not None:
            pairs.append((pred, meas, str(row.get("case_id") or "")))
    if len(pairs) >= 2:
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(5.2, 4.4))
        ax.scatter([p for p, _, _ in pairs], [m for _, m, _ in pairs])
        low = min(min(p for p, _, _ in pairs), min(m for _, m, _ in pairs))
        high = max(max(p for p, _, _ in pairs), max(m for _, m, _ in pairs))
        ax.plot([low, high], [low, high], linestyle="--", linewidth=1.0)
        ax.set_xlabel("Predicted pipeline cycle [ms]")
        ax.set_ylabel("Measured pipeline cycle [ms]")
        fig.tight_layout()
        for ext in ("pdf", "png"):
            path = figure_dir / f"predicted_vs_measured_cycle.{ext}"
            fig.savefig(path, bbox_inches="tight", dpi=220 if ext == "png" else None)
            written.append(str(path.relative_to(report_root)))
        plt.close(fig)

    perf = [r for r in rows if r.get("performance_eligible") is True and _safe_float(r.get("throughput_fps")) is not None]
    if perf:
        fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(max(6.0, len(perf) * 0.45), 3.8))
        x = list(range(len(perf)))
        ax.bar(x, [float(r["throughput_fps"]) for r in perf])
        ax.set_ylabel("Throughput [FPS]")
        ax.set_xticks(x)
        ax.set_xticklabels([f"{r.get('backend')}\n{r.get('case_id')}" for r in perf], rotation=45, ha="right")
        fig.tight_layout()
        for ext in ("pdf", "png"):
            path = figure_dir / f"eligible_throughput.{ext}"
            fig.savefig(path, bbox_inches="tight", dpi=220 if ext == "png" else None)
            written.append(str(path.relative_to(report_root)))
        plt.close(fig)
    return written


def _write_v60_scientific_report(root: Path, rows_by_tag: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]], plan: Dict[str, Any], cases: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None) -> None:
    """Invoke the canonical self-contained v60 scientific reporter.

    Generated suites always vendor ``scientific_reporter_v60.py`` next to this
    script.  A reporter failure is explicit: the superseded claim tables and
    ``ranking_validation.*`` artefacts are never regenerated as a fallback.
    """
    try:
        from scientific_reporter_v60 import write_scientific_report as _v60_external_report

        _v60_external_report(root, rows_by_tag, plan, cases=cases)
    except Exception as exc:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "v60 scientific reporter failed; no legacy report was emitted: "
            f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
        ) from exc


def _write_v42_pipeline_summary(root: Path, rows_by_tag: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]]) -> None:
    """Write a thesis-oriented summary for single-system FPS vs hetero streaming FPS."""
    flat: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    full_candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    streaming_candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for tag, rows in (rows_by_tag or {}).items():
        for r in rows or []:
            item = {
                "tag": tag,
                "run_id": r.get("run_id"),
                "provider": r.get("provider"),
                "stage1_provider": r.get("stage1_provider"),
                "stage2_provider": r.get("stage2_provider"),
                "case_dir": r.get("case_dir"),
                "boundary": r.get("boundary"),
                "cut_mib": r.get("cut_mib"),
                "n_cut_tensors": r.get("n_cut_tensors"),
                "full_mean_ms": r.get("full_mean_ms"),
                "full_e2e_mean_ms": r.get("full_e2e_mean_ms"),
                "composed_mean_ms": r.get("composed_mean_ms"),
                "throughput_fps_makespan": r.get("throughput_fps_makespan"),
                "throughput_cycle_est_ms": r.get("throughput_cycle_est_ms"),
                "pipeline_cycle_selected_ms": r.get("pipeline_cycle_selected_ms"),
                "pipeline_cycle_with_transfer_ms": r.get("pipeline_cycle_with_transfer_ms"),
                "pipeline_fps_selected": r.get("pipeline_fps_selected"),
                "pipeline_speedup_full_over_cycle": r.get("pipeline_speedup_full_over_cycle"),
                "mini_coco_ap50_primary": r.get("mini_coco_ap50_primary"),
                "mini_coco_ap50_full": r.get("mini_coco_ap50_full"),
                "semantic_validation_passed": r.get("semantic_validation_passed"),
                "final_pass_all": r.get("final_pass_all"),
            }
            tag_l = str(tag or "").lower()
            st1_l = str(r.get("stage1_provider") or "").lower()
            st2_l = str(r.get("stage2_provider") or "").lower()
            is_hetero_tag = ("hailo" in st1_l and ("trt" in st2_l or "tensorrt" in st2_l)) or (("trt" in st1_l or "tensorrt" in st1_l) and "hailo" in st2_l) or "_to_" in tag_l
            # Preferred full FPS: fair E2E if present, otherwise legacy full mean.
            # Do not let mixed-run helper rows become the single-system baseline.
            full_ms = (
                _safe_float(r.get("full_e2e_mean_ms"))
                or _safe_float(r.get("full_e2e_latency_ms"))
                or _safe_float(r.get("total_latency_ms"))
                or _safe_float(r.get("full_latency_ms"))
                or _safe_float(r.get("full_mean_ms"))
            )
            if full_ms:
                item["full_e2e_mean_ms"] = item.get("full_e2e_mean_ms") or full_ms
                item["full_mean_ms"] = item.get("full_mean_ms") or full_ms
            if full_ms and full_ms > 0 and not is_hetero_tag:
                cand = dict(item)
                cand["baseline_ms_used"] = full_ms
                cand["baseline_fps"] = 1000.0 / full_ms
                full_candidates.append(cand)
            stream_fps = _safe_float(r.get("throughput_fps_makespan"))
            if stream_fps and stream_fps > 0:
                cand = dict(item)
                cand["streaming_fps"] = stream_fps
                streaming_candidates.append(cand)
            flat.append(item)

    best_full = max(full_candidates, key=lambda x: float(x.get("baseline_fps") or 0.0), default=None)
    best_stream = max(streaming_candidates, key=lambda x: float(x.get("streaming_fps") or 0.0), default=None)
    gain_vs_best_full = None
    if best_full and best_stream and _safe_float(best_full.get("baseline_fps")):
        gain_vs_best_full = (float(best_stream["streaming_fps"]) / float(best_full["baseline_fps"]) - 1.0) * 100.0

    payload = {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "description": "v42 thesis summary: compare single-system full FPS against heterogenous streaming/pipelined split FPS.",
        "best_full_baseline": best_full,
        "best_heterogeneous_streaming": best_stream,
        "gain_vs_best_full_percent": gain_vs_best_full,
        "num_rows": len(flat),
        "num_full_candidates": len(full_candidates),
        "num_streaming_candidates": len(streaming_candidates),
    }
    _write_json(root / "v42_pipeline_summary.json", payload)

    csv_path = root / "v42_pipeline_summary.csv"
    keys = [
        "tag", "run_id", "provider", "stage1_provider", "stage2_provider", "case_dir", "boundary",
        "full_mean_ms", "full_e2e_mean_ms", "composed_mean_ms", "throughput_fps_makespan",
        "throughput_cycle_est_ms", "pipeline_cycle_selected_ms", "pipeline_fps_selected", "pipeline_speedup_full_over_cycle", "mini_coco_ap50_primary", "mini_coco_ap50_full",
        "semantic_validation_passed", "final_pass_all",
    ]
    with csv_path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=keys)
        w.writeheader()
        for row in flat:
            w.writerow({k: row.get(k) for k in keys})

    md_lines = [
        "# v42 Pipeline Summary",
        "",
        "This summary separates single-frame latency from heterogeneous streaming throughput.",
        "",
    ]
    if best_full:
        md_lines.append(f"Best full baseline: `{best_full.get('tag')}` / `{best_full.get('case_dir')}` = {best_full.get('baseline_fps'):.3f} FPS ({best_full.get('baseline_ms_used'):.3f} ms used).")
    else:
        md_lines.append("Best full baseline: not available.")
    if best_stream:
        md_lines.append(f"Best heterogeneous streaming: `{best_stream.get('tag')}` / `{best_stream.get('case_dir')}` = {best_stream.get('streaming_fps'):.3f} FPS.")
    else:
        md_lines.append("Best heterogeneous streaming: not available.")
    if gain_vs_best_full is not None:
        md_lines.append(f"Gain vs best full baseline: **{gain_vs_best_full:.2f}%**.")
    md_lines.append("")
    md_lines.append("| tag | case | full ms | composed latency ms | pipeline cycle ms | pipeline FPS | streaming FPS measured | AP50 primary | semantic pass |")
    md_lines.append("|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|")
    def _fmt(v: Any) -> str:
        x = _safe_float(v)
        return "-" if x is None else f"{x:.3f}"
    for row in sorted(flat, key=lambda x: float(_safe_float(x.get("throughput_fps_makespan")) or -1.0), reverse=True)[:25]:
        md_lines.append(
            f"| {row.get('tag')} | {row.get('case_dir')} | {_fmt(row.get('full_mean_ms'))} | {_fmt(row.get('composed_mean_ms'))} | {_fmt(row.get('pipeline_cycle_selected_ms'))} | {_fmt(row.get('pipeline_fps_selected'))} | {_fmt(row.get('throughput_fps_makespan'))} | {_fmt(row.get('mini_coco_ap50_primary'))} | {row.get('semantic_validation_passed')} |"
        )
    (root / "v42_pipeline_summary.md").write_text("\n".join(md_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    # v58w: v47 summarizes any true heterogeneous accelerator/host pipeline,
    # not only Hailo<->TensorRT.  This is needed for DeepX->TensorRT and
    # TensorRT->DeepX evaluation runs as well.
    def _is_true_heterogeneous_pipeline(cand: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        tag = str(cand.get("tag") or cand.get("run_id") or "").lower()
        st1 = str(cand.get("stage1_provider") or "").lower()
        st2 = str(cand.get("stage2_provider") or "").lower()
        if st1 and st2 and st1 != st2:
            return True
        if any(tok in tag for tok in ("deepx_m1_to_tensorrt", "tensorrt_to_deepx", "hailo8_to_trt", "trt_to_hailo", "hailo10_to_tensorrt", "tensorrt_to_hailo")):
            return True
        # Conservative fallback: *_to_* run IDs are mixed pipelines unless they are explicitly same-backend diagnostics.
        return "_to_" in tag and not tag.startswith("ort_")

    hetero_streaming = [cand for cand in streaming_candidates if _is_true_heterogeneous_pipeline(cand)]
    best_hetero = max(hetero_streaming, key=lambda x: float(x.get("streaming_fps") or 0.0), default=None)
    gain_hetero_vs_best_full = None
    if best_full and best_hetero and _safe_float(best_full.get("baseline_fps")):
        gain_hetero_vs_best_full = (float(best_hetero["streaming_fps"]) / float(best_full["baseline_fps"]) - 1.0) * 100.0
    v47_payload = dict(payload)
    v47_payload.update({
        "schema_version": 3,
        "description": "v47/v58w thesis summary: single-system full baselines vs true heterogeneous streaming/pipeline throughput.",
        "best_heterogeneous_streaming": best_hetero,
        "gain_vs_best_full_percent": gain_hetero_vs_best_full,
        "num_heterogeneous_streaming_candidates": len(hetero_streaming),
    })
    _write_json(root / "v47_pipeline_summary.json", v47_payload)
    v47_csv_path = root / "v47_pipeline_summary.csv"
    with v47_csv_path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=keys)
        w.writeheader()
        for row in flat:
            w.writerow({k: row.get(k) for k in keys})
    v47_lines = [
        "# v47 Pipeline Summary",
        "",
        "This summary highlights true heterogeneous streaming/pipeline throughput separately from full-backend latency. Hailo, DeepX and TensorRT mixed pipelines are included; same-backend split diagnostics are excluded.",
        "",
    ]
    if best_full:
        v47_lines.append(f"Best full baseline: `{best_full.get('tag')}` / `{best_full.get('case_dir')}` = {best_full.get('baseline_fps'):.3f} FPS ({best_full.get('baseline_ms_used'):.3f} ms used).")
    else:
        v47_lines.append("Best full baseline: not available.")
    if best_hetero:
        v47_lines.append(f"Best heterogeneous streaming: `{best_hetero.get('tag')}` / `{best_hetero.get('case_dir')}` = {best_hetero.get('streaming_fps'):.3f} FPS.")
    else:
        v47_lines.append("Best heterogeneous streaming: not available.")
    if gain_hetero_vs_best_full is not None:
        v47_lines.append(f"Gain vs best full baseline: **{gain_hetero_vs_best_full:.2f}%**.")
    v47_lines.append("")
    v47_lines.append("| tag | case | full e2e ms | composed ms | streaming FPS | AP50 primary | semantic pass |")
    v47_lines.append("|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|")
    for row in sorted(flat, key=lambda x: float(_safe_float(x.get("throughput_fps_makespan")) or -1.0), reverse=True)[:30]:
        v47_lines.append(
            f"| {row.get('tag')} | {row.get('case_dir')} | {_fmt(row.get('full_e2e_mean_ms'))} | {_fmt(row.get('composed_mean_ms'))} | {_fmt(row.get('throughput_fps_makespan'))} | {_fmt(row.get('mini_coco_ap50_primary'))} | {row.get('semantic_validation_passed')} |"
        )
    (root / "v47_pipeline_summary.md").write_text("\n".join(v47_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def _v263_status_fields(row: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Keep execution, interface, task quality and evidence completeness separate."""
    def tri(value: Any) -> Optional[bool]:
        if isinstance(value, bool):
            return value
        if value is None:
            return None
        token = str(value).strip().lower()
        if token in {"1", "true", "yes", "ok", "pass", "passed"}:
            return True
        if token in {"0", "false", "no", "fail", "failed", "error"}:
            return False
        return None

    build_ok = tri(row.get("buildable"))
    if build_ok is None:
        build_ok = tri(row.get("compile_ok"))
    if build_ok is None:
        build_ok = tri(row.get("build_ok"))
    runtime_ok = tri(row.get("runtime_executable"))
    if runtime_ok is None:
        runtime_ok = tri(row.get("runtime_ok"))
    execution_ok = bool(build_ok is not False and runtime_ok is True)

    eligibility = row.get("row_eligibility") if isinstance(row.get("row_eligibility"), dict) else {}
    interface = tri(row.get("interface_contract_pass"))
    if interface is None:
        interface = tri(row.get("interface_check_pass"))
    if interface is None:
        interface = tri(eligibility.get("io_contract_pass"))
    if interface is None:
        interface = tri(row.get("contract_consistent"))

    task = str(row.get("benchmark_task") or row.get("benchmark_task_used") or row.get("task") or "").lower()
    family = str(row.get("contract_family") or row.get("output_format") or "").lower()
    raw_status = str(row.get("raw_head_contract_status") or "").lower()
    raw_head = task == "detection" and ("raw_head" in family or "raw_head" in raw_status or tri(row.get("raw_head_contract_present")) is True)
    if raw_head:
        decoder = tri(row.get("decoder_contract_pass"))
        if decoder is None:
            decoder = tri(row.get("raw_head_decode_ok"))
        nms = tri(row.get("nms_ok"))
        host = tri(row.get("host_tail_available"))
        decoder_id = str(row.get("decoder_id") or row.get("decoder_family") or row.get("host_tail_model") or "").strip()
        if not (decoder is True and nms is True and host is True and decoder_id):
            interface = False
            row["contract_gate_reason"] = "raw_head_decoder_postprocess_contract_unresolved"

    backend = str(row.get("backend") or row.get("provider") or row.get("run_id") or "").lower().replace("-", "_")
    stage1 = str(row.get("stage1_provider") or "").lower()
    stage2 = str(row.get("stage2_provider") or "").lower()
    hailo_to_trt = (stage1.startswith("hailo") and stage2 in {"trt", "tensorrt", "ort_tensorrt"}) or ("hailo" in backend and "_to_trt" in backend)
    if hailo_to_trt and tri(row.get("interface_contract_pass")) is not True and tri(row.get("interface_check_pass")) is not True:
        interface = False
        row["contract_gate_reason"] = "hailo_trt_interface_contract_failed_or_missing"

    gate = row.get("task_quality_gate") if isinstance(row.get("task_quality_gate"), dict) else {}
    decision = str(gate.get("decision") or row.get("task_quality_gate_decision") or "unavailable").strip().lower()
    quality_valid = decision in {"pass", "reference"}
    quality_evaluated = decision in {"pass", "fail", "reference"}
    pending = decision == "pending_central_evaluation"
    cpu_reference = backend in {"cpu", "cpu_ort", "ort_cpu", "canonical_full_onnx"} or tri(row.get("canonical_reference_row")) is True
    policy_match = tri(row.get("accuracy_gate_policy_match"))
    if policy_match is None:
        policy_match = True
    thesis_valid = bool(execution_ok and interface is True and quality_valid and policy_match and not cpu_reference)
    reason = ""
    if cpu_reference:
        reason = "semantic_reference_only"
    elif pending:
        reason = "pending_central_evaluation"
    elif not execution_ok:
        reason = "execution_failed"
    elif interface is not True:
        reason = "interface_failed_or_unavailable"
    elif not quality_valid:
        reason = "quality_failed_or_unavailable"
    row.update({
        "execution_ok": execution_ok,
        "interface_valid": bool(interface is True),
        "interface_status": "pass" if interface is True else ("fail" if interface is False else "unavailable"),
        "quality_valid": quality_valid,
        "quality_evaluation_pending": pending,
        "evidence_complete": bool(execution_ok and interface in {True, False} and quality_evaluated and policy_match),
        "semantic_reference_only": cpu_reference,
        "ranking_eligible": thesis_valid,
        "eligible_for_ranking": thesis_valid,
        "performance_eligible": thesis_valid,
        "energy_eligible": False,
        "pareto_eligible": thesis_valid,
        "thesis_valid": thesis_valid,
        "ranking_exclusion_reason": reason,
    })
    return row


def _fail_closed_for_nonzero_runner_rc(
    row: Dict[str, Any],
    runner_rc: int,
    *,
    report_present: bool,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Project a non-zero case-runner exit as a terminal row failure.

    A runner can write ``validation_report.json`` and then crash while flushing
    another artefact or shutting down a provider.  The old collector trusted
    the already-written success fields and merely attached ``_runner_rc``.  A
    SIGSEGV (observed as ``-11``) could therefore be reported as a successful
    measurement.  Keep the report on disk untouched, and preserve its status
    fields in this collected row for diagnosis, but make the authoritative
    execution/eligibility projection fail closed for every non-zero return
    code.
    """
    rc = int(runner_rc or 0)
    if rc == 0:
        return row

    raw_status_keys = (
        "ok",
        "status",
        "runtime_ok",
        "runtime_executable",
        "execution_ok",
        "validation_ok",
        "final_pass",
        "final_pass_all",
        "ranking_eligible",
        "eligible_for_ranking",
        "performance_eligible",
        "energy_eligible",
        "pareto_eligible",
        "thesis_valid",
        "error_class",
        "error_detail",
    )
    raw_status = {
        key: row.get(key)
        for key in raw_status_keys
        if key in row
    }
    raw_variants = row.get("variant_status")
    if isinstance(raw_variants, dict):
        row["raw_report_variant_status"] = dict(raw_variants)
    if raw_status:
        row["raw_report_status_before_runner_failure"] = raw_status

    if rc < 0:
        signal_number = abs(rc)
        signal_name = "SIGSEGV" if signal_number == 11 else f"SIGNAL_{signal_number}"
        error_class = (
            "case_runner_signal_sigsegv"
            if signal_number == 11
            else "case_runner_signal_failure"
        )
        failure_kind = "signal"
        detail = f"case runner terminated by {signal_name} (rc={rc})"
