        row["runner_signal_number"] = signal_number
        row["runner_signal_name"] = signal_name
    else:
        error_class = "case_runner_nonzero_exit"
        failure_kind = "nonzero_exit"
        detail = f"case runner returned non-zero rc={rc}"
    if not report_present:
        detail += " and did not produce validation_report.json"

    requested = row.get("requested_variants")
    variant_names = (
        [str(value).strip().lower() for value in requested if str(value).strip()]
        if isinstance(requested, list)
        else []
    )
    if not variant_names and isinstance(raw_variants, dict):
        variant_names = [str(value).strip().lower() for value in raw_variants if str(value).strip()]
    if not variant_names:
        variant_names = ["full", "part1", "part2", "composed"]

    eligibility = row.get("row_eligibility")
    eligibility = dict(eligibility) if isinstance(eligibility, dict) else {}
    eligibility.update({
        "runtime_pass": False,
        "ranking_eligible": False,
        "performance_eligible": False,
        "energy_eligible": False,
        "pareto_eligible": False,
        "exclusion_reason": "case_runner_terminal_failure",
    })
    row.update({
        "_runner_rc": rc,
        "runner_returncode": rc,
        "runner_terminal_failure": True,
        "runner_failure_kind": failure_kind,
        "runner_failure_overrode_report": bool(report_present),
        "raw_validation_report_preserved": bool(report_present),
        "ok": False,
        "status": "terminal_failure",
        "runtime_ok": False,
        "runtime_executable": False,
        "execution_ok": False,
        "validation_ok": False,
        "final_pass": False,
        "final_pass_all": False,
        "evidence_complete": False,
        "ranking_eligible": False,
        "eligible_for_ranking": False,
        "performance_eligible": False,
        "energy_eligible": False,
        "pareto_eligible": False,
        "thesis_valid": False,
        "ranking_exclusion_reason": "case_runner_terminal_failure",
        "error_class": error_class,
        "error_detail": detail,
        "variant_status": {
            variant: "runner_terminal_failure"
            for variant in variant_names
        },
        "row_eligibility": eligibility,
    })
    return row


def _collect_case_result(case_dir: Path, out_dir_name: str, run_id: str, provider: str, image_scale: str, stage1: Optional[str] = None, stage2: Optional[str] = None, variants: Optional[List[str]] = None, case_meta: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, *, runner_rc: int = 0, stage2_gate: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Load and normalize an existing per-case validation report.

    This is used both after a freshly executed case and in --resume mode to
    reuse results that are already present on disk.
    """
    report = case_dir / out_dir_name / "validation_report.json"
    if not report.exists():
        report_auto = case_dir / "results_auto" / "validation_report.json"
        report = report_auto if report_auto.exists() else report
    if not report.exists():
        print(f"[warn] missing report: {report}")
        if int(runner_rc or 0) != 0:
            # v52j: keep failed cases visible in benchmark_results_*.json instead
            # of returning an empty run result.  This makes provider/runtime
            # failures explicit in summaries and status matrices.
            return _fail_closed_for_nonzero_runner_rc({
                "schema_version": 1,
                "run_id": run_id,
                "provider": provider,
                "case_dir": case_dir.name,
                "stage1_provider": stage1,
                "stage2_provider": stage2,
                "runtime_ok": False,
                "validation_ok": False,
                "final_pass_all": False,
                "variant_status": {"full": "missing", "part1": "error", "part2": "error", "composed": "error"},
            }, int(runner_rc or 0), report_present=False)
        return None

    r = _read_json(report)
    if not isinstance(r, dict):
        print(f"[warn] invalid report payload: {report}")
        if int(runner_rc or 0) != 0:
            return _fail_closed_for_nonzero_runner_rc({
                "schema_version": 1,
                "run_id": run_id,
                "provider": provider,
                "case_dir": case_dir.name,
                "stage1_provider": stage1,
                "stage2_provider": stage2,
                "raw_validation_report_invalid": True,
            }, int(runner_rc or 0), report_present=True)
        return None

    # Attach suite-side metadata
    r.setdefault("run_id", run_id)
    r.setdefault("provider", provider)
    r.setdefault("case_dir", case_dir.name)
    if stage1:
        r.setdefault("stage1_provider", str(stage1))
    if stage2:
        r.setdefault("stage2_provider", str(stage2))
    # v51: keep the provider roles explicit. Matrix rows such as
    # hailo8_to_trt carry a TensorRT full-reference timing, but their row
    # backend is mixed. Reports use full_provider/full_backend to avoid
    # treating hailo8_to_tensorrt as a full baseline.
    r.setdefault("full_provider", str(provider))
    r.setdefault("backend_tokens", {
        "full": str(provider),
        "stage1": str(stage1 or provider),
        "stage2": str(stage2 or provider),
    })
    if variants:
        r.setdefault("requested_variants", list(variants))
    if isinstance(stage2_gate, dict):
        r.setdefault("stage2_accelerator_calibration_gate", dict(stage2_gate))
        if not bool(stage2_gate.get("available", False)):
            r.setdefault("counts_as_split_benchmark", False)
            # Preserve successful full/part1 evidence while making Stage2 invalid explicit.
            vs = r.get("variant_status")
            if not isinstance(vs, dict):
                vs = {}
                r["variant_status"] = vs
            for _vv in (stage2_gate.get("blocked_variants") or ["part2", "composed"]):
                if str(_vv).strip():
                    vs[str(_vv).strip().lower()] = "requires_feature_tensor_calibration"
            r.setdefault("stage2_accelerator_gate_reason", stage2_gate.get("reason") or "requires_feature_tensor_calibration")
    r["_runner_rc"] = runner_rc

    # Flatten predicted fields from newer runner versions (runner may store them under "predicted")
    pred = r.get("predicted")
    if isinstance(pred, dict):
        for k, v in pred.items():
            if k not in r:
                r[k] = v
        # common aliases used by this suite
        if "n_cut_tensors" not in r:
            if "crossing_tensors_all" in pred:
                r["n_cut_tensors"] = pred["crossing_tensors_all"]
            elif "crossing_tensors_known" in pred:
                r["n_cut_tensors"] = pred["crossing_tensors_known"]
            elif isinstance(r.get("cut_tensors"), list):
                r["n_cut_tensors"] = len(r["cut_tensors"])
        if "score" in pred and "score_pred" not in r:
            r["score_pred"] = pred["score"]
        if "imbalance" in pred and "imbalance_pred" not in r:
            r["imbalance_pred"] = pred["imbalance"]
        if "total_flops" in pred and "flops_total" not in r:
            r["flops_total"] = pred["total_flops"]
    # derive total flops if possible
    if "flops_total" not in r and ("flops_left" in r) and ("flops_right" in r):
        try:
            r["flops_total"] = float(r["flops_left"]) + float(r["flops_right"])
        except Exception:
            pass

    # enrich with static case meta if present
    manifest = case_dir / "split_manifest.json"
    if manifest.exists():
        m = _read_json(manifest)
        for k in ["boundary", "cut_mib", "n_cut_tensors", "flops_left", "flops_right", "imbalance_pred", "score_pred"]:
            if k in m and k not in r:
                r[k] = m[k]
        for k, v in m.items():
            r.setdefault(k, v)

    # Merge __BENCH_JSON__ metadata (predicted fields) if provided
    if isinstance(case_meta, dict):
        mp = case_meta.get("predicted")
        if isinstance(mp, dict):
            if not isinstance(r.get("predicted"), dict):
                r["predicted"] = mp
            else:
                for kk, vv in mp.items():
                    r["predicted"].setdefault(kk, vv)
        for kk in ["boundary_index", "boundary_id", "boundary_name", "case_dir", "folder"]:
            if kk in case_meta:
                r.setdefault(kk, case_meta.get(kk))
        for kk, vv in _flatten_hailo_compile_meta(case_meta).items():
            r.setdefault(kk, vv)

    pred2 = r.get("predicted")
    if isinstance(pred2, dict):
        for k, v in pred2.items():
            r.setdefault(k, v)

    if "score_pred" not in r and "score" in r:
        r["score_pred"] = r.get("score")
    if "imbalance_pred" not in r and "imbalance" in r:
        r["imbalance_pred"] = r.get("imbalance")
    if "n_cut_tensors" not in r:
        if "cut_tensors_toggle" in r:
            r["n_cut_tensors"] = r.get("cut_tensors_toggle")
        elif "cut_tensors" in r and isinstance(r.get("cut_tensors"), (int, float)):
            r["n_cut_tensors"] = int(r.get("cut_tensors"))
        elif isinstance(r.get("cut_tensors"), (list, tuple)):
            r["n_cut_tensors"] = len(r.get("cut_tensors"))
        else:
            pred_tmp = r.get("predicted")
            if isinstance(pred_tmp, dict):
                if isinstance(pred_tmp.get("crossing_tensors_all"), (list, tuple)):
                    r["n_cut_tensors"] = len(pred_tmp.get("crossing_tensors_all"))
                elif isinstance(pred_tmp.get("crossing_tensors_known"), (list, tuple)):
                    r["n_cut_tensors"] = len(pred_tmp.get("crossing_tensors_known"))
    if "eps_pass" not in r and "ok" in r:
        r["eps_pass"] = bool(r.get("ok"))

    tms = r.get("timing_ms") or r.get("timing") or r.get("timings")
    if isinstance(tms, dict):
        for _name in ("full", "part1", "part2", "composed"):
            blk = tms.get(_name)
            if isinstance(blk, dict):
                mean = blk.get("mean", blk.get("mean_ms"))
                std = blk.get("std", blk.get("std_ms"))
                if mean is not None:
                    r.setdefault(f"{_name}_mean_ms", mean)
                if std is not None:
                    r.setdefault(f"{_name}_std_ms", std)
                if "measurement_endpoint" in blk:
                    r.setdefault(f"{_name}_measurement_endpoint", blk["measurement_endpoint"])
        # Copy an observed timing endpoint, never the requested run scope.
        # An explicitly empty endpoint on a failed measurement stays empty.
        primary_timing_variant = str(r.get("primary_variant") or r.get("variant") or "").lower()
        primary_timing_block = tms.get(primary_timing_variant)
        if isinstance(primary_timing_block, dict) and "measurement_endpoint" in primary_timing_block:
            r.setdefault("measurement_endpoint", primary_timing_block["measurement_endpoint"])


    # v42 flattened timing diagnostics.
    if isinstance(tms, dict):
        full_blk = tms.get("full") if isinstance(tms.get("full"), dict) else {}
        part2_blk = tms.get("part2") if isinstance(tms.get("part2"), dict) else {}
        comp_blk = tms.get("composed") if isinstance(tms.get("composed"), dict) else {}
        if isinstance(full_blk, dict):
            for src, dst in (
                ("host_tail_mean_ms", "full_host_tail_mean_ms"),
                ("host_tail_std_ms", "full_host_tail_std_ms"),
                ("e2e_mean_ms", "full_e2e_mean_ms"),
                ("e2e_std_ms", "full_e2e_std_ms"),
                ("endpoint_mode", "full_endpoint_mode"),
            ):
                if src in full_blk:
                    r.setdefault(dst, full_blk.get(src))
        if isinstance(part2_blk, dict):
            for src, dst in (
                ("accelerator_prefix_mean_ms", "part2_prefix_mean_ms"),
                ("accelerator_prefix_std_ms", "part2_prefix_std_ms"),
                ("host_tail_mean_ms", "part2_host_tail_mean_ms"),
                ("host_tail_std_ms", "part2_host_tail_std_ms"),
                ("endpoint_mode", "part2_endpoint_mode"),
            ):
                if src in part2_blk:
                    r.setdefault(dst, part2_blk.get(src))
        if isinstance(comp_blk, dict):
            pp = comp_blk.get("phase_profile")
            if isinstance(pp, dict):
                for section, prefix in (
                    ("wall", "composed_phase_wall"),
                    ("stage1", "composed_phase_stage1"),
                    ("stage2_input_mapping", "composed_phase_stage2_input_mapping"),
                    ("stage2_prefix", "composed_phase_stage2_prefix"),
                    ("stage2_host_tail", "composed_phase_stage2_host_tail"),
                    ("stage2_total", "composed_phase_stage2_total"),
                ):
                    blk = pp.get(section)
                    if isinstance(blk, dict):
                        if blk.get("mean_ms") is not None:
                            r.setdefault(f"{prefix}_mean_ms", blk.get("mean_ms"))
                        if blk.get("std_ms") is not None:
                            r.setdefault(f"{prefix}_std_ms", blk.get("std_ms"))
                r.setdefault("composed_phase_stage2_uses_hailo_prefix", pp.get("stage2_uses_hailo_prefix"))
                r.setdefault("composed_phase_stage2_uses_host_tail", pp.get("stage2_uses_host_tail"))

    rh = r.get("raw_head_tensor_compare") or (r.get("timing_breakdown") or {}).get("raw_head_drift") if isinstance(r.get("timing_breakdown"), dict) else r.get("raw_head_tensor_compare")
    if isinstance(rh, dict):
        r.setdefault("raw_head_drift_status", rh.get("status"))
        r.setdefault("raw_head_drift_pass", rh.get("pass"))
        r.setdefault("raw_head_drift_max_abs", rh.get("max_abs"))
        r.setdefault("raw_head_drift_mean_abs_mean", rh.get("mean_abs_mean"))
        r.setdefault("raw_head_drift_num_compared_tensors", rh.get("num_compared_tensors"))

    throughput = r.get("throughput")
    if isinstance(throughput, dict):
        for key in (
            "mode",
            "queue_depth",
            "warmup_frames",
            "measured_frames",
            "fps_makespan",
            "fps_cycle_est",
            "cycle_est_ms",
            "makespan_ms",
            "latency_mean_ms",
            "latency_p50_ms",
            "latency_p95_ms",
            "stage1_mean_ms",
            "stage2_mean_ms",
            "frame_count_total",
        ):
            if key in throughput:
                r.setdefault(f"throughput_{key}", throughput.get(key))

    gate = r.get("stage2_accelerator_calibration_gate")
    if isinstance(gate, dict):
        r.setdefault("stage2_accel_calibration_required", gate.get("required"))
        r.setdefault("stage2_accel_calibration_available", gate.get("available"))
        r.setdefault("stage2_accel_calibration_status", gate.get("status"))
        r.setdefault("stage2_accel_calibration_manifest", gate.get("manifest"))
        r.setdefault("stage2_accel_calibration_reason", gate.get("reason"))
        r.setdefault("stage2_calibration_source", gate.get("source"))
        r.setdefault("stage2_calibration_trust_level", gate.get("trust_level"))
        r.setdefault("stage2_calibration_producer_exact", gate.get("producer_exact"))
        r.setdefault("stage2_calibration_sample_count", gate.get("sample_count"))

    variant_contracts = r.get("deployment_contracts_by_variant")
    if not isinstance(variant_contracts, dict) and isinstance(r.get("deployment_contract"), dict):
        variant_contracts = (r.get("deployment_contract") or {}).get("contracts_by_variant")
    primary_contract_variant = str(r.get("primary_variant") or r.get("variant") or "").strip().lower()
    dcs = (
        variant_contracts.get(primary_contract_variant)
        if isinstance(variant_contracts, dict) and isinstance(variant_contracts.get(primary_contract_variant), dict)
        else None
    )
    dcs = dcs or r.get("deployment_contract_summary") or ((r.get("deployment_contract") or {}).get("contract_summary") if isinstance(r.get("deployment_contract"), dict) else None)
    if isinstance(dcs, dict):
        r.setdefault("raw_head_contract_present", dcs.get("raw_head_contract_present"))
        r.setdefault("raw_head_contract_status", dcs.get("raw_head_contract_status"))
        r.setdefault("host_tail_required", dcs.get("host_tail_required"))
        r.setdefault("host_tail_available", dcs.get("host_tail_available"))
        r.setdefault("host_tail_model", dcs.get("host_tail_model"))
        r.setdefault("stage2_input_contract_kind", dcs.get("stage2_input_contract_kind"))
        r.setdefault("stage2_calibration_required", dcs.get("stage2_calibration_required"))
        r.setdefault("stage2_calibration_available", dcs.get("stage2_calibration_available"))
        r.setdefault("stage2_calibration_source", dcs.get("stage2_calibration_source"))
        r.setdefault("stage2_calibration_trust_level", dcs.get("stage2_calibration_trust_level"))

    iface = r.get("interface_checks")
    if isinstance(iface, dict):
        r.setdefault("interface_check_status", iface.get("status"))
        r.setdefault("interface_check_pass", iface.get("pass"))
        r.setdefault("interface_gate_override", iface.get("gate_override"))
        r.setdefault("interface_gate_blocking", iface.get("gate_blocking"))
        checks = iface.get("checks") if isinstance(iface.get("checks"), dict) else {}
        p1 = iface.get("stage1_vs_ort") if isinstance(iface.get("stage1_vs_ort"), dict) else checks.get("stage1_vs_ort", {})
        if isinstance(p1, dict):
            r.setdefault("interface_stage1_pass", p1.get("pass"))
            r.setdefault("interface_stage1_status", p1.get("status"))
            r.setdefault("interface_stage1_max_abs", p1.get("max_abs"))
            r.setdefault("interface_stage1_mean_abs_mean", p1.get("mean_abs_mean"))
            r.setdefault("interface_stage1_num_compared_tensors", p1.get("num_compared_tensors"))
        dx_contract = checks.get("deepx_stage2_contract", {}) if isinstance(checks, dict) else {}
        if isinstance(dx_contract, dict):
            r.setdefault("deepx_stage2_contract_status", dx_contract.get("status"))
            r.setdefault("deepx_stage2_contract_pass", dx_contract.get("pass"))
            r.setdefault("deepx_stage2_contract_probe_samples", dx_contract.get("probe_sample_count"))
            r.setdefault("deepx_stage2_contract_selected_score", dx_contract.get("selected_score"))
            r.setdefault("deepx_stage2_contract_selected_mean_score", dx_contract.get("selected_mean_score"))
            r.setdefault("deepx_stage2_contract_selected_pass_ratio", dx_contract.get("selected_pass_ratio"))
            r.setdefault("deepx_stage2_contract_selected_top1_match_ratio", dx_contract.get("selected_top1_match_ratio"))
            r.setdefault("deepx_stage2_contract_selected_mean_cosine", dx_contract.get("selected_mean_cosine_similarity"))
            r.setdefault("deepx_stage2_contract_candidate_count", dx_contract.get("candidate_count"))
            r.setdefault("deepx_stage2_contract_candidate_evaluations", dx_contract.get("candidate_evaluations"))
            r.setdefault("deepx_stage2_contract_rejected_candidate_count", dx_contract.get("rejected_candidate_count"))

    backend_drift = r.get("backend_drift")
    if isinstance(backend_drift, dict):
        r.setdefault("backend_drift_enabled", bool(backend_drift.get("enabled")))
        r.setdefault("backend_drift_status", backend_drift.get("status"))
        r.setdefault("backend_drift_mode", backend_drift.get("mode"))
        r.setdefault("backend_drift_variant", backend_drift.get("variant"))
        r.setdefault("backend_drift_provider", backend_drift.get("provider"))
        r.setdefault("backend_drift_reference_source", backend_drift.get("reference_source"))
        single = backend_drift.get("single_sample") if isinstance(backend_drift.get("single_sample"), dict) else {}
        elem = single.get("elementwise") if isinstance(single.get("elementwise"), dict) else {}
        proxy = single.get("proxy") if isinstance(single.get("proxy"), dict) else {}
        if isinstance(elem, dict):
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_single_max_abs", elem.get("max_abs"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_single_mean_abs", elem.get("mean_abs"))
        if isinstance(proxy, dict):
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_single_match_ratio", proxy.get("match_ratio"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_single_mean_iou", proxy.get("mean_iou_matched"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_single_count_delta_abs", proxy.get("count_delta_abs"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_single_pass", proxy.get("pass"))
        cls_proxy = single.get("classification_proxy") if isinstance(single.get("classification_proxy"), dict) else {}
        if isinstance(cls_proxy, dict):
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_single_top1_match", cls_proxy.get("top1_match"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_single_top5_agreement", cls_proxy.get("top5_agreement"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_single_cosine_similarity", cls_proxy.get("cosine_similarity"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_single_mean_abs_cls", cls_proxy.get("mean_abs"))
        dataset = backend_drift.get("dataset") if isinstance(backend_drift.get("dataset"), dict) else {}
        metrics = dataset.get("metrics") if isinstance(dataset.get("metrics"), dict) else {}
        if isinstance(dataset, dict):
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_dataset_pass", dataset.get("pass"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_dataset_requested_images", dataset.get("requested_images"))
        if isinstance(metrics, dict):
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_dataset_n_images", metrics.get("n_images"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_dataset_image_pass_ratio", metrics.get("image_pass_ratio"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_dataset_global_match_ratio", metrics.get("global_match_ratio"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_dataset_global_mean_iou", metrics.get("global_mean_iou_matched"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_dataset_count_delta_abs", metrics.get("count_delta_abs"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_dataset_top1_agreement", metrics.get("top1_agreement"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_dataset_top5_agreement", metrics.get("top5_agreement"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_dataset_mean_cosine_similarity", metrics.get("mean_cosine_similarity"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_dataset_top1_accuracy", metrics.get("top1_accuracy"))
            r.setdefault("backend_drift_dataset_top5_accuracy", metrics.get("top5_accuracy"))

    mini_coco = r.get("mini_coco_ap50")
    if isinstance(mini_coco, dict):
        r.setdefault("mini_coco_ap50_enabled", bool(mini_coco.get("enabled")))
        r.setdefault("mini_coco_ap50_status", mini_coco.get("status"))
        r.setdefault("mini_coco_ap50_reference_source", mini_coco.get("reference_source"))
        variants_blk = mini_coco.get("variants") if isinstance(mini_coco.get("variants"), dict) else {}
        for vv, payload in variants_blk.items():
            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
                continue
            key = str(vv).strip().lower()
            if key:
                r.setdefault(f"mini_coco_ap50_{key}", payload.get("ap50"))
        primary_variant = str(r.get("primary_variant") or "").strip().lower()
        if primary_variant and f"mini_coco_ap50_{primary_variant}" in r:
            r.setdefault("mini_coco_ap50_primary_variant", primary_variant)
            r.setdefault("mini_coco_ap50_primary", r.get(f"mini_coco_ap50_{primary_variant}"))
        if r.get("mini_coco_ap50_primary") is not None and r.get("mini_coco_ap50_full") is not None:
            try:
                r.setdefault("mini_coco_ap50_delta_primary_minus_full", float(r.get("mini_coco_ap50_primary")) - float(r.get("mini_coco_ap50_full")))
            except Exception:
                pass

    mini_cls = r.get("mini_classification_eval")
    if isinstance(mini_cls, dict):
        r.setdefault("mini_classification_eval_enabled", bool(mini_cls.get("enabled")))
        r.setdefault("mini_classification_eval_status", mini_cls.get("status"))
        variants_blk = mini_cls.get("variants") if isinstance(mini_cls.get("variants"), dict) else {}
        for vv, payload in variants_blk.items():
            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
                continue
            key = str(vv).strip().lower()
            if not key:
                continue
            r.setdefault(f"mini_classification_eval_{key}_top1", payload.get("top1_accuracy"))
            r.setdefault(f"mini_classification_eval_{key}_top5", payload.get("top5_accuracy"))
            r.setdefault(f"mini_classification_eval_{key}_images", payload.get("images"))
        primary_variant = str(r.get("primary_variant") or "").strip().lower()
        if primary_variant:
            if f"mini_classification_eval_{primary_variant}_top1" in r:
                r.setdefault("mini_classification_eval_primary_variant", primary_variant)
                r.setdefault("mini_classification_eval_primary_top1", r.get(f"mini_classification_eval_{primary_variant}_top1"))
                r.setdefault("mini_classification_eval_primary_top5", r.get(f"mini_classification_eval_{primary_variant}_top5"))
        if r.get("mini_classification_eval_primary_top1") is not None and r.get("mini_classification_eval_full_top1") is not None:
            try:
                r.setdefault("mini_classification_eval_delta_primary_minus_full_top1", float(r.get("mini_classification_eval_primary_top1")) - float(r.get("mini_classification_eval_full_top1")))
            except Exception:
                pass
        if r.get("mini_classification_eval_primary_top5") is not None and r.get("mini_classification_eval_full_top5") is not None:
            try:
                r.setdefault("mini_classification_eval_delta_primary_minus_full_top5", float(r.get("mini_classification_eval_primary_top5")) - float(r.get("mini_classification_eval_full_top5")))
            except Exception:
                pass

    # v60 canonical task-quality and eligibility fields.  Legacy mini-* fields
    # above remain diagnostics; all selection/reporting decisions consume this
    # explicit non-inferiority block.
    task_gate = r.get("task_quality_gate")
    if isinstance(task_gate, dict):
        primary_gate = task_gate.get("primary") if isinstance(task_gate.get("primary"), dict) else {}
        r.setdefault("task_quality_gate_status", task_gate.get("status"))
        r.setdefault("task_quality_gate_decision", task_gate.get("decision"))
        r.setdefault("task_quality_gate_tier", task_gate.get("tier"))
        r.setdefault("task_quality_primary_metric", primary_gate.get("metric"))
        r.setdefault("task_quality_candidate", primary_gate.get("candidate"))
        r.setdefault("task_quality_reference", primary_gate.get("reference"))
        r.setdefault("task_quality_delta", primary_gate.get("delta"))
        r.setdefault("task_quality_ci_low", primary_gate.get("ci_low"))
        r.setdefault("task_quality_ci_high", primary_gate.get("ci_high"))
        r.setdefault("task_quality_margin", primary_gate.get("margin"))
        r.setdefault("task_quality_n", task_gate.get("n") or primary_gate.get("n"))
        r.setdefault("task_quality_reference_source", task_gate.get("canonical_reference"))
    requests = r.get("task_quality_input_requests_by_variant")
    if isinstance(requests, dict):
        r.setdefault("quality_execution_location", "central_management")
        r.setdefault("quality_input_request_count", len(requests))
        primary_variant = str(r.get("primary_variant") or "").strip().lower()
        if primary_variant and isinstance(requests.get(primary_variant), dict):
            r.setdefault("quality_input_request", requests.get(primary_variant))
            r.setdefault(
                "candidate_execution_completion_contract_sha256",
                str(
                    requests[primary_variant].get(
                        "candidate_execution_completion_contract_sha256"
                    ) or ""
                ),
            )
    eligibility = r.get("row_eligibility")
    if isinstance(eligibility, dict):
        for _key in (
            "build_pass", "runtime_pass", "io_contract_pass", "task_quality_pass",
            "ranking_eligible", "performance_eligible", "energy_eligible",
            "pareto_eligible", "exclusion_reason",
        ):
            if _key in eligibility:
                r.setdefault(_key, eligibility.get(_key))

    validation_dataset = r.get("validation_dataset")
    if isinstance(validation_dataset, dict):
        r.setdefault("validation_dataset_task", validation_dataset.get("task"))
        variants_blk = validation_dataset.get("variants") if isinstance(validation_dataset.get("variants"), dict) else {}
        primary_variant = str(r.get("primary_variant") or "").strip().lower()
        primary_payload = variants_blk.get(primary_variant) if primary_variant else None
        if isinstance(primary_payload, dict):
            metrics_blk = primary_payload.get("metrics") if isinstance(primary_payload.get("metrics"), dict) else {}
            r.setdefault("validation_dataset_primary_pass", primary_payload.get("pass"))
            r.setdefault("validation_dataset_primary_image_pass_ratio", metrics_blk.get("image_pass_ratio"))
            r.setdefault("validation_dataset_primary_top1_agreement", metrics_blk.get("top1_agreement"))
            r.setdefault("validation_dataset_primary_top5_agreement", metrics_blk.get("top5_agreement"))
            r.setdefault("validation_dataset_primary_top1_accuracy", metrics_blk.get("top1_accuracy"))
            r.setdefault("validation_dataset_primary_top5_accuracy", metrics_blk.get("top5_accuracy"))

    try:
        p1 = float(r.get("part1_mean_ms", float("nan")))
        p2 = float(r.get("part2_mean_ms", float("nan")))
        comp = float(r.get("composed_mean_ms", float("nan")))
        if math.isfinite(p1) and math.isfinite(p2):
            r["sum_parts_ms"] = p1 + p2
            if math.isfinite(comp):
                r["overhead_ms"] = comp - (p1 + p2)
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        _derive_interleaved_pipeline_metrics(r)
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        full = float(r.get("full_mean_ms", float("nan")))
        comp = float(r.get("composed_mean_ms", float("nan")))
        if math.isfinite(full) and math.isfinite(comp) and comp > 0:
            r["speedup_full_over_composed"] = full / comp
    except Exception:
        pass

    sessions = r.get("sessions")
    if isinstance(sessions, dict):
        runtime_sessions = sessions.get("ort_runtime") if isinstance(sessions.get("ort_runtime"), dict) else {}
        baseline_sessions = sessions.get("baseline_ort_cpu") if isinstance(sessions.get("baseline_ort_cpu"), dict) else {}

        def _pick_session_payload(kind: str, provider_hint: Optional[str]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
            hint = str(provider_hint or "").strip().lower()
            candidates: List[str] = []
            if hint:
                candidates.append(f"{kind}:{hint}")
            default_hint = str(provider or "").strip().lower()
            if default_hint and f"{kind}:{default_hint}" not in candidates:
                candidates.append(f"{kind}:{default_hint}")
            for key in candidates:
                payload = runtime_sessions.get(key)
                if isinstance(payload, dict):
                    return payload
            payload = baseline_sessions.get(kind)
            return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else None

        for kind, hint in (("full", provider), ("part1", stage1 or provider), ("part2", stage2 or provider)):
            payload = _pick_session_payload(kind, hint)
            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
                continue
            build_s = payload.get("build_seconds")
            if build_s is not None:
                r.setdefault(f"{kind}_session_build_s", build_s)
            req = payload.get("providers_requested")
            if isinstance(req, list):
                r.setdefault(f"{kind}_session_providers_requested", "|".join(str(x) for x in req))
            use = payload.get("providers_in_use")
            if isinstance(use, list):
                r.setdefault(f"{kind}_session_providers_in_use", "|".join(str(x) for x in use))

    r.setdefault("provider", provider)
    r.setdefault("image_scale", image_scale)
    if "benchmark_task_used" in r:
        r.setdefault("benchmark_task", r.get("benchmark_task_used"))
    elif "benchmark_task_requested" in r:
        r.setdefault("benchmark_task", r.get("benchmark_task_requested"))
    normalized = _v263_status_fields(r)
    return _fail_closed_for_nonzero_runner_rc(
        normalized,
        int(runner_rc or 0),
        report_present=True,
    )


def _path_looks_like_semantic_validation_source(path: Path) -> bool:
    image_exts = {'.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.bmp', '.webp'}
    list_exts = {'.txt', '.json'}
    try:
        if path.is_file():
            return path.suffix.lower() in image_exts | list_exts
        if path.is_dir():
            if (path / "manifest.json").is_file():
                return True
            for cand in path.rglob('*'):
                if cand.is_file() and cand.suffix.lower() in image_exts:
                    return True
            for cand in path.iterdir():
                if cand.is_file() and cand.suffix.lower() in list_exts:
                    return True
    except Exception:
        return False
    return False


def _resolve_semantic_validation_source(raw: Optional[str], root: Path, task: str = "auto") -> Optional[Path]:
    """Resolve validation_images values used by plan rows and CLI overrides.

    Important v52i fix: bare classification preset names such as
    ``imagenette_val_mini_200`` must resolve to the suite-local manifest under
    resources/validation/classification/... when it was bundled.  Older code
    resolved them as ``suite/imagenette_val_mini_200`` and then disabled
    dataset validation.
    """
    s = str(raw or "").strip()
    if not s:
        return None
    p0 = Path(s).expanduser()
    candidates: List[Path] = []
    if p0.is_absolute():
        candidates.append(p0)
    else:
        candidates.extend([
            root / p0,
            root / "resources" / "validation" / "classification" / s / "manifest.json",
            root / "resources" / "validation" / "classification" / s,
            root / "resources" / "validation" / "classification" / f"{s}.json",
            root / "resources" / "validation" / "detection" / s,
            root / "resources" / "validation" / s,
        ])
    # A manifest path is preferred over the parent directory for classification,
    # because per-case runners use labels from the manifest.
    if str(task or "").lower() == "classification":
        candidates = sorted(candidates, key=lambda x: 0 if x.name == "manifest.json" else 1)
    seen = set()
    for cand in candidates:
        try:
            c = cand.resolve()
            key = str(c)
            if key in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(key)
            if _path_looks_like_semantic_validation_source(c):
                return c
        except Exception:
            continue
    return None


def _deterministic_pick(items: List[Path], seed_key: str) -> Optional[Path]:
    if not items:
        return None
    try:
        idx = sum(ord(ch) for ch in str(seed_key or "")) % len(items)
        return items[idx]
    except Exception:
        return items[0]


def _select_visual_sample_image(source: Optional[Path], root: Path, task: str, seed_key: str) -> Optional[Path]:
    """Choose one deterministic validation image for visual verification.

    Dataset validation can use many images.  Visual verification intentionally
    uses one reproducible sample per case/run so debug packs contain quick
    human-readable evidence without exploding in size.
    """
    if source is None:
        return None
    try:
        src = Path(source)
        if not src.is_absolute():
            src = (root / src).resolve()
        exts = {".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".bmp", ".webp"}
        candidates: List[Path] = []
        if src.is_file() and src.suffix.lower() in exts:
            return src
        if src.is_file() and src.suffix.lower() == ".json":
            obj = _read_json(src)
            base = src.parent
            if isinstance(obj, dict):
                for rec in list(obj.get("samples") or obj.get("images") or []):
                    if isinstance(rec, dict):
                        v = rec.get("image") or rec.get("path") or rec.get("file_name") or rec.get("filename")
                    else:
                        v = rec
                    if not v:
                        continue
                    q = Path(str(v))
                    if not q.is_absolute():
                        q1 = base / q
                        q2 = base / "images" / q
                        q = q1 if q1.exists() else q2
                    if q.exists() and q.suffix.lower() in exts:
                        candidates.append(q.resolve())
            return _deterministic_pick(sorted(set(candidates)), seed_key)
        if src.is_dir():
            for rr in (src / "images", src):
                if rr.is_dir():
                    for q in rr.rglob("*"):
                        if q.is_file() and q.suffix.lower() in exts:
                            candidates.append(q.resolve())
                    if candidates:
                        break
            return _deterministic_pick(sorted(set(candidates)), seed_key)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[viz] failed to select validation visual sample: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
    return None


def _management_cpu_reference_context(
    *, run_id: str, provider: str, stage1: Optional[str], stage2: Optional[str],
    variants: Optional[List[str]], case_meta: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]],
    warmup: int, runs: int, phase_runs: int, throughput_frames: int,
    throughput_warmup_frames: int, measurement_only: bool,
    quality_evidence_only: bool, native_split_quality_binding: str,
    full_only_quality_identity: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]],
    quality_evidence_model_id: str, quality_evidence_source_run_id: str,
    task_quality_gate: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]],
) -> bool:
    """Admit only the launcher-bound semantic CPU recipe, before any dispatch.

    This is a role check, not a new artifact identity. Normal CPU candidates
    retain all Generic candidate gates, including when their IDs resemble a
    reference. The child runner uses the same existing environment marker.
    """
    requested = str(os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_CPU_REFERENCE_ONLY", "")).strip().lower() in {
        "1", "true", "yes", "on",
    }
    meta = case_meta if isinstance(case_meta, Mapping) else {}
    row = meta.get("_run") if isinstance(meta.get("_run"), Mapping) else {}
    reference_row = str(row.get("id") or row.get("run_id") or "") == "management_cpu_reference"
    if not requested:
        if reference_row:
            raise RuntimeError("management_cpu_reference_context_missing:CPU_REFERENCE_ONLY")
        return False

    errors = []
    if not reference_row or run_id != "management_cpu_reference":
        errors.append("selected_run")
    if row.get("semantic_reference_only") is not True:
        errors.append("semantic_reference_only")
    if row.get("type") != "onnxruntime":
        errors.append("run_type")
    cpu_aliases = {"cpu", "ort_cpu", "onnxruntime_cpu", "cpu_ort"}
    cpu = lambda value: str(value or "").strip().lower() in cpu_aliases
    if not all(cpu(value) for value in (provider, stage1, stage2, row.get("provider"))):
        errors.append("cpu_providers")
    for key in ("stage1", "stage2"):
        stage = row.get(key)
        if not isinstance(stage, Mapping) or stage.get("type") != "onnxruntime" or not cpu(stage.get("provider")):
            errors.append(key)
    if variants != ["full"] or row.get("variants") != ["full"]:
        errors.append("full_variant")
    if measurement_only or quality_evidence_only or str(native_split_quality_binding or "").strip() or full_only_quality_identity:
        errors.append("candidate_or_measurement_binding")
    for key in ("measurement_only", "energy_measurement_only", "quality_evidence_only", "native_split_quality_binding"):
        if row.get(key):
            errors.append(key)
    for key in ("performance_eligible", "energy_eligible", "ranking_eligible", "pareto_eligible"):
        if row.get(key) is not False:
            errors.append(key)
    for key in ("include_in_latency_fps_energy", "include_in_ranking", "include_in_pareto"):
        if key in row and row.get(key) is not False:
            errors.append(key)
    for key, effective, required in (
        ("warmup", warmup, 0), ("runs", runs, 1),
        ("phase_runs", phase_runs, 0), ("throughput_frames", throughput_frames, 0),
        ("throughput_warmup_frames", throughput_warmup_frames, 0),
    ):
        if type(row.get(key)) is not int or row.get(key) != required or effective != required:
            errors.append(key)
    gate = task_quality_gate if isinstance(task_quality_gate, Mapping) else {}
    statistics = gate.get("statistics") if isinstance(gate.get("statistics"), Mapping) else {}
    location = str(statistics.get("execution_location") or gate.get("execution_location") or "local").strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
    if location in {"management", "management_node", "central", "central_cpu"}:
        location = "central_management"
    if location != "central_management" or row.get("execution_location") != "central_management":
        errors.append("execution_location")
    plan = meta.get("_management_reference_plan")
    plan = plan if isinstance(plan, Mapping) else {}
    binding = plan.get("management_cpu_reference")
    binding = binding if isinstance(binding, Mapping) else {}
    if binding.get("semantic_reference_only") is not True or binding.get("excluded_from_performance") is not True:
        errors.append("reference_plan")
    # Full ownership annotations are private dispatch additions. All original
    # selected recipe fields must still be exactly bound to both plan aliases.
    public_row = {key: value for key, value in row.items() if not key.startswith("_")}
    for alias in ("runs", "planned_runs"):
        planned = plan.get(alias)
        if not isinstance(planned, list) or len(planned) != 1 or not isinstance(planned[0], Mapping) or {
            key: value for key, value in planned[0].items() if not key.startswith("_")
        } != public_row:
            errors.append("selected_plan_binding:" + alias)
    model_id = str(meta.get("_suite_model_id") or "").strip()
    if not model_id or str(binding.get("model_id") or "").strip() != model_id or str(quality_evidence_model_id or "").strip() != model_id:
        errors.append("model_binding")
    if plan.get("model_id") and str(plan["model_id"]).strip() != model_id:
        errors.append("plan_model_binding")
    if quality_evidence_source_run_id != run_id:
        errors.append("source_run_binding")
    if errors:
        detail = ""
        if "model_binding" in errors or "plan_model_binding" in errors:
            detail = "; model_binding=" + json.dumps({
                "expected_model_id": str(binding.get("model_id") or "").strip(),
                "actual_suite_model_id": model_id,
                "actual_quality_evidence_model_id": str(quality_evidence_model_id or "").strip(),
                "actual_plan_model_id": str(plan.get("model_id") or "").strip(),
                "benchmark_set_identity": meta.get("_suite_model_identity", {}),
            }, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=True)
        raise RuntimeError("management_cpu_reference_context_invalid:" + ",".join(errors) + detail)
    return True


def _run_case(case_dir: Path, run_id: str, provider: str, image: str, preset: str, image_scale: str, warmup: int, runs: int, timeout_s: Optional[int], stage1: Optional[str] = None, stage2: Optional[str] = None, variants: Optional[List[str]] = None, case_meta: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, *, resume: bool = False, verbose_runs: bool = False, progress_every: int = 25, trt_cache_root: Optional[str] = None, trt_runtime: str = "native_preferred", native_trt_precision: str = "fp16", native_trt_workspace_mb: int = 4096, native_trt_build_timeout_s: int = 7200, native_trt_build: bool = True, boundary_mode: str = "canonical_float32", handoff_profile: str = "off", streaming_impl: str = "auto", throughput_frames: int = 24, throughput_warmup_frames: int = 6, throughput_queue_depth: int = 2, phase_runs: int = 5, validation_images: Optional[str] = None, validation_max_images: int = 0, validation_reference_mode: str = "auto", mini_coco_ap50: bool = False, benchmark_task: str = "auto", mini_classification_eval: bool = False, task_quality_gate: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, measurement_only: bool = False, quality_evidence_only: bool = False, quality_evidence_eval_id: str = "", quality_evidence_model_id: str = "", quality_evidence_setup_id: str = "", quality_evidence_source_run_id: str = "", quality_evidence_originating_plan_run_id: str = "", full_only_quality_identity: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None, native_trt_build_onnx_path: str = "", native_trt_engine_path: str = "", native_trt_build_receipt: str = "", native_split_quality_binding: str = "", native_split_quality_preparation_failure: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None, out_dir_override: Optional[Path] = None) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    management_reference_only = _management_cpu_reference_context(
        run_id=run_id, provider=provider, stage1=stage1, stage2=stage2,
        variants=variants, case_meta=case_meta, warmup=warmup, runs=runs,
        phase_runs=phase_runs, throughput_frames=throughput_frames,
        throughput_warmup_frames=throughput_warmup_frames,
        measurement_only=measurement_only, quality_evidence_only=quality_evidence_only,
        native_split_quality_binding=native_split_quality_binding,
        full_only_quality_identity=full_only_quality_identity,
        quality_evidence_model_id=quality_evidence_model_id,
        quality_evidence_source_run_id=quality_evidence_source_run_id,
        task_quality_gate=task_quality_gate,
    )
    if isinstance(native_split_quality_preparation_failure, Mapping) and native_split_quality_preparation_failure:
        failure = dict(native_split_quality_preparation_failure)
        failure_class = str(failure.get("exception_class") or "RuntimeError")
        failure_error = str(failure.get("error") or "native split-quality binding preparation failed")
        return {
            "schema_version": 2,
            "run_id": run_id,
            "provider": provider,
            "backend": provider,
            "case_id": case_dir.name,
            "case_dir": case_dir.name,
            "stage1_provider": stage1,
            "stage2_provider": stage2,
            "compile_ok": False,
            "runtime_ok": False,
            "latency_ok": False,
            "semantic_validation_ok": False,
            "validation_ok": False,
            "final_pass_all": False,
            "buildable": False,
            "runtime_executable": False,
            "contract_consistent": False,
            "task_valid": False,
            "accuracy_gate_pass": False,
            "accuracy_gate_source": "none",
            "accuracy_gate_reason": "native_split_quality_binding_materialization_failed",
            "eligible_for_ranking": False,
            "performance_eligible": False,
            "energy_eligible": False,
            "pareto_eligible": False,
            "claim_eligible": False,
            "counts_as_split_benchmark": True,
            "error_class": "native_split_quality_binding_materialization_failed",
            "error_detail": f"{failure_class}: {failure_error}",
            "variant_status": {
                "full": "missing",
                "part1": "not_measured",
                "part2": "error",
                "composed": "error",
            },
            "native_split_quality_preparation_failure": failure,
            "native_split_quality_preparation_failure_path": str(
                failure.get("failure_path") or ""
            ),
        }
    # Each case contains run_split_onnxruntime.py
    runner = case_dir / "run_split_onnxruntime.py"
    if not runner.exists():
        print(f"[warn] missing runner: {runner}")
        return None
    try:
        # The generated runner is larger than 50 KiB; the argparse block lives
        # near the end.  The old prefix-only probe therefore falsely reported
        # that current runners lacked --phase-runs even after a successful
        # suite refresh.
        _runner_probe = runner.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
        runner_supports_phase_runs = "--phase-runs" in _runner_probe
    except Exception:
        runner_supports_phase_runs = False

    if _cache_verify_only():
        # Do not trust plan/default arguments to carry the final build policy;
        # this is the last suite-side decision before the child command exists.
        native_trt_build = False
        uses_trt = any(
            str(token or "").strip().lower() in {
                "tensorrt", "trt", "ort_tensorrt",
            }
            for token in (provider, stage1, stage2)
        )
        if uses_trt and str(trt_runtime or "").strip().lower() == "ort":
            raise _cache_verify_block(
                compiler="onnxruntime_tensorrt_ep",
                artifact=f"case:{case_dir.name}:run:{run_id}",
            )

    stage2_gate = _stage2_accelerator_gate(case_dir, stage1, stage2, variants, case_meta)
    if isinstance(stage2_gate, dict) and stage2_gate.get("required") and not stage2_gate.get("available"):
        blocked = {str(v).strip().lower() for v in (stage2_gate.get("blocked_variants") or ["part2", "composed"])}
        variants_keep = [str(v) for v in (variants or []) if str(v).strip().lower() not in blocked]
        # For heterogeneous Stage2-accelerator runs, part1-only or full-only
        # leftovers are diagnostics, not the requested split benchmark.  Report a
        # structured pending row instead of running TensorRT part1 and marking
        # final_pass=True, which was misleading for TensorRT->DeepX/Hailo.
        if not _stage2_gate_is_same_backend_full_only(stage1, stage2, variants):
            print(f"[gate] {case_dir.name}/{run_id}: Stage2 accelerator variants {sorted(blocked)} are blocked; no complete split benchmark remains")
            return _requires_feature_tensor_calibration_row(run_id, provider, stage1, stage2, case_dir, stage2_gate)
        if not variants_keep:
            print(f"[gate] {case_dir.name}/{run_id}: Stage2 accelerator requires feature-tensor calibration; no safe variants remain")
            return _requires_feature_tensor_calibration_row(run_id, provider, stage1, stage2, case_dir, stage2_gate)
        print(f"[gate] {case_dir.name}/{run_id}: removing Stage2 accelerator variants {sorted(blocked)} until ActivationCalibrationCache exists; keeping {variants_keep}")
        variants = variants_keep

    provider_reason = _provider_unavailable_reason(
        _native_trt_ort_preflight_tokens(_provider_tokens_from_stage(provider, stage1, stage2), trt_runtime)
    )
    if provider_reason is not None:
        print(
            f"[warn] provider unavailable for {case_dir.name}/{run_id}: "
            f"missing={provider_reason.get('missing_execution_providers')} "
            f"available={provider_reason.get('available_providers')}"
        )
        return _provider_unavailable_row(run_id, provider, provider_reason, stage1=stage1, stage2=stage2, case_id=case_dir.name)

    out_tag = _slug(run_id)
    out_dir_name = str(Path(out_dir_override).resolve()) if out_dir_override is not None else f"results_{out_tag}"

    if resume and not quality_evidence_only:
        existing = _collect_case_result(
            case_dir,
            out_dir_name,
            run_id,
            provider,
            image_scale,
            stage1=stage1,
            stage2=stage2,
            variants=variants,
            case_meta=case_meta,
            runner_rc=0,
            stage2_gate=stage2_gate,
        )
        if existing is not None:
            existing.setdefault("_resumed", True)
            print(f"[resume] {case_dir.name}: using existing results from {out_dir_name}/validation_report.json")
            return existing

    provider_for_cmd = provider
    try:
        _vset_for_cmd = {str(v).strip().lower() for v in (variants or [])}
        # If a Stage2 accelerator run was downgraded to part1-only by the gate,
        # use the Stage1 backend token for the per-case runner.  Otherwise a
        # TensorRT→DeepX row with only part1 left would still call the runner with
        # --provider deepx_m1, which is misleading and older generated runners
        # rejected it outright.
        if _vset_for_cmd and _vset_for_cmd.issubset({"part1"}) and stage1:
            provider_for_cmd = str(stage1)
    except Exception:
        provider_for_cmd = provider

    cmd = [
        sys.executable,
        str(runner),
        "--provider",
        provider_for_cmd,
        "--image-scale",
        image_scale,
        "--warmup",
        str(warmup),
        "--runs",
        str(runs),
        "--out-dir",
        out_dir_name,
        "--throughput-frames",
        str(max(0, int(throughput_frames))),
        "--throughput-warmup-frames",
        str(max(0, int(throughput_warmup_frames))),
        "--throughput-queue-depth",
        str(max(1, int(throughput_queue_depth))),
    ]
    if str(quality_evidence_model_id or "").strip():
        # This is the suite's ordinary model identity as well as the quality
        # identity.  Supplying it on every invocation prevents numbered remote
        # attempt directories (for example ``.../1/suite``) from being
        # misidentified as the model by Full raw-head postprocessing.
        cmd += ["--model-id", str(quality_evidence_model_id).strip()]
    if isinstance(full_only_quality_identity, Mapping):
        cmd += [
            "--full-only-quality-identity-json",
            json.dumps(
                dict(full_only_quality_identity),
                ensure_ascii=False,
                sort_keys=True,
                separators=(",", ":"),
            ),
        ]
    quality_gate_payload = (
        dict(task_quality_gate)
        if isinstance(task_quality_gate, Mapping) else {}
    )
    quality_statistics = (
        dict(quality_gate_payload.get("statistics") or {})
        if isinstance(quality_gate_payload.get("statistics"), Mapping)
        else {}
    )
    quality_location = str(
        quality_statistics.get("execution_location")
        or quality_gate_payload.get("execution_location") or "local"
    ).strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
    if quality_location in {
        "management", "management_node", "central", "central_cpu",
    }:
        quality_location = "central_management"
    generic_composed_identity_required = bool(
        not management_reference_only
        and not quality_evidence_only
        and not str(native_split_quality_binding or "").strip()
        and not measurement_only
        and quality_location == "central_management"
        and "composed" in {
            str(value or "").strip().lower() for value in (variants or [])
        }
    )
    generic_full_identity_required = bool(
        not management_reference_only
        and not quality_evidence_only
        and not str(native_split_quality_binding or "").strip()
        and not measurement_only
        and quality_location == "central_management"
        and "full" in {
            str(value or "").strip().lower() for value in (variants or [])
        }
    )
    if generic_composed_identity_required or generic_full_identity_required:
        generic_identity = {
            "quality-evidence-eval-id": str(
                quality_evidence_eval_id or ""
            ).strip(),
            "quality-evidence-model-id": str(
                quality_evidence_model_id or ""
            ).strip(),
            "quality-evidence-setup-id": str(
                quality_evidence_setup_id or ""
            ).strip(),
            "quality-evidence-source-run-id": str(
                quality_evidence_source_run_id or ""
            ).strip(),
        }
        missing_generic_identity = [
            name for name, value in generic_identity.items() if not value
        ]
        if missing_generic_identity:
            raise RuntimeError(
                ("generic composed central-quality dispatch identity is "
                 if generic_composed_identity_required
                 else "generic Full central-quality dispatch identity is ")
                +
                "incomplete: " + ",".join(missing_generic_identity)
            )
        for option_name, option_value in generic_identity.items():
            cmd += ["--" + option_name, option_value]
    elif not quality_evidence_only and not str(native_split_quality_binding or "").strip():
        # Preserve the ordinary dispatch identity also for measurement-only
        # and local Generic Full runs.  This does not enable quality-only mode.
        for option_name, option_value in (
            ("quality-evidence-eval-id", quality_evidence_eval_id),
            ("quality-evidence-model-id", quality_evidence_model_id),
            ("quality-evidence-setup-id", quality_evidence_setup_id),
            ("quality-evidence-source-run-id", quality_evidence_source_run_id),
        ):
            if str(option_value or "").strip():
                cmd += ["--" + option_name, str(option_value).strip()]
    if quality_evidence_only:
        cmd += [
            "--quality-evidence-only",
            "--quality-evidence-eval-id", str(quality_evidence_eval_id),
            "--quality-evidence-model-id", str(quality_evidence_model_id),
            "--quality-evidence-setup-id", str(quality_evidence_setup_id),
            "--quality-evidence-source-run-id", str(quality_evidence_source_run_id),
            "--quality-evidence-originating-plan-run-id", str(
                quality_evidence_originating_plan_run_id
            ),
            "--native-trt-build-onnx-path", str(native_trt_build_onnx_path),
            "--native-trt-engine-path", str(native_trt_engine_path),
            "--native-trt-build-receipt", str(native_trt_build_receipt),
        ]
    if str(native_split_quality_binding or "").strip():
        cmd += [
            "--native-split-quality-binding",
            str(Path(native_split_quality_binding).expanduser().resolve()),
            "--quality-evidence-eval-id", str(quality_evidence_eval_id),
            "--quality-evidence-model-id", str(quality_evidence_model_id),
            "--quality-evidence-setup-id", str(quality_evidence_setup_id),
            "--quality-evidence-source-run-id", str(quality_evidence_source_run_id),
        ]
    if measurement_only:
        # Energy measurement windows must measure inference timing only.
        # Disable semantic dataset validation, report plots and visualization so
        # u.RECS does not include Top-K/AP50 computation, PNG/PDF generation, or
        # other report-time work.  Keep the latency/streaming benchmark loops.
        cmd += ["--validation-mode", "off", "--no-report-plots", "--viz", "none", "--energy-measurement-only"]
        validation_images = None
        validation_max_images = 0
        mini_coco_ap50 = False
        mini_classification_eval = False
        validation_reference_mode = "auto"
        phase_runs = 0
    if runner_supports_phase_runs:
        cmd += ["--phase-runs", str(max(0, int(phase_runs)))]
    elif int(phase_runs or 0) > 0:
        print(f"[warn] runner does not support --phase-runs yet: {runner.name}; v42 phase diagnostics will be skipped for this case")
    if variants:
        cmd += ["--variants", ",".join([str(v) for v in variants])]
    if verbose_runs:
        cmd += ["--verbose-runs"]
    if stage1:
        cmd += ["--stage1-provider", str(stage1)]
    if stage2:
        cmd += ["--stage2-provider", str(stage2)]
    task_norm = str(benchmark_task or "auto").strip().lower() or "auto"
    val_img: Optional[Path] = None
    default_val = (case_dir.parent / "resources" / "validation" / "detection" / "coco_50_data").resolve()
    if validation_images:
        val_img = _resolve_semantic_validation_source(str(validation_images), case_dir.parent, task_norm)
        if val_img is None:
            # benchmark_plan.json lives at suite root; resolve relative semantic
            # validation paths against the suite root, not the case directory.
            raw_val = Path(str(validation_images))
            val_img_dbg = raw_val if raw_val.is_absolute() else (case_dir.parent / raw_val).resolve()
            if task_norm == "classification":
                print(f"[warn] validation_images={val_img_dbg} does not look like a labeled classification validation source; disabling dataset validation")
                val_img = None
                validation_max_images = 0
            else:
                print(f"[warn] validation_images={val_img_dbg} does not look like a semantic validation source; falling back to prepared COCO-50")
                val_img = default_val if default_val.exists() else None
                if val_img is not None and int(validation_max_images or 0) <= 0:
                    validation_max_images = 50
    else:
        if task_norm != "classification" and default_val.exists():
            val_img = default_val
            if int(validation_max_images or 0) <= 0:
                validation_max_images = 50
    if (not measurement_only) and val_img is not None:
        cmd += ["--validation-images", str(val_img)]
    if (not measurement_only) and int(validation_max_images or 0) > 0:
        cmd += ["--validation-max-images", str(int(validation_max_images))]
    if str(validation_reference_mode or "").strip():
        cmd += ["--validation-reference-mode", str(validation_reference_mode)]
    if str(benchmark_task or "").strip():
        cmd += ["--benchmark-task", str(benchmark_task)]
    if bool(mini_coco_ap50):
        cmd += ["--mini-coco-ap50"]
    if bool(mini_classification_eval):
        cmd += ["--mini-classification-eval"]
    if (not measurement_only) and isinstance(task_quality_gate, dict):
        cmd += ["--quality-gate-json", json.dumps(task_quality_gate, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))]

    def _uses_tensorrt_token(token: Optional[str]) -> bool:
        return str(token or "").strip().lower() in {"tensorrt", "trt"}

    if trt_cache_root and any(_uses_tensorrt_token(tok) for tok in (provider, stage1, stage2)):
        cache_root = Path(str(trt_cache_root))
        if not cache_root.is_absolute():
            cache_root = (case_dir.parent / cache_root).resolve()
        cache_dir = cache_root / "splits" / case_dir.name
        cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        # Split engines remain case-bound.  Full-model engines are model-bound
        # and must therefore use the stable model namespace directly: changing
        # b132 to b045 must not create another identical Full engine.
        cmd += [
            "--trt-cache-dir", str(cache_dir),
            "--trt-full-cache-dir", str(cache_root),
        ]

    # v59ac: native TensorRT execution path for TensorRT full / Stage2.
    if any(_uses_tensorrt_token(tok) for tok in (provider, stage1, stage2)):
        cmd += ["--trt-runtime", str(trt_runtime or "native_preferred")]
        cmd += ["--native-trt-precision", str(native_trt_precision or "fp16")]
        cmd += ["--native-trt-workspace-mb", str(int(native_trt_workspace_mb or 4096))]
        cmd += ["--native-trt-build-timeout-s", str(int(native_trt_build_timeout_s or 7200))]
        cmd += ["--boundary-mode", str(boundary_mode or "canonical_float32")]
        cmd += ["--handoff-profile", str(handoff_profile or "off")]
        cmd += ["--streaming-impl", str(streaming_impl or "auto")]
        if bool(native_trt_build):
            cmd += ["--native-trt-build"]
        else:
            cmd += ["--no-native-trt-build"]

    # Visual verification: if no explicit image was selected, use one
    # deterministic sample from the semantic validation source.  The per-case
    # runner then writes classification_*.png or detections_*.png overlays.
    image_arg = str(image or "default")
    if measurement_only:
        # Keep default synthetic/auto input.  Do not spend energy-window time
        # locating/copying visual verification images.
        pass
    elif image_arg.lower() != "default":
        cmd += ["--image", image_arg]
    else:
        visual_sample = _select_visual_sample_image(
            val_img,
            case_dir.parent,
            task_norm,
            seed_key=f"{case_dir.name}:{run_id}:{provider}:{stage1 or ''}:{stage2 or ''}",
        )
        if visual_sample is not None:
            cmd += ["--image", str(visual_sample)]
            print(f"[viz] {case_dir.name}/{run_id}: visual sample={visual_sample}")
    rc = 0
    try:
        env = os.environ.copy()
        env["ONNX_SPLITPOINT_TRT_BUILD_CASE"] = case_dir.name
        evidence_json = _stage2_calibration_evidence_env(stage2_gate)
        if evidence_json:
            env["ONNX_SPLITPOINT_STAGE2_CALIBRATION_EVIDENCE_JSON"] = evidence_json
        res = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=str(case_dir), timeout=timeout_s, env=env)
        rc = int(getattr(res, "returncode", 0) or 0)
        if rc != 0:
            print(f"[warn] case failed: {case_dir.name} (rc={rc})")
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        print(f"[warn] timeout: {case_dir.name} (>{timeout_s}s)")
        return None

    if quality_evidence_only:
        quality_report_path = Path(out_dir_name) / "quality_evidence_report.json"
        if rc != 0 or not quality_report_path.is_file():
            print(
                f"[quality-first][error] missing quality evidence for {case_dir.name}/{run_id}: "
                f"rc={rc} report={quality_report_path}"
            )
            return None
        try:
            quality_report = _read_json(quality_report_path)
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[quality-first][error] invalid report: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
            return None
        if (
            not isinstance(quality_report, dict)
            or quality_report.get("quality_evidence_only") is not True
            or quality_report.get("performance_claims_emitted") is not False
        ):
            print("[quality-first][error] report is not a quality-only evidence report")
            return None
        forbidden_claim_keys = {
            "fps", "fps_makespan", "latency", "latency_mean_ms",
            "latency_p50_ms", "latency_p95_ms", "throughput",
            "measured_makespan_s", "duration_s", "completed_work_units",
            "performance_row", "performance_rows",
        }
        def _contains_performance_claim(value: Any) -> bool:
            if isinstance(value, Mapping):
                for key, item in value.items():
                    token = str(key).strip().lower()
                    if token in forbidden_claim_keys:
                        return True
                    if _contains_performance_claim(item):
                        return True
            elif isinstance(value, list):
                return any(_contains_performance_claim(item) for item in value)
            return False
        if _contains_performance_claim(quality_report):
            print("[quality-first][error] quality report contains performance fields")
            return None
        return quality_report

    return _collect_case_result(
        case_dir,
        out_dir_name,
        run_id,
        provider,
        image_scale,
        stage1=stage1,
        stage2=stage2,
        variants=variants,
        case_meta=case_meta,
        runner_rc=rc,
        stage2_gate=stage2_gate,
    )


def _canonical_full_onnx_for_native(root: Path) -> Optional[Path]:
    """Mirror native_trt_from_benchmarkset.py's canonical Full-ONNX choice."""
    models = Path(root) / "models"
    if models.is_dir():
        candidates = sorted(models.glob("*.onnx"))
        if candidates:
            return candidates[0].resolve()
    candidates = [
        path for path in sorted(Path(root).glob("*.onnx"))
        if "part" not in path.name.lower()
    ]
    return candidates[0].resolve() if candidates else None


def _prepare_native_split_quality_for_case(
    *, root: Path, case_dir: Path, bench: Mapping[str, Any],
    plan: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]], run: Mapping[str, Any], args: Any,
    run_id: str, stage1: str, stage2: str,
    variants: Optional[Sequence[str]],
) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Build and seal the exact vendor-Part1 -> TRT-Part2 quality producer.

    This is a live call-site, not a policy lookup.  It executes before the
    composed semantic candidate and forces that candidate to consume the newly
    sealed engine with build/fallback disabled.
    """
    stage1_token = str(stage1 or "").strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
    stage2_token = str(stage2 or "").strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
    vendor_tokens = {
        "hailo8", "hailo8l", "hailo8r",
        "hailo10", "hailo10h", "hailo10n", "hailo10p",
        "deepx", "deepx_m1", "dx_m1",
    }
    variant_tokens = {str(value).strip().lower() for value in list(variants or [])}
    relevant = bool(
        stage1_token in vendor_tokens
        and stage2_token in {"tensorrt", "trt"}
        and (not variant_tokens or "composed" in variant_tokens)
    )
    if not relevant:
        return None
    selection_container = plan or {}
    if "native_split_quality_selection" in selection_container:
        raw_selection = selection_container.get(
            "native_split_quality_selection"
        )
        if not isinstance(raw_selection, Mapping):
            raise RuntimeError("native_split_quality_selection_invalid")
        selection = dict(raw_selection)
        raw_backends = selection.get("split_backends")
        source = str(
            selection.get("split_selection_source") or ""
        ).strip()
        applicable = selection.get("applicable")
        try:
            selection_version = int(selection.get("schema_version") or 0)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            selection_version = 0
        if (
            selection.get("schema")
            != "onnx-splitpoint/native-split-quality-selection"
            or selection_version != 1
            or not isinstance(applicable, bool)
            or not isinstance(raw_backends, list)
            or not source
        ):
            raise RuntimeError("native_split_quality_selection_invalid")

        aliases = {
            "hailo8": "hailo8", "hailo8l": "hailo8",
            "hailo8r": "hailo8",
            "hailo10": "hailo10h", "hailo10h": "hailo10h",
            "hailo10n": "hailo10h", "hailo10p": "hailo10h",
            "deepx": "deepx", "deepx_m1": "deepx",
            "dx_m1": "deepx",
        }
        selected_backends = [
            aliases.get(str(value or "").strip().lower().replace("-", "_"), "")
            for value in raw_backends
        ]
        if (
            any(not value for value in selected_backends)
            or len(selected_backends) != len(set(selected_backends))
            or bool(selected_backends) != applicable
            or (
                source == "native_disabled"
                and (applicable or selected_backends)
            )
        ):
            raise RuntimeError("native_split_quality_selection_invalid")
        if aliases.get(stage1_token, "") not in selected_backends:
            return None
    if bool(getattr(args, "energy_measurement_only", False)):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "native_split_quality_binding_must_be_replayed_from_native_command_for_energy"
        )
    eval_run_id = str(getattr(args, "quality_evidence_eval_id", "") or "").strip()
    setup_id = str(getattr(args, "quality_evidence_setup_id", "") or "").strip()
    model_id = str(
        getattr(args, "quality_evidence_model_id", "")
        or bench.get("model_id") or bench.get("model")
        or (plan or {}).get("model_id") or ""
    ).strip()
    cache_root_text = str(getattr(args, "trt_cache_root", "") or "").strip()
    cache_root = Path(cache_root_text).expanduser()
    if not eval_run_id or not setup_id or not model_id:
        raise RuntimeError("native_split_quality_identity_incomplete")
    if not cache_root_text or not cache_root.is_absolute():
        raise RuntimeError("native_split_quality_requires_absolute_persistent_trt_cache_root")
    from splitpoint_runners.native_split_quality_runtime import (
        prepare_native_split_quality_binding,
    )

    output_path = (
        case_dir / f"results_{_slug(run_id)}" / "task_quality_inputs"
        / "native_split_quality_binding.json"
    )
    try:
        prepared = prepare_native_split_quality_binding(
            benchmark_set=root,
            case_id=case_dir.name,
            model_id=model_id,
            setup_id=setup_id,
            backend=stage1_token,
            eval_run_id=eval_run_id,
            source_run_id=run_id,
            cache_root=cache_root,
            output_path=output_path,
            workspace_mb=int(getattr(args, "native_trt_workspace_mb", 4096) or 4096),
            timeout_s=int(getattr(args, "native_trt_build_timeout_s", 7200) or 7200),
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        error = str(exc)
        if not error.startswith("native_split_quality_boundary_layout_"):
            raise
        failure_path = output_path.with_name(
            "native_split_quality_preparation_failure.json"
        )
        failure = {
            "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/native-split-quality-preparation-failure",
            "schema_version": 1,
            "model_id": model_id,
            "case_id": case_dir.name,
            "setup_id": setup_id,
            "source_run_id": run_id,
            "stage1": stage1_token,
            "stage2": stage2_token,
            "reason": "boundary_layout_resolution_failed",
            "exception_class": type(exc).__name__,
            "error": error,
            "generic_execution_continues": False,
            "suite_execution_continues": True,
            "claim_eligible": False,
            "failure_path": str(failure_path),
        }
        _write_json(failure_path, failure)
        print(
            "[quality-first][split][failure] "
            f"setup={setup_id} model={model_id} case={case_dir.name} "
            f"run={run_id} reason={error}; case has no claim; suite continues"
        )
        return {
            "status": "technical_failure",
            "failure": failure,
            "failure_path": str(failure_path),
        }
    except RuntimeError as exc:
        error = str(exc)
        if not error.startswith("native_split_quality_part2_input_count:"):
            raise
        skip_path = output_path.with_name(
            "native_split_quality_capability_skip.json"
        )
        _write_json(skip_path, {
            "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/native-split-quality-capability-skip",
            "schema_version": 1,
            "model_id": model_id,
            "case_id": case_dir.name,
            "setup_id": setup_id,
            "source_run_id": run_id,
            "reason": "part2_input_count_not_one",
            "error": error,
            "generic_execution_continues": True,
        })
        print(
            "[quality-first][split][skip] "
            f"setup={setup_id} model={model_id} case={case_dir.name} "
            f"run={run_id} reason={error}; Generic execution continues"
        )
        return None
    print(
        "[quality-first][split] "
        f"setup={setup_id} model={model_id} case={case_dir.name} "
        f"run={run_id} precision={prepared['precision']} "
        f"binding={prepared['binding']['binding_sha256']}"
    )
    return prepared


def _native_full_trt_quality_endpoint_id(
    run: Mapping[str, Any], *, args: Any,
) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    """Resolve one concrete endpoint before any plan row is executed.

    Explicit Full-only canaries carry setup-indexed identities in the plan.
    Standard/Quality runs receive the same immutable endpoint from the
    management-side setup dispatcher.  Both sources may be present, but they
    must agree exactly.
    """

    setup_id = str(
        getattr(args, "quality_evidence_setup_id", "") or ""
    ).strip()
    endpoint_ids = [
        str(value or "").strip()
        for value in list(run.get("quality_canary_endpoint_ids") or [])
        if str(value or "").strip()
    ]
    declared_endpoint_setups = [
        str(value or "").strip()
        for value in list(run.get("quality_canary_setup_ids") or [])
        if str(value or "").strip()
    ]
    if declared_endpoint_setups:
        if len(declared_endpoint_setups) != len(endpoint_ids):
            return "", "plan_endpoint_setup_cardinality_mismatch"
        plan_ids = [
            endpoint_id
            for endpoint_id, endpoint_setup in zip(
                endpoint_ids, declared_endpoint_setups
            )
            if endpoint_setup == setup_id
        ]
    else:
        plan_ids = endpoint_ids if len(endpoint_ids) == 1 else []
    plan_ids = list(dict.fromkeys(plan_ids))
    if len(plan_ids) > 1:
        return "", "plan_endpoint_identity_count_not_one"

    scheduler_id = str(
        getattr(args, "quality_evidence_endpoint_id", "") or ""
    ).strip()
    plan_id = plan_ids[0] if plan_ids else ""
    if scheduler_id and plan_id and scheduler_id != plan_id:
        return "", "scheduler_plan_endpoint_identity_mismatch"
    resolved = scheduler_id or plan_id
    if not resolved:
        return "", "setup_endpoint_identity_missing"
    return resolved, ""


def _bind_native_full_trt_quality_companion(
    runs: List[Dict[str, Any]], *, args: Any,
    quality_only_run_ids: set[str],
) -> Tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], List[str]]:
    """Bind or reject the companion before the first provider executes."""

    prepared = [dict(run or {}) for run in list(runs or [])]
    candidates = [
        run for run in prepared
        if run.get("_native_full_trt_quality_companion") is True
    ]
    identity_values = (
        str(getattr(args, "quality_evidence_eval_id", "") or "").strip(),
        str(getattr(args, "quality_evidence_model_id", "") or "").strip(),
        str(getattr(args, "quality_evidence_setup_id", "") or "").strip(),
    )
    endpoint_value = str(
        getattr(args, "quality_evidence_endpoint_id", "") or ""
    ).strip()
    quality_only_trt = any(
        str(value or "").strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
        in {"ort_tensorrt", "tensorrt", "trt"}
        for value in quality_only_run_ids
    )
    plan_endpoint_authority = any(
        any(
            str(value or "").strip()
            for value in list(run.get("quality_canary_endpoint_ids") or [])
        )
        for run in candidates
    )
    requested = bool(
        endpoint_value
        or quality_only_trt
        or plan_endpoint_authority
    )
    if not requested:
        # Manual benchmark-suite use remains a normal performance run.
        for run in candidates:
            run["_native_full_trt_quality_companion"] = False
        return prepared, []
    errors: List[str] = []
    if not all(identity_values):
