        errors.append("physical_quality_identity_incomplete")
    if len(candidates) != 1:
        errors.append(
            f"native_full_tensorrt_companion_recipe_count_not_one:"
            f"{len(candidates)}"
        )
    if errors:
        return prepared, errors
    endpoint_id, endpoint_error = _native_full_trt_quality_endpoint_id(
        candidates[0], args=args,
    )
    if endpoint_error:
        errors.append(endpoint_error)
    else:
        candidates[0]["_native_full_trt_quality_companion_id"] = endpoint_id
    return prepared, errors


def _run_native_full_trt_quality_companion(
    *, root: Path, bench: Mapping[str, Any], plan: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]],
    run: Mapping[str, Any], run_cases: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]], args: Any,
) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Create setup-local Full TRT quality evidence before Native performance.

    The companion deliberately returns a quality report, not a benchmark row.
    It owns exactly one canonical engine/receipt per model and physical setup.
    """
    if run.get("_native_full_trt_quality_companion") is not True:
        return {}
    eval_run_id = str(getattr(args, "quality_evidence_eval_id", "") or "").strip()
    setup_id = str(getattr(args, "quality_evidence_setup_id", "") or "").strip()
    model_id = str(
        getattr(args, "quality_evidence_model_id", "")
        or bench.get("model_id") or bench.get("model")
        or (plan or {}).get("model_id") or ""
    ).strip()
    run_id = str(run.get("id") or run.get("name") or "").strip()
    if not all((eval_run_id, model_id, setup_id, run_id)):
        print(
            "[quality-first][error] physical Quality identity is incomplete: "
            f"eval_run_id={eval_run_id!r} model_id={model_id!r} "
            f"setup_id={setup_id!r} source_run_id={run_id!r}"
        )
        return None
    companion_id = str(
        run.get("_native_full_trt_quality_companion_id") or ""
    ).strip()
    if not companion_id:
        companion_id, endpoint_error = (
            _native_full_trt_quality_endpoint_id(run, args=args)
        )
    else:
        endpoint_error = ""
    if endpoint_error or not companion_id:
        print(
            f"[quality-first][error] {run_id}: expected exactly one "
            "setup-local TensorRT quality-canary endpoint identity"
            + (f" ({endpoint_error})" if endpoint_error else "")
        )
        return None
    full_only_quality_identity = {
        "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/full-only-quality-request-identity",
        "schema_version": 1,
        "quality_canary_id": companion_id,
        "eval_run_id": eval_run_id,
        "model_id": model_id,
        "setup_id": setup_id,
        "source_run_id": "native_full_tensorrt",
        "backend": "tensorrt",
        "variant": "full",
        "execution_role": "full_quality_only",
        "performance_claims_emitted": False,
    }
    if not run_cases:
        print(f"[quality-first][error] run {run_id!r} selected no deterministic case container")
        return None
    canonical_full = _canonical_full_onnx_for_native(root)
    if canonical_full is None or not canonical_full.is_file():
        print("[quality-first][error] canonical benchmark-set Full ONNX is missing")
        return None
    cache_root_text = str(getattr(args, "trt_cache_root", "") or "").strip()
    cache_root = Path(cache_root_text).expanduser()
    if not cache_root_text or not cache_root.is_absolute():
        print(
            "[quality-first][error] --trt-cache-root must be an absolute persistent "
            "host path; suite-local caches are deleted before Native Full"
        )
        return None
    cache_root = cache_root.resolve()
    source_sha256 = _v60_file_sha256(canonical_full)
    source_digest = source_sha256.split(":", 1)[-1]
    precision = str(getattr(args, "native_trt_precision", "fp16") or "fp16").strip().lower()
    if precision in {"uint8_cast_fp16", "uint8_dequant_fp16", "float32_layout_fp16"}:
        precision = "fp16"

    # Use the exact model-bound NativeTRT Full namespace consumed by Generic.
    # Quality executes first and seals the engine; every split of this model
    # then observes the same cache entry instead of rebuilding Full per case.
    case = dict(run_cases[0])
    boundary = case.get("boundary")
    case_dir_key = case.get("case_dir") or case.get("folder")
    if case_dir_key is None:
        case_dir_key = f"b{int(boundary):03d}" if boundary is not None else "case"
    case_dir = (root / Path(str(case_dir_key))).resolve()
    engine_dir = cache_root / "full" / source_digest / precision
    cache_verify_only = _cache_verify_only() or _trt_build_forbidden("full", case_id=case_dir.name)
    if not cache_verify_only:
        engine_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    persistent_build_onnx = engine_dir / "source.onnx"
    if persistent_build_onnx.exists():
        if (
            not persistent_build_onnx.is_file()
            or _v60_file_sha256(persistent_build_onnx) != source_sha256
        ):
            if cache_verify_only:
                raise _cache_verify_block(
                    compiler="trtexec",
                    artifact="native_full_quality_source_onnx_conflict",
                )
            print("[quality-first][error] persistent build-ONNX cache entry is inconsistent")
            return None
    else:
        if cache_verify_only:
            raise _cache_verify_block(
                compiler="trtexec",
                artifact="native_full_quality_source_onnx",
            )
        temporary_onnx = persistent_build_onnx.with_suffix(".onnx.tmp")
        try:
            shutil.copy2(canonical_full, temporary_onnx)
            if _v60_file_sha256(temporary_onnx) != source_sha256:
                raise RuntimeError("copied build ONNX SHA-256 mismatch")
            temporary_onnx.replace(persistent_build_onnx)
        except Exception as exc:
            try:
                temporary_onnx.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            except Exception:
                pass
            print(
                f"[quality-first][error] cannot materialize persistent build ONNX: "
                f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            )
            return None
    engine_path = engine_dir / f"full_{precision}.engine"
    receipt_path = engine_dir / "engine_build_receipt.json"
    if receipt_path.is_file() and not engine_path.is_file():
        if cache_verify_only:
            raise _cache_verify_block(
                compiler="trtexec",
                artifact="native_full_quality_receipt_without_engine",
            )
        print("[quality-first][error] persistent receipt exists without its engine")
        return None
    if cache_verify_only and not (
        engine_path.is_file() and receipt_path.is_file()
    ):
        raise _cache_verify_block(
            compiler="trtexec",
            artifact="native_full_quality_engine_and_receipt",
        )
    unsealed_engine_backup: Optional[Path] = None
    quality_out_dir = (
        root / "native_full_quality" / "quality_inputs" / setup_id
        / "results_native_full_tensorrt"
    ).resolve()

    cli_validation_images = str(getattr(args, "validation_images", "") or "").strip()
    validation_images = cli_validation_images or str(run.get("validation_images") or "").strip()
    validation_max_images = (
        int(getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0)
        if cli_validation_images else _effective_cli_validation_max(args, dict(run))
    )
    if (
        not bool(run.get("validation_budget_authoritative"))
        and int(getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0) > 0
    ):
        validation_max_images = int(getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0)
    benchmark_task = str(
        run.get("benchmark_task") or getattr(args, "benchmark_task", "auto") or "auto"
    ).strip().lower()
    quality_gate = (
        dict(run.get("task_quality_gate") or {})
        if isinstance(run.get("task_quality_gate"), Mapping)
        else dict((plan or {}).get("quality_gate") or {})
        if isinstance((plan or {}).get("quality_gate"), Mapping)
        else {}
    )
    if not validation_images or not quality_gate:
        print(
            f"[quality-first][error] {run_id}: frozen validation source or quality policy is missing"
        )
        return None
    print(
        f"[quality-first] setup={setup_id} model={model_id} source_run={run_id} "
        f"engine={engine_path}"
    )
    try:
        if engine_path.is_file() and not receipt_path.is_file():
            if cache_verify_only:
                raise _cache_verify_block(
                    compiler="trtexec",
                    artifact="native_full_quality_unsealed_engine",
                )
            # 2.70 Generic builds did not write the Quality-FIRST receipt.
            # Migrate only after every Quality prerequisite has been checked,
            # and keep the original recoverable across any rebuild failure.
            backup_candidate = engine_path.with_name(
                engine_path.name + ".pre_2_70a_unsealed"
            )
            if backup_candidate.exists():
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "unresolved unsealed-engine migration backup exists"
                )
            engine_path.replace(backup_candidate)
            unsealed_engine_backup = backup_candidate
        result = _run_case(
            case_dir,
            run_id="native_full_tensorrt_quality",
            provider="tensorrt",
            stage1="tensorrt",
            stage2="tensorrt",
            variants=["full"],
            image=str(getattr(args, "image", "default") or "default"),
            preset=str(getattr(args, "preset", "auto") or "auto"),
            image_scale=str(run.get("image_scale") or getattr(args, "image_scale", "auto") or "auto"),
            warmup=0,
            runs=1,
            phase_runs=0,
            timeout_s=getattr(args, "timeout", None),
            case_meta={**case, "_run": dict(run)},
            resume=False,
            verbose_runs=bool(getattr(args, "verbose_runs", False)),
            progress_every=int(getattr(args, "progress_every", 25) or 25),
            trt_cache_root=getattr(args, "trt_cache_root", None),
            throughput_frames=0,
            throughput_warmup_frames=0,
            throughput_queue_depth=1,
            validation_images=validation_images,
            validation_max_images=int(validation_max_images),
            validation_reference_mode=str(
                run.get("validation_reference_mode")
                or getattr(args, "validation_reference_mode", "auto") or "auto"
            ),
            benchmark_task=benchmark_task,
            task_quality_gate=quality_gate,
            trt_runtime="native",
            native_trt_precision=precision,
            native_trt_workspace_mb=int(getattr(args, "native_trt_workspace_mb", 4096) or 4096),
            native_trt_build_timeout_s=int(getattr(args, "native_trt_build_timeout_s", 7200) or 7200),
            native_trt_build=(
                bool(getattr(args, "native_trt_build", True))
                and not cache_verify_only
            ),
            quality_evidence_only=True,
            quality_evidence_eval_id=eval_run_id,
            quality_evidence_model_id=model_id,
            quality_evidence_setup_id=setup_id,
            quality_evidence_source_run_id="native_full_tensorrt",
            quality_evidence_originating_plan_run_id=run_id,
            full_only_quality_identity=full_only_quality_identity,
            native_trt_build_onnx_path=str(persistent_build_onnx),
            native_trt_engine_path=str(engine_path),
            native_trt_build_receipt=str(receipt_path),
            out_dir_override=quality_out_dir,
        )
        if result is None or not engine_path.is_file() or not receipt_path.is_file():
            raise RuntimeError("quality-first Full TensorRT engine/receipt build incomplete")
        quality_request = (
            result.get("quality_input_request")
            if isinstance(result, Mapping)
            and isinstance(result.get("quality_input_request"), Mapping)
            else None
        )
        request_descriptor = (
            quality_request.get("request")
            if isinstance(quality_request, Mapping)
            and isinstance(quality_request.get("request"), Mapping)
            else None
        )
        request_path = Path(str(
            request_descriptor.get("path") if isinstance(request_descriptor, Mapping)
            else ""
        )).expanduser()
        if not request_path.is_absolute():
            request_path = (quality_out_dir / request_path).resolve()
        request_payload = _read_json(request_path) if request_path.is_file() else None
        candidate_descriptor = (
            request_payload.get("candidate")
            if isinstance(request_payload, Mapping)
            and isinstance(request_payload.get("candidate"), Mapping)
            else None
        )
        candidate_path = (
            (request_path.parent / str(candidate_descriptor.get("path") or "")).resolve()
            if isinstance(candidate_descriptor, Mapping) else Path()
        )
        candidate_payload = (
            _read_json(candidate_path) if candidate_path.is_file() else None
        )
        identity_sha256 = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(
            full_only_quality_identity
        )

        def _sealed(payload: Any) -> bool:
            return bool(
                isinstance(payload, Mapping)
                and payload.get("full_only_plan_identity_required") is True
                and payload.get("full_only_plan_identity")
                == full_only_quality_identity
                and str(payload.get("full_only_plan_identity_sha256") or "")
                == identity_sha256
                and str(payload.get("quality_canary_id") or "")
                == companion_id
                and str(payload.get("eval_run_id") or "") == eval_run_id
            )

        if (
            not _sealed(quality_request)
            or not _sealed(request_payload)
            or not _sealed(candidate_payload)
            or not isinstance(candidate_descriptor, Mapping)
            or candidate_path.parent != request_path.parent
            or str(candidate_descriptor.get("sha256") or "").removeprefix(
                "sha256:"
            ) != _deepx_quality_file_sha256(candidate_path)
            or int(candidate_descriptor.get("size_bytes") or 0)
            != candidate_path.stat().st_size
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "quality-first Full TensorRT request/candidate lacks exact "
                "Full-only plan identity"
            )
        if unsealed_engine_backup is not None:
            unsealed_engine_backup.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        return result
    except Exception:
        if unsealed_engine_backup is not None and unsealed_engine_backup.is_file():
            try:
                if engine_path.exists():
                    engine_path.unlink()
                receipt_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                unsealed_engine_backup.replace(engine_path)
            except Exception:
                pass
        raise


def _claim_native_full_trt_quality_companion(
    seen: set[Tuple[str, str, str]],
    identity: Tuple[str, str, str],
) -> bool:
    """Claim the sole setup/model quality producer trigger for this process."""
    if identity in seen:
        return False
    seen.add(identity)
    return True


def _cleanup_full_quality_only_benchmark_artifacts(
    root: Path, run_id: str, *result_dirs: Path,
) -> List[str]:
    """Remove stale files that could impersonate a quality-only benchmark.

    Portable central-quality request/candidate files and semantic records are
    intentionally retained.  Timing reports, validation reports and root-level
    benchmark row files are forbidden for this dispatch, including after a
    failed or interrupted endpoint execution.
    """
    removed: List[str] = []
    root = Path(root).resolve()
    patterns = (
        f"benchmark_results_{_slug(run_id)}_*.json",
        f"benchmark_results_{_slug(run_id)}_*.csv",
        f"benchmark_results_{_slug(run_id)}_*.error.txt",
    )
    for pattern in patterns:
        for path in root.glob(pattern):
            if path.is_file():
                path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                removed.append(str(path))
    forbidden_names = {
        "validation_report.json", "validation_report.png",
        "validation_report.pdf", "deepx_prepared_feed_benchmark.json",
        "deepx_prepared_feed.completed_task_result_artifact.json",
        "run_model_stdout.txt", "run_model_stderr.txt",
    }
    for directory in result_dirs:
        base = Path(directory).resolve()
        try:
            base.relative_to(root)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        for name in forbidden_names:
            path = base / name
            if path.is_file():
                path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                removed.append(str(path))
    return removed


def _cleanup_incomplete_deepx_full_quality_outputs(
    results_dir: Path,
) -> List[str]:
    """Remove a prior or partially-written DeepX Full quality transaction."""
    removed: List[str] = []
    for path in (
        results_dir / "task_quality_inputs" / "full_request.json",
        results_dir / "task_quality_inputs" / "full_candidate.json",
        results_dir / "full_quality_evidence_report.json",
    ):
        if path.is_file():
            path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            removed.append(str(path))
    return removed


def _run_declared_full_quality_only_endpoint(
    *, root: Path, bench: Mapping[str, Any], plan: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]],
    run: Dict[str, Any], run_cases: List[Dict[str, Any]], args: Any,
) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Emit one Full central-quality request and no benchmark result row.

    TensorRT uses the stricter receipt-sealed helper above.  DeepX is dispatched
    directly through its DXNN/dx_engine Full implementation; it must never
    enter the generic per-case ORT runner.  Other vendor Full endpoints retain
    their normal semantic runner.  In every case the portable request/candidate
    files remain the only claim-bearing outputs.
    """

    run_id = str(run.get("id") or run.get("name") or "").strip()
    setup_id = str(getattr(args, "quality_evidence_setup_id", "") or "").strip()
    eval_run_id = str(getattr(args, "quality_evidence_eval_id", "") or "").strip()
    model_id = str(
        getattr(args, "quality_evidence_model_id", "")
        or bench.get("model_id") or bench.get("model")
        or (plan or {}).get("model_id") or ""
    ).strip()
    declared_setups = {
        str(value).strip() for value in list(
            run.get("quality_canary_setup_ids") or []
        ) if str(value).strip()
    }
    variants = _normalize_variants(run.get("variants"))
    if (
        not run_id or not setup_id or not eval_run_id or not model_id
        or variants != ["full"]
        or str(run.get("execution_scope") or "") != "full_only"
        or str(run.get("execution_role") or "") != "full_quality_only"
        or run.get("performance_claims_emitted") is not False
        or setup_id not in declared_setups
    ):
        print(
            f"[quality-first][error] {run_id or '<missing>'}: invalid "
            "declared Full-only endpoint identity"
        )
        return None
    if not run_cases:
        print(f"[quality-first][error] {run_id}: no deterministic Full container")
        return None
    run_type = str(run.get("type") or run.get("kind") or "onnxruntime").strip().lower()
    backend = _full_backend_for_run(run)
    if backend == "tensorrt" or run_type in {"matrix", "split", "mixed", "mixed_backend"}:
        print(f"[quality-first][error] {run_id}: vendor helper received non-vendor Full recipe")
        return None
    if run_type == "hailo":
        provider = str(run.get("hw_arch") or run.get("arch") or backend).strip()
    elif run_type in {"deepx", "deepx_m1", "dx_m1", "dxm1"}:
        provider = "deepx_m1"
    elif run_type in {"onnxruntime", "ort"}:
        provider = str(run.get("provider") or backend).strip().lower()
    elif run_type == "same_backend_reference":
        # Contract/profile projection may intentionally preserve the logical
        # profile type instead of the generator's executable type.  Admit it
        # only after the homogeneous backend/stage checks below: the provider
        # is derived from that sealed Full recipe, never from the label alone.
        if backend in {"hailo8", "hailo10"}:
            provider = str(
                run.get("hw_arch") or run.get("arch")
                or run.get("full") or backend
            ).strip()
        elif backend == "deepx_m1":
            provider = "deepx_m1"
        elif backend in {"cpu", "cuda_ort", "tensorrt"}:
            provider = str(run.get("provider") or backend).strip().lower()
        else:
            print(
                f"[quality-first][error] {run_id}: unsupported "
                f"same-backend Full endpoint={backend}"
            )
            return None
    else:
        print(f"[quality-first][error] {run_id}: unsupported Full endpoint type={run_type}")
        return None
    stage1 = _resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage1")) or provider
    stage2 = _resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage2")) or provider
    if (
        _canonical_full_backend(stage1) != backend
        or _canonical_full_backend(stage2) != backend
    ):
        print(f"[quality-first][error] {run_id}: heterogeneous Full endpoint")
        return None

    cli_validation_images = str(getattr(args, "validation_images", "") or "").strip()
    validation_images = cli_validation_images or str(run.get("validation_images") or "").strip()
    validation_max_images = (
        int(getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0)
        if cli_validation_images else _effective_cli_validation_max(args, dict(run))
    )
    if (
        not bool(run.get("validation_budget_authoritative"))
        and int(getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0) > 0
    ):
        validation_max_images = int(getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0)
    benchmark_task = str(
        run.get("benchmark_task") or getattr(args, "benchmark_task", "auto")
        or "auto"
    ).strip().lower()
    quality_gate = (
        dict(run.get("task_quality_gate") or {})
        if isinstance(run.get("task_quality_gate"), Mapping)
        else dict((plan or {}).get("quality_gate") or {})
        if isinstance((plan or {}).get("quality_gate"), Mapping)
        else {}
    )
    if not validation_images or not quality_gate:
        print(
            f"[quality-first][error] {run_id}: frozen validation source or "
            "quality policy is missing"
        )
        return None
    case = dict(run_cases[0])
    boundary = case.get("boundary")
    case_dir_key = case.get("case_dir") or case.get("folder")
    if case_dir_key is None:
        case_dir_key = f"b{int(boundary):03d}" if boundary is not None else "case"
    case_dir = (root / Path(str(case_dir_key))).resolve()
    out_dir = (case_dir / f"results_{_slug(run_id)}").resolve()
    endpoint_ids = [
        str(value or "").strip()
        for value in list(run.get("quality_canary_endpoint_ids") or [])
        if str(value or "").strip()
    ]
    full_only_quality_identity = {
        "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/full-only-quality-request-identity",
        "schema_version": 1,
        "quality_canary_id": endpoint_ids[0] if endpoint_ids else "",
        "eval_run_id": eval_run_id,
        "model_id": model_id,
        "setup_id": setup_id,
        "source_run_id": run_id,
        "backend": (
            "deepx_m1" if backend == "deepx_m1"
            else str(run.get("full") or provider)
        ),
        "variant": "full",
        "execution_role": "full_quality_only",
        "performance_claims_emitted": False,
    }

    if backend == "deepx_m1":
        if len(endpoint_ids) != 1:
            print(
                f"[quality-first][error] {run_id}: expected exactly one "
                "DeepX quality-canary endpoint identity"
            )
            return None
        # DeepX Full is model-level and owns a dedicated DXNN/dx_engine path.
        # Sending it through _run_case would invoke the generated ONNX Runtime
        # adapter and can silently produce a CPU result under a DeepX label.
        deepx_results_dir = (root / "results" / run_id).resolve()
        _cleanup_full_quality_only_benchmark_artifacts(
            root, run_id, deepx_results_dir, out_dir,
        )
        _cleanup_incomplete_deepx_full_quality_outputs(deepx_results_dir)
        try:
            deepx_run = dict(run)
            deepx_run.update({
                "model_id": model_id,
                "model": model_id,
                "model_suite": {"primary": model_id},
                "backend": "deepx_m1",
                "provider": "deepx_m1",
                "variant": "full",
                "variants": ["full"],
                "stage1": {"backend": "deepx_m1"},
                "stage2": {"backend": "deepx_m1"},
                "setup_id": setup_id,
                "validation_images": validation_images,
                "validation_max_images": int(validation_max_images),
                "benchmark_task": benchmark_task,
                "task_quality_gate": quality_gate,
            })
            deepx_identity = _deepx_verified_suite_run_identity(
                bench, deepx_run,
            )
            if deepx_identity != {
                "model_id": model_id,
                "backend": "deepx_m1",
                "variant": "full",
            }:
                raise RuntimeError("deepx_full_only_suite_identity_mismatch")
            deepx_args = argparse.Namespace(**vars(args))
            deepx_args.runs = 1
            deepx_args.warmup = 0
            deepx_args.throughput_frames = 0
            deepx_args.validation_images = validation_images
            deepx_args.validation_max_images = int(validation_max_images)
            deepx_args.benchmark_task = benchmark_task
            deepx_args.quality_evidence_eval_id = eval_run_id
            deepx_args.quality_evidence_model_id = model_id
            deepx_args.quality_evidence_setup_id = setup_id
            rows = _run_deepx_full_run(
                root, deepx_run, deepx_args,
                expected_endpoint_identity=deepx_identity,
                full_only_quality_identity=full_only_quality_identity,
            )
            row = rows[0] if len(rows) == 1 else {}
            requests = (
                row.get("task_quality_input_requests_by_variant")
                if isinstance(row, Mapping) else None
            )
            request = (
                requests.get("full") if isinstance(requests, Mapping)
                else None
            )
            declared_dxnn_text = str(
                deepx_run.get("dxnn_path")
                or deepx_run.get("artifact")
                or "deepx/deepx_m1/full/model.dxnn"
            ).strip()
            declared_dxnn = (
                Path(declared_dxnn_text).resolve()
                if os.path.isabs(declared_dxnn_text)
                else (root / declared_dxnn_text).resolve()
            )
            dxnn_path = Path(str(row.get("dxnn_path") or "")).resolve()
            expected_dxnn_sha = (
                _deepx_quality_file_sha256(dxnn_path)
                if dxnn_path.is_file() else ""
            )
            expected_precision = f"deepx_dxnn_sha256:{expected_dxnn_sha}"
            request_identity = (
                request.get("full_only_plan_identity")
                if isinstance(request, Mapping) else None
            )
            producer = (
                request.get("producer_identity")
                if isinstance(request, Mapping) else None
            )
            producer_model = (
                producer.get("model")
                if isinstance(producer, Mapping) else None
            )
            candidate_descriptor = (
                request.get("candidate")
                if isinstance(request, Mapping) else None
            )
            candidate_base = (
                deepx_results_dir / "task_quality_inputs"
            ).resolve()
            candidate_path = (
                (
                    candidate_base
                    / str(candidate_descriptor.get("path") or "")
                ).resolve()
                if isinstance(candidate_descriptor, Mapping) else Path()
            )
            candidate_payload = (
                _read_json(candidate_path)
                if candidate_path.is_file() else None
            )
            expected_plan_identity_sha = _deepx_quality_json_sha256(
                full_only_quality_identity
            )
            if (
                not isinstance(request, Mapping)
                or str(request.get("schema") or "")
                != "onnx-splitpoint/central-quality-evaluation-request"
                or int(request.get("record_count") or 0) < 1
                or request.get("performance_claims_emitted") is not False
                or request_identity != full_only_quality_identity
                or str(request.get("full_only_plan_identity_sha256") or "")
                != expected_plan_identity_sha
                or str(request.get("runtime_precision_identity") or "")
                != expected_precision
                or not isinstance(producer, Mapping)
                or str(producer.get("backend") or "") != "deepx_m1"
                or str(producer.get("runtime_precision_identity") or "")
                != expected_precision
                or not isinstance(producer_model, Mapping)
                or str(
                    producer_model.get("runtime_artifact_sha256") or ""
                ) != expected_dxnn_sha
                or dxnn_path != declared_dxnn
                or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", expected_dxnn_sha)
                or not isinstance(candidate_descriptor, Mapping)
                or candidate_path.parent != candidate_base
                or not candidate_path.is_file()
                or str(candidate_descriptor.get("sha256") or "")
                != _deepx_quality_file_sha256(candidate_path)
                or int(candidate_descriptor.get("size_bytes") or 0)
                != candidate_path.stat().st_size
                or not isinstance(candidate_payload, Mapping)
                or candidate_payload.get("performance_claims_emitted")
                is not False
                or candidate_payload.get("full_only_plan_identity")
                != full_only_quality_identity
                or str(
                    candidate_payload.get("runtime_precision_identity") or ""
                ) != expected_precision
                or str(
                    candidate_payload.get(
                        "full_only_plan_identity_sha256"
                    ) or ""
                ) != expected_plan_identity_sha
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "deepx_full_only_central_quality_request_invalid"
                )
            report = {
                "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/full-only-quality-evidence-report",
                "schema_version": 1,
                "quality_evidence_only": True,
                "performance_claims_emitted": False,
                "status": str(
                    request.get("status")
                    or "pending_central_evaluation"
                ),
                "eval_run_id": eval_run_id,
                "model_id": model_id,
                "setup_id": setup_id,
                "source_run_id": run_id,
                "variant": "full",
                "backend": "deepx_m1",
                "execution_role": "full_quality_only",
                "quality_ids": endpoint_ids,
                "record_count": int(request.get("record_count") or 0),
                "runtime_precision_identity": expected_precision,
                "request": dict(request),
            }
            deepx_results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            _write_json(
                deepx_results_dir / "full_quality_evidence_report.json",
                report,
            )
            return report
        except Exception as exc:
            _cleanup_incomplete_deepx_full_quality_outputs(
                deepx_results_dir,
            )
            print(
                f"[quality-first][error] {run_id}: DeepX DXNN Full "
                f"quality failed closed: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            )
            return None
        finally:
            _cleanup_full_quality_only_benchmark_artifacts(
                root, run_id, deepx_results_dir, out_dir,
            )

    result = _run_case(
        case_dir,
        run_id=run_id,
        provider=provider,
        stage1=stage1,
        stage2=stage2,
        variants=["full"],
        image=str(getattr(args, "image", "default") or "default"),
        preset=str(getattr(args, "preset", "auto") or "auto"),
        image_scale=str(
            run.get("image_scale") or getattr(args, "image_scale", "auto")
            or "auto"
        ),
        warmup=0,
        runs=1,
        phase_runs=0,
        timeout_s=getattr(args, "timeout", None),
        case_meta={**case, "_run": dict(run)},
        resume=False,
        verbose_runs=bool(getattr(args, "verbose_runs", False)),
        progress_every=int(getattr(args, "progress_every", 25) or 25),
        trt_cache_root=getattr(args, "trt_cache_root", None),
        throughput_frames=0,
        throughput_warmup_frames=0,
        throughput_queue_depth=1,
        validation_images=validation_images,
        validation_max_images=int(validation_max_images),
        validation_reference_mode=str(
            run.get("validation_reference_mode")
            or getattr(args, "validation_reference_mode", "auto") or "auto"
        ),
        benchmark_task=benchmark_task,
        task_quality_gate=quality_gate,
        trt_runtime=str(
            getattr(args, "trt_runtime", "native_preferred")
            or "native_preferred"
        ),
        native_trt_precision=str(
            getattr(args, "native_trt_precision", "fp16") or "fp16"
        ),
        native_trt_workspace_mb=int(
            getattr(args, "native_trt_workspace_mb", 4096) or 4096
        ),
        native_trt_build_timeout_s=int(
            getattr(args, "native_trt_build_timeout_s", 7200) or 7200
        ),
        native_trt_build=bool(getattr(args, "native_trt_build", True)),
        out_dir_override=out_dir,
        quality_evidence_eval_id=eval_run_id,
        quality_evidence_model_id=model_id,
        quality_evidence_setup_id=setup_id,
        quality_evidence_source_run_id=run_id,
        full_only_quality_identity=full_only_quality_identity,
    )
    if not isinstance(result, Mapping):
        return None
    requests = result.get("task_quality_input_requests_by_variant")
    request = requests.get("full") if isinstance(requests, Mapping) else None
    if (
        not isinstance(request, Mapping)
        or str(request.get("schema") or "")
        != "onnx-splitpoint/central-quality-evaluation-request"
        or str(request.get("variant") or "") != "full"
        or int(request.get("record_count") or 0) < 1
    ):
        print(f"[quality-first][error] {run_id}: Full central-quality request missing")
        return None
    report = {
        "schema": "onnx-splitpoint/full-only-quality-evidence-report",
        "schema_version": 1,
        "quality_evidence_only": True,
        "performance_claims_emitted": False,
        "status": str(request.get("status") or "pending_central_evaluation"),
        "eval_run_id": eval_run_id,
        "model_id": model_id,
        "setup_id": setup_id,
        "source_run_id": run_id,
        "variant": "full",
        "backend": backend,
        "execution_role": "full_quality_only",
        "quality_ids": list(run.get("quality_canary_endpoint_ids") or []),
        "record_count": int(request.get("record_count") or 0),
        "request": dict(request),
    }
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    _write_json(out_dir / "full_quality_evidence_report.json", report)
    # This execution is a semantic producer, not a benchmark.  Keep the
    # portable request/candidate files but remove all benchmark-shaped reports
    # so result ingestion cannot materialise an accidental performance row.
    for stale in (
        "validation_report.json", "validation_report.png",
        "validation_report.pdf",
    ):
        (out_dir / stale).unlink(missing_ok=True)
    return report


def _deepx_verified_suite_run_identity(
    benchmark_set: Mapping[str, Any], run: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> Dict[str, str]:
    """Bind a DeepX run to the exact model/backend/variant in its suite."""
    model_id = str(benchmark_set.get("model_name") or "").strip()
    model_ref = str(benchmark_set.get("model") or "").strip()
    artifact_manifest = (
        benchmark_set.get("artifact_manifest")
        if isinstance(benchmark_set.get("artifact_manifest"), Mapping) else {}
    )
    manifest_files = (
        artifact_manifest.get("files")
        if isinstance(artifact_manifest.get("files"), Mapping) else {}
    )
    manifest_counts = (
        artifact_manifest.get("counts")
        if isinstance(artifact_manifest.get("counts"), Mapping) else {}
    )
    declared_models = list(manifest_files.get("models") or [])
    declared_model_id = str(benchmark_set.get("model_id") or "").strip()
    if (
        benchmark_set.get("schema") != "onnx-splitpoint/benchmark-set"
        or int(benchmark_set.get("schema_version") or 0) != 2
        or not model_id or model_id in {".", ".."}
        or re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+", model_id) is None
        or model_ref != f"models/{model_id}.onnx"
        or (declared_model_id and declared_model_id != model_id)
        or artifact_manifest.get("schema")
        != "onnx-splitpoint/benchmark-set"
        or int(artifact_manifest.get("schema_version") or 0) != 2
        or declared_models != [model_ref]
        or manifest_counts.get("models") != 1
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_suite_model_identity_invalid")

    stage1 = run.get("stage1") if isinstance(run.get("stage1"), Mapping) else {}
    stage2 = run.get("stage2") if isinstance(run.get("stage2"), Mapping) else {}
    backends = {
        str(value or "").strip()
        for value in (
            run.get("backend"), run.get("provider"),
            stage1.get("backend"), stage2.get("backend"),
        )
        if str(value or "").strip()
    }
    raw_variants = run.get("variants")
    variants = {
        str(value or "").strip()
        for value in (
            [run.get("variant")]
            + (list(raw_variants) if isinstance(raw_variants, (list, tuple)) else [])
        )
        if str(value or "").strip()
    }
    if len(backends) != 1 or len(variants) != 1:
        raise RuntimeError("deepx_suite_run_identity_missing_or_conflicting")
    return {
        "model_id": model_id,
        "backend": next(iter(backends)),
        "variant": next(iter(variants)),
    }


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--provider", default="auto", choices=["auto", "cpu", "cuda", "tensorrt", "deepx_m1", "deepx", "dx_m1", "hailo8", "hailo10", "hailo10h", "hailo10n"], help="Execution provider/backend override. In plan mode this filters plan rows, e.g. deepx_m1 for a quick DeepX-full smoke.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--plan",
        default=None,
        help="Optional benchmark plan JSON (default: benchmark_plan.json if present). If set, runs all runs listed in the plan.",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--run-id", default=None, help="If using --plan, run only this run id.")
    parser.add_argument("--run-ids", default=None, help="Comma-separated plan run ids. Used by EvalRun to upload a suite once per physical setup.")
    parser.add_argument("--quality-only-run-ids", default="", help="Scheduler-owned comma-separated run ids that stop after setup-local quality evidence and must not emit performance rows.")
    parser.add_argument("--quality-evidence-eval-id", default=os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_EVAL_RUN_ID", ""), help="Exact EvalRun ID sealed into setup-local TensorRT quality evidence.")
    parser.add_argument("--quality-evidence-setup-id", default=os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_SETUP_ID", ""), help="Exact physical hardware-target ID (remote flat_prefix) sealed into TensorRT quality evidence.")
    parser.add_argument("--quality-evidence-model-id", default=os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_MODEL_ID", ""), help="Optional explicit model ID; defaults to benchmark_set.json.")
    parser.add_argument("--quality-evidence-endpoint-id", default=os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_QUALITY_ENDPOINT_ID", ""), help="Scheduler-materialized setup-local TensorRT Full quality endpoint ID.")
    parser.add_argument("--energy-target-case", "--case", "--only-case", dest="energy_target_case", default="", help="Restrict execution to one case id/folder such as b066. --energy-target-case is kept as the u.RECS alias.")
    parser.add_argument("--energy-target-variant", default="", help="u.RECS measurement-only helper: restrict execution to one variant such as full or composed.")
    parser.add_argument("--list-runs", action="store_true", help="List run ids from the plan and exit.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--image",
        default="default",
        help=(
            "Image for CV inputs. Either a path or one of: 'default'|'coco'|'imagenet'. "
            "(Passed through to per-case runners.)"
        ),
    )
    parser.add_argument("--preset", default="auto", choices=["auto", "classification", "detection"], help="Output agreement preset.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--image-scale",
        default="auto",
        choices=["auto", "norm", "raw", "imagenet", "clip"],
        help="Input image scaling mode for the runner. In plan-mode, runs can override this via run.image_scale.",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--validation-images", default="", help="Optional semantic-validation dataset source (detection image list / classification labeled dataset). In plan-mode, runs can override this via run.validation_images.")
    parser.add_argument("--validation-max-images", type=int, default=0, help="Max samples from --validation-images to use per case (0 disables dataset semantic validation for classification; detection falls back to prepared COCO-50 when appropriate).")
    parser.add_argument("--validation-reference-mode", default="auto", choices=["auto", "same_backend_full", "cpu_full", "annotations"], help="Reference policy for semantic validation. In plan-mode, runs can override this via run.validation_reference_mode.")
    parser.add_argument("--benchmark-task", default="auto", choices=["auto", "detection", "classification"], help="Task-aware validation/reporting mode. In plan-mode, runs can override this via run.benchmark_task.")
    parser.add_argument("--mini-coco-ap50", action="store_true", help="Optional report-only Mini-COCO AP50 on the semantic validation set for detection tasks. In plan-mode, runs can override this via run.mini_coco_ap50.")
    parser.add_argument("--mini-classification-eval", action="store_true", help="Optional report-only Top-1/Top-5 classification summary on the semantic validation set. In plan-mode, runs can override this via run.mini_classification_eval.")
    parser.add_argument("--warmup", type=int, default=5)
    parser.add_argument("--runs", type=int, default=10)
    parser.add_argument("--phase-runs", type=int, default=5, help="Forward v42 diagnostic phase timing runs to per-case runners (0 disables).")
    parser.add_argument("--throughput-frames", type=int, default=24, help="Measured streaming/interleaving frames for heterogeneous stage1→stage2 pipelines (0 disables).")
    parser.add_argument("--throughput-warmup-frames", type=int, default=6, help="Warmup frames before measured streaming/interleaving throughput.")
    parser.add_argument("--throughput-queue-depth", type=int, default=2, help="Queue depth for the streaming/interleaving throughput probe.")
    parser.add_argument("--verbose-runs", action="store_true", help="Forward --verbose-runs to the per-case runner.")
    parser.add_argument("--progress-every", type=int, default=25, help="Forward sparse progress reporting to the per-case runner (0 disables it).")
    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=int, default=None, help="Per-case timeout in seconds.")
    parser.add_argument("--resume", action="store_true", help="Reuse existing per-case results_<run_id>/validation_report.json files and skip already finished cases.")
    parser.add_argument("--trt-cache-root", default=None, help="Optional stable model TensorRT cache root. Full engines use this root directly; split engines use <root>/<case_dir>/ so Full is reusable across split changes while Part1/Part2 remain split-bound.")
    parser.add_argument("--trt-runtime", "--trt-runtime-mode", dest="trt_runtime", default=os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_TRT_RUNTIME", "native_preferred"), choices=["ort", "native", "native_preferred"], help="TensorRT runtime passed to per-case runners. native_preferred uses native .engine when possible and falls back to ORT TRT EP. --trt-runtime-mode is accepted as a backwards-compatible alias.")
    parser.add_argument("--native-trt-precision", default=os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_NATIVE_TRT_PRECISION", "fp16"), choices=["fp32", "fp16", "int8", "uint8_cast_fp16", "uint8_dequant_fp16", "float32_layout_fp16"], help="Native TRT engine precision/tag. Quality-FIRST split runs materialize the exact boundary bridge from Part1 metadata.")
    parser.add_argument("--native-trt-build", dest="native_trt_build", action="store_true", default=os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_NATIVE_TRT_BUILD", "1") not in {"0", "false", "False"}, help="Build missing native TRT engines on demand.")
    parser.add_argument("--no-native-trt-build", dest="native_trt_build", action="store_false", help="Disable native TRT on-demand builds.")
    parser.add_argument("--boundary-mode", default=os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_BOUNDARY_MODE", "canonical_float32"), choices=["canonical_float32", "canonical_float16", "raw_uint8_hailo", "raw_uint8_to_float32", "quantized_int8_contract", "uint8_cast_bridge", "auto"], help="Boundary contract passed to per-case runners. raw_uint8_hailo/quantized_int8_contract require a matching native TRT uint8/int8 input engine. raw_uint8_to_float32 is an experimental Hailo raw-output smoke path for Paper-style handoff diagnostics.")
    parser.add_argument("--handoff-profile", default=os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_HANDOFF_PROFILE", "off"), choices=["off", "sampled", "diagnostic"], help="Optional handoff diagnostics for optimization runs.")
    parser.add_argument("--streaming-impl", default=os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_STREAMING_IMPL", "auto"), choices=["auto", "generic", "native_fifo"], help="Streaming implementation passed to per-case runners. auto uses native_fifo for native TensorRT hetero runs. native_fifo avoids defensive FIFO deep-copies and reports paper-style P1/P2 thread timing.")
    parser.add_argument("--objective", default="composed", choices=["composed", "sum_parts", "max_parts", "pipeline_cycle", "cycle", "throughput", "full"], help="Objective for ranking/summaries. Use pipeline_cycle/cycle for interleaved throughput estimates.")
    parser.add_argument("--topk", type=int, default=10, help="Top-k for highlights/tables.")
    parser.add_argument("--no-csv", action="store_true", help="Do not export CSV.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-plot",
        action="store_true",
        help="Disable plot generation (paper figures / overview plots).",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--energy-measurement-only", action="store_true", help="Measurement-only mode for u.RECS windows: run latency/streaming loops but skip semantic validation, visualizations and report plots.")
    parser.add_argument("--prepared-feed-image", default="", help="Exact single image used for prepared-feed performance and energy loops. Independent of dataset semantic validation.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--prepared-input-manifest", default="",
        help=(
            "DeepX Full only: exact native_full_semantic_dump input manifest. "
            "The prepared-feed loop replays its sealed runtime tensor bytes and "
            "refuses to reconstruct input from an image."
        ),
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if bool(getattr(args, "energy_measurement_only", False)):
        # u.RECS windows should measure inference loops only.  Keep the small
        # benchmark_results JSON/CSV files needed by the caller, but suppress
        # semantic datasets, visualization and heavy report generation.
        args.no_plot = True
        args.mini_coco_ap50 = False
        args.mini_classification_eval = False
        args.validation_images = ""
        args.validation_max_images = 0
        print("[energy] measurement-only mode: semantic datasets/reports/figures disabled")

    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    # v60 replaces the former per-run summary/table/figure families with one
    # canonical suite-level scientific_report/ tree. Remove stale artefacts
    # before execution so resume/rerun bundles cannot expose mixed schemas.
    removed_legacy = _v60_cleanup_legacy(root)
    if removed_legacy:
        print(f"[v60] Removed {len(removed_legacy)} superseded report artefact(s)")
    bench = _read_json(root / "__BENCH_JSON__")
    cases: List[Dict[str, Any]] = bench.get("cases", [])
    cli_case_filter = _parse_case_filter_values(getattr(args, "case_filter", []) or [])
    if not cli_case_filter and str(getattr(args, "energy_target_case", "") or "").strip():
        # v59ai: --case/--only-case are accepted aliases for energy_target_case
        # in older generated suites. Treat the value as a normal case filter as well.
        cli_case_filter = _parse_case_filter_values(str(getattr(args, "energy_target_case", "") or ""))
    if cli_case_filter:
        before_cases = len(cases)
        cases = _filter_cases_cli(cases, cli_case_filter)
        print(f"[case-filter] selected {len(cases)}/{before_cases} case(s): {sorted(cli_case_filter)}")
        if not cases:
            print(f"[err] --case selected no cases: {sorted(cli_case_filter)}")
            return 2
    if not cases:
        print("[warn] __BENCH_JSON__ has no cases")
        return 1

    energy_target_case = str((getattr(args, "case_filter", "") or getattr(args, "energy_target_case", "") or "")).strip()
    energy_target_variant = str(getattr(args, "energy_target_variant", "") or "").strip().lower()

    def _case_matches_energy_target(c: Dict[str, Any], case_dir_key: object) -> bool:
        if not energy_target_case:
            return True
        wanted = energy_target_case.strip().lower()
        labels = set()
        for key in ("case_id", "id", "name", "folder", "case_dir"):
            val = c.get(key)
            if val is not None:
                labels.add(str(val).strip().lower())
        b = c.get("boundary")
        if b is not None:
            try:
                labels.add(f"b{int(b):03d}")
                labels.add(str(int(b)))
            except Exception:
                labels.add(str(b).strip().lower())
        if case_dir_key is not None:
            labels.add(str(case_dir_key).strip().lower())
            labels.add(Path(str(case_dir_key)).name.strip().lower())
        return wanted in labels

    def _apply_energy_target_variant(run_id: str, variants_eff):
        if not energy_target_variant:
            return variants_eff
        target = energy_target_variant.strip().lower()
        if target not in {"full", "part1", "part2", "composed"}:
            print(f"[warn] invalid --energy-target-variant={target!r}; ignoring")
            return variants_eff
        if variants_eff is not None:
            lowered = [str(v).strip().lower() for v in variants_eff]
            if target not in lowered:
                print(f"[energy] run {run_id}: requested variant {target!r} not available in {lowered}; skipping case")
                return []
        return [target]

    if energy_target_case or energy_target_variant:
        print(f"[case-filter] target filter: case={energy_target_case or '*'} variant={energy_target_variant or '*'}")
    suite_hailo_hefs = ((bench.get("hailo") or {}).get("hefs") if isinstance(bench.get("hailo"), dict) else None)
    suite_hailo_hefs = suite_hailo_hefs if isinstance(suite_hailo_hefs, dict) else {}

    # Decide between plan-mode and single provider mode.
    plan_path: Optional[Path] = None
    if args.plan:
        plan_path = (root / args.plan).resolve() if not os.path.isabs(args.plan) else Path(args.plan)
    else:
        # Auto-enable plan mode only if the user did not explicitly request a provider.
        # (provider='auto' is the default)
        default_plan = root / "benchmark_plan.json"
        if args.provider == "auto" and default_plan.exists():
            plan_path = default_plan

    plan: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    runs_plan: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    if plan_path is not None and plan_path.exists():
        try:
            plan = _read_json(plan_path)
            if isinstance(plan, dict) and isinstance(plan.get("runs"), list):
                runs_plan = [r for r in plan.get("runs") if isinstance(r, dict)]
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[warn] Failed to read plan file {plan_path}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
            plan = None
            runs_plan = []

    def _plan_run_deferred(row: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        if bool(row.get("deferred")):
            return True
        status = str(row.get("status") or row.get("build_status") or "").strip().lower()
        return status in {"deferred", "deferred_cold_build", "deferred_cold_full_cache_miss"}

    if args.list_runs:
        if not runs_plan:
            print("(no plan / no runs)")
            return 0
        for r in runs_plan:
            rid = r.get("id") or r.get("name") or "(unnamed)"
            rty = r.get("type") or r.get("kind") or "?"
            suffix = "\tdeferred" if _plan_run_deferred(r) else ""
            print(f"{rid}\t{rty}{suffix}")
        return 0

    if runs_plan:
        deferred_rows = [r for r in runs_plan if _plan_run_deferred(r)]
        if deferred_rows:
            print("[plan] skipping intentionally deferred runs: " + ",".join(str(r.get("id") or r.get("name") or "") for r in deferred_rows))
            runs_plan = [r for r in runs_plan if not _plan_run_deferred(r)]
        # Optional filter
        selected_run_ids = []
        if args.run_ids:
            selected_run_ids.extend([x.strip() for x in str(args.run_ids).split(",") if x.strip()])
        if args.run_id:
            selected_run_ids.append(str(args.run_id).strip())
        if selected_run_ids:
            selected_set = {
                _canonical_plan_run_id(value)
                for value in selected_run_ids
            }
            runs_plan = [
                r for r in runs_plan
                if _canonical_plan_run_id(
                    r.get("id") or r.get("name") or ""
                ) in selected_set
            ]
            if not runs_plan:
                print(f"[err] run ids not found in plan: {','.join(selected_run_ids)}")
                return 2
            print(f"[plan] selected run ids: {','.join(str(r.get('id') or r.get('name') or '') for r in runs_plan)}")
        elif str(args.provider or "auto").strip().lower() not in {"", "auto"}:
            _before = len(runs_plan)
            runs_plan = [r for r in runs_plan if _run_matches_provider_override(r, args.provider)]
            print(f"[plan] provider override {args.provider!r}: selected {len(runs_plan)}/{_before} plan runs")
            if not runs_plan:
                print(f"[err] provider override selected no plan runs: {args.provider}")
                return 2
        runs = runs_plan
    else:
        # Fallback: single provider run
        runs = [{"id": f"ort_{args.provider}", "type": "onnxruntime", "provider": args.provider, "image_scale": args.image_scale}]

    quality_only_run_ids = {
        _canonical_plan_run_id(value)
        for value in str(
            getattr(args, "quality_only_run_ids", "") or ""
        ).split(",")
        if _canonical_plan_run_id(value)
    }
    available_run_ids = {
        _canonical_plan_run_id(
            run.get("id") or run.get("name") or ""
        )
        for run in runs if isinstance(run, dict)
    }
    missing_quality_only = quality_only_run_ids - available_run_ids
    if missing_quality_only:
        print(
            "[quality-first][error] quality-only run ids are not selected: "
            + ",".join(sorted(missing_quality_only))
        )
        return 2
    for run in runs:
        run_id = str(run.get("id") or run.get("name") or "").strip()
        canonical_run_id = _canonical_plan_run_id(run_id)
        if canonical_run_id not in quality_only_run_ids:
            continue
        stage1 = _canonical_full_backend(_resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage1")))
        stage2 = _canonical_full_backend(_resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage2")))
        variants = _normalize_variants(run.get("variants"))
        declared_full_only = bool(
            str(run.get("execution_scope") or "") == "full_only"
            and str(run.get("execution_role") or "")
            == "full_quality_only"
            and run.get("performance_claims_emitted") is False
            and variants == ["full"]
            and stage1 and stage1 == stage2 == _full_backend_for_run(run)
        )
        legacy_trt_quality_only = bool(
            run_id == "ort_tensorrt"
            and _full_backend_for_run(run) == "tensorrt"
            and stage1 in {"", "tensorrt"}
            and stage2 in {"", "tensorrt"}
        )
        if not (declared_full_only or legacy_trt_quality_only):
            print(
                "[quality-first][error] quality-only selection is not the "
                "exact declared Full-only recipe: " + run_id
            )
            return 2

    # Full is a model/backend-level measurement, not a split-case companion.
    # Make its single physical owner explicit before any provider branch starts.
    runs = _assign_full_baseline_owners(runs)
    runs, quality_companion_errors = (
        _bind_native_full_trt_quality_companion(
            runs,
            args=args,
            quality_only_run_ids=quality_only_run_ids,
        )
    )
    if quality_companion_errors:
        print(
            "[quality-first][preflight][error] "
            + ",".join(quality_companion_errors)
        )
        return 2

    user_case_filter = _parse_case_filter(getattr(args, "case_filter", "") or "")
    if user_case_filter:
        print(f"[case-filter] selected cases: {', '.join(user_case_filter)}")

    # Execute each run.
    # Robust exit code policy:
    # - If at least one configured run produced results, return rc=0 (success)
    #   and write a status file listing runs with no results.
    # - If *no* run produced results, return rc=2.
    overall_rc = 0
    any_rows = False
    any_quality_evidence = False
    quality_evidence_reports: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    failed_runs: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    rows_by_tag: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}
    quality_companion_identities: set[Tuple[str, str, str]] = set()
    for run in runs:
        rtype = str(run.get("type") or run.get("kind") or "onnxruntime").strip().lower()
        run_id = str(run.get("id") or run.get("name") or rtype).strip() or rtype
        cpu_reference_requested = str(os.environ.get("ONNX_SPLITPOINT_CPU_REFERENCE_ONLY", "")).strip().lower() in {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}
        if cpu_reference_requested and (run_id != "management_cpu_reference" or rtype != "onnxruntime"):
            raise RuntimeError("management_cpu_reference_context_invalid:selected_run_or_run_type")
        if run_id == "management_cpu_reference" and not cpu_reference_requested:
            raise RuntimeError("management_cpu_reference_context_missing:CPU_REFERENCE_ONLY")
        quality_only_requested = (
            _canonical_plan_run_id(run_id) in quality_only_run_ids
        )

        if (
            run.get("_native_full_trt_quality_companion") is True
            and not bool(getattr(args, "energy_measurement_only", False))
        ):
            companion_identity = (
                str(getattr(args, "quality_evidence_eval_id", "") or "").strip(),
                str(getattr(args, "quality_evidence_setup_id", "") or "").strip(),
                str(
                    getattr(args, "quality_evidence_model_id", "")
                    or bench.get("model_id") or bench.get("model")
                    or (plan or {}).get("model_id") or ""
                ).strip(),
            )
            execute_companion = _claim_native_full_trt_quality_companion(
                quality_companion_identities, companion_identity,
            )
            if execute_companion:
                companion_cases = _cases_for_run(
                    _filter_cases_for_user_selection(cases, user_case_filter), run,
                )
                quality_companion = _run_native_full_trt_quality_companion(
                    root=root,
                    bench=bench,
                    plan=plan,
                    run=run,
                    run_cases=companion_cases,
                    args=args,
                )
            else:
                quality_companion = {}
                print(
                    f"[quality-first] {run_id}: skipped_same_identity "
                    f"eval/setup/model={companion_identity}"
                )
            if execute_companion and (
                not isinstance(quality_companion, dict) or not quality_companion
            ):
                failed_runs.append({
                    "run_id": run_id,
                    "type": rtype,
                    "reason": "native_full_tensorrt_quality_evidence_failed",
                })
                print(
                    f"[quality-first][error] {run_id}: setup-local Native Full "
                    "TensorRT quality evidence failed closed"
                )
                return 2
            elif execute_companion:
                any_quality_evidence = True
                quality_evidence_reports.append(dict(quality_companion))
                print(
                    f"[quality-first] {run_id}: emitted "
                    f"{quality_companion.get('record_count')} records without performance claims"
                )

            # Hailo setup groups need the exact setup-local TRT producer for
            # the central quality join, but only the DeepX group owns the
            # model-wide TRT latency/FPS row. Stop after the sealed quality
            # report for a scheduler-declared quality-only run.
            if quality_only_requested:
                print(
                    f"[quality-first] {run_id}: quality-only dispatch complete; "
                    "performance execution intentionally skipped"
                )
                continue

        if quality_only_requested:
            endpoint_report = _run_declared_full_quality_only_endpoint(
                root=root,
                bench=bench,
                plan=plan,
                run=run,
                run_cases=_cases_for_run(
                    _filter_cases_for_user_selection(
                        cases, user_case_filter,
                    ),
                    run,
                ),
                args=args,
            )
            if not isinstance(endpoint_report, Mapping):
                print(
                    f"[quality-first][error] {run_id}: Full-only quality "
                    "endpoint failed closed"
                )
                return 2
            any_quality_evidence = True
            quality_evidence_reports.append(dict(endpoint_report))
            print(
                f"[quality-first] {run_id}: emitted "
                f"{endpoint_report.get('record_count')} Full records without "
                "performance claims"
            )
            continue

        if rtype in {"onnxruntime", "ort"}:
            provider = str(run.get("provider") or args.provider).strip().lower()
            if provider not in {"auto", "cpu", "cuda", "tensorrt"}:
                print(f"[warn] Unknown provider '{provider}' for run {run_id}; skipping")
                continue
            # Optional stage config / variants (used for matrix-style runs).
            stage1_tok = _resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage1")) or provider
            stage2_tok = _resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage2")) or provider
            variants = _normalize_variants(run.get("variants"))

            tag = f"{run_id}_{args.preset}"
            rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
            run_cases = _cases_for_run(
                _filter_cases_for_user_selection(cases, user_case_filter), run,
            )
            if not run_cases:
                print(f"[warn] run {run_id!r} selected no cases; skipping")
                failed_runs.append({"run_id": run_id, "type": rtype, "provider": provider, "reason": "case_filter_empty"})
                continue
            provider_reason = _provider_unavailable_reason(
                _native_trt_ort_preflight_tokens([provider], str(getattr(args, "trt_runtime", "native_preferred") or "native_preferred"))
            )
            if provider_reason:
                print(f"[preflight] run {run_id!r}: provider unavailable: {provider_reason}")
                rows = [_provider_unavailable_row(run_id, provider, provider_reason)]
                failed_runs.append({"run_id": run_id, "type": rtype, "provider": provider, "reason": provider_reason.get("skip_reason") or "provider_unavailable"})
                results_json = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.json"
                _augment_result_rows_throughput_semantics(rows)
                _write_json(results_json, rows)
                rows_by_tag[str(tag)] = list(rows)
                print(f"Wrote {results_json.name}")
                if not args.no_csv:
                    results_csv = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.csv"
                    _write_results_csv(results_csv, rows)
                    print(f"Wrote {results_csv.name}")
                continue
            for i, c in enumerate(run_cases):
                b = c.get("boundary")
                case_dir_key = c.get("case_dir") or c.get("folder")
                if case_dir_key is None:
                    case_dir_key = f"b{int(b):03d}" if b is not None else "case"
                rel = Path(str(case_dir_key))
                case_dir = (root / rel).resolve()
                variants_eff = _prune_case_variants_for_run(run, variants, c, suite_hailo_hefs)
                if not _case_matches_energy_target(c, case_dir_key):
                    if energy_target_case:
                        print(f"[case-filter] skipping {case_dir.name}: does not match target case {energy_target_case}")
                    continue
                variants_eff = _apply_energy_target_variant(run_id, variants_eff)
                variants_eff = _apply_full_owner_case_variant(run, variants_eff, i)
                if (variants is not None or energy_target_variant) and not variants_eff:
                    print(f"\n[{run_id}] [{i+1}/{len(run_cases)}] Skipping {case_dir.name} (no usable variants after Hailo availability pruning)")
                    continue

                print(f"\n[{run_id}] [{i+1}/{len(run_cases)}] Running {case_dir.name} (provider={provider})")

                # Per-run image scaling (passed through to run_split_onnxruntime.py).
                image_scale = str(run.get("image_scale") or getattr(args, "image_scale", "auto")).strip().lower()
                if image_scale not in ("auto", "norm", "raw", "imagenet", "clip"):
                    print(f"[warn] invalid image_scale={image_scale!r} in plan for run {run_id!r}; falling back to 'auto'")
                    image_scale = "auto"
                cli_validation_images = str(getattr(args, "validation_images", "") or "").strip()
                if cli_validation_images:
                    validation_images = cli_validation_images
                    validation_max_images = int(getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0)
                else:
                    validation_images = str(run.get("validation_images") or "").strip()
                    validation_max_images = _effective_cli_validation_max(args, run)
                # v59ai: CLI max-images is a smoke-test override even when the plan
                # already contains validation_max_images=50 from COCO-50 defaults.
                if (not bool(run.get("validation_budget_authoritative"))) and int(getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0) > 0:
                    validation_max_images = int(getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0)
                validation_reference_mode = str(run.get("validation_reference_mode") or getattr(args, "validation_reference_mode", "auto") or "auto").strip().lower()
                if validation_reference_mode not in ("auto", "same_backend_full", "cpu_full", "annotations"):
                    validation_reference_mode = "auto"
                benchmark_task = str(run.get("benchmark_task") or getattr(args, "benchmark_task", "auto") or "auto").strip().lower()
                if benchmark_task not in ("auto", "detection", "classification"):
                    benchmark_task = "auto"
                mini_coco_ap50 = bool(run.get("mini_coco_ap50") if "mini_coco_ap50" in run else getattr(args, "mini_coco_ap50", False))
                mini_classification_eval = bool(run.get("mini_classification_eval") if "mini_classification_eval" in run else getattr(args, "mini_classification_eval", False))
                # v52d validation routing guard: never run COCO AP50 on
                # classification and never run classification top-k on detection.
                if benchmark_task == "classification":
                    mini_coco_ap50 = False
                    if str(validation_images or "").strip():
                        mini_classification_eval = True
                elif benchmark_task == "detection":
                    mini_classification_eval = False
                    if str(validation_images or "").strip():
                        mini_coco_ap50 = True
                else:
                    mini_coco_ap50 = False
                    mini_classification_eval = False

                split_quality = _prepare_native_split_quality_for_case(
                    root=root, case_dir=case_dir, bench=bench, plan=plan,
                    run=run, args=args, run_id=run_id,
                    stage1=stage1_tok, stage2=stage2_tok,
                    variants=variants_eff,
                )
                r = _run_case(
                    case_dir,
                    run_id=run_id,
                    provider=provider,
                    stage1=stage1_tok,
                    stage2=stage2_tok,
                    variants=variants_eff,
                    image=args.image,
                    preset=args.preset,
                    image_scale=image_scale,
                    warmup=args.warmup,
                    runs=args.runs,
                    phase_runs=args.phase_runs,
                    timeout_s=args.timeout,
                    case_meta={
                        **c, "_run": run,
                        "_management_reference_plan": plan,
                        # The real generator stores the ONNX path in model,
                        # and its logical name in model_name. An explicit
                        # model_id remains authoritative; never stem-match a
                        # path to make a conflicting reference pass.
                        "_suite_model_id": str(bench.get("model_id") or bench.get("model_name") or bench.get("model") or ""),
                        "_suite_model_identity": {
                            key: bench.get(key) for key in ("model_id", "model_name", "model")
                        },
                    },
                    resume=getattr(args, "resume", False),
                    verbose_runs=args.verbose_runs,
                    progress_every=args.progress_every,
                    trt_cache_root=args.trt_cache_root,
                    throughput_frames=args.throughput_frames,
                    throughput_warmup_frames=args.throughput_warmup_frames,
                    throughput_queue_depth=args.throughput_queue_depth,
                    validation_images=validation_images,
                    validation_max_images=validation_max_images,
                    validation_reference_mode=validation_reference_mode,
                    mini_coco_ap50=mini_coco_ap50,
                    benchmark_task=benchmark_task,
                    mini_classification_eval=mini_classification_eval,
                    task_quality_gate=(dict(run.get("task_quality_gate") or {}) if isinstance(run.get("task_quality_gate"), dict) else (dict((plan or {}).get("quality_gate") or {}) if isinstance((plan or {}).get("quality_gate"), dict) else {})),
                    trt_runtime=str(getattr(args, "trt_runtime", "native_preferred") or "native_preferred"),
                    native_trt_precision=str(
                        (split_quality or {}).get("precision")
                        or getattr(args, "native_trt_precision", "fp16") or "fp16"
                    ),
                    native_trt_workspace_mb=int(getattr(args, "native_trt_workspace_mb", 4096) or 4096),
                    native_trt_build_timeout_s=int(getattr(args, "native_trt_build_timeout_s", 7200) or 7200),
                    native_trt_build=(
                        False if split_quality
                        else bool(getattr(args, "native_trt_build", True))
                    ),
                    boundary_mode=str(
                        (split_quality or {}).get("boundary_mode")
                        or getattr(args, "boundary_mode", "canonical_float32")
                        or "canonical_float32"
                    ),
                    handoff_profile=str(getattr(args, "handoff_profile", "off") or "off"),
                    streaming_impl=str(getattr(args, "streaming_impl", "auto") or "auto"),
                    measurement_only=bool(getattr(args, "energy_measurement_only", False)),
                    native_split_quality_binding=str(
                        (split_quality or {}).get("binding_path") or ""
                    ),
                    native_split_quality_preparation_failure=(
                        (split_quality or {}).get("failure")
                        if (split_quality or {}).get("status") == "technical_failure"
                        else None
                    ),
                    quality_evidence_eval_id=str(
                        getattr(args, "quality_evidence_eval_id", "") or ""
                    ),
                    quality_evidence_model_id=str(
                        getattr(args, "quality_evidence_model_id", "")
                        or bench.get("model_id") or bench.get("model") or ""
                    ),
                    quality_evidence_setup_id=str(
                        getattr(args, "quality_evidence_setup_id", "") or ""
                    ),
                    quality_evidence_source_run_id=run_id,
                )
                if r is not None:
                    r["full_baseline_owner"] = run.get("_full_baseline_owner") is True
                    r["full_baseline_owner_run_id"] = str(run.get("_full_baseline_owner_run_id") or "")
                    r["full_baseline_owner_backend"] = str(run.get("_full_baseline_owner_backend") or "")
                    r["full_measurement_requested_in_case"] = bool(variants_eff and "full" in variants_eff)
                    r.setdefault("evaluation_role", str(run.get("evaluation_role") or (plan or {}).get("evaluation_role") or "development"))
                    r.setdefault("validation_tier", str(run.get("validation_tier") or (plan or {}).get("validation_tier") or "screening"))
                    r.setdefault("candidate_universe_complete", bool(run.get("candidate_universe_complete") if run.get("candidate_universe_complete") is not None else (plan or {}).get("candidate_universe_complete", False)))
                    if str(run.get("holdout_group") or "").strip():
                        r.setdefault("holdout_group", str(run.get("holdout_group")).strip())
                    rows.append(r)
                    if (split_quality or {}).get("status") == "technical_failure":
                        failed_runs.append({
                            "run_id": run_id,
                            "type": rtype,
                            "provider": provider,
                            "case_id": case_dir.name,
                            "reason": "native_split_quality_binding_materialization_failed",
                        })

            if not rows:
                print(f"[warn] No results collected for run {run_id}")
                failed_runs.append({"run_id": run_id, "type": rtype, "provider": provider})

                # Still write placeholder artifacts so UIs/parsers can show the run as "empty".
                results_json = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.json"
                _augment_result_rows_throughput_semantics(rows)
                _write_json(results_json, rows)
                rows_by_tag[str(tag)] = list(rows)
                try:
                    (root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.error.txt").write_text(
                        f"No results collected for run {run_id} (provider={provider}).\n",
                        encoding="utf-8",
                    )
                except Exception:
                    pass
                continue

            any_rows = True

            results_json = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.json"
            _augment_result_rows_throughput_semantics(rows)
            _write_json(results_json, rows)
            rows_by_tag[str(tag)] = list(rows)
            print(f"Wrote {results_json.name}")

            if not args.no_csv:
                results_csv = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.csv"
                _write_results_csv(results_csv, rows)
                print(f"Wrote {results_csv.name}")
            if bool(getattr(args, "energy_measurement_only", False)):
                print(f"[energy] measurement-only: skipped summary/table/figure generation for {tag}")
                continue
            # v60 intentionally defers all presentation output until the complete
            # suite row set is available. Per-tag benchmark_summary_*,
            # benchmark_table_* and paper_figures_* artefacts are superseded.


        elif rtype in {"deepx", "deepx_m1", "dx_m1"}:
            deepx_run = dict(run)
            if not isinstance(deepx_run.get("task_quality_gate"), dict) and isinstance((plan or {}).get("quality_gate"), dict):
                deepx_run["task_quality_gate"] = dict((plan or {}).get("quality_gate") or {})
            deepx_identity = _deepx_verified_suite_run_identity(
                bench, deepx_run,
            )
            rows = _run_deepx_full_run(
                root, deepx_run, args,
                expected_endpoint_identity=deepx_identity,
            )
            tag = f"{run_id}_{args.preset}"
            if rows and any(bool(r.get("runtime_ok")) for r in rows):
