                any_rows = True
            else:
                failed_runs.append({"run_id": run_id, "type": rtype, "provider": "deepx_m1", "reason": "deepx_runtime_failed_or_missing"})
            results_json = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.json"
            _augment_result_rows_throughput_semantics(rows)
            _write_json(results_json, rows)
            rows_by_tag[str(tag)] = list(rows)
            print(f"Wrote {results_json.name}")
            if not args.no_csv:
                results_csv = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.csv"
                _write_results_csv(results_csv, rows)
                print(f"Wrote {results_csv.name}")

        elif rtype in {"pending_deepx_split"}:
            print(f"[info] DeepX split run {run_id!r} is planned but not executable yet: {run.get('reason') or 'part1 DXNN binding pending'}")
            failed_runs.append({"run_id": run_id, "type": rtype, "provider": "deepx_m1", "reason": str(run.get("reason") or "pending_deepx_split_binding")})
            results_json = root / f"benchmark_results_{run_id}_{args.preset}.json"
            _write_json(results_json, [])
            rows_by_tag[f"{run_id}_{args.preset}"] = []


        elif rtype in {"matrix", "split"}:
            # Matrix run: stage1/stage2 can be on different backends (ORT vs Hailo, etc.)
            stage1_tok = _resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage1"))
            stage2_tok = _resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage2"))
            if not stage1_tok or not stage2_tok:
                print(f"[warn] matrix run {run_id!r} is missing stage1/stage2; skipping")
                continue

            # Default provider passed to the per-case runner (acts as a fallback). Use stage1.
            provider_tag = str(run.get("provider") or stage1_tok).strip().lower() or stage1_tok

            variants = _normalize_variants(run.get("variants"))

            tag = f"{run_id}_{args.preset}"
            rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
            run_cases = _cases_for_run(
                _filter_cases_for_user_selection(cases, user_case_filter), run,
            )
            if not run_cases:
                print(f"[warn] matrix run {run_id!r} selected no cases; skipping")
                failed_runs.append({"run_id": run_id, "type": rtype, "provider": provider_tag, "stage1": stage1_tok, "stage2": stage2_tok, "reason": "case_filter_empty"})
                continue
            provider_reason = _provider_unavailable_reason(
                _native_trt_ort_preflight_tokens([stage1_tok, stage2_tok], str(getattr(args, "trt_runtime", "native_preferred") or "native_preferred"))
            )
            if provider_reason:
                print(f"[preflight] matrix run {run_id!r}: provider unavailable: {provider_reason}")
                rows = [_provider_unavailable_row(run_id, provider_tag, provider_reason, stage1=stage1_tok, stage2=stage2_tok)]
                failed_runs.append({"run_id": run_id, "type": rtype, "provider": provider_tag, "stage1": stage1_tok, "stage2": stage2_tok, "reason": provider_reason.get("skip_reason") or "provider_unavailable"})
                results_json = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.json"
                _augment_result_rows_throughput_semantics(rows)
                _write_json(results_json, rows)
                rows_by_tag[str(tag)] = list(rows)
                print(f"Wrote {results_json.name}")
                if not args.no_csv:
                    results_csv = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.csv"
                    _write_results_csv(results_csv, rows)
                    print(f"Wrote {results_csv.name}")
                continue
            for i, c in enumerate(run_cases):
                b = c.get("boundary")
                case_dir_key = c.get("case_dir") or c.get("folder")
                if case_dir_key is None:
                    case_dir_key = f"b{int(b):03d}" if b is not None else "case"
                rel = Path(str(case_dir_key))
                case_dir = (root / rel).resolve()
                variants_eff = _prune_case_variants_for_run(run, variants, c, suite_hailo_hefs)
                if not _case_matches_energy_target(c, case_dir_key):
                    if energy_target_case:
                        print(f"[case-filter] skipping {case_dir.name}: does not match target case {energy_target_case}")
                    continue
                variants_eff = _apply_energy_target_variant(run_id, variants_eff)
                variants_eff = _apply_full_owner_case_variant(run, variants_eff, i)
                if (variants is not None or energy_target_variant) and not variants_eff:
                    print(
                        f"\n[{run_id}] [{i+1}/{len(run_cases)}] Skipping {case_dir.name} "
                        f"(no usable variants after Hailo availability pruning)"
                    )
                    continue

                print(
                    f"\n[{run_id}] [{i+1}/{len(run_cases)}] Running {case_dir.name} "
                    f"(stage1={stage1_tok}, stage2={stage2_tok})"
                )

                # Per-run image scaling (passed through to run_split_onnxruntime.py).
                image_scale = str(run.get("image_scale") or getattr(args, "image_scale", "auto")).strip().lower()
                if image_scale not in ("auto", "norm", "raw", "imagenet", "clip"):
                    print(f"[warn] invalid image_scale={image_scale!r} in plan for run {run_id!r}; falling back to 'auto'")
                    image_scale = "auto"
                cli_validation_images = str(getattr(args, "validation_images", "") or "").strip()
                if cli_validation_images:
                    validation_images = cli_validation_images
                    validation_max_images = int(getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0)
                else:
                    validation_images = str(run.get("validation_images") or "").strip()
                    validation_max_images = _effective_cli_validation_max(args, run)
                # v59ai: CLI max-images is a smoke-test override even when the plan
                # already contains validation_max_images=50 from COCO-50 defaults.
                if (not bool(run.get("validation_budget_authoritative"))) and int(getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0) > 0:
                    validation_max_images = int(getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0)
                validation_reference_mode = str(run.get("validation_reference_mode") or getattr(args, "validation_reference_mode", "auto") or "auto").strip().lower()
                if validation_reference_mode not in ("auto", "same_backend_full", "cpu_full", "annotations"):
                    validation_reference_mode = "auto"
                benchmark_task = str(run.get("benchmark_task") or getattr(args, "benchmark_task", "auto") or "auto").strip().lower()
                if benchmark_task not in ("auto", "detection", "classification"):
                    benchmark_task = "auto"
                mini_coco_ap50 = bool(run.get("mini_coco_ap50") if "mini_coco_ap50" in run else getattr(args, "mini_coco_ap50", False))
                mini_classification_eval = bool(run.get("mini_classification_eval") if "mini_classification_eval" in run else getattr(args, "mini_classification_eval", False))
                # v52d validation routing guard: never run COCO AP50 on
                # classification and never run classification top-k on detection.
                if benchmark_task == "classification":
                    mini_coco_ap50 = False
                    if str(validation_images or "").strip():
                        mini_classification_eval = True
                elif benchmark_task == "detection":
                    mini_classification_eval = False
                    if str(validation_images or "").strip():
                        mini_coco_ap50 = True
                else:
                    mini_coco_ap50 = False
                    mini_classification_eval = False

                split_quality = _prepare_native_split_quality_for_case(
                    root=root, case_dir=case_dir, bench=bench, plan=plan,
                    run=run, args=args, run_id=run_id,
                    stage1=stage1_tok, stage2=stage2_tok,
                    variants=variants_eff,
                )
                r = _run_case(
                    case_dir,
                    run_id=run_id,
                    provider=provider_tag,
                    stage1=stage1_tok,
                    stage2=stage2_tok,
                    variants=variants_eff,
                    image=args.image,
                    preset=args.preset,
                    image_scale=image_scale,
                    warmup=args.warmup,
                    runs=args.runs,
                    phase_runs=args.phase_runs,
                    timeout_s=args.timeout,
                    case_meta=c,
                    resume=getattr(args, "resume", False),
                    verbose_runs=args.verbose_runs,
                    progress_every=args.progress_every,
                    trt_cache_root=args.trt_cache_root,
                    throughput_frames=args.throughput_frames,
                    throughput_warmup_frames=args.throughput_warmup_frames,
                    throughput_queue_depth=args.throughput_queue_depth,
                    validation_images=validation_images,
                    validation_max_images=validation_max_images,
                    validation_reference_mode=validation_reference_mode,
                    mini_coco_ap50=mini_coco_ap50,
                    benchmark_task=benchmark_task,
                    mini_classification_eval=mini_classification_eval,
                    task_quality_gate=(dict(run.get("task_quality_gate") or {}) if isinstance(run.get("task_quality_gate"), dict) else (dict((plan or {}).get("quality_gate") or {}) if isinstance((plan or {}).get("quality_gate"), dict) else {})),
                    trt_runtime=str(getattr(args, "trt_runtime", "native_preferred") or "native_preferred"),
                    native_trt_precision=str(
                        (split_quality or {}).get("precision")
                        or getattr(args, "native_trt_precision", "fp16") or "fp16"
                    ),
                    native_trt_workspace_mb=int(getattr(args, "native_trt_workspace_mb", 4096) or 4096),
                    native_trt_build_timeout_s=int(getattr(args, "native_trt_build_timeout_s", 7200) or 7200),
                    native_trt_build=(
                        False if split_quality
                        else bool(getattr(args, "native_trt_build", True))
                    ),
                    boundary_mode=str(
                        (split_quality or {}).get("boundary_mode")
                        or getattr(args, "boundary_mode", "canonical_float32")
                        or "canonical_float32"
                    ),
                    handoff_profile=str(getattr(args, "handoff_profile", "off") or "off"),
                    streaming_impl=str(getattr(args, "streaming_impl", "auto") or "auto"),
                    measurement_only=bool(getattr(args, "energy_measurement_only", False)),
                    native_split_quality_binding=str(
                        (split_quality or {}).get("binding_path") or ""
                    ),
                    native_split_quality_preparation_failure=(
                        (split_quality or {}).get("failure")
                        if (split_quality or {}).get("status") == "technical_failure"
                        else None
                    ),
                    quality_evidence_eval_id=str(
                        getattr(args, "quality_evidence_eval_id", "") or ""
                    ),
                    quality_evidence_model_id=str(
                        getattr(args, "quality_evidence_model_id", "")
                        or bench.get("model_id") or bench.get("model") or ""
                    ),
                    quality_evidence_setup_id=str(
                        getattr(args, "quality_evidence_setup_id", "") or ""
                    ),
                    quality_evidence_source_run_id=run_id,
                )
                if r is not None:
                    r["full_baseline_owner"] = run.get("_full_baseline_owner") is True
                    r["full_baseline_owner_run_id"] = str(run.get("_full_baseline_owner_run_id") or "")
                    r["full_baseline_owner_backend"] = str(run.get("_full_baseline_owner_backend") or "")
                    r["full_measurement_requested_in_case"] = bool(variants_eff and "full" in variants_eff)
                    rows.append(r)
                    if (split_quality or {}).get("status") == "technical_failure":
                        failed_runs.append({
                            "run_id": run_id,
                            "type": rtype,
                            "provider": provider_tag,
                            "stage1": stage1_tok,
                            "stage2": stage2_tok,
                            "case_id": case_dir.name,
                            "reason": "native_split_quality_binding_materialization_failed",
                        })

            if not rows:
                print(f"[warn] No results collected for run {run_id}")
                failed_runs.append({"run_id": run_id, "type": rtype, "provider": provider_tag, "stage1": stage1_tok, "stage2": stage2_tok})

                # Still write placeholder artifacts so UIs/parsers can show the run as "empty".
                results_json = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.json"
                _augment_result_rows_throughput_semantics(rows)
                _write_json(results_json, rows)
                rows_by_tag[str(tag)] = list(rows)
                try:
                    (root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.error.txt").write_text(
                        f"No results collected for run {run_id} (stage1={stage1_tok}, stage2={stage2_tok}).\n",
                        encoding="utf-8",
                    )
                except Exception:
                    pass
                continue

            any_rows = True

            results_json = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.json"
            _augment_result_rows_throughput_semantics(rows)
            _write_json(results_json, rows)
            rows_by_tag[str(tag)] = list(rows)
            print(f"Wrote {results_json.name}")

            if not args.no_csv:
                results_csv = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.csv"
                _write_results_csv(results_csv, rows)
                print(f"Wrote {results_csv.name}")
            if bool(getattr(args, "energy_measurement_only", False)):
                print(f"[energy] measurement-only: skipped summary/table/figure generation for {tag}")
                continue

            # v60 intentionally defers all presentation output until the complete
            # suite row set is available. Per-tag benchmark_summary_*,
            # benchmark_table_* and paper_figures_* artefacts are superseded.


        elif rtype in {"hailo"}:
            # Hailo benchmark: reuse the per-case runner script, but select a provider tag
            # that the runner interprets as a Hailo backend (e.g. "hailo8", "hailo8l",
            # "hailo10h", "hailo10p").
            hw_arch = str(run.get("hw_arch") or run.get("arch") or run_id or "").strip()
            provider_tag = hw_arch or "hailo"

            stage1_tok = _resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage1")) or provider_tag
            stage2_tok = _resolve_stage_token(run.get("stage2")) or provider_tag
            variants = _normalize_variants(run.get("variants"))

            tag = f"{run_id}_{args.preset}"
            rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
            run_cases = _cases_for_run(
                _filter_cases_for_user_selection(cases, user_case_filter), run,
            )
            if not run_cases:
                print(f"[warn] Hailo run {run_id!r} selected no cases; skipping")
                failed_runs.append({"run_id": run_id, "type": rtype, "provider": provider_tag, "reason": "case_filter_empty"})
                continue
            for i, c in enumerate(run_cases):
                b = c.get("boundary")
                case_dir_key = c.get("case_dir") or c.get("folder")
                if case_dir_key is None:
                    case_dir_key = f"b{int(b):03d}" if b is not None else "case"
                rel = Path(str(case_dir_key))
                case_dir = (root / rel).resolve()
                variants_eff = _prune_case_variants_for_run(run, variants, c, suite_hailo_hefs)
                if not _case_matches_energy_target(c, case_dir_key):
                    if energy_target_case:
                        print(f"[case-filter] skipping {case_dir.name}: does not match target case {energy_target_case}")
                    continue
                variants_eff = _apply_energy_target_variant(run_id, variants_eff)
                variants_eff = _apply_full_owner_case_variant(run, variants_eff, i)
                if (variants is not None or energy_target_variant) and not variants_eff:
                    print(f"\n[{run_id}] [{i+1}/{len(run_cases)}] Skipping {case_dir.name} (no usable variants after Hailo availability pruning)")
                    continue

                print(f"\n[{run_id}] [{i+1}/{len(run_cases)}] Running {case_dir.name} (provider={provider_tag})")

                # Per-run image scaling (passed through to run_split_onnxruntime.py).
                image_scale = str(run.get("image_scale") or getattr(args, "image_scale", "auto")).strip().lower()
                if image_scale not in ("auto", "norm", "raw", "imagenet", "clip"):
                    print(f"[warn] invalid image_scale={image_scale!r} in plan for run {run_id!r}; falling back to 'auto'")
                    image_scale = "auto"
                cli_validation_images = str(getattr(args, "validation_images", "") or "").strip()
                if cli_validation_images:
                    validation_images = cli_validation_images
                    validation_max_images = int(getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0)
                else:
                    validation_images = str(run.get("validation_images") or "").strip()
                    validation_max_images = _effective_cli_validation_max(args, run)
                # v59ai: CLI max-images is a smoke-test override even when the plan
                # already contains validation_max_images=50 from COCO-50 defaults.
                if (not bool(run.get("validation_budget_authoritative"))) and int(getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0) > 0:
                    validation_max_images = int(getattr(args, "validation_max_images", 0) or 0)
                validation_reference_mode = str(run.get("validation_reference_mode") or getattr(args, "validation_reference_mode", "auto") or "auto").strip().lower()
                if validation_reference_mode not in ("auto", "same_backend_full", "cpu_full", "annotations"):
                    validation_reference_mode = "auto"
                benchmark_task = str(run.get("benchmark_task") or getattr(args, "benchmark_task", "auto") or "auto").strip().lower()
                if benchmark_task not in ("auto", "detection", "classification"):
                    benchmark_task = "auto"
                mini_coco_ap50 = bool(run.get("mini_coco_ap50") if "mini_coco_ap50" in run else getattr(args, "mini_coco_ap50", False))
                mini_classification_eval = bool(run.get("mini_classification_eval") if "mini_classification_eval" in run else getattr(args, "mini_classification_eval", False))
                # v52d validation routing guard: never run COCO AP50 on
                # classification and never run classification top-k on detection.
                if benchmark_task == "classification":
                    mini_coco_ap50 = False
                    if str(validation_images or "").strip():
                        mini_classification_eval = True
                elif benchmark_task == "detection":
                    mini_classification_eval = False
                    if str(validation_images or "").strip():
                        mini_coco_ap50 = True
                else:
                    mini_coco_ap50 = False
                    mini_classification_eval = False

                split_quality = _prepare_native_split_quality_for_case(
                    root=root, case_dir=case_dir, bench=bench, plan=plan,
                    run=run, args=args, run_id=run_id,
                    stage1=stage1_tok, stage2=stage2_tok,
                    variants=variants_eff,
                )
                r = _run_case(
                    case_dir,
                    run_id=run_id,
                    provider=provider_tag,
                    stage1=stage1_tok,
                    stage2=stage2_tok,
                    variants=variants_eff,
                    image=args.image,
                    preset=args.preset,
                    image_scale=image_scale,
                    warmup=args.warmup,
                    runs=args.runs,
                    phase_runs=args.phase_runs,
                    timeout_s=args.timeout,
                    case_meta=c,
                    resume=getattr(args, "resume", False),
                    verbose_runs=args.verbose_runs,
                    progress_every=args.progress_every,
                    trt_cache_root=args.trt_cache_root,
                    throughput_frames=args.throughput_frames,
                    throughput_warmup_frames=args.throughput_warmup_frames,
                    throughput_queue_depth=args.throughput_queue_depth,
                    validation_images=validation_images,
                    validation_max_images=validation_max_images,
                    validation_reference_mode=validation_reference_mode,
                    mini_coco_ap50=mini_coco_ap50,
                    benchmark_task=benchmark_task,
                    mini_classification_eval=mini_classification_eval,
                    task_quality_gate=(dict(run.get("task_quality_gate") or {}) if isinstance(run.get("task_quality_gate"), dict) else (dict((plan or {}).get("quality_gate") or {}) if isinstance((plan or {}).get("quality_gate"), dict) else {})),
                    trt_runtime=str(getattr(args, "trt_runtime", "native_preferred") or "native_preferred"),
                    native_trt_precision=str(
                        (split_quality or {}).get("precision")
                        or getattr(args, "native_trt_precision", "fp16") or "fp16"
                    ),
                    native_trt_workspace_mb=int(getattr(args, "native_trt_workspace_mb", 4096) or 4096),
                    native_trt_build_timeout_s=int(getattr(args, "native_trt_build_timeout_s", 7200) or 7200),
                    native_trt_build=(
                        False if split_quality
                        else bool(getattr(args, "native_trt_build", True))
                    ),
                    boundary_mode=str(
                        (split_quality or {}).get("boundary_mode")
                        or getattr(args, "boundary_mode", "canonical_float32")
                        or "canonical_float32"
                    ),
                    handoff_profile=str(getattr(args, "handoff_profile", "off") or "off"),
                    streaming_impl=str(getattr(args, "streaming_impl", "auto") or "auto"),
                    measurement_only=bool(getattr(args, "energy_measurement_only", False)),
                    native_split_quality_binding=str(
                        (split_quality or {}).get("binding_path") or ""
                    ),
                    native_split_quality_preparation_failure=(
                        (split_quality or {}).get("failure")
                        if (split_quality or {}).get("status") == "technical_failure"
                        else None
                    ),
                    quality_evidence_eval_id=str(
                        getattr(args, "quality_evidence_eval_id", "") or ""
                    ),
                    quality_evidence_model_id=str(
                        getattr(args, "quality_evidence_model_id", "")
                        or bench.get("model_id") or bench.get("model") or ""
                    ),
                    quality_evidence_setup_id=str(
                        getattr(args, "quality_evidence_setup_id", "") or ""
                    ),
                    quality_evidence_source_run_id=run_id,
                )
                if r is not None:
                    r["full_baseline_owner"] = run.get("_full_baseline_owner") is True
                    r["full_baseline_owner_run_id"] = str(run.get("_full_baseline_owner_run_id") or "")
                    r["full_baseline_owner_backend"] = str(run.get("_full_baseline_owner_backend") or "")
                    r["full_measurement_requested_in_case"] = bool(variants_eff and "full" in variants_eff)
                    rows.append(r)
                    if (split_quality or {}).get("status") == "technical_failure":
                        failed_runs.append({
                            "run_id": run_id,
                            "type": rtype,
                            "provider": provider_tag,
                            "case_id": case_dir.name,
                            "reason": "native_split_quality_binding_materialization_failed",
                        })

            if not rows:
                print(f"[warn] No results collected for run {run_id}")
                failed_runs.append({"run_id": run_id, "type": rtype, "provider": provider_tag})

                # Still write placeholder artifacts so UIs/parsers can show the run as "empty".
                results_json = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.json"
                _augment_result_rows_throughput_semantics(rows)
                _write_json(results_json, rows)
                rows_by_tag[str(tag)] = list(rows)
                try:
                    (root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.error.txt").write_text(
                        f"No results collected for run {run_id} (provider={provider_tag}).\n",
                        encoding="utf-8",
                    )
                except Exception:
                    pass
                continue

            any_rows = True

            results_json = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.json"
            _augment_result_rows_throughput_semantics(rows)
            _write_json(results_json, rows)
            rows_by_tag[str(tag)] = list(rows)
            print(f"Wrote {results_json.name}")

            if not args.no_csv:
                results_csv = root / f"benchmark_results_{tag}.csv"
                _write_results_csv(results_csv, rows)
                print(f"Wrote {results_csv.name}")
            if bool(getattr(args, "energy_measurement_only", False)):
                print(f"[energy] measurement-only: skipped summary/table/figure generation for {tag}")
                continue
            # v60 intentionally does not create the former per-tag
            # benchmark_summary_*, benchmark_table_* and paper_figures_*
            # families.  The suite-level scientific_report/ subtree below is
            # the single reporting implementation and is generated from the
            # complete row set after all run profiles have finished.
        else:
            print(f"[warn] Unknown run type '{rtype}' for run {run_id}; skipping")
            continue

    if rows_by_tag and bool(getattr(args, "energy_measurement_only", False)):
        print("[energy] measurement-only: skipped canonical scientific report")

    # v60: the canonical report is generated as part of the benchmark run.
    # It replaces the historical per-tag summary/table/figure artefacts and
    # records insufficient screening/hold-out evidence explicitly.
    if rows_by_tag and not bool(getattr(args, "energy_measurement_only", False)):
        try:
            _v60_cleanup_legacy(root)
            _write_v60_scientific_report(root, rows_by_tag, dict(plan or {}), cases=cases)
            print("Wrote scientific_report/ (canonical task-quality, eligibility, frozen Cut Bytes workflow selector plus four alternative ranking baselines, TeX and figures)")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[warn] Failed to write canonical scientific report: {e}")

    # Always write a small status file so UIs can show partial success.
    try:
        status = {
            "any_rows": any_rows,
            "any_quality_evidence": any_quality_evidence,
            "quality_evidence_report_count": len(quality_evidence_reports),
            "quality_evidence_reports": quality_evidence_reports,
            "performance_claims_emitted": bool(any_rows),
            "failed_runs": failed_runs,
            "total_runs": len(runs),
            "total_cases": len(cases),
        }
        status_path = root / "benchmark_suite_status.json"
        _write_json(status_path, status)
        if any_rows:
            try:
                _write_status_matrix(root, runs, preset=args.preset, rows_by_tag=rows_by_tag)
                print("Wrote benchmark_suite_status_matrix.md/csv/json")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[warn] Failed to write benchmark_suite_status_matrix: {e}")
        if failed_runs:
            print(f"[warn] Some runs or cases failed; see {status_path.name}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[warn] Failed to write benchmark_suite_status.json: {e}")

    print("\nDone.")

    # Final exit code.
    if not any_rows and not any_quality_evidence:
        overall_rc = 2
    else:
        overall_rc = 0
    return overall_rc



# v60m: child runtime runners inherit the profile-normalized validation budget.
# Final profiles set the task-specific limit to zero (uncapped).
def _v60m_prepare_validation_env():
    task=str(os.environ.get('SPLITPOINT_MODEL_TASK','')).lower()
    if 'detect' in task or 'yolo' in task:
        v=os.environ.get('SPLITPOINT_VALIDATION_MAX_DETECTION','200')
    else:
        v=os.environ.get('SPLITPOINT_VALIDATION_MAX_CLASSIFICATION','500')
    os.environ.setdefault('SPLITPOINT_VALIDATION_MAX_ITEMS',v)
    os.environ.setdefault('SPLITPOINT_TASK_QUALITY_CACHE','1')
_v60m_prepare_validation_env()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
